`AGENTS.md`:

```md
# AGENTS.md — Contributor & Agent Guide

Guidance for coding agents working on the **Moorhuhn Adventure 2: Der Fluch des Goldes — Native Linux x86_64 Remastered Engine**.

> `CLAUDE.md` is a symlink to this file so all agent configurations read one source of truth.

---

## 1. Project Overview

- **What this is:** A standalone static x86 → C recompilation of the original 2005 Windows game `mha2.exe` (`recomp/lift.py`) paired with a clean-room Win32/DirectX/OpenGL 3.3 Core Remastered v5.2 runtime (`runtime/`).
- **Game assets:** Loaded dynamically at runtime from user-supplied `game_files/` (`mha2.dat`, `mha2.exe`, `*.avi`) and 4K WebP/MP4 packs in `hd_assets/`.
- **Active branch:** `main`

---

## 2. Environment & Build Commands

### NixOS Host Notes
- `/` and `/var/tmp` are mounted on a small **1 GB `tmpfs`**, while `/home` has large disk capacity (`zroot/home`) and `/tmp` is 16 GB.
- Always keep temporary container/build staging under `work/` (e.g., `TMPDIR="$ROOT/work/tmp"` as done in `tools/package_portable_linux.sh`), never `/var/tmp`.

### Native Build & Re-Lifting
- **Build native binary (`./mha2_linux`):**
  ```bash
  nix-shell --run "make -j$(nproc)"
  ```
- **Re-lift `game_files/mha2.exe` into `recomp/gen/` and rebuild** (required after editing `recomp/lift.py`):
  ```bash
  nix-shell --run "make lift && make -j$(nproc)"
  ```
- **Run locally:**
  ```bash
  ./play.sh
  ```

### Portable Linux Release Build (`work/dist/`)
- Built inside an **Ubuntu 22.04** (`glibc 2.35`) builder container defined in `tools/Containerfile.portable` using `podman` (or `docker`), outputting object files to `work/build_portable/` so local NixOS `./build/` and `./mha2_linux` are untouched:
  ```bash
  bash tools/package_portable_linux.sh              # stages bundle + builds .tar.gz (~5.4 GB)
  bash tools/package_portable_linux.sh --no-archive # stages bundle + runs ABI checks without tarballing
  ```
- **Never bundle NixOS `.so` libraries, `libSDL3.so.0`, or `SDL_DYNAMIC_API` in portable releases:** Nixpkgs binaries/libs pull `GLIBC_2.42`, `GLIBCXX_3.4.32`, `CXXABI_1.3.15`, unversioned `DT_NEEDED: libvulkan.so`, Nixpkgs `libstdc++`, and hardcoded `/nix/store` paths, breaking Ubuntu/Mint/Fedora/SteamOS.
- Keep minimal static FFmpeg (`--prefix=/opt/ffmpeg-min` built with `nasm` SIMD) and verified ABI/font checks inside `tools/package_portable_linux.sh` (max `GLIBC_2.35`, zero `/nix/store` strings, zero hard `DT_NEEDED` on `libvulkan.so`/`libpipewire`/`libpulse`/`libwayland`/`libEGL`/`libgbm`/`libdecor`, bundled `fonts/DejaVu*.ttf`).

---

## 3. Repository & Architecture Map

### Static Recompiler (`recomp/`)
- `recomp/lift.py`: Standalone x86-32 PE → C static lifter (uses `capstone` + `pefile`-free PE parser; discovers 8,134 functions & 121 switch tables, emits `recomp/gen/chunk_*.c`, `dispatch.c`, `funcs.h`, `imports.h`).
- `recomp/gen/`: Generated C translation units (gitignored; regenerated by `make lift`).

### Native Runtime (`runtime/`)
- `runtime/main.c`: CLI argument parsing, `mha2_settings.ini` load ordering, guest Core 0 affinity pinning (`runtime_unpin_current_thread()`), entry point.
- `runtime/cpu.c`, `runtime/cpu.h`: 32-bit guest flat memory (`mmap`), 24-bin segregated free-list `guest_alloc()`, Win32 stdcall/cdecl dispatch, x87 FPU stack emulation.
- `runtime/runtime.h`: Shared `EngineConfig g_cfg`, guest/runtime state declarations, cross-module hooks.
- `runtime/win32_kernel.c`: Win32 threads, events/semaphores/mutexes, handles, case-insensitive VFS/save paths, file I/O, timers.
- `runtime/win32_ddraw.c`: DirectDraw 7 surfaces, hardware-accelerated/emulated blits, RLE sprite hooks (`hook_rle_both`, `hook_rle_common`), `surface_blt()`, palette/color-key conversion, decoupled cursor tracking.
- `runtime/hd_assets.c`, `runtime/hd_assets.h`: `HDS5` 24×24 signature matching, 4K WebP background & sprite index caches (`.hd_index.cache`, `.hd_sprite_index.cache`), strip cell auto-discovery (`hd_sprites_find_strip_cell()`), background preloader thread, sprite room allowlisting (`sprite_allowed_in_active_room()`).
- `runtime/gl_renderer.c`, `runtime/gl_renderer.h`: OpenGL 3.3 Core renderer, 4K HD canvas FBO, shaders (Bicubic CAS, Lanczos, Edge Smooth, FSR1, CRT, Bloom, Color Grading), widescreen `16:10`/`16:9` HUD/dialog split composition, `F1` interactive settings menu, `Ctrl+L` Dialog Log, vector typography (`SDL2_ttf`), GPU post-FX & compare split-screen (`F2`), decoupled cursor, hotspot overlay.
- `runtime/game_patches.c`: Original 2005 game bugfixes (spinlock CPU burn, savegame EOF hang, loading sleep throttle, null derefs, handle leaks), hot-path native replacements, and exact read-only scene-graph hotspot collector (`game_collect_room_hotspots()`).
- `runtime/win32_user_gdi.c`: SDL2 window/event loop, input state, GDI font/TextOutA hooking (Windows-1252 → UTF-8), gamepad cursor emulation.
- `runtime/win32_dinput.c`: DirectInput keyboard/mouse COM stubs.
- `runtime/win32_dsound.c`: DirectSound mixing + SDL2 audio callback + reverb/EQ DSP.
- `runtime/win32_avi.c`: FFmpeg-backed DirectShow (`IGraphBuilder`/`IMediaControl`/`IMediaEventEx`/`IVideoWindow`/`IBasicVideo`) & `AVIFIL32`/`DrawDibDraw` video playback with automatic `hd_assets/videos/*.mp4` 4K override.

### Tooling (`tools/`)
- `tools/Containerfile.portable`, `tools/package_portable_linux.sh`: Self-contained Ubuntu 22.04 portable Linux builder & packager.
- `tools/extract_game.py`, `tools/extract_assets.py`: Legal-copy installer/disc extractor and `mha2.dat` VDrv/`_img`/IT3 unpacker.
- `tools/upscale_transformer.py`, `tools/upscale_alpha_sprites.py`, `tools/remaster_videos_4k.py`, `tools/polish_cursors.py`, `tools/compress_hd_lossless.py`: 4K AI upscaling, IT3 `tbl1+tbl2` compositing, SDF cursor polishing, lossless WebP recompression.
- `tools/install_desktop.sh`, `play.sh`: Local NixOS launcher & `.desktop` installer.

---

## 4. Key Technical Invariants & Pitfalls (Do Not Break)

1. **Animated JPEG Filmstrip Sprites (`img_type == 0`, `nf > 1`):**
   - Never upscale multi-frame JPEG vertical filmstrips as a single image (`objKerzen`, `gsNormal`, `objBrunnen`, etc.) — neural models bleed adjacent frames into dark horizontal trenches that blink on animation boundaries.
   - Always split and upscale per-frame with edge-replicate padding into `_f000.webp`..`_f{N-1}.webp` (`tools/upscale_transformer.py --apply-jpeg-strips`), and keep `--apply-backgrounds` skipping `nf > 1`.

2. **Split-Background Outdoor Rooms (`l/011`, `l/014`, `l/022`, `l/023`, `l/026`, `l/027`, `l/028`, `l/036`):**
   - In `sprite_allowed_in_active_room()` (`runtime/hd_assets.c`), the allowlist for `/objBgO`, `/objBgU`, `/mskBg.`, and `/objWolken` **must stay before** `if (g_spr_room_prefix_len > 0)`.
   - `maybe_lock_split_room_from_sprite()` (`runtime/win32_ddraw.c`) may lock room state only from `/objBgO`, `/objBgU`, or `/mskBg.` — **never** from shared desert clouds `/objWolken`.
   - Keep `lookup_rle_both_sprite()` max width `>= 2048` (`l/023/img/mskBg` is `1600×212`).

3. **HD Asset Caches (`.hd_index.cache`, `.hd_sprite_index.cache`):**
   - Adding, replacing, or deleting files in `hd_assets/` requires invalidating/rebuilding `.hd_index.cache` and/or `.hd_sprite_index.cache` (or ensuring cache mtime/generation invalidates them).

4. **Guest Core 0 Pinning & Win32 Handle Refcounting:**
   - The original 2005 binary uses non-atomic refcounts across main, loading, and processing threads; guest threads must stay pinned to Core 0 while native worker threads (audio callback, HD pre-decoder) call `runtime_unpin_current_thread()`.
   - When accessing `WinHandle` payloads (`SyncObj` or `ThreadObj`) in `runtime/win32_kernel.c`, always lock `g_hdl_mtx`, extract the pointer strictly matching `htype`, atomically increment `refcount` **before** unlocking `g_hdl_mtx`, and unref afterward.

5. **COM Vtable Slot Accuracy & Lifter Quirks:**
   - Every DirectShow / DirectDraw / DirectSound / DirectInput COM slot must match its exact Windows vtable index (`byte_offset / 4`) and stdcall parameter count (including `this`); wrong counts corrupt guest `ESP`. Check lifted call sites in `recomp/gen/` when implementing slots.
   - Capstone returns 2 operands `[ST(0), ST(i)]` for `fxch D9 C8+i` even though Intel syntax displays `fxch st(i)` for many instructions; verify any new `recomp/lift.py` mnemonic handler directly against Capstone's `ins.operands` output and inspect regenerated `recomp/gen/`.

6. **Hotspot Highlights & Widescreen Dialogs:**
   - `Space`/`H` hotspot highlights must come from `game_collect_room_hotspots()` in `runtime/game_patches.c`, not sprite-name guesses.
   - In `16:10`/`16:9` widescreen modes, bottom dialog choices reach `y=525..536` (`update_hud_bar_masks()`, `uDialogBar`) — keep dialog bar detection, shader UV remapping, and pointer coordinate mapping synchronized.

---

## 5. Coding, Testing & Git Conventions

- **Compiler strictness:** All `runtime/*.c` files must compile cleanly with **zero warnings** under `-Wall -Wextra`.
- **Code hygiene:** Avoid duplicating logic; keep helpers small and single-purpose; preserve existing docstrings and unrelated comments.
- **No copyrighted / generated commits:** Never commit files matching `.gitignore` (`game_files/`, `hd_assets/`, `work/`, `recomp/gen/`, `build/`, `mha2_linux`, `mha2_settings.ini`, `tools/models/`, `*.exe`, `*.dat`, `*.avi`, `*.sav`).
- **Git commit identity:** Use your configured Git identity (no hardcoded local names or placeholder emails):
  ```bash
  git commit -m "..."
  ```
- **Verification before claiming done:** Rebuild after edits (`nix-shell --run "make -j$(nproc)"`, plus `make lift` if `recomp/lift.py` changed) and inspect generated output / runtime logs / ABI checks directly.

```

`CHANGELOG.md`:

```md
# Changelog

All notable changes to the **Moorhuhn Adventure 2: Der Fluch des Goldes — Native Linux x86_64 Static Recompilation & Remaster Engine** are documented in this file.

---

## [v5.3 — Engine Accuracy Overhaul, Split-Room & Filmstrip Fixes, Audit Hardening & Portable Builder] (2026-11-23)

### Core Engine, Widescreen UX & 4K Scene Accuracy (Post-`v5.2`)
- **24-Bin Segregated Free Lists & 1.42 GB Guest Heap (`runtime/cpu.c`, `runtime/cpu.h`)**: Upgraded guest heap allocator to 24 size-class bins with O(1) bitmask jump + 1.42 GB virtual address space (`#10` also extends exact-bin scans past 64 blocks on first-fit fallback).
- **Exact Read-Only Scene-Graph Hotspot Collector (`runtime/game_patches.c`, `runtime/gl_renderer.c`)**: Replaced sprite-name heuristics for `Space`/`H` hotspot highlights with exact read-only traversal of live room object hitboxes (`game_collect_room_hotspots()`).
- **Widescreen Dialog Bar Detection & Left-Aligned 4K Choices (`runtime/gl_renderer.c`)**: Added pixel-based detection of bottom dialog choices spanning `y=500..599` (fixing clipped `y=525..536` lines in 16:9/16:10) and left-aligned 4K vector dialog choices to match original layout.
- **Animated JPEG Filmstrip Seam Fix (`tools/upscale_transformer.py`, `runtime/hd_assets.c`, `runtime/win32_ddraw.c`)**: Split and 4×-upscaled all 50 multi-frame JPEG strips (462 frames) individually with 16px edge-replicate padding (`--apply-jpeg-strips`), eliminating blinking dark horizontal seams, and added `hd_sprites_find_strip_cell()` for clipped-first-blit auto-discovery.
- **Split-Background Outdoor Rooms Fix (`runtime/hd_assets.c`, `runtime/win32_ddraw.c`)**: Fixed 1× blurry fallback across all 8 split-background rooms (`l/011`, `l/014`, `l/022`, `l/023`, `l/026`, `l/027`, `l/028`, `l/036`) by prioritizing `/objBgO`/`/objBgU`/`/mskBg.`/`/objWolken` allowlisting, adding `maybe_lock_split_room_from_sprite()`, and raising `lookup_rle_both_sprite()` max cell width/height to 2048 (`l/023/img/mskBg` `1600×212`).

### Static Recompiler & x87 FPU (`recomp/lift.py`, `runtime/cpu.h`, `runtime/cpu.c`)
- **Fixed `fxch st(2..7)` Lifting Bug**: Decoded Capstone's 2-operand `fxch D9 C8+i` encoding (`[ST(0), ST(i)]`) accurately instead of defaulting to `st(1)` across 15 live instructions in `mha2.exe`.
- **Completed x87 FPU & Instruction Semantics**: Added FPU state re-initialization in `fpu_save()` (`fnsave`), cleared/set `C2` with `2^63` range guards in `fsin`/`fcos`/`fsincos`/`fptan`, separated `fprem` (`fmodl`) from IEEE 754 `fprem1` (`remquol`) with `C0`/`C3`/`C1` quotient bits, fixed 8/16-bit `alu_rol`/`alu_ror` count masking and flag updates, fixed 16-bit and immediate `bt`/`bts`/`btr`/`btc`, fixed 16-bit `pop [esp+disp]` ordering, made memory `xchg`/`lock xchg` and 8/16/32-bit `lock inc`/`dec`/`add` atomic via `__atomic_*`, and routed `Jcc` and fallthrough transitions through `g_code_table` for `HOOKABLE_FUNCS`.

### Runtime Stability, Threading & Playback (`runtime/`)
- **DirectShow COM Vtable & Video Robustness (`runtime/win32_avi.c`)**: Fixed `IVideoWindow::SetWindowPosition` (`slot 39`, `+0x9C`) / `GetWindowPosition` (`slot 40`, `+0xA0`) stack corruption (`stdcall` arg counts `37..43`), added missing `IBasicVideo` getters (`slot 15/19` and neighbors), preserved playback elapsed ticks across `Pause` → `Run`, bounded `ff_decode_next_frame()` against codec/flush loops, and validated `DrawDibDraw` fallback dimensions and guest memory bounds.
- **Win32 Handle & GL Shutdown Thread Safety (`runtime/win32_kernel.c`, `runtime/gl_renderer.c`, `runtime/win32_user_gdi.c`)**: Added `ThreadObj` refcounting under `g_hdl_mtx` in `WaitForSingleObject`, `ResumeThread`, and `GetExitCodeThread`; guarded GL/SDL teardown to the GL context thread in `gl_renderer_shutdown()` and `ExitProcess`/`TerminateProcess`; freed `g_dialog_log_tex` on shutdown; and switched live audio config writes (`master_volume`, `muted`, `audio_reverb`, `audio_eq`) to atomic stores.
- **100% HD Sprite Coverage (`runtime/hd_assets.c`, `runtime/win32_ddraw.c`)**: Expanded `HDS5` `24×24` matching (`is_small_prop` via `sw <= 64 || sh <= 64`, `max_cell_da` tuning, and low-opacity probe handling) recovering ~140 previously missed IT3 props/characters; fixed runtime `F6` toggle and `--no-hd-sprites` gating across all blitters & `g_hd_canvas`; and added 2-frame vertical cursor strip support (`cuHelp`) for decoupled rendering.
- **Input, Widescreen & Tooling UX (`runtime/gl_renderer.c`, `runtime/win32_user_gdi.c`, `runtime/main.c`)**: Fixed stuck `Shift` / `Space` / `H` states on window focus loss, aligned `uDialogBar` in `F2` widescreen split-screen comparison, gated widescreen top-corner routing via `g_top_bar_has_buttons`, clamped gamepad cursor bounds to the visible widescreen playfield, and updated CLI `--help` docs.

### Portable Linux Packaging, Licensing & Build System (`tools/`, `Makefile`, `shell.nix`, `docs/`)
- **Cross-Distro Portable Linux Builder (`tools/Containerfile.portable`, `tools/package_portable_linux.sh`)**: Added a reproducible Ubuntu 22.04 (`glibc 2.35`) builder container compiling minimal static SIMD FFmpeg and portable SDL2 (`libSDL2-2.0.so.0`), removing `GLIBC_2.42`/`GLIBCXX_3.4.32`/unversioned `libvulkan.so`/`SDL_DYNAMIC_API` NixOS leaks, bundling `fonts/DejaVu*.ttf` (with relative `./fonts/` + Fedora font fallback), and enforcing automated ABI verification.
- **Build, Tooling & Release Docs**: Added `-MMD -MP` header dependency tracking, explicit `pkg-config`/`recomp/gen/dispatch.c` checks, and `?= ` overrides in `Makefile`; exported `MHA2_GAME_FONT_PATH` in `shell.nix`; fixed argument quoting, cursor batching (`mc*`), extension cleanup, temp file error cleanup, and CPU/subprocess safety across `play.sh` and `tools/*.py`/`*.sh`; added `docs/images/banner.png` (`1280×640` Remastered banner) and MIT [LICENSE](LICENSE).

---

## [v5.2 — Original Game Bugfixes, Multi-Core Threading & Runtime Hardening] (2026-11-23)

### Original 2005 Game Bugfixes & Optimizations (`runtime/game_patches.c`, `runtime/win32_kernel.c`, `recomp/lift.py`)
- **Fixed Non-Atomic Spinlock & Forced Lock-Steal (`0x004443f0`, `0x00444230`)**: Replaced the original game's non-atomic `DAT_0051b3b8++` spinlock (which forcibly stole the lock after 200ms while another thread was still inside the critical section) with true atomic CAS (`__atomic_compare_exchange_n`) and zero lock-steal.
- **Fixed Infinite Loop on Truncated/Corrupt Savegames (`0x0044a200`)**: Guarded `ReadFile` EOF (`bytesRead == 0`) and `GetFileSize` overflow so corrupted/truncated save files fail cleanly instead of hanging forever or wrapping `operator_new(size + 0x20)`.
- **Eliminated Loading Screen 50–250ms Artificial Stall (`0x00453cd0`)**: Clamped the original loading loop's `Sleep(250 - p1 - p2)` stall to `<= 4ms` for noticeably snappier room transitions.
- **Native Hardware `ceil()` & `__ftol()` for Scaled RLE Blitters (`0x004a7a5e`, `0x004a71cc`)**: Replaced the original CRT's slow emulated x87 FPU control-word-flipping `ceil`/`__ftol` routines called per-span inside all 8 scaled RLE blitters with direct native hardware instructions.
- **Fixed Room Global Object Loader Null Dereference (`0x00474fd0`)**: Guarded the two `ESI` writes (`0x0047530e`, `0x00475317`) that occurred before `test esi, esi; je`.
- **Fixed Startup `FindFirstFileA` Handle Leak (`0x00447820`)**: Closed the `data\g` directory probe `DIR*` immediately when matched instead of leaking it for the lifetime of the process.

### Native Linux Runtime Stability & Performance (`runtime/*.c`)
- **Multi-Core Unpinning for Native Worker Threads (`runtime/main.c`, `runtime/hd_assets.c`, `runtime/win32_dsound.c`)**: Saved the host's multi-core affinity mask before pinning guest threads to Core 0, then unpinned the background HD WebP pre-decoder thread and SDL audio DSP thread across all CPU cores (`0..N-1`) for zero main-thread contention.
- **Explicit Free-List Guest Heap Allocator (`runtime/cpu.c`)**: Upgraded `guest_alloc` from an `O(N_all)` full-heap scan to an explicit doubly-linked free list (`O(N_free)`), added mutex locking to `guest_alloc_size`, added size-overflow/OOM guards, and enforced `guest_alloc_static` bounds.
- **Fixed DirectSound Use-After-Free & Audio Thread Races (`runtime/win32_dsound.c`)**: Added a refcounted `DSoundData` PCM pool so releasing an original sound buffer never frees `data_va` out from under a playing `DuplicateSoundBuffer` clone; added atomic loads for `g_cfg` audio settings and fixed `IDirectSound::Release` COM leak.
- **Thread Slot Recycling & Handle Exhaustion Safety (`runtime/cpu.c`, `runtime/win32_kernel.c`)**: Refcounted `ThreadObj` and recycled guest thread slots via a 36-slot bitmask (eliminating stack OOB after 40 threads and `ThreadObj` leaks); guarded all `alloc_handle() == 0` call sites and fixed `DeleteCriticalSection`/`FindClose` mutex races.
- **DirectDraw & DirectShow Safety (`runtime/win32_ddraw.c`, `runtime/win32_avi.c`)**: Fixed primary surface backbuffer leak on `Release`, locked `QueryInterface`/`AddRef` refcounts, guarded `alloc_surface`/`CreateSurface`/`ff_open` OOM paths, freed DirectShow COM wrapper objects on `Release`, and eliminated the 25Hz black-frame intro/cutscene video flicker (`ddraw_present_surface` no longer overwrites active `AVIFile`/`DrawDibDraw` frames with the empty DirectDraw backbuffer) while making 4K `DrawDibDraw` zero-copy.
- **4K SDF Cursor Matching & `cuExit`/`cuHelp` Polish (`runtime/hd_assets.c`, `tools/polish_cursors.py`)**: Relaxed single-cell 2×2 peak outlier caps (`max_cell_da`, `max_cell_drgb`) for `<=64×64` sprites in `HDS5` so all 4K SDF cursors (`mcArrow`, `mcLook`, `mcTake`, `mcTalk`, `mcUse`, `cuExit`, `cuHelp`) match reliably instead of falling back to blurry 1× sprites, and polished `cuExit` and `cuHelp` with vector-clean SDF contours.
- **Native 16:9 & 16:10 Widescreen Main Menu (`runtime/gl_renderer.c`, `runtime/hd_assets.c`, `hd_assets/x/001/img/objBg_wide.webp`)**: Added seamless 4K `4268×2400` widescreen extended wings for the Main Menu (`x/001`), keeping the exact untouched 4K `3200×2400` center aligned 1:1 with all interactive menu buttons while filling 16:9 and 16:10 monitors edge-to-edge with matching color grading (and respecting `F12` 4:3 / 16:10 / 16:9 modes).
- **Input & UX Polish (`runtime/win32_user_gdi.c`, `runtime/gl_renderer.c`)**: Coalesced consecutive `WM_MOUSEMOVE` events in the Win32 message queue (eliminating lag on 500–1000Hz mice against the original game's 9-msg/frame cap), and rebound Dialog & Clue History Log from bare `L` to **`Ctrl+L`** so players can freely type `'l'` in savegame names.

---

## [v5.1 — 4K ClearRealityV1 Overhaul, Audio Remastering & Portable Build] (2026-11-22)

### Neural 4K Asset Pipeline (`4x-ClearRealityV1` Full Remaster)
- **Complete 100% Faithful Super-Resolution Overhaul** (`tools/upscale_transformer.py`):
  - Replaced legacy 2021 ESRGAN (`4x_NMKD-Siax_200k` / `ultrasharp-4x`) weights with modern **`4x-ClearRealityV1`** (SPAN) neural super-resolution — eliminating 2005 JPEG crackle, carpet moiré, and jagged staircases while preserving 100% of original art, crests, cobwebs, and shading.
  - Remastered **all 348** JPEG-backed backgrounds, scrolling panoramas, menus, and screens (`hd_assets/`).
  - Remastered **all 8,098** IT3 sprites (`≥16px`) using RGB-dilation + separate neural alpha upscaling + 1.0px 4K Gaussian silhouette AA for non-`tbl2` props.
- **True 4K (`3200×2400`) FMV Cutscenes** (`tools/remaster_videos_4k.py`):
  - Added streaming FP16 GPU cutscene remastering (`ffmpeg` → RTX GPU `4x-ClearRealityV1` → `libx264` CRF 17 MP4 with zero temporary disk frames).
  - Remastered all 4,721 frames of `mha2il`, `mha2ol`, and `mha2sl` to native `3200×2400` MP4 (with `mha2ih`/`mha2oh`/`mha2sh` symlinked to 4K masters).
- **Vector-Clean SDF Cursors** (`tools/polish_cursors.py`):
  - Replaced bumpy 2005 `tbl2` outer cursor fringes across all 10 cursor frames (`mcArrow`, `mcLook`, `mcTake`, `mcTalk`, `mcUse`) with smooth signed-distance-field (SDF) anti-aliased contours and soft drop shadows.
- **100% Bit-Exact Lossless WebP Optimization** (`tools/compress_hd_lossless.py`, `runtime/hd_assets.c`):
  - Converted all 8,644 HD PNGs to bit-exact lossless WebP (`lossless=True, exact=True`, `max_pixel_diff=0`, preserving dilated RGB inside transparent pixels) — saving **2.16 GB (38.2%)** with zero quality loss.
- **Zero-Stutter Preloading & QoL Suite** (`runtime/hd_assets.c`, `runtime/gl_renderer.c`, `runtime/win32_ddraw.c`):
  - Added a background room-sprite pre-decoder worker thread + expanded GPU sprite LRU cache (`256 → 512` slots).
  - Added **Dialog & Clue History Log (`L` key)** capturing recent multi-word dialog/clue lines into a scrollable overlay.
  - Added **Rolling Auto-Save Backup** (`game_files/mha2.sav/autosave_backup/`, throttled every 3 min on room changes).
  - Added **Multi-Monitor Target Display Picker** in `F1` → `1. DISPLAY`.

### Graphics, Shaders & Audio Remastering
- **Audio Mastering EQ + M/S Stereo Widener** (`runtime/win32_dsound.c`):
  - Added real-time 2nd-order RBJ stereo biquad Low-Shelf + High-Shelf EQ and Mid/Side stereo widening (`OFF` / `Warm Remaster` / `Studio Crisp`) in `F1` → `3. ASSETS & TEXT`.
- **Upgraded Wide-Radius Debanding** (`runtime/gl_renderer.c`):
  - Replaced the 1.8px debander with an mpv-style 2-ring wide-radius (`7–14px` & `18–32px`) jittered edge-preserving gradient debander + triangular dither.
- **Fixed AMD FidelityFX FSR 1.0 on 4K Canvas** (`runtime/gl_renderer.c`):
  - Enabled true 12-tap directional EASU directly on the `3200×2400` 4K canvas and replaced double-RCAS with authentic per-channel RGB 2-scale RCAS with soft overshoot clamping.
- **Engine-Side Edge Polish** (`runtime/gl_renderer.c`):
  - Added `SMAA + 3x Edge AA` mode (structure-tensor tangent AA targeting baked-in 1× staircases) and `FS_OSD` HD sprite edge feathering & dark-halo defringe.

### Bug Fixes & Stability
- **Safe (`Tresor`) Rotary Wheel & Puzzle Digit Fix (`HDS5`)** (`runtime/hd_assets.c`):
  - Upgraded the sprite matcher from `8×8` to **`24×24`** thumbnails (9× higher spatial detail) with un-truncated fixed-point SAD + peak-cell penalty (`max_drgb * 4`), fixing all 10/40 safe wheel digit mismatches (`40/40` exact matches).
- **Death's Office (`l/006`) Black Rectangle Fix** (`runtime/gl_renderer.c`, `hd_assets/`):
  - Downscaled 13 oversized `>16384px` multi-frame vertical filmstrips (`objTodIdle/Talk/Action`, `objGolem`, `objExplosion`, `objMischen`) to crisp `2×` and added a `GL_MAX_TEXTURE_SIZE` guard before `glTexImage2D`.
- **Gaussian-AA Room Prop Matching Fix (`HDS4`)** (`runtime/hd_assets.c`):
  - Lowered core-alpha threshold from `245` → `160` and relaxed tiny-prop `max_cell_da` to `230`, restoring 100% 4K matching on small room props (`invToteFledermaus`, `invAbacus`, `xobjKerze`, `objVase`, portraits) and SDF cursors.
- **Reliable Edge Back-Cursor Default** (`runtime/gl_renderer.c`):
  - Defaulted `decoupled_cursor` to `OFF` so edge-clipped back cursors (e.g., Clock close-up) always render reliably (`F10` toggle still available).

### Packaging & Documentation
- **Portable Linux x86_64 Packager** (`tools/package_portable_linux.sh`):
  - Added `./fonts/` lookup fallback in `gl_renderer.c` and a packager script that bundles `mha2_linux` (patched to `/lib64/ld-linux-x86-64.so.2`, `RUNPATH=$ORIGIN/lib`), all 86 non-GPU shared libraries (including `libSDL3.so.0`), DejaVu fonts, `game_files/`, and full 4K `hd_assets/` into a distro-agnostic `.tar.gz`.
- **README Showcase** (`README.md`, `docs/images/`):
  - Added labeled 4K split-screen, 1:1 macro zoom, and Clock hotspot-highlighter showcase screenshots plus updated v5 hotkey reference.

---

## [v5.0 — Neural Remaster & Quality-of-Life Suite] (`v5-neural-remaster`, 2026-11-20 – 2026-11-22)

### Rendering & Typography
- **Persistent 4K GPU DirectDraw Canvas (`3200×2400`)** (`runtime/gl_renderer.c`, `runtime/win32_ddraw.c`):
  - Mirrored all DirectDraw blits in exact painter's Z-order on dual 4K FBOs, eliminating cursor erase artifacts, flicker, and IT3 cell-size mismatches.
  - Added 16-tap Catmull-Rom supersampling + 2-scale (`1px` + `2px`) screen-pixel FidelityFX CAS sharpening.
- **Full IT3 Dual-Layer (`tbl1` + `tbl2`) 4K Compositing** (`runtime/win32_ddraw.c`, `tools/upscale_alpha_sprites.py`):
  - Hooked all 8 software IT3 RLE blitters and `Blitter::BlitBoth` (`0x00494b80`) to match & render `tbl1+tbl2` in a single 4K pass and suppress blurry 1× `tbl2` edge rings.
- **Native 4K Vector Outlined Typography** (`runtime/win32_ddraw.c`, `runtime/gl_renderer.c`):
  - Hooked `Font::DrawText` (`0x00486c40`) & `Font::MeasureText` (`0x00486ad0`) for invariant-bounds 4K TrueType text (`Clean Sans` / `Adventure Serif`) with optional soft readability backing, eliminating dirty-rect text morphing.

### Post-Processing & Shaders
- **3-Tab Interactive F1 Settings Menu** (`1. DISPLAY`, `2. POST-FX`, `3. ASSETS & TEXT`) with auto-persistence to `mha2_settings.ini`.
- **Multi-Pass OpenGL 3.3 Post-FX Pipeline**:
  - **AMD FidelityFX FSR 1.0** (`EASU` + `RCAS`), **Bicubic + CAS**, **Lanczos3 + CAS**, **Edge-Smooth**, **Bilinear**, **Nearest**, **Subtle CRT**.
  - **Anti-Aliasing**: `OFF` / `FXAA 3.11` (28px span) / `SMAA 1x (Crisp)` + live `AA Strength` (`0–100%`).
  - **Emissive Bloom Glow**: warm-weighted `0.72/0.14` bright-pass extract + 3-pass wide Gaussian halo (torch/candle/gold glow with zero midtone haze).
  - **Filmic HDR Tone-Map** (adjustable `0–100%`), **Local Contrast / Clarity** (adjustable `0–100%`), **Color Grading** (`Original` / `Remastered Rich` / `Cinematic Warm` / `Crisp Daylight`), **Living Atmosphere FX** (torch flicker + drifting dust motes), **Cinematic Vignette**, **210 ms Smooth Room Crossfades**, and **8-tap 2-radius Cutscene Deblock + Sharpen**.

### Quality-of-Life, Audio & Desktop Integration
- **Hotspot Highlighter**: Hold `Space` / `H` (or Gamepad `X`) to display pulsing golden markers on interactive room objects.
- **Fast-Walk / Speed-Up**: Hold `Shift` for `2×` speed or toggle `1.0x` / `1.5x` / `2.0x` in F1 menu via virtual clock scaling (`runtime/win32_kernel.c`).
- **Audio Remastering** (`runtime/win32_dsound.c`): Anti-pop master gain ramping + Schroeder stereo castle reverb (`OFF` / `Subtle Castle` / `Rich Hall`).
- **Native Gamepad Support**: Full `SDL_GameController` support (left stick cursor, A/B click/right-click, X hotspots, Start F1 menu).
- **Live Split-Screen Before/After (`F2`)** & **Clean 4K PNG Screenshots (`PrintScreen` / `Ctrl+P` → `screenshots/`)**.
- **Linux Desktop Integration** (`tools/install_desktop.sh`): Installs `~/.local/share/applications/mha2-remastered.desktop` and icon.

---

## [v4.0 — Zero-Stretch Widescreen & In-Game Menu] (`v4-widescreen-remaster`, 2026-11-19)
- Added automatic **16:10 Smart** (`800×500` → 0% stretch on `2560×1600`), **16:9 Cinema** (`800×450`), and **4:3** with blurred ambient sidebars (`F12`).
- Built the interactive mouse + keyboard **F1 Graphics Settings Menu** and persistent `.hd_index.cache` for instant HD asset startup.

---

## [v3.0 — Standalone Recompiler & First HD Pipeline] (`v3-full-remaster`, 2026-11-19)
- Completed **100% standalone x86-32 → C static recompiler** (`recomp/lift.py`) with automatic discovery of 8,100+ functions, 121 switch tables, and MSVC C++ EH `FuncInfo` handlers (zero Ghidra dependency).
- Added legal-copy disc/installer extractor (`tools/extract_game.py`) and `mha2.dat` asset extractor (`tools/extract_assets.py`).
- Introduced initial HD background replacement, native vector text overlay, and high-refresh decoupled cursor.

---

## [v2.0 — Modernized OpenGL 3.3 Engine] (`v2-remastered-engine`, 2026-11-18)
- Upgraded renderer from software SDL2 to **OpenGL 3.3 Core** with 32-bit TrueColor (`Mode 888`), GPU upscaling shaders, high-refresh frame pacing, and 48 kHz Float32 Catmull-Rom cubic audio mixing.

---

## [v1.0 — Initial Native Linux Static Recompilation] (`v1-working-recomp`, 2026-11-17)
- First fully playable native Linux x86_64 static recompilation of `mha2.exe` with custom Win32 / DirectDraw / DirectInput / DirectSound / AVI compatibility runtime (no Wine, no emulator).

`Makefile`:

```
CC       ?= gcc
PKG_CFG  ?= pkg-config
PKG_DEPS := sdl2 SDL2_ttf libavformat libavcodec libswscale libavutil gl

SDL_CFLAGS ?= $(shell $(PKG_CFG) --cflags $(PKG_DEPS))
SDL_LIBS   ?= $(shell $(PKG_CFG) --libs   $(PKG_DEPS))

ifneq ($(MAKECMDGOALS),clean)
ifneq ($(MAKECMDGOALS),lift)
ifeq ($(wildcard recomp/gen/dispatch.c),)
$(error Generated C sources in recomp/gen/ are missing. Run 'make lift' first)
endif
ifeq ($(strip $(SDL_LIBS)),)
$(error Failed to resolve development libraries ($(PKG_DEPS)) via '$(PKG_CFG)'. Run inside 'nix-shell' or pass SDL_CFLAGS/SDL_LIBS)
endif
endif
endif

CFLAGS_COMMON := -O2 -Iruntime -Irecomp/gen -fno-strict-aliasing -MMD -MP
CFLAGS_RT     := $(CFLAGS_COMMON) -Wall -Wextra $(SDL_CFLAGS)
CFLAGS_GEN    := $(CFLAGS_COMMON) -Wno-unused-label -Wno-unused-variable -Wno-parentheses
LDFLAGS       := $(SDL_LIBS) -lpthread -lm

RT_SRCS := \
	runtime/main.c \
	runtime/cpu.c \
	runtime/gl_renderer.c \
	runtime/hd_assets.c \
	runtime/win32_kernel.c \
	runtime/win32_user_gdi.c \
	runtime/win32_ddraw.c \
	runtime/win32_dinput.c \
	runtime/win32_dsound.c \
	runtime/win32_avi.c \
	runtime/game_patches.c

GEN_SRCS := recomp/gen/dispatch.c $(wildcard recomp/gen/chunk_*.c)

BUILD_DIR ?= build
RT_OBJS   := $(patsubst runtime/%.c,$(BUILD_DIR)/rt_%.o,$(RT_SRCS))
GEN_OBJS  := $(patsubst recomp/gen/%.c,$(BUILD_DIR)/gen_%.o,$(GEN_SRCS))
RT_DEPS   := $(RT_OBJS:.o=.d)
GEN_DEPS  := $(GEN_OBJS:.o=.d)

TARGET ?= mha2_linux

.PHONY: all clean lift

all: $(TARGET)

lift:
	python3 recomp/lift.py --standalone

$(BUILD_DIR):
	mkdir -p $(BUILD_DIR)

$(BUILD_DIR)/rt_%.o: runtime/%.c runtime/cpu.h runtime/runtime.h runtime/gl_renderer.h runtime/hd_assets.h recomp/gen/imports.h recomp/gen/funcs.h | $(BUILD_DIR)
	$(CC) $(CFLAGS_RT) -c $< -o $@

$(BUILD_DIR)/gen_%.o: recomp/gen/%.c runtime/cpu.h recomp/gen/funcs.h | $(BUILD_DIR)
	$(CC) $(CFLAGS_GEN) -c $< -o $@

$(TARGET): $(RT_OBJS) $(GEN_OBJS)
	$(CC) $(RT_OBJS) $(GEN_OBJS) -o $@.tmp $(LDFLAGS)
	mv -f $@.tmp $@
	@echo "Built native Linux static recompilation: ./$(TARGET)"

clean:
	rm -rf $(BUILD_DIR) $(TARGET)

-include $(RT_DEPS) $(GEN_DEPS)

```

# Moorhuhn Adventure 2: Der Fluch des Goldes — Native Linux Remastered Engine (v5)

Ein vollständiger **eigenständiger x86-32-zu-Linux-x86_64 Static Recompiler** sowie eine **modernisierte OpenGL-3.3-Core-Remaster-Engine** für *Moorhuhn Adventure 2: Der Fluch des Goldes* (`mha2.exe`) — nativ portiert auf Linux mit **OpenGL 3.3 Core**, **SDL2**, **SDL2_ttf** und **FFmpeg**, ganz **ohne Wine, ohne Emulator, ohne Windows-DLLs und ohne Ghidra zur Build-Zeit**.

> **Rechtlicher Hinweis / IP-Hinweis:** *Moorhuhn* / *Crazy Chicken* ist eine eingetragene Marke der ak tronic Software & Services GmbH. Dieses Repository enthält **ausschließlich** Clean-Room-Recompiler-Tooling, Asset-Extraktoren und eine native Linux-Kompatibilitätsschicht. Es sind keinerlei Original-Binärdateien, urheberrechtlich geschützte Spielinhalte oder aus der Exe generierter C-Code enthalten — du benötigst deine eigene legal erworbene Kopie des Spiels.
>
> Siehe [CHANGELOG.md](CHANGELOG.md) für den vollständigen Versionsverlauf von `v1` (erste statische Rekompilierung) bis `v5.3`.

## Modernisierte Engine-Features (v5)

- **100 % eigenständiger Static Recompiler** ([recomp/lift.py](recomp/lift.py)):
  - Erkennt automatisch alle 8.100+ Funktionen, 121 MSVC-Switch-Tabellen (inklusive negativ indizierter und 1-basierter `memcpy`/`memmove`-Tabellen) sowie alle MSVC-C++-EH-`FuncInfo`-Unwind/Catch-Handler direkt aus deiner bereitgestellten `mha2.exe` mit **null Lift-Fehlern** und ohne Ghidra-Abhängigkeit.
  - Enthält einen eigenständigen Extraktor für legale Spielkopien ([tools/extract_game.py](tools/extract_game.py)) mit Unterstützung für Wise `Setup.EXE` und Mode-1/2352-CD-ROM-Images (`.bin`/`.iso`) inklusive vollständiger CRC-32-Prüfung.

- **Unverzerrtes echtes Widescreen (`16:10` & `16:9`) + Ambient-Blur-4:3-Sidebars (`F12`)**:
  - Erkennt automatisch native `800×450`-Spielräume/-Minispiele (16:9) gegenüber `800×600`-Menüs (4:3).
  - **16:10 Smart (`800×500` → exakte `3,2×`-Skalierung auf `2560×1600`, 0 % Streckung, 0 px schwarze Balken)**: voller `800×450`-Raum + dediziertes `50 px` Inventar-Dock unten mit sanft schattierten HUD-Overlays oben.
  - **16:9 Cinema (`800×450`)**: randloses 16:9-Gameplay mit transparenten On-Demand-Overlays oben & unten.
  - **4:3-Menüs**: seitenverhältnistreue Darstellung mit optionalen weichgezeichneten Ambient-Sidebars.

- **Interaktives 3-Tab-Grafik- & Remaster-FX-Menü im Spiel (`F1`) + Auto-Save (`mha2_settings.ini`)**:
  - Per Maus und Tastatur bedienbares 3-Tab-Menü (`1. Display & Scaling`, `2. Remaster FX & AA`, `3. Assets, HUD & Audio`) für Auflösungs-Presets (`800×600`–`2560×1600`), Seitenverhältnis, Shader, Bloom, Color Grading, Silhouetten-AA, Vignette, Szenenübergänge, HD-Assets sowie Lautstärke/Hall/EQ — automatisch gespeichert in `mha2_settings.ini`.

- **Multi-Pass OpenGL 3.3 FBO Post-Processing & weiche Szenen-Überblendungen**:
  - **Emissiver Bloom** (`Off / Subtle / Medium / Rich`): Viertelauflösungs-`GL_RGBA16F`-Bright-Pass + zweifacher separabler 9-Tap-Gauß-Blur für Kerzen, Fackeln, Kronleuchter und Goldartefakte.
  - **Color Grading & Vibrance** (`Original / Remastered Rich / Cinematic Warm / Crisp Daylight`).
  - **Sprite-Silhouetten-AA & Anti-Banding-Dithering**: leichtes FXAA-Kantenglätten + Dreiecksrauschen-Dithering gegen Banding.
  - **Weiche 210 ms Cubic-Ease-Out-Raumblenden**: Zero-Copy-Dual-FBO-Übergänge zwischen Räumen und Menüs.

- **KI-4K-Hintergründe + native GPU-4×-RGBA-Sprites (`F6`)** ([runtime/hd_assets.c](runtime/hd_assets.c), [tools/upscale_ai.py](tools/upscale_ai.py)):
  - Kombiniert 4K-Hintergründe (`3200×1800` / `3200×2400`) sowohl für Menüs (`800×600`) als auch für Spielräume (`800×450`) mit einem O(1)-dimensionsgruppierten LRU-GPU-Renderer für 4×-RGBA-Charaktere, Requisiten, Inventarsymbole, Buttons und Cursor (`hd_assets/sprites/`), inkl. Festplatten-Indexcache für sofortigen Start.

- **Monitor-DPI-Vektortext & Untertitel (`F5`)** & **High-Refresh-Cursor (`F10`)**:
  - Gestochen scharfer TrueType-Vektortext in nativer Monitor-Auflösung (nach dem Post-FX gerendert) sowie ein entkoppelter 4×-HD-Cursor mit voller Monitor-Bildwiederholrate (120/144/240 Hz).

- **Echte 32-Bit-Farbtiefe (`Mode 888`), 7 Upscaling-Shader (inkl. AMD FidelityFX FSR 1.0) & 48-kHz-Audio-Mixer**:
  - Bicubic+CAS, Lanczos3+CAS, Edge-Smooth, Bilinear, Nearest, Subtle CRT und AMD FidelityFX FSR 1.0 (`EASU + RCAS`); Float32-48-kHz-Catmull-Rom-Mixer mit Hall und Mastering-EQ (`F7`/`F8`/`F9`).

- **Remastered FMV-Zwischensequenzen** ([runtime/win32_avi.c](runtime/win32_avi.c), [tools/upscale_videos.py](tools/upscale_videos.py)):
  - Echtzeit-GPU-Deblocking, Anti-Ringing und Schärfung für die 2005er Cinepak-AVIs sowie automatische lippensynchrone Wiedergabe optionaler HD-MP4/MKV-Cutscenes aus `hd_assets/videos/`.

## Kompilieren aus deiner eigenen legalen Spielkopie

<br>

### NixOS (`nix-shell`)

```bash
# 1. Legal erworbenes CD-Image (.bin/.iso) oder Wise Setup.EXE nach game_files/ extrahieren:
nix-shell --run "python3 tools/extract_game.py '/pfad/zu/Setup.EXE' -o game_files"
# (alternativ bestehendes Installationsverzeichnis mit mha2.exe, mha2.dat, *.avi nach game_files/ kopieren)

# 2. mha2.exe in C liften (100 % eigenständig, kein Ghidra nötig) und natives x86_64-ELF kompilieren:
nix-shell --run "make lift && make -j$(nproc)"

# 3. Spielen!
./play.sh
```

<br>

### Standard-Linux (Ubuntu / Debian / Fedora / Arch)

Benötigte Build-Abhängigkeiten installieren:

- **Compiler & Build-Tools:** `gcc`, `make`, `pkg-config`, `python3`, `python3-capstone`
- **Entwicklungs-Header:** `SDL2` (`libsdl2-dev`), `SDL2_ttf` (`libsdl2-ttf-dev`), `FFmpeg` (`libavformat-dev`, `libavcodec-dev`, `libswscale-dev`, `libavutil-dev`) und `OpenGL` (`libgl-dev`)

```bash
# 1. Eigene legale Spieldateien in game_files/ ablegen (oder mit tools/extract_game.py extrahieren):
python3 tools/extract_game.py '/pfad/zu/Setup.EXE' -o game_files

# 2. mha2.exe liften und das native x86_64-Binary kompilieren:
make lift && make -j"$(nproc)"

# 3. Starten:
./mha2_linux
```

<br>

### Portables Linux-Release-Bundle

Erstellt ein eigenständiges, distributionsübergreifendes `.tar.gz`-Bundle über den Ubuntu-22.04-Builder-Container (`glibc 2.35`, `podman` oder `docker`):

```bash
make lift
bash tools/package_portable_linux.sh
```

<br>

<br>

## 4K-KI-HD-Asset-Pack erstellen (`hd_assets/`)

```bash
# 4K-Hintergründe + 4×-RGBA-Sprites per Vulkan-GPU generieren:
nix-shell --run "python3 tools/upscale_ai.py --all"

# FMV-Zwischensequenzen (mha2il/ol/sl.avi) nach hd_assets/videos/ hochskalieren:
nix-shell --run "python3 tools/upscale_videos.py --scale 2"

# Oder originale 1×-Assets manuell inspizieren / extrahieren:
nix-shell --run "python3 tools/extract_assets.py --list"
nix-shell --run "python3 tools/extract_assets.py --images -o extracted_assets"
```

## Starten & CLI-Optionen

```bash
./play.sh                                       # Standard Remastered v5 (lädt mha2_settings.ini)
./play.sh --fullscreen --hud                    # Rahmenloser Vollbildmodus + Live-HUD
./play.sh --aspect 16:10 --grade remastered     # 16:10-Smart-Widescreen & Color Grading erzwingen
./play.sh --16bit --no-hd-assets --no-hd-sprites --no-hd-text --no-bloom --grade original  # Purer 2005er-Look
```

<br>

### Tastenkürzel im Spiel

| Taste | Aktion |
|---|---|
| **F1** | Interaktives 3-Tab-Display-, Post-FX- & Audio-Menü öffnen/schließen |
| **F2** | Live-Splitscreen-Vergleich Vorher/Nachher (2005 vs. 4K-Remaster) |
| **F3** | Live-GPU-Statistik-HUD ein-/ausblenden |
| **F4** | VSync / High-FPS-Modus durchschalten |
| **F5** | Monitor-DPI-Vektortext & Untertitel ein-/ausblenden |
| **F6** | HD-Asset-Pack-Override (`hd_assets/`) ein-/ausschalten |
| **F7 / F8 / F9** | Gesamtlautstärke leiser / lauter / stumm |
| **F10** | Entkoppelten High-Refresh-Cursor ein-/ausschalten |
| **F11** oder **Alt+Enter** | Rahmenlosen Vollbildmodus umschalten |
| **F12** | Widescreen-Seitenverhältnis wechseln (`Auto` / `16:10` / `16:9` / `4:3`) |
| **Leertaste** oder **H** | Gedrückt halten, um interaktive Hotspots im Raum hervorzuheben |
| **Strg+L** (`Ctrl+L`) | Dialog- & Hinweisverlauf öffnen/schließen |
| **Umschalt** (`Shift`) | Gedrückt halten für 2× Schnelllauf-Geschwindigkeit |
| **Strg+P** oder **Druck** | Sauberen 4K-PNG-Screenshot speichern (`screenshots/`) |
| **ESC** | Zwischensequenzen überspringen / Spielmenü / F1-Menü schließen |

# Moorhuhn Adventure 2: Der Fluch des Goldes — Native Linux Remastered Engine (v5)

A full **standalone x86-32 to native Linux x86_64 static recompiler** and **modernized OpenGL 3.3 Core remaster engine** for *Moorhuhn Adventure 2: Der Fluch des Goldes* (`mha2.exe`), ported natively to Linux with **OpenGL 3.3 Core**, **SDL2**, **SDL2_ttf**, and **FFmpeg** — with **no Wine, no emulator, no Windows DLLs, and no Ghidra required at build time**.

> **Legal / IP Notice:** *Moorhuhn* / *Crazy Chicken* is a registered trademark of ak tronic Software & Services GmbH. This repository contains **only** original clean-room recompiler tooling, asset extractors, and a Linux runtime compatibility layer. No original game binaries, copyrighted assets, or lifted C code are included — you must supply your own legally owned copy of the game.
>

![4K Remaster v5 Clock Close-Up with Hotspot Highlighter](docs/images/remaster_showcase_clock_hotspots.png)

<br>

<br>

## Modernized Engine Features (v5)

- **100% Standalone Static Recompiler** ([recomp/lift.py](recomp/lift.py)):
  - Automatically discovers all 8,100+ functions, 121 MSVC switch tables (including negative-indexed and 1-based `memcpy`/`memmove` tables), and all MSVC C++ EH `FuncInfo` unwind/catch handlers directly from a user-supplied `mha2.exe` with **zero lift errors** and zero Ghidra dependency.
  - Includes a standalone legal-copy disc/installer extractor ([tools/extract_game.py](tools/extract_game.py)) supporting both Wise `Setup.EXE` and raw Mode 1/2352 `.bin`/`.iso` CD-ROM images with full CRC-32 verification.

- **Zero-Stretch True Widescreen (`16:10` & `16:9`) + Ambient Blur 4:3 Sidebars (`F12`)**:
  - Automatically detects native `800×450` (16:9) gameplay rooms/minigames vs `800×600` (4:3) menus.
  - **16:10 Smart (`800×500` → exact `3.2×` on `2560×1600`, 0% stretch, 0px black bars)**: full `800×450` room + dedicated `50px` bottom inventory dock with soft drop-shadow top HUD overlays.
  - **16:9 Cinema (`800×450`)**: edge-to-edge 16:9 gameplay with transparent on-demand top & bottom overlays.
  - **4:3 Menus**: aspect-correct with optional blurred ambient sidebars.

- **Interactive 3-Tab In-Game Graphics & Remaster FX Menu (`F1`) + Auto-Save (`mha2_settings.ini`)**:
  - Mouse + keyboard 3-tab menu (`1. Display & Scaling`, `2. Remaster FX & AA`, `3. Assets, HUD & Audio`) controlling resolution presets (`800×600`–`2560×1600`), aspect mode, shaders, bloom, color grading, silhouette AA, vignette, transitions, HD assets, and volume/reverb/EQ — automatically saved to `mha2_settings.ini`.

- **Multi-Pass OpenGL 3.3 FBO Post-Processing & Smooth Scene Crossfades**:
  - **Emissive Bloom** (`Off / Subtle / Medium / Rich`): quarter-res `GL_RGBA16F` warm-weighted bright-pass + dual separable 9-tap Gaussian blur for candles, torches, chandeliers, and gold artifacts.
  - **Color Grading & Vibrance** (`Original / Remastered Rich / Cinematic Warm / Crisp Daylight`).
  - **Sprite Silhouette AA & Anti-Banding Dithering**: lightweight FXAA edge smoothing + triangular-noise output dithering.
  - **Smooth 210 ms Cubic Ease-Out Room Crossfades**: zero-copy dual-FBO transitions between rooms and menus.

- **AI 4K Neural Backgrounds + Native GPU 4× RGBA Sprites (`F6`)** ([runtime/hd_assets.c](runtime/hd_assets.c), [tools/upscale_ai.py](tools/upscale_ai.py)):
  - Composites 4K (`3200×1800` / `3200×2400`) Real-ESRGAN backgrounds for both menus (`800×600`) and gameplay rooms (`800×450`), plus an O(1) dimension-bucketed LRU GPU renderer for 4× RGBA foreground characters, props, inventory icons, buttons, and cursors (`hd_assets/sprites/`), all cached to disk for instant startup.

- **Native Monitor-DPI Vector Text & Subtitles (`F5`)** & **High-Refresh Decoupled Cursor (`F10`)**:
  - Crisp native monitor-DPI TrueType text rendered post-FX, plus a 4× HD decoupled cursor rendered at full monitor refresh rate (120/144/240Hz).

- **True 32-bit Color (`Mode 888`), 7 Upscaling Shaders (incl. AMD FidelityFX FSR 1.0), & 48 kHz Cubic Audio Mixer**:
  - Bicubic+CAS, Lanczos3+CAS, Edge-Smooth, Bilinear, Nearest, Subtle CRT, and AMD FidelityFX FSR 1.0 (`EASU + RCAS`); Float32 48 kHz Catmull-Rom audio mixer with reverb and mastering EQ (`F7`/`F8`/`F9`).

- **Remastered FMV Cutscenes** ([runtime/win32_avi.c](runtime/win32_avi.c), [tools/upscale_videos.py](tools/upscale_videos.py)):
  - Real-time GPU deblocking + anti-ringing + sharpening for 2005 Cinepak AVIs, plus automatic drop-in HD MP4/MKV cutscene playback from `hd_assets/videos/` in full audio sync.

## Building From Your Own Legal Copy

<br>

### NixOS (`nix-shell`)

```bash
# 1. Extract your legally owned CD image (.bin/.iso) or Wise Setup.EXE into game_files/:
nix-shell --run "python3 tools/extract_game.py '/path/to/Setup.EXE' -o game_files"
# (or copy your existing installed game directory containing mha2.exe, mha2.dat, *.avi into game_files/)

# 2. Lift mha2.exe to C (100% standalone, no Ghidra required) and compile native x86_64 ELF:
nix-shell --run "make lift && make -j$(nproc)"

# 3. Play!
./play.sh
```

<br>

### Standard Linux (Ubuntu / Debian / Fedora / Arch)

Install the build dependencies:

- **Compilers & build tools:** `gcc`, `make`, `pkg-config`, `python3`, `python3-capstone`
- **Development headers:** `SDL2` (`libsdl2-dev`), `SDL2_ttf` (`libsdl2-ttf-dev`), `FFmpeg` (`libavformat-dev`, `libavcodec-dev`, `libswscale-dev`, `libavutil-dev`), and `OpenGL` (`libgl-dev`)

```bash
# 1. Supply your legally owned game files in game_files/ (or extract them with tools/extract_game.py):
python3 tools/extract_game.py '/path/to/Setup.EXE' -o game_files

# 2. Lift mha2.exe to C and build the native x86_64 executable:
make lift && make -j"$(nproc)"

# 3. Launch:
./mha2_linux
```

<br>

### Portable Linux Release Bundle

To package a self-contained portable x86_64 `.tar.gz` bundle using the Ubuntu 22.04 (`glibc 2.35`) builder container (`podman` or `docker`):

```bash
make lift
bash tools/package_portable_linux.sh
```

nix-shell --run "python3 tools/upscale_ai.py --all"

# Remaster the FMV cutscenes (mha2il/ol/sl.avi) to 1600x1200 H.264 MP4 in hd_assets/videos/:

### In-Game Hotkeys

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| **F1** | Open/Close Interactive 3-Tab Display, Post-FX & Audio Settings Menu |
| **F2** | Toggle Live Split-Screen Before / After Comparison (2005 vs 4K Remaster) |
| **F3** | Toggle live GPU stats HUD |
| **F4** | Cycle VSync / High-FPS mode |
| **F5** | Toggle Native Monitor-DPI Vector Text & Subtitles |
| **F6** | Toggle HD Asset Pack Override (`hd_assets/`) |
| **F7 / F8 / F9** | Master volume down / up / mute |
| **F10** | Toggle High-Refresh Decoupled Cursor |
| **F11** or **Alt+Enter** | Toggle borderless fullscreen |
| **F12** | Cycle Widescreen Aspect (`Auto` / `16:10` / `16:9` / `4:3`) |
| **Space** or **H** | Hold to highlight interactive hotspots in the room |
| **Ctrl+L** | Open/Close Dialog & Clue History Log |
| **Shift** | Hold for 2× fast-walk speed |
| **Ctrl + P** or **PrintScreen** | Save clean 4K PNG screenshot (`screenshots/`) |
| **ESC** | Skip cutscenes / open game menu / close F1 menu |

```

`play.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")"
if [ ! -x ./mha2_linux ]; then
  nix-shell --run "make -j\$(nproc)"
fi
cmd="./mha2_linux"
for a in "$@"; do
  cmd+=" $(printf '%q' "$a")"
done
exec nix-shell --run "$cmd"

```

`recomp/lift.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Static Recompiler (x86-32 PE -> Native C) for Moorhuhn Adventure 2: Der Fluch des Goldes (mha2.exe).
Uses Capstone disassembly + REA/Ghidra function inventory + recursive CFG/vtable/switch recovery.
"""
import os
import sys
import json
import struct
import argparse
import collections
import capstone
from capstone import x86 as cs_x86

EXE_PATH = "game_files/mha2.exe"
JOURNAL_PATH = "work/decompiled/decompile-journal.jsonl"
OUT_DIR = "recomp/gen"
NUM_CHUNKS = 32

# Register mapping tables
REG32_MAP = {
    cs_x86.X86_REG_EAX: "c->eax",
    cs_x86.X86_REG_ECX: "c->ecx",
    cs_x86.X86_REG_EDX: "c->edx",
    cs_x86.X86_REG_EBX: "c->ebx",
    cs_x86.X86_REG_ESP: "c->esp",
    cs_x86.X86_REG_EBP: "c->ebp",
    cs_x86.X86_REG_ESI: "c->esi",
    cs_x86.X86_REG_EDI: "c->edi",
}
REG16_MAP = {
    cs_x86.X86_REG_AX: "c->eax",
    cs_x86.X86_REG_CX: "c->ecx",
    cs_x86.X86_REG_DX: "c->edx",
    cs_x86.X86_REG_BX: "c->ebx",
    cs_x86.X86_REG_SP: "c->esp",
    cs_x86.X86_REG_BP: "c->ebp",
    cs_x86.X86_REG_SI: "c->esi",
    cs_x86.X86_REG_DI: "c->edi",
}
REG8_LO_MAP = {
    cs_x86.X86_REG_AL: "c->eax",
    cs_x86.X86_REG_CL: "c->ecx",
    cs_x86.X86_REG_DL: "c->edx",
    cs_x86.X86_REG_BL: "c->ebx",
}
REG8_HI_MAP = {
    cs_x86.X86_REG_AH: "c->eax",
    cs_x86.X86_REG_CH: "c->ecx",
    cs_x86.X86_REG_DH: "c->edx",
    cs_x86.X86_REG_BH: "c->ebx",
}
ST_REGS = {
    cs_x86.X86_REG_ST0: 0,
    cs_x86.X86_REG_ST1: 1,
    cs_x86.X86_REG_ST2: 2,
    cs_x86.X86_REG_ST3: 3,
    cs_x86.X86_REG_ST4: 4,
    cs_x86.X86_REG_ST5: 5,
    cs_x86.X86_REG_ST6: 6,
    cs_x86.X86_REG_ST7: 7,
}

JCC_COND = {
    "je":  "(c->zf)",
    "jz":  "(c->zf)",
    "jne": "(!c->zf)",
    "jnz": "(!c->zf)",
    "jb":  "(c->cf)",
    "jc":  "(c->cf)",
    "jnae":"(c->cf)",
    "jae": "(!c->cf)",
    "jnb": "(!c->cf)",
    "jnc": "(!c->cf)",
    "jbe": "(c->cf || c->zf)",
    "jna": "(c->cf || c->zf)",
    "ja":  "(!c->cf && !c->zf)",
    "jnbe":"(!c->cf && !c->zf)",
    "jl":  "(c->sf != c->of)",
    "jnge":"(c->sf != c->of)",
    "jge": "(c->sf == c->of)",
    "jnl": "(c->sf == c->of)",
    "jle": "(c->zf || (c->sf != c->of))",
    "jng": "(c->zf || (c->sf != c->of))",
    "jg":  "(!c->zf && (c->sf == c->of))",
    "jnle":"(!c->zf && (c->sf == c->of))",
    "js":  "(c->sf)",
    "jns": "(!c->sf)",
    "jo":  "(c->of)",
    "jno": "(!c->of)",
    "jp":  "(c->pf)",
    "jpe": "(c->pf)",
    "jnp": "(!c->pf)",
    "jpo": "(!c->pf)",
    "jecxz": "(c->ecx == 0)",
    "jcxz":  "((c->ecx & 0xFFFFu) == 0)",
}

SETCC_COND = {
    "sete":  "(c->zf)",
    "setz":  "(c->zf)",
    "setne": "(!c->zf)",
    "setnz": "(!c->zf)",
    "setb":  "(c->cf)",
    "setc":  "(c->cf)",
    "setae": "(!c->cf)",
    "setnb": "(!c->cf)",
    "setbe": "(c->cf || c->zf)",
    "setna": "(c->cf || c->zf)",
    "seta":  "(!c->cf && !c->zf)",
    "setnbe":"(!c->cf && !c->zf)",
    "setl":  "(c->sf != c->of)",
    "setge": "(c->sf == c->of)",
    "setle": "(c->zf || (c->sf != c->of))",
    "setg":  "(!c->zf && (c->sf == c->of))",
    "sets":  "(c->sf)",
    "setns": "(!c->sf)",
    "seto":  "(c->of)",
    "setno": "(!c->of)",
    "setp":  "(c->pf)",
    "setnp": "(!c->pf)",
}

INVALID_START_MNEMONICS = {
    "int", "int3", "hlt", "in", "out", "insb", "insw", "insd", "outsb", "outsw", "outsd",
    "arpl", "bound", "daa", "das", "aaa", "aas", "aam", "aad", "into",
    "iret", "iretd", "lds", "les", "salc", "icebp", "ud2", "cli", "sti",
    "lgdt", "lidt", "lldt", "ltr", "sgdt", "sidt", "sldt", "str", "invd",
    "wbinvd", "invlpg", "wrmsr", "rdmsr", "rdpmc", "lmsw", "smsw",
}


class PEImage:
    def __init__(self, path):
        with open(path, "rb") as f:
            self.raw = f.read()
        e_lfanew = struct.unpack_from("<I", self.raw, 0x3c)[0]
        n_sec = struct.unpack_from("<H", self.raw, e_lfanew + 6)[0]
        opt_size = struct.unpack_from("<H", self.raw, e_lfanew + 20)[0]
        self.image_base = struct.unpack_from("<I", self.raw, e_lfanew + 24 + 28)[0]
        self.entry_va = self.image_base + struct.unpack_from("<I", self.raw, e_lfanew + 24 + 16)[0]
        self.sections = []
        sec_off = e_lfanew + 24 + opt_size
        for i in range(n_sec):
            s = self.raw[sec_off + i * 40 : sec_off + (i + 1) * 40]
            name = s[:8].rstrip(b"\x00").decode("latin1")
            vsize, vaddr, rsize, roff = struct.unpack_from("<IIII", s, 8)
            self.sections.append((name, self.image_base + vaddr, vsize, roff, rsize))
            if name == ".text":
                self.text_va = self.image_base + vaddr
                self.text_end = self.text_va + vsize
                self.text_bytes = self.raw[roff : roff + vsize]
            elif name == ".rdata":
                self.rdata_va = self.image_base + vaddr
                self.rdata_bytes = self.raw[roff : roff + min(vsize, rsize)]
            elif name == ".data":
                self.data_va = self.image_base + vaddr
                self.data_bytes = self.raw[roff : roff + min(vsize, rsize)]

        # Parse imports
        imp_rva, _ = struct.unpack_from("<II", self.raw, e_lfanew + 24 + 96 + 8)
        self.imports = []  # (import_id, iat_va, dll, name)
        self.iat_map = {}  # iat_va -> (import_id, dll, name)
        off = self.rva2off(imp_rva)
        imp_id = 0
        while off is not None:
            ilt, ts, fwd, name_rva, iat = struct.unpack_from("<IIIII", self.raw, off)
            if not (ilt or name_rva or iat):
                break
            noff = self.rva2off(name_rva)
            dll = self.raw[noff : self.raw.find(b"\x00", noff)].decode("latin1")
            toff = self.rva2off(ilt or iat)
            idx = 0
            while toff is not None:
                val = struct.unpack_from("<I", self.raw, toff)[0]
                if val == 0:
                    break
                iat_va = self.image_base + iat + idx * 4
                if val & 0x80000000:
                    fname = f"Ordinal_{val & 0xffff}"
                else:
                    foff = self.rva2off(val)
                    fname = self.raw[foff + 2 : self.raw.find(b"\x00", foff + 2)].decode("latin1")
                self.imports.append((imp_id, iat_va, dll, fname))
                self.iat_map[iat_va] = (imp_id, dll, fname)
                imp_id += 1
                toff += 4
                idx += 1
            off += 20

    def rva2off(self, rva):
        va = self.image_base + rva
        return self.va2off(va)

    def va2off(self, va):
        for _, sva, vsz, roff, rsz in self.sections:
            if sva <= va < sva + rsz:
                return roff + (va - sva)
        return None

    def read_u32(self, va):
        off = self.va2off(va)
        if off is None or off + 4 > len(self.raw):
            return None
        return struct.unpack_from("<I", self.raw, off)[0]


def make_cs():
    md = capstone.Cs(capstone.CS_ARCH_X86, capstone.CS_MODE_32)
    md.detail = True
    return md


def is_valid_code_start(pe, md, va):
    if not (pe.text_va <= va < pe.text_end):
        return False
    off = va - pe.text_va
    b = pe.text_bytes[off]
    if b in (0xcc, 0x00):
        return False
    insns = list(md.disasm(pe.text_bytes[off : off + 16], va, count=1))
    if not insns:
        return False
    return insns[0].mnemonic not in INVALID_START_MNEMONICS


def recover_switch_targets(pe, md, table_va, func_va, max_entries=256):
    def is_tgt(v):
        if v is None:
            return False
        if not (pe.text_va <= v < pe.text_end):
            return False
        if v < func_va - 0x400 or v > func_va + 0x4000:
            return False
        return is_valid_code_start(pe, md, v)

    targets = []
    # Forward scan starting at i=0, also allow 1-based tables (dummy entry at i=0, valid at i=1)
    start_i = 0
    v0 = pe.read_u32(table_va)
    if not is_tgt(v0):
        v1 = pe.read_u32(table_va + 4)
        if is_tgt(v1):
            start_i = 1
        else:
            start_i = None
    if start_i is not None:
        cur = table_va + start_i * 4
        for _ in range(max_entries):
            v = pe.read_u32(cur)
            if not is_tgt(v):
                break
            targets.append(v)
            cur += 4
    # Also scan negative indices (i = -1, -2, ...) for MSVC memcpy/memmove negative-indexed tables
    cur = table_va - 4
    for _ in range(16):
        v = pe.read_u32(cur)
        if not is_tgt(v):
            break
        targets.append(v)
        cur -= 4
    return targets


def scan_msvc_eh_tables(pe, md):
    """Scan .rdata for MSVC C++ EH FuncInfo (magic 0x19930520) unwind/catch handlers."""
    found = set()
    rdata = pe.rdata_bytes
    for off in range(0, len(rdata) - 24, 4):
        magic = struct.unpack_from("<I", rdata, off)[0]
        if magic != 0x19930520:
            continue
        max_state, p_unwind, n_try, p_try = struct.unpack_from("<IIII", rdata, off + 4)
        if 0 < max_state <= 256 and p_unwind:
            for i in range(max_state):
                action_va = pe.read_u32(p_unwind + i * 8 + 4)
                if action_va and is_valid_code_start(pe, md, action_va):
                    found.add(action_va)
        if 0 < n_try <= 64 and p_try:
            for i in range(n_try):
                n_catches = pe.read_u32(p_try + i * 20 + 12)
                p_handlers = pe.read_u32(p_try + i * 20 + 16)
                if n_catches and n_catches <= 32 and p_handlers:
                    for j in range(n_catches):
                        h_va = pe.read_u32(p_handlers + j * 16 + 12)
                        if h_va and is_valid_code_start(pe, md, h_va):
                            found.add(h_va)
    return found


def discover_functions(pe, md, use_ghidra=True):
    func_entries = set()
    func_entries.add(pe.entry_va)

    # 1. Optional Ghidra function inventory from REA decompile-journal.jsonl (not required!)
    ghidra_ranges = {}
    if use_ghidra and JOURNAL_PATH and os.path.exists(JOURNAL_PATH):
        for line in open(JOURNAL_PATH):
            j = json.loads(line)
            if j.get("kind") == "function" and j.get("status") != "external":
                va = int(j["address"], 16)
                if pe.text_va <= va < pe.text_end:
                    func_entries.add(va)
                    ranges = (j.get("body") or {}).get("ranges") or []
                    ghidra_ranges[va] = [(int(r["start"], 16), int(r["end"], 16)) for r in ranges]

    # 2. All 32-bit aligned pointers in .rdata and .data into .text (vtables, _initterm, callbacks)
    # Reject only obvious ASCII 4-byte strings (all printable ASCII/NUL) or invalid opcodes
    for buf in (pe.rdata_bytes, pe.data_bytes):
        for off in range(0, len(buf) - 3, 4):
            v = struct.unpack_from("<I", buf, off)[0]
            if not is_valid_code_start(pe, md, v):
                continue
            # Check if bytes of v itself are 3 printable uppercase letters + 0x00 (e.g. "MEM\0", "SOF\0")
            b0, b1, b2, b3 = v & 0xff, (v >> 8) & 0xff, (v >> 16) & 0xff, (v >> 24) & 0xff
            if b3 == 0 and all(0x20 <= b <= 0x7e for b in (b0, b1, b2)):
                prev_b = pe.text_bytes[v - pe.text_va - 1] if v > pe.text_va else 0x90
                if not ((v & 0xf) == 0 or prev_b in (0x90, 0xcc, 0xc3, 0xc2)):
                    continue
            func_entries.add(v)

    # 3. Standalone MSVC C++ EH FuncInfo unwind/catch handler scan
    eh_funcs = scan_msvc_eh_tables(pe, md)
    func_entries.update(eh_funcs)

    # 4. Recursive sweep to find all direct call targets, callback immediates, and switch tables
    seen_insns = set()
    switch_tables = {}  # jmp_va -> (table_va, [targets])

    def run_sweep(initial_queue):
        queue = list(initial_queue)
        while queue:
            va = queue.pop()
            cur = va
            while pe.text_va <= cur < pe.text_end and cur not in seen_insns:
                seen_insns.add(cur)
                off = cur - pe.text_va
                insns = list(md.disasm(pe.text_bytes[off : off + 16], cur, count=1))
                if not insns:
                    break
                ins = insns[0]
                m = ins.mnemonic

                # Check immediate code pointers (push/mov imm32)
                if m in ("push", "mov") and len(ins.operands) >= 1:
                    op = ins.operands[-1]
                    if op.type == cs_x86.X86_OP_IMM:
                        imm = op.imm & 0xffffffff
                        if pe.text_va <= imm < pe.text_end and imm not in func_entries and is_valid_code_start(pe, md, imm):
                            prev_b = pe.text_bytes[imm - pe.text_va - 1] if imm > pe.text_va else 0x90
                            if (imm & 0xf) == 0 or prev_b in (0x90, 0xcc, 0xc3, 0xc2):
                                func_entries.add(imm)
                                queue.append(imm)

                if m == "call":
                    op = ins.operands[0]
                    if op.type == cs_x86.X86_OP_IMM:
                        tgt = op.imm & 0xffffffff
                        if pe.text_va <= tgt < pe.text_end:
                            if tgt not in func_entries:
                                func_entries.add(tgt)
                            if tgt not in seen_insns:
                                queue.append(tgt)
                    cur += ins.size
                elif m == "jmp":
                    op = ins.operands[0]
                    if op.type == cs_x86.X86_OP_IMM:
                        tgt = op.imm & 0xffffffff
                        if pe.text_va <= tgt < pe.text_end and tgt not in seen_insns:
                            queue.append(tgt)
                    elif op.type == cs_x86.X86_OP_MEM:
                        # Check switch table: [index*4 + disp] with base == 0
                        if op.mem.base == 0 and op.mem.index != 0 and op.mem.scale == 4 and op.mem.disp != 0:
                            tva = op.mem.disp & 0xffffffff
                            tgts = recover_switch_targets(pe, md, tva, cur)
                            if tgts:
                                switch_tables[cur] = (tva, tgts)
                                for t in tgts:
                                    if t not in seen_insns:
                                        queue.append(t)
                    break
                elif m in JCC_COND:
                    op = ins.operands[0]
                    if op.type == cs_x86.X86_OP_IMM:
                        tgt = op.imm & 0xffffffff
                        if pe.text_va <= tgt < pe.text_end and tgt not in seen_insns:
                            queue.append(tgt)
                    cur += ins.size
                elif m in ("ret", "retn", "int3", "ud2", "hlt"):
                    break
                else:
                    cur += ins.size

    run_sweep(func_entries)

    # 5. Standalone gap/prologue scan for unreached 16-byte aligned functions after 0x90/0xCC padding
    #    (static ctors/dtors, CRT helpers, inlined MSVC prologues)
    text = pe.text_bytes
    text_len = len(text)
    gap_seeds = []
    # Restrict alignment scan to before any switch data tables, and validate clean prologue
    for off in range(16, text_len - 16, 16):
        va = pe.text_va + off
        if va in seen_insns or va in func_entries:
            continue
        prev = text[off - 1]
        if prev not in (0x90, 0xcc):
            continue
        b0, b1, b2 = text[off], text[off + 1], text[off + 2]
        # Recognize standard MSVC x86 function prologues:
        #   55 8b ec       push ebp; mov ebp, esp
        #   6a ff 68       push -1; push <SEH>
        #   64 a1 00 00    mov eax, fs:[0]
        #   83 ec xx       sub esp, imm8
        #   81 ec xx       sub esp, imm32
        #   56 8b f1       push esi; mov esi, ecx
        #   56 6a 01       push esi; push 1 (MSVC vector/scalar deleting dtor)
        #   56 57          push esi; push edi
        #   c7 01          mov dword ptr [ecx], <vtable>
        #   68 xx xx 4x 00 e8  push <dtor>; call atexit (static ctor)
        is_pro = (
            (b0 == 0x55 and b1 == 0x8b and b2 == 0xec)
            or (b0 == 0x6a and b1 == 0xff and b2 == 0x68)
            or (b0 == 0x64 and b1 == 0xa1 and b2 == 0x00)
            or (b0 == 0x83 and b1 == 0xec)
            or (b0 == 0x81 and b1 == 0xec)
            or (b0 == 0x56 and b1 in (0x8b, 0x6a, 0x57))
            or (b0 == 0xc7 and b1 == 0x01)
            or (b0 == 0x68 and text[off + 5] == 0xe8)
        )
        if is_pro and is_valid_code_start(pe, md, va):
            func_entries.add(va)
            gap_seeds.append(va)
    if gap_seeds:
        run_sweep(gap_seeds)

    return sorted(func_entries), switch_tables


# ---- Operand helpers ----
def mem_addr_expr(op):
    parts = []
    if op.mem.segment == cs_x86.X86_REG_FS:
        parts.append("c->teb_addr")
    if op.mem.base != 0:
        parts.append(REG32_MAP[op.mem.base])
    if op.mem.index != 0:
        idx = REG32_MAP[op.mem.index]
        if op.mem.scale == 1:
            parts.append(idx)
        else:
            parts.append(f"({idx} * {op.mem.scale}u)")
    disp = op.mem.disp
    if disp != 0 or not parts:
        parts.append(f"0x{disp & 0xffffffff:x}u")
    if len(parts) == 1:
        return parts[0]
    return "(uint32_t)(" + " + ".join(parts) + ")"


def read_op(op, size_override=None):
    sz = size_override or op.size
    if op.type == cs_x86.X86_OP_IMM:
        mask = {1: 0xff, 2: 0xffff, 4: 0xffffffff}[sz]
        return f"0x{op.imm & mask:x}u"
    elif op.type == cs_x86.X86_OP_REG:
        r = op.reg
        if r in REG32_MAP:
            return REG32_MAP[r]
        if r in REG16_MAP:
            return f"r16({REG16_MAP[r]})"
        if r in REG8_LO_MAP:
            return f"r8_lo({REG8_LO_MAP[r]})"
        if r in REG8_HI_MAP:
            return f"r8_hi({REG8_HI_MAP[r]})"
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported read reg: {r}")
    elif op.type == cs_x86.X86_OP_MEM:
        addr = mem_addr_expr(op)
        return {1: f"mem_r8({addr})", 2: f"mem_r16({addr})", 4: f"mem_r32({addr})", 8: f"mem_r64({addr})"}[sz]
    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported op type: {op.type}")


def write_op(op, val_expr, size_override=None):
    sz = size_override or op.size
    if op.type == cs_x86.X86_OP_REG:
        r = op.reg
        if r in REG32_MAP:
            return f"{REG32_MAP[r]} = (uint32_t)({val_expr});"
        if r in REG16_MAP:
            return f"w16(&{REG16_MAP[r]}, (uint16_t)({val_expr}));"
        if r in REG8_LO_MAP:
            return f"w8_lo(&{REG8_LO_MAP[r]}, (uint8_t)({val_expr}));"
        if r in REG8_HI_MAP:
            return f"w8_hi(&{REG8_HI_MAP[r]}, (uint8_t)({val_expr}));"
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported write reg: {r}")
    elif op.type == cs_x86.X86_OP_MEM:
        addr = mem_addr_expr(op)
        return {
            1: f"mem_w8({addr}, (uint8_t)({val_expr}));",
            2: f"mem_w16({addr}, (uint16_t)({val_expr}));",
            4: f"mem_w32({addr}, (uint32_t)({val_expr}));",
            8: f"mem_w64({addr}, (uint64_t)({val_expr}));",
        }[sz]
    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported write op type: {op.type}")


def read_fpu_mem(op):
    addr = mem_addr_expr(op)
    if op.size == 4:
        return f"(long double)mem_rf32({addr})"
    elif op.size == 8:
        return f"(long double)mem_rf64({addr})"
    elif op.size == 10:
        return f"mem_rf80({addr})"
    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported FPU mem size {op.size}")


def lift_instruction(ins, pe, func_va, func_entries_set, local_insns_set, switch_tables):
    m = ins.mnemonic
    ops = ins.operands
    next_va = ins.address + ins.size
    lines = []

    # NOP / WAIT / FNCLEX / CLD / STD / SAHF / LAHF / CDQ / CWDE / CBW / CWD / LEAVE
    if m in ("nop", "wait", "fwait", "fnclex", "fclex", "fnop"):
        return []
    if m == "cld":
        return ["c->df = 0;"]
    if m == "std":
        return ["c->df = 1;"]
    if m == "sahf":
        return ["alu_sahf(c);"]
    if m == "lahf":
        return ["alu_lahf(c);"]
    if m == "cdq":
        return ["c->edx = ((int32_t)c->eax < 0) ? 0xFFFFFFFFu : 0u;"]
    if m == "cwde":
        return ["c->eax = (uint32_t)(int32_t)(int16_t)(uint16_t)c->eax;"]
    if m == "cbw":
        return ["w16(&c->eax, (uint16_t)(int16_t)(int8_t)(uint8_t)c->eax);"]
    if m == "cwd":
        return ["w16(&c->edx, ((int16_t)(uint16_t)c->eax < 0) ? 0xFFFFu : 0u);"]
    if m == "leave":
        return ["c->esp = c->ebp; c->ebp = pop32(c);"]
    if m == "clc":
        return ["c->cf = 0;"]
    if m == "stc":
        return ["c->cf = 1;"]
    if m == "cmc":
        return ["c->cf ^= 1u;"]
    if m == "pushfd":
        return ["push32(c, (c->cf) | 2u | (c->pf<<2) | (c->zf<<6) | (c->sf<<7) | (c->df<<10) | (c->of<<11));"]
    if m == "popfd":
        return ["{ uint32_t f = pop32(c); c->cf = f&1; c->pf = (f>>2)&1; c->zf = (f>>6)&1; c->sf = (f>>7)&1; c->df = (f>>10)&1; c->of = (f>>11)&1; }"]
    if m == "pushal":
        return ["{ uint32_t t = c->esp; push32(c, c->eax); push32(c, c->ecx); push32(c, c->edx); push32(c, c->ebx); push32(c, t); push32(c, c->ebp); push32(c, c->esi); push32(c, c->edi); }"]
    if m == "popal":
        return ["c->edi = pop32(c); c->esi = pop32(c); c->ebp = pop32(c); c->esp += 4; c->ebx = pop32(c); c->edx = pop32(c); c->ecx = pop32(c); c->eax = pop32(c);"]

    # MOV / MOVZX / MOVSX / LEA / XCHG / PUSH / POP / BSWAP
    if m == "mov":
        return [write_op(ops[0], read_op(ops[1], ops[0].size))]
    if m == "movzx":
        return [write_op(ops[0], read_op(ops[1]))]
    if m == "movsx":
        src = read_op(ops[1])
        st = {1: "(int32_t)(int8_t)", 2: "(int32_t)(int16_t)"}[ops[1].size]
        return [write_op(ops[0], f"(uint32_t)({st}({src}))")]
    if m == "lea":
        return [write_op(ops[0], mem_addr_expr(ops[1]))]
    if m == "xchg" or m == "lock xchg":
        sz = ops[0].size
        ut = {1: "uint8_t", 2: "uint16_t", 4: "uint32_t"}[sz]
        if ops[0].type == cs_x86.X86_OP_MEM:
            a = mem_addr_expr(ops[0])
            return [f"{{ uint32_t _a = {a}; {ut} _t = __atomic_exchange_n(({ut} *)(g_mem + _a), ({ut})({read_op(ops[1])}), __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST); {write_op(ops[1], '_t')} }}"]
        elif ops[1].type == cs_x86.X86_OP_MEM:
            a = mem_addr_expr(ops[1])
            return [f"{{ uint32_t _a = {a}; {ut} _t = __atomic_exchange_n(({ut} *)(g_mem + _a), ({ut})({read_op(ops[0])}), __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST); {write_op(ops[0], '_t')} }}"]
        else:
            return [f"{{ {ut} _t = ({ut})({read_op(ops[0])}); {write_op(ops[0], read_op(ops[1]))} {write_op(ops[1], '_t')} }}"]
    if m == "bswap":
        r = read_op(ops[0])
        return [write_op(ops[0], f"__builtin_bswap32({r})")]
    if m == "push":
        if ops[0].size == 2:
            return [f"push16(c, (uint16_t)({read_op(ops[0])}));"]
        return [f"push32(c, (uint32_t)({read_op(ops[0], 4)}));"]
    if m == "pop":
        sz = ops[0].size
        ut = {2: "uint16_t", 4: "uint32_t"}[sz]
        pop_fn = f"pop{sz*8}(c)"
        if ops[0].type == cs_x86.X86_OP_MEM and (ops[0].mem.base == cs_x86.X86_REG_ESP or ops[0].mem.index == cs_x86.X86_REG_ESP):
            # Intel SDM: POP [ESP+disp] (16- or 32-bit) increments ESP BEFORE computing effective address!
            return [f"{{ {ut} _v = {pop_fn}; {write_op(ops[0], '_v')} }}"]
        return [write_op(ops[0], pop_fn)]

    # ALU binary ops: add, adc, sub, sbb, and, or, xor
    if m in ("add", "adc", "sub", "sbb", "and", "or", "xor"):
        sz = ops[0].size
        bits = sz * 8
        ut = {1: "uint8_t", 2: "uint16_t", 4: "uint32_t"}[sz]
        if m in ("and", "or", "xor"):
            opch = {"and": "&", "or": "|", "xor": "^"}[m]
            fn = f"alu_logic{bits}"
            if ops[0].type == cs_x86.X86_OP_MEM:
                return [f"{{ uint32_t _a = {mem_addr_expr(ops[0])}; mem_w{bits}(_a, {fn}(c, ({ut})(mem_r{bits}(_a) {opch} ({ut})({read_op(ops[1], sz)})))); }}"]
            return [write_op(ops[0], f"{fn}(c, ({ut})({read_op(ops[0])} {opch} ({ut})({read_op(ops[1], sz)})))")]
        else:
            fn = f"alu_{m}{bits}"
            if ops[0].type == cs_x86.X86_OP_MEM:
                return [f"{{ uint32_t _a = {mem_addr_expr(ops[0])}; mem_w{bits}(_a, {fn}(c, mem_r{bits}(_a), ({ut})({read_op(ops[1], sz)}))); }}"]
            return [write_op(ops[0], f"{fn}(c, {read_op(ops[0])}, ({ut})({read_op(ops[1], sz)}))")]

    # LOCK ADD / SUB / INC / DEC / XADD / CMPXCHG
    if m == "lock inc":
        sz = ops[0].size
        bits = sz * 8
        ut = {1: "uint8_t", 2: "uint16_t", 4: "uint32_t"}[sz]
        return [f"{{ uint32_t _a = {mem_addr_expr(ops[0])}; {ut} _old = __atomic_fetch_add(({ut} *)(g_mem + _a), ({ut})1u, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST); alu_inc{bits}(c, _old); }}"]
    if m == "lock dec":
        sz = ops[0].size
        bits = sz * 8
        ut = {1: "uint8_t", 2: "uint16_t", 4: "uint32_t"}[sz]
        return [f"{{ uint32_t _a = {mem_addr_expr(ops[0])}; {ut} _old = __atomic_fetch_sub(({ut} *)(g_mem + _a), ({ut})1u, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST); alu_dec{bits}(c, _old); }}"]
    if m == "lock add":
        sz = ops[0].size
        bits = sz * 8
        ut = {1: "uint8_t", 2: "uint16_t", 4: "uint32_t"}[sz]
        return [f"{{ uint32_t _a = {mem_addr_expr(ops[0])}; {ut} _add = ({ut})({read_op(ops[1], sz)}); {ut} _old = __atomic_fetch_add(({ut} *)(g_mem + _a), _add, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST); alu_add{bits}(c, _old, _add); }}"]

    # CMP / TEST
    if m == "cmp":
        sz = ops[0].size
        bits = sz * 8
        ut = {1: "uint8_t", 2: "uint16_t", 4: "uint32_t"}[sz]
        return [f"alu_sub{bits}(c, ({ut})({read_op(ops[0])}), ({ut})({read_op(ops[1], sz)}));"]
    if m == "test":
        sz = ops[0].size
        bits = sz * 8
        ut = {1: "uint8_t", 2: "uint16_t", 4: "uint32_t"}[sz]
        return [f"alu_logic{bits}(c, ({ut})(({ut})({read_op(ops[0])}) & ({ut})({read_op(ops[1], sz)})));"]

    # Unary ALU: inc, dec, neg, not
    if m in ("inc", "dec", "neg"):
        sz = ops[0].size
        bits = sz * 8
        fn = f"alu_{m}{bits}"
        if ops[0].type == cs_x86.X86_OP_MEM:
            return [f"{{ uint32_t _a = {mem_addr_expr(ops[0])}; mem_w{bits}(_a, {fn}(c, mem_r{bits}(_a))); }}"]
        return [write_op(ops[0], f"{fn}(c, {read_op(ops[0])})")]
    if m == "not":
        sz = ops[0].size
        bits = sz * 8
        if ops[0].type == cs_x86.X86_OP_MEM:
            return [f"{{ uint32_t _a = {mem_addr_expr(ops[0])}; mem_w{bits}(_a, ~mem_r{bits}(_a)); }}"]
        return [write_op(ops[0], f"~({read_op(ops[0])})")]

    # Shifts / Rotates
    if m in ("shl", "sal", "shr", "sar", "rol", "ror", "rcr", "rcl"):
        nm = "shl" if m == "sal" else m
        sz = ops[0].size
        bits = sz * 8
        cnt = read_op(ops[1], 1) if len(ops) == 2 else "1u"
        if nm in ("rcr", "rcl") and bits != 32:
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported {nm} width {bits}")
        fn = f"alu_{nm}{bits}"
        if ops[0].type == cs_x86.X86_OP_MEM:
            return [f"{{ uint32_t _a = {mem_addr_expr(ops[0])}; mem_w{bits}(_a, {fn}(c, mem_r{bits}(_a), {cnt})); }}"]
        return [write_op(ops[0], f"{fn}(c, {read_op(ops[0])}, {cnt})")]

    if m in ("shld", "shrd"):
        sz = ops[0].size
        if sz != 4:
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported {m} width {sz}")
        fn = f"alu_{m}32"
        cnt = read_op(ops[2], 1)
        if ops[0].type == cs_x86.X86_OP_MEM:
            return [f"{{ uint32_t _a = {mem_addr_expr(ops[0])}; mem_w32(_a, {fn}(c, mem_r32(_a), {read_op(ops[1])}, {cnt})); }}"]
        return [write_op(ops[0], f"{fn}(c, {read_op(ops[0])}, {read_op(ops[1])}, {cnt})")]

    # MUL / IMUL / DIV / IDIV
    if m == "mul":
        bits = ops[0].size * 8
        return [f"alu_mul{bits}(c, {read_op(ops[0])});"]
    if m == "imul":
        if len(ops) == 1:
            bits = ops[0].size * 8
            return [f"alu_imul1_{bits}(c, {read_op(ops[0])});"]
        elif len(ops) == 2:
            bits = ops[0].size * 8
            return [write_op(ops[0], f"alu_imul2_{bits}(c, {read_op(ops[0])}, {read_op(ops[1], ops[0].size)})")]
        elif len(ops) == 3:
            bits = ops[0].size * 8
            return [write_op(ops[0], f"alu_imul2_{bits}(c, {read_op(ops[1])}, {read_op(ops[2], ops[0].size)})")]
    if m == "div":
        bits = ops[0].size * 8
        return [f"alu_div{bits}(c, {read_op(ops[0])});"]
    if m == "idiv":
        bits = ops[0].size * 8
        return [f"alu_idiv{bits}(c, {read_op(ops[0])});"]

    # Bit test: bt, bts, btr, btc, bsf, bsr
    if m in ("bt", "bts", "btr", "btc"):
        sz = ops[0].size
        bits = sz * 8
        ut = {2: "uint16_t", 4: "uint32_t"}[sz]
        it = {2: "int16_t", 4: "int32_t"}[sz]
        mask = bits - 1
        shift = 4 if sz == 2 else 5
        bit = read_op(ops[1])
        if ops[0].type == cs_x86.X86_OP_MEM:
            addr = mem_addr_expr(ops[0])
            if ops[1].type == cs_x86.X86_OP_IMM:
                base_code = [f"{{ uint32_t _a = {addr}; {ut} _m = ({ut})(1u << (({bit}) & {mask}u)); {ut} _v = mem_r{bits}(_a); c->cf = (_v & _m) != 0;"]
            else:
                base_code = [f"{{ int32_t _b = (int32_t)({it})({bit}); uint32_t _a = {addr} + (uint32_t)((_b >> {shift}) * {sz}); {ut} _m = ({ut})(1u << (_b & {mask})); {ut} _v = mem_r{bits}(_a); c->cf = (_v & _m) != 0;"]
            if m == "bts":
                base_code.append(f"mem_w{bits}(_a, ({ut})(_v | _m));")
            elif m == "btr":
                base_code.append(f"mem_w{bits}(_a, ({ut})(_v & ~_m));")
            elif m == "btc":
                base_code.append(f"mem_w{bits}(_a, ({ut})(_v ^ _m));")
            base_code.append("}")
            return [" ".join(base_code)]
        else:
            v = read_op(ops[0])
            code = f"{{ {ut} _m = ({ut})(1u << (({bit}) & {mask}u)); {ut} _v = ({ut})({v}); c->cf = (_v & _m) != 0;"
            if m == "bts":
                code += f" {write_op(ops[0], '(_v | _m)')}"
            elif m == "btr":
                code += f" {write_op(ops[0], '(_v & ~_m)')}"
            elif m == "btc":
                code += f" {write_op(ops[0], '(_v ^ _m)')}"
            code += " }"
            return [code]
    if m in ("bsf", "bsr"):
        src = read_op(ops[1])
        fn = "__builtin_ctz" if m == "bsf" else "31u - (uint32_t)__builtin_clz"
        return [f"{{ uint32_t _s = {src}; c->zf = (_s == 0); if (_s) {{ {write_op(ops[0], f'{fn}(_s)')} }} }}"]

    # SETcc / CMOVcc
    if m in SETCC_COND:
        return [write_op(ops[0], f"({SETCC_COND[m]} ? 1u : 0u)")]
    if m.startswith("cmov"):
        cc = "j" + m[4:]
        if cc in JCC_COND:
            return [f"if ({JCC_COND[cc]}) {{ {write_op(ops[0], read_op(ops[1]))} }}"]

    # String ops
    STR_MAP = {
        "movsb": "str_movsb(c);", "movsw": "str_movsw(c);", "movsd": "str_movsd(c);",
        "rep movsb": "rep_movsb(c);", "rep movsw": "rep_movsw(c);", "rep movsd": "rep_movsd(c);",
        "stosb": "str_stosb(c);", "stosw": "str_stosw(c);", "stosd": "str_stosd(c);",
        "rep stosb": "rep_stosb(c);", "rep stosw": "rep_stosw(c);", "rep stosd": "rep_stosd(c);",
        "repe cmpsb": "repe_cmpsb(c);", "repe cmpsw": "repe_cmpsw(c);", "repe cmpsd": "repe_cmpsd(c);",
        "repne scasb": "repne_scasb(c);", "repe scasb": "repe_scasb(c);",
    }
    if m in STR_MAP:
        return [STR_MAP[m]]

    # Control flow: CALL, RET, JMP, Jcc
    if m == "call":
        op = ops[0]
        if op.type == cs_x86.X86_OP_IMM:
            tgt = op.imm & 0xffffffff
            if tgt in HOOKABLE_FUNCS:
                return [f"push32(c, 0x{next_va:08x}u); g_code_table[0x{tgt - pe.text_va:x}u](c);"]
            return [f"push32(c, 0x{next_va:08x}u); sub_{tgt:08x}(c);"]
        elif op.type == cs_x86.X86_OP_MEM and op.mem.base == 0 and op.mem.index == 0 and (op.mem.disp & 0xffffffff) in pe.iat_map:
            imp_id, dll, fname = pe.iat_map[op.mem.disp & 0xffffffff]
            return [f"push32(c, 0x{next_va:08x}u); win32_dispatch(c, {imp_id}u); /* {dll}!{fname} */"]
