```py
        else:
            return [f"{{ uint32_t _t = {read_op(op)}; push32(c, 0x{next_va:08x}u); dispatch_call(c, _t); }}"]

    if m in ("ret", "retn"):
        imm = (ops[0].imm & 0xffff) if len(ops) == 1 else 0
        return [f"c->esp += {4 + imm}u; return;"]

    if m == "jmp":
        op = ops[0]
        if op.type == cs_x86.X86_OP_IMM:
            tgt = op.imm & 0xffffffff
            if tgt in local_insns_set:
                return [f"goto L_{tgt:08x};"]
            elif tgt in HOOKABLE_FUNCS:
                return [f"g_code_table[0x{tgt - pe.text_va:x}u](c); return;"]
            elif tgt in func_entries_set:
                return [f"sub_{tgt:08x}(c); return;"]
            else:
                return [f"dispatch_call(c, 0x{tgt:08x}u); return;"]
        elif op.type == cs_x86.X86_OP_MEM and op.mem.base == 0 and op.mem.index == 0 and (op.mem.disp & 0xffffffff) in pe.iat_map:
            imp_id, dll, fname = pe.iat_map[op.mem.disp & 0xffffffff]
            return [f"win32_dispatch(c, {imp_id}u); return; /* {dll}!{fname} */"]
        elif ins.address in switch_tables:
            _, tgts = switch_tables[ins.address]
            unique_tgts = sorted(set(tgts))
            s = [f"switch ({read_op(op)}) {{"]
            for t in unique_tgts:
                if t in local_insns_set:
                    s.append(f"  case 0x{t:08x}u: goto L_{t:08x};")
                else:
                    s.append(f"  case 0x{t:08x}u: dispatch_call(c, 0x{t:08x}u); return;")
            s.append(f"  default: dispatch_call(c, {read_op(op)}); return;")
            s.append("}")
            return s
        else:
            return [f"dispatch_call(c, {read_op(op)}); return;"]

    if m in JCC_COND:
        tgt = ops[0].imm & 0xffffffff
        cond = JCC_COND[m]
        if tgt in local_insns_set:
            return [f"if ({cond}) goto L_{tgt:08x};"]
        elif tgt in HOOKABLE_FUNCS:
            return [f"if ({cond}) {{ g_code_table[0x{tgt - pe.text_va:x}u](c); return; }}"]
        elif tgt in func_entries_set:
            return [f"if ({cond}) {{ sub_{tgt:08x}(c); return; }}"]
        else:
            return [f"if ({cond}) {{ dispatch_call(c, 0x{tgt:08x}u); return; }}"]

    if m == "loop":
        tgt = ops[0].imm & 0xffffffff
        return [f"c->ecx--; if (c->ecx != 0) goto L_{tgt:08x};"]

    # ---- x87 FPU ----
    if m == "fld":
        if ops[0].type == cs_x86.X86_OP_REG:
            i = ST_REGS[ops[0].reg]
            return [f"{{ long double _v = fpu_st(c, {i}); fpu_push(c, _v); }}"]
        return [f"fpu_push(c, {read_fpu_mem(ops[0])});"]
    if m == "fild":
        addr = mem_addr_expr(ops[0])
        cast = {2: "(int16_t)mem_r16", 4: "(int32_t)mem_r32", 8: "(int64_t)mem_r64"}[ops[0].size]
        return [f"fpu_push(c, (long double)({cast}({addr})));"]
    if m == "fldz":
        return ["fpu_push(c, 0.0L);"]
    if m == "fld1":
        return ["fpu_push(c, 1.0L);"]
    if m == "fldpi":
        return ["fpu_push(c, 3.14159265358979323846L);"]
    if m == "fldln2":
        return ["fpu_push(c, 0.69314718055994530942L);"]
    if m == "fldl2e":
        return ["fpu_push(c, 1.44269504088896340736L);"]
    if m == "fldl2t":
        return ["fpu_push(c, 3.32192809488736234787L);"]
    if m == "fldlg2":
        return ["fpu_push(c, 0.30102999566398119521L);"]

    if m in ("fst", "fstp"):
        pop_code = " fpu_pop(c);" if m == "fstp" else ""
        if ops[0].type == cs_x86.X86_OP_REG:
            i = ST_REGS[ops[0].reg]
            return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, {i}) = fpu_st(c, 0);{pop_code}"]
        addr = mem_addr_expr(ops[0])
        if ops[0].size == 4:
            return [f"mem_wf32({addr}, (float)fpu_st(c, 0));{pop_code}"]
        elif ops[0].size == 8:
            return [f"mem_wf64({addr}, (double)fpu_st(c, 0));{pop_code}"]
        elif ops[0].size == 10:
            return [f"mem_wf80({addr}, fpu_st(c, 0));{pop_code}"]
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported {m} size {ops[0].size}")

    if m in ("fist", "fistp"):
        pop_code = " fpu_pop(c);" if m == "fistp" else ""
        addr = mem_addr_expr(ops[0])
        wfn = {2: "mem_w16", 4: "mem_w32", 8: "mem_w64"}[ops[0].size]
        ut = {2: "uint16_t", 4: "uint32_t", 8: "uint64_t"}[ops[0].size]
        return [f"{wfn}({addr}, ({ut})fpu_round_i64(c, fpu_st(c, 0)));{pop_code}"]

    if m in ("fadd", "faddp", "fmul", "fmulp", "fsub", "fsubp", "fsubr", "fsubrp", "fdiv", "fdivp", "fdivr", "fdivrp"):
        pop = m.endswith("p")
        base_m = m[:-1] if pop else m
        b0 = ins.bytes[0]
        if len(ops) == 0:
            # Default ST(1) op= ST(0); pop
            idx = 1
            if base_m == "fadd":
                return ["*fpu_st_ptr(c, 1) += fpu_st(c, 0); fpu_pop(c);"]
            if base_m == "fmul":
                return ["*fpu_st_ptr(c, 1) *= fpu_st(c, 0); fpu_pop(c);"]
            if base_m == "fsub":
                return ["*fpu_st_ptr(c, 1) = fpu_st(c, 1) - fpu_st(c, 0); fpu_pop(c);"]
            if base_m == "fsubr":
                return ["*fpu_st_ptr(c, 1) = fpu_st(c, 0) - fpu_st(c, 1); fpu_pop(c);"]
            if base_m == "fdiv":
                return ["*fpu_st_ptr(c, 1) = fpu_st(c, 1) / fpu_st(c, 0); fpu_pop(c);"]
            if base_m == "fdivr":
                return ["*fpu_st_ptr(c, 1) = fpu_st(c, 0) / fpu_st(c, 1); fpu_pop(c);"]
        elif len(ops) == 1 and ops[0].type == cs_x86.X86_OP_MEM:
            val = read_fpu_mem(ops[0])
            if base_m == "fadd":  return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) += {val};"]
            if base_m == "fmul":  return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) *= {val};"]
            if base_m == "fsub":  return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) -= {val};"]
            if base_m == "fsubr": return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) = ({val}) - fpu_st(c, 0);"]
            if base_m == "fdiv":  return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) /= {val};"]
            if base_m == "fdivr": return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) = ({val}) / fpu_st(c, 0);"]
        else:
            # Register-register x87 op. Distinguish dst/src using opcode byte (0xD8 -> dst=ST0, 0xDC/0xDE -> dst=STi)
            if len(ops) == 1:
                i = ST_REGS[ops[0].reg]
                dst_is_sti = (b0 in (0xDC, 0xDE))
            else:
                d_idx = ST_REGS[ops[0].reg]
                s_idx = ST_REGS[ops[1].reg]
                dst_is_sti = (d_idx != 0) or (b0 in (0xDC, 0xDE))
                i = d_idx if dst_is_sti else s_idx
            pop_s = " fpu_pop(c);" if pop else ""
            if not dst_is_sti:
                # D8: ST(0) = ST(0) op ST(i) (or reversed)
                if base_m == "fadd":  return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) += fpu_st(c, {i});{pop_s}"]
                if base_m == "fmul":  return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) *= fpu_st(c, {i});{pop_s}"]
                if base_m == "fsub":  return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) = fpu_st(c, 0) - fpu_st(c, {i});{pop_s}"]
                if base_m == "fsubr": return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) = fpu_st(c, {i}) - fpu_st(c, 0);{pop_s}"]
                if base_m == "fdiv":  return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) = fpu_st(c, 0) / fpu_st(c, {i});{pop_s}"]
                if base_m == "fdivr": return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) = fpu_st(c, {i}) / fpu_st(c, 0);{pop_s}"]
            else:
                # DC/DE: ST(i) = ST(i) op ST(0) (or reversed for fsubr/fdivr!)
                if base_m == "fadd":  return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, {i}) += fpu_st(c, 0);{pop_s}"]
                if base_m == "fmul":  return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, {i}) *= fpu_st(c, 0);{pop_s}"]
                if base_m == "fsub":  return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, {i}) = fpu_st(c, {i}) - fpu_st(c, 0);{pop_s}"]
                if base_m == "fsubr": return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, {i}) = fpu_st(c, 0) - fpu_st(c, {i});{pop_s}"]
                if base_m == "fdiv":  return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, {i}) = fpu_st(c, {i}) / fpu_st(c, 0);{pop_s}"]
                if base_m == "fdivr": return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, {i}) = fpu_st(c, 0) / fpu_st(c, {i});{pop_s}"]

    if m in ("fiadd", "fisub", "fisubr", "fimul", "fidiv", "fidivr"):
        addr = mem_addr_expr(ops[0])
        cast = {2: "(int16_t)mem_r16", 4: "(int32_t)mem_r32"}[ops[0].size]
        val = f"(long double)({cast}({addr}))"
        if m == "fiadd":  return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) += {val};"]
        if m == "fimul":  return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) *= {val};"]
        if m == "fisub":  return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) -= {val};"]
        if m == "fisubr": return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) = ({val}) - fpu_st(c, 0);"]
        if m == "fidiv":  return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) /= {val};"]
        if m == "fidivr": return [f"*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) = ({val}) / fpu_st(c, 0);"]

    if m in ("fcom", "fcomp", "fucom", "fucomp"):
        pop_s = " fpu_pop(c);" if m.endswith("p") else ""
        if len(ops) == 0:
            return [f"fpu_cmp(c, fpu_st(c, 0), fpu_st(c, 1));{pop_s}"]
        elif ops[0].type == cs_x86.X86_OP_REG:
            i = ST_REGS[ops[0].reg]
            return [f"fpu_cmp(c, fpu_st(c, 0), fpu_st(c, {i}));{pop_s}"]
        else:
            return [f"fpu_cmp(c, fpu_st(c, 0), {read_fpu_mem(ops[0])});{pop_s}"]
    if m in ("fcompp", "fucompp"):
        return ["fpu_cmp(c, fpu_st(c, 0), fpu_st(c, 1)); fpu_pop(c); fpu_pop(c);"]
    if m == "ftst":
        return ["fpu_cmp(c, fpu_st(c, 0), 0.0L);"]
    if m == "fxch":
        if len(ops) == 0:
            i = 1
        elif len(ops) == 1:
            i = ST_REGS[ops[0].reg]
        else:
            # Capstone emits 2 operands [ST(0), ST(i)] (or [ST(i), ST(0)]) for D9 C8+i
            r0 = ST_REGS[ops[0].reg]
            r1 = ST_REGS[ops[1].reg]
            i = r1 if r0 == 0 else r0
        if i == 0:
            return ["(void)0; /* fxch st(0) */"]
        return [f"{{ long double _t = fpu_st(c, 0); *fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) = fpu_st(c, {i}); *fpu_st_ptr(c, {i}) = _t; }}"]
    if m == "fchs":
        return ["*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) = -fpu_st(c, 0);"]
    if m == "fabs":
        return ["*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) = fabsl(fpu_st(c, 0));"]
    if m == "fsqrt":
        return ["*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) = sqrtl(fpu_st(c, 0));"]
    if m == "fsin":
        return ["{ long double _a = fpu_st(c, 0); if (isfinite(_a) && fabsl(_a) < 0x1p63L) { *fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) = sinl(_a); c->fpu_sw &= (uint16_t)~0x0400u; } else { c->fpu_sw |= 0x0400u; } }"]
    if m == "fcos":
        return ["{ long double _a = fpu_st(c, 0); if (isfinite(_a) && fabsl(_a) < 0x1p63L) { *fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) = cosl(_a); c->fpu_sw &= (uint16_t)~0x0400u; } else { c->fpu_sw |= 0x0400u; } }"]
    if m == "fsincos":
        return ["{ long double _a = fpu_st(c, 0); if (isfinite(_a) && fabsl(_a) < 0x1p63L) { *fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) = sinl(_a); fpu_push(c, cosl(_a)); c->fpu_sw &= (uint16_t)~0x0400u; } else { c->fpu_sw |= 0x0400u; } }"]
    if m == "fptan":
        return ["{ long double _a = fpu_st(c, 0); if (isfinite(_a) && fabsl(_a) < 0x1p63L) { *fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) = tanl(_a); fpu_push(c, 1.0L); c->fpu_sw &= (uint16_t)~0x0400u; } else { c->fpu_sw |= 0x0400u; } }"]
    if m == "fpatan":
        return ["*fpu_st_ptr(c, 1) = atan2l(fpu_st(c, 1), fpu_st(c, 0)); fpu_pop(c);"]
    if m == "f2xm1":
        return ["*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) = exp2l(fpu_st(c, 0)) - 1.0L;"]
    if m == "fyl2x":
        return ["*fpu_st_ptr(c, 1) = fpu_st(c, 1) * log2l(fpu_st(c, 0)); fpu_pop(c);"]
    if m == "fyl2xp1":
        return ["*fpu_st_ptr(c, 1) = fpu_st(c, 1) * log2l(fpu_st(c, 0) + 1.0L); fpu_pop(c);"]
    if m == "fscale":
        return ["*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) = ldexpl(fpu_st(c, 0), (int)truncl(fpu_st(c, 1)));"]
    if m == "frndint":
        return ["*fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) = fpu_round_ld(c, fpu_st(c, 0));"]
    if m == "fprem":
        return ["{ long double _a = fpu_st(c, 0), _b = fpu_st(c, 1); *fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) = fmodl(_a, _b); long double _qf = (_b != 0.0L) ? fabsl(truncl(_a / _b)) : 0.0L; uint32_t _uq = (_qf < 0x1p52L) ? (uint32_t)fmodl(_qf, 8.0L) : 0u; c->fpu_sw &= (uint16_t)~0x4700u; if (_uq & 1u) c->fpu_sw |= 0x0200u; if (_uq & 2u) c->fpu_sw |= 0x4000u; if (_uq & 4u) c->fpu_sw |= 0x0100u; }"]
    if m == "fprem1":
        return ["{ long double _a = fpu_st(c, 0), _b = fpu_st(c, 1); int _q = 0; *fpu_st_ptr(c, 0) = remquol(_a, _b, &_q); uint32_t _uq = (_q < 0) ? (0u - (uint32_t)_q) : (uint32_t)_q; c->fpu_sw &= (uint16_t)~0x4700u; if (_uq & 1u) c->fpu_sw |= 0x0200u; if (_uq & 2u) c->fpu_sw |= 0x4000u; if (_uq & 4u) c->fpu_sw |= 0x0100u; }"]
    if m in ("fnstsw", "fstsw"):
        return [write_op(ops[0], "fpu_get_sw(c)")]
    if m in ("fnstcw", "fstcw"):
        return [write_op(ops[0], "c->fpu_cw")]
    if m == "fldcw":
        return [f"c->fpu_cw = {read_op(ops[0], 2)};"]
    if m in ("fnsave", "fsave"):
        return [f"fpu_save(c, {mem_addr_expr(ops[0])});"]
    if m == "frstor":
        return [f"fpu_rstor(c, {mem_addr_expr(ops[0])});"]
    if m == "fninit" or m == "finit":
        return ["c->fpu_cw = 0x037Fu; c->fpu_sw = 0; c->fpu_top = 0;"]

    # Rare/fallback
    if m in ("int3", "ud2", "hlt"):
        return [f'fprintf(stderr, "TRAP {m} at 0x{ins.address:08x}\\n"); return;']

    raise ValueError(f"Unhandled instruction at 0x{ins.address:08x}: {m} {ins.op_str}")


# Original-game functions patched at runtime via g_code_table (runtime/game_patches.c).
# Direct calls to these VAs are dispatched through g_code_table so runtime patches take effect.
HOOKABLE_FUNCS = {
    0x00444230,  # ObjMutex::TryLock (non-atomic spinlock + forced lock-steal fix)
    0x004443F0,  # MetaSpinLock::Lock (non-atomic spinlock + forced lock-steal fix)
    0x004A7A5E,  # CRT ceil(double) (native hardware replacement for RLE blitters)
    0x004A71CC,  # CRT __ftol (native hardware replacement for RLE blitters)
}

# Exact instruction VAs in 0x00474fd0 that dereference ESI before `test esi, esi; je`
ESI_NULL_GUARD_VAS = {0x0047530E, 0x00475317}


def lift_function(pe, md, func_va, func_entries_set, switch_tables):
    # Collect all local instructions belonging to this function via intra-procedural BFS
    local_insns = {}  # va -> capstone insn
    jump_targets = set()
    queue = [func_va]
    visited = set()

    while queue:
        cur = queue.pop()
        while pe.text_va <= cur < pe.text_end and cur not in visited:
            visited.add(cur)
            off = cur - pe.text_va
            insns = list(md.disasm(pe.text_bytes[off : off + 16], cur, count=1))
            if not insns:
                break
            ins = insns[0]
            local_insns[cur] = ins
            m = ins.mnemonic
            nxt = cur + ins.size

            if m == "jmp":
                op = ins.operands[0]
                if op.type == cs_x86.X86_OP_IMM:
                    tgt = op.imm & 0xffffffff
                    # Tail-call if jumping to a different function entry
                    if tgt != func_va and tgt in func_entries_set and tgt not in jump_targets:
                        break
                    if pe.text_va <= tgt < pe.text_end:
                        jump_targets.add(tgt)
                        if tgt not in visited:
                            queue.append(tgt)
                elif cur in switch_tables:
                    _, tgts = switch_tables[cur]
                    for t in tgts:
                        jump_targets.add(t)
                        if t not in visited:
                            queue.append(t)
                break
            elif m in JCC_COND or m == "loop":
                op = ins.operands[0]
                if op.type == cs_x86.X86_OP_IMM:
                    tgt = op.imm & 0xffffffff
                    if pe.text_va <= tgt < pe.text_end and not (tgt != func_va and tgt in func_entries_set and abs(tgt - func_va) > 0x2000):
                        jump_targets.add(tgt)
                        if tgt not in visited:
                            queue.append(tgt)
                cur = nxt
            elif m in ("ret", "retn", "int3", "ud2", "hlt"):
                break
            else:
                cur = nxt

    sorted_vas = sorted(local_insns.keys())
    local_set = set(sorted_vas)
    if sorted_vas and sorted_vas[0] != func_va:
        jump_targets.add(func_va)
    # Pre-scan fallthrough gotos so every target gets a label
    for idx, va in enumerate(sorted_vas):
        ins = local_insns[va]
        m = ins.mnemonic
        if m not in ("jmp", "ret", "retn", "int3", "ud2", "hlt"):
            nxt = va + ins.size
            next_in_list = sorted_vas[idx + 1] if idx + 1 < len(sorted_vas) else None
            if nxt != next_in_list and nxt in local_set:
                jump_targets.add(nxt)

    out = [f"void sub_{func_va:08x}(CpuCtx *c) {{"]
    if sorted_vas and sorted_vas[0] != func_va:
        out.append(f"  goto L_{func_va:08x};")

    for idx, va in enumerate(sorted_vas):
        ins = local_insns[va]
        if va in jump_targets:
            out.append(f"L_{va:08x}:;")
        stmts = lift_instruction(ins, pe, func_va, func_entries_set, local_set, switch_tables)
        if va in ESI_NULL_GUARD_VAS:
            for st in stmts:
                out.append(f"  if (c->esi) {{ {st} }}")
        else:
            for st in stmts:
                out.append(f"  {st}")
        # If this instruction falls through and next sorted va is not its fallthrough, emit explicit goto
        m = ins.mnemonic
        falls_through = m not in ("jmp", "ret", "retn", "int3", "ud2", "hlt")
        if falls_through:
            nxt = va + ins.size
            next_in_list = sorted_vas[idx + 1] if idx + 1 < len(sorted_vas) else None
            if nxt != next_in_list:
                if nxt in local_set:
                    out.append(f"  goto L_{nxt:08x};")
                elif nxt in HOOKABLE_FUNCS:
                    out.append(f"  g_code_table[0x{nxt - pe.text_va:x}u](c); return;")
                elif nxt in func_entries_set:
                    out.append(f"  sub_{nxt:08x}(c); return;")
    out.append("}")
    return "\n".join(out), len(local_insns)


def main():
    global EXE_PATH, OUT_DIR, JOURNAL_PATH
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Static x86-32 PE -> C recompiler for Moorhuhn Adventure 2 (mha2.exe)")
    ap.add_argument("--exe", default=EXE_PATH, help="Path to mha2.exe (default: game_files/mha2.exe)")
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=OUT_DIR, help="Output directory for generated C chunks (default: recomp/gen)")
    ap.add_argument("--standalone", action="store_true", help="Ignore Ghidra journal; discover all functions purely from PE")
    ap.add_argument("--journal", default=JOURNAL_PATH, help="Optional REA/Ghidra decompile-journal.jsonl")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    EXE_PATH = args.exe
    OUT_DIR = args.out
    JOURNAL_PATH = args.journal
    use_ghidra = (not args.standalone) and os.path.exists(JOURNAL_PATH)

    os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    pe = PEImage(EXE_PATH)
    md = make_cs()
    mode_str = "Ghidra+Standalone" if use_ghidra else "100% Standalone (no Ghidra)"
    print(f"Loaded PE {EXE_PATH} [{mode_str}]: image_base={pe.image_base:#x} entry={pe.entry_va:#x} .text={pe.text_va:#x}..{pe.text_end:#x} imports={len(pe.imports)}")

    func_entries, switch_tables = discover_functions(pe, md, use_ghidra=use_ghidra)
    func_entries_set = set(func_entries)
    print(f"Discovered {len(func_entries)} functions and {len(switch_tables)} switch tables")

    # 1. Write imports.h
    with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "imports.h"), "w") as f:
        f.write("#ifndef MHA2_IMPORTS_H\n#define MHA2_IMPORTS_H\n#include <stdint.h>\n\n")
        f.write(f"#define MHA2_IMPORT_COUNT {len(pe.imports)}\n")
        f.write(f"#define MHA2_ENTRY_VA 0x{pe.entry_va:08x}u\n\n")
        f.write("typedef struct ImportEntry { uint32_t id; uint32_t iat_va; const char *dll; const char *name; } ImportEntry;\n\n")
        f.write("static const ImportEntry g_imports[MHA2_IMPORT_COUNT] = {\n")
        for imp_id, iat_va, dll, fname in pe.imports:
            f.write(f'  {{ {imp_id}u, 0x{iat_va:08x}u, "{dll}", "{fname}" }},\n')
        f.write("};\n\n")
        for imp_id, iat_va, dll, fname in pe.imports:
            safe = f"IMP_{dll.replace('.', '_').upper()}_{fname}"
            f.write(f"#define {safe} {imp_id}u\n")
        f.write("#endif\n")

    # 2. Write funcs.h
    with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "funcs.h"), "w") as f:
        f.write("#ifndef MHA2_FUNCS_H\n#define MHA2_FUNCS_H\n#include \"cpu.h\"\n\n")
        for va in func_entries:
            f.write(f"void sub_{va:08x}(CpuCtx *c);\n")
        f.write("#endif\n")

    # 3. Write dispatch.c
    with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "dispatch.c"), "w") as f:
        f.write('#include "cpu.h"\n#include "funcs.h"\n\n')
        f.write("RecompFn g_code_table[GUEST_TEXT_SIZE];\n\n")
        f.write("void init_code_table(void) {\n")
        for va in func_entries:
            f.write(f"  g_code_table[0x{va - pe.text_va:x}u] = sub_{va:08x};\n")
        f.write("}\n")

    # 4. Lift all functions across NUM_CHUNKS C files
    chunk_files = [open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"chunk_{i:02d}.c"), "w") for i in range(NUM_CHUNKS)]
    for cf in chunk_files:
        cf.write('#include "cpu.h"\n#include "funcs.h"\n\n')

    total_lifted_insns = 0
    errors = []
    for idx, va in enumerate(func_entries):
        try:
            code, n_ins = lift_function(pe, md, va, func_entries_set, switch_tables)
            total_lifted_insns += n_ins
            cf = chunk_files[idx % NUM_CHUNKS]
            cf.write(code)
            cf.write("\n\n")
        except Exception as e:
            errors.append((va, str(e)))
            cf = chunk_files[idx % NUM_CHUNKS]
            cf.write(f'void sub_{va:08x}(CpuCtx *c) {{ (void)c; fprintf(stderr, "LIFT ERROR in 0x{va:08x}: {e}\\n"); abort(); }}\n\n')

    for cf in chunk_files:
        cf.close()

    print(f"Lifted {len(func_entries)} functions ({total_lifted_insns} instructions) into {NUM_CHUNKS} C chunks.")
    print(f"Lift errors: {len(errors)}")
    for va, err in errors[:30]:
        print(f"  ERROR 0x{va:08x}: {err}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`runtime/cpu.c`:

```c
#include "runtime.h"
#include "imports.h"
#include <sys/mman.h>
#include <pthread.h>

uint8_t *g_mem = NULL;
char g_game_dir[1024] = "game_files";
volatile int g_quit_requested = 0;
int g_debug_log = 0;

/* ---- Segregated size-class free-list, thread-safe coalescing guest heap allocator ---- */
typedef struct HeapBlock {
    uint32_t size;      /* usable payload size in bytes */
    uint32_t is_free;   /* 1 = free, 0 = allocated */
    uint32_t next;      /* guest addr of next HeapBlock (0 if end) */
    uint32_t prev;      /* guest addr of prev HeapBlock (0 if head) */
} HeapBlock;

/* Stored in the first 8 bytes of a free block's payload (payload >= 16 bytes) */
typedef struct FreeLinks {
    uint32_t next_free;
    uint32_t prev_free;
} FreeLinks;

#define HEAP_HDR_SIZE    16u
#define HEAP_NUM_BINS    24u
#define MAX_THREAD_SLOTS 36u
static pthread_mutex_t g_heap_mtx = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER;
static uint32_t g_heap_head = 0;
static uint32_t g_free_bins[HEAP_NUM_BINS];
static uint32_t g_bin_mask = 0;
static uint32_t g_static_bump = GUEST_COM_VTABLES_BASE;
static uint32_t g_com_vtables[32];
static uint64_t g_thread_slot_mask = 0;

static inline FreeLinks *free_links(uint32_t block_va) {
    return (FreeLinks *)(g_mem + block_va + HEAP_HDR_SIZE);
}

/* Bin k covers [16 << k, 32 << k) bytes; bin 23 covers >= 128 MB */
static inline uint32_t size_to_bin(uint32_t sz) {
    if (sz < 32u) return 0u;
    uint32_t b = 27u - (uint32_t)__builtin_clz(sz); /* clz(32)=26 -> b=1 */
    return (b < HEAP_NUM_BINS) ? b : (HEAP_NUM_BINS - 1u);
}

static inline void free_list_insert(uint32_t block_va, uint32_t sz) {
    uint32_t b = size_to_bin(sz);
    FreeLinks *fl = free_links(block_va);
    fl->prev_free = 0;
    fl->next_free = g_free_bins[b];
    if (g_free_bins[b]) {
        free_links(g_free_bins[b])->prev_free = block_va;
    }
    g_free_bins[b] = block_va;
    g_bin_mask |= (1u << b);
}

static inline void free_list_remove(uint32_t block_va, uint32_t sz) {
    uint32_t b = size_to_bin(sz);
    FreeLinks *fl = free_links(block_va);
    if (fl->prev_free) {
        free_links(fl->prev_free)->next_free = fl->next_free;
    } else {
        g_free_bins[b] = fl->next_free;
        if (!g_free_bins[b]) g_bin_mask &= ~(1u << b);
    }
    if (fl->next_free) {
        free_links(fl->next_free)->prev_free = fl->prev_free;
    }
    fl->prev_free = 0;
    fl->next_free = 0;
}

void runtime_mem_init(void) {
    g_mem = (uint8_t *)mmap(NULL, GUEST_MEM_SIZE, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE,
                            MAP_PRIVATE | MAP_ANONYMOUS, -1, 0);
    if (g_mem == MAP_FAILED) {
        perror("mmap guest memory");
        exit(1);
    }

    /* Initialize single free block covering GUEST_HEAP_BASE..GUEST_HEAP_END */
    g_heap_head = GUEST_HEAP_BASE;
    memset(g_free_bins, 0, sizeof(g_free_bins));
    g_bin_mask = 0;
    HeapBlock *hb = (HeapBlock *)(g_mem + g_heap_head);
    hb->size = (GUEST_HEAP_END - GUEST_HEAP_BASE) - HEAP_HDR_SIZE;
    hb->is_free = 1;
    hb->next = 0;
    hb->prev = 0;
    free_list_insert(g_heap_head, hb->size);
}

uint32_t guest_alloc(uint32_t size) {
    if (size > 0x10000000u) {
        fprintf(stderr, "FATAL: guest_alloc(%u) request too large!\n", size);
        return 0;
    }
    if (size == 0) size = 16;
    size = (size + 15u) & ~15u;

    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_heap_mtx);
    uint32_t start_bin = size_to_bin(size);
    uint32_t cur = 0;

    /* 1. Best-fit scan (bounded to 64 steps, falling back to first-fit in the same bin) */
    uint32_t scan = g_free_bins[start_bin];
    uint32_t best = 0;
    uint32_t best_sz = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
    int steps = 0;
    while (scan && (steps < 64 || !best)) {
        HeapBlock *hb = (HeapBlock *)(g_mem + scan);
        if (hb->size >= size && hb->size < best_sz) {
            best = scan;
            best_sz = hb->size;
            if (best_sz == size || steps >= 64) break;
        }
        scan = free_links(scan)->next_free;
        steps++;
    }
    if (best) {
        cur = best;
    } else if (start_bin + 1u < HEAP_NUM_BINS) {
        /* 2. O(1) jump to the smallest non-empty larger bin (every block is >= size) */
        uint32_t hi_mask = g_bin_mask & (~0u << (start_bin + 1u));
        if (hi_mask) {
            uint32_t b = (uint32_t)__builtin_ctz(hi_mask);
            cur = g_free_bins[b];
        }
    }

    if (cur) {
        HeapBlock *hb = (HeapBlock *)(g_mem + cur);
        free_list_remove(cur, hb->size);
        if (hb->size >= size + HEAP_HDR_SIZE + 32u) {
            /* Split block */
            uint32_t next_va = cur + HEAP_HDR_SIZE + size;
            HeapBlock *nhb = (HeapBlock *)(g_mem + next_va);
            nhb->size = hb->size - size - HEAP_HDR_SIZE;
            nhb->is_free = 1;
            nhb->next = hb->next;
            nhb->prev = cur;
            if (hb->next) {
                ((HeapBlock *)(g_mem + hb->next))->prev = next_va;
            }
            hb->size = size;
            hb->next = next_va;
            free_list_insert(next_va, nhb->size);
        }
        hb->is_free = 0;
        uint32_t payload = cur + HEAP_HDR_SIZE;
        pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_heap_mtx);
        return payload;
    }
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_heap_mtx);
    fprintf(stderr, "FATAL: guest_alloc(%u) out of guest heap memory!\n", size);
    return 0;
}

void guest_free(uint32_t addr) {
    if (addr < GUEST_HEAP_BASE + HEAP_HDR_SIZE || addr >= GUEST_HEAP_END) return;
    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_heap_mtx);
    uint32_t cur = addr - HEAP_HDR_SIZE;
    HeapBlock *hb = (HeapBlock *)(g_mem + cur);
    if (hb->is_free) {
        pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_heap_mtx);
        return;
    }
    hb->is_free = 1;

    /* Coalesce with next */
    if (hb->next) {
        uint32_t next_va = hb->next;
        HeapBlock *nhb = (HeapBlock *)(g_mem + next_va);
        if (nhb->is_free) {
            free_list_remove(next_va, nhb->size);
            hb->size += HEAP_HDR_SIZE + nhb->size;
            hb->next = nhb->next;
            if (hb->next) {
                ((HeapBlock *)(g_mem + hb->next))->prev = cur;
            }
        }
    }
    /* Coalesce with prev */
    if (hb->prev) {
        uint32_t prev_va = hb->prev;
        HeapBlock *phb = (HeapBlock *)(g_mem + prev_va);
        if (phb->is_free) {
            free_list_remove(prev_va, phb->size);
            phb->size += HEAP_HDR_SIZE + hb->size;
            phb->next = hb->next;
            if (hb->next) {
                ((HeapBlock *)(g_mem + hb->next))->prev = prev_va;
            }
            free_list_insert(prev_va, phb->size);
            pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_heap_mtx);
            return;
        }
    }
    free_list_insert(cur, hb->size);
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_heap_mtx);
}

uint32_t guest_alloc_size(uint32_t addr) {
    if (addr < GUEST_HEAP_BASE + HEAP_HDR_SIZE || addr >= GUEST_HEAP_END) return 0;
    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_heap_mtx);
    HeapBlock *hb = (HeapBlock *)(g_mem + addr - HEAP_HDR_SIZE);
    uint32_t sz = hb->is_free ? 0u : hb->size;
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_heap_mtx);
    return sz;
}

uint32_t guest_realloc(uint32_t addr, uint32_t new_size) {
    if (addr == 0) return guest_alloc(new_size);
    if (new_size == 0) { guest_free(addr); return 0; }
    uint32_t old_sz = guest_alloc_size(addr);
    if (old_sz >= new_size) return addr;
    uint32_t naddr = guest_alloc(new_size);
    if (!naddr) return 0;
    memcpy(g_mem + naddr, g_mem + addr, old_sz);
    guest_free(addr);
    return naddr;
}

uint32_t guest_alloc_static(const void *data, uint32_t size) {
    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_heap_mtx);
    uint32_t va = (g_static_bump + 15u) & ~15u;
    if (size > GUEST_TEB_BASE || va > GUEST_TEB_BASE - size) {
        pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_heap_mtx);
        fprintf(stderr, "FATAL: guest_alloc_static(%u) out of static region!\n", size);
        return 0;
    }
    g_static_bump = va + size;
    if (data && size) memcpy(g_mem + va, data, size);
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_heap_mtx);
    return va;
}

uint32_t guest_alloc_cstr(const char *s) {
    uint32_t len = (uint32_t)strlen(s) + 1u;
    return guest_alloc_static(s, len);
}

uint32_t com_get_vtable(uint32_t iface_id, uint32_t num_slots) {
    if (iface_id < 32 && g_com_vtables[iface_id]) {
        return g_com_vtables[iface_id];
    }
    uint32_t va = guest_alloc_static(NULL, num_slots * 4u);
    if (!va) return 0;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < num_slots; i++) {
        mem_w32(va + i * 4u, HLE_COM_BASE | (iface_id << 12) | i);
    }
    if (iface_id < 32) g_com_vtables[iface_id] = va;
    return va;
}

uint32_t com_create_obj(uint32_t iface_id, uint32_t num_slots, uint32_t handle_val) {
    uint32_t vtbl = com_get_vtable(iface_id, num_slots);
    uint32_t obj = guest_alloc(16);
    if (!obj) return 0;
    mem_w32(obj + 0, vtbl);
    mem_w32(obj + 4, handle_val);
    mem_w32(obj + 8, iface_id);
    return obj;
}

uint32_t com_obj_handle(uint32_t obj_va) {
    if (!obj_va) return 0;
    return mem_r32(obj_va + 4);
}

uint32_t guest_alloc_thread_slot(uint32_t *out_teb, uint32_t *out_stack_top) {
    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_heap_mtx);
    uint32_t slot = MAX_THREAD_SLOTS;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < MAX_THREAD_SLOTS; i++) {
        if ((g_thread_slot_mask & (1ULL << i)) == 0) {
            g_thread_slot_mask |= (1ULL << i);
            slot = i;
            break;
        }
    }
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_heap_mtx);
    if (slot >= MAX_THREAD_SLOTS) {
        fprintf(stderr, "FATAL: guest_alloc_thread_slot exhausted (%u slots)!\n", MAX_THREAD_SLOTS);
        return 0;
    }
    uint32_t teb = GUEST_TEB_BASE + slot * 4096u;
    uint32_t stack_top = GUEST_STACK_BASE + (slot + 1u) * GUEST_STACK_SIZE - 256u;
    memset(g_mem + teb, 0, 4096);
    mem_w32(teb + 0x00, 0xFFFFFFFFu); /* SEH chain end */
    mem_w32(teb + 0x04, stack_top);
    mem_w32(teb + 0x08, stack_top - GUEST_STACK_SIZE + 256u);
    mem_w32(teb + 0x18, teb);         /* Self pointer */
    mem_w32(teb + 0x20, 1000u);       /* PID */
    mem_w32(teb + 0x24, 1000u + slot);/* TID */
    if (out_teb) *out_teb = teb;
    if (out_stack_top) *out_stack_top = stack_top;
    return 1000u + slot;
}

void guest_free_thread_slot(uint32_t tid) {
    if (tid < 1000u || tid >= 1000u + MAX_THREAD_SLOTS) return;
    uint32_t slot = tid - 1000u;
    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_heap_mtx);
    g_thread_slot_mask &= ~(1ULL << slot);
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_heap_mtx);
}

/* ---- PE Loader ---- */
int runtime_load_pe(const char *exe_path) {
    FILE *f = fopen(exe_path, "rb");
    if (!f) {
        fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: cannot open PE image '%s'\n", exe_path);
        return -1;
    }
    fseek(f, 0, SEEK_END);
    long sz = ftell(f);
    fseek(f, 0, SEEK_SET);
    uint8_t *buf = (uint8_t *)malloc((size_t)sz);
    if (!buf || fread(buf, 1, (size_t)sz, f) != (size_t)sz) {
        fclose(f);
        free(buf);
        return -1;
    }
    fclose(f);

    uint32_t e_lfanew;
    memcpy(&e_lfanew, buf + 0x3c, 4);
    uint16_t n_sec, opt_size;
    memcpy(&n_sec, buf + e_lfanew + 6, 2);
    memcpy(&opt_size, buf + e_lfanew + 20, 2);
    uint32_t image_base, hdr_size;
    memcpy(&image_base, buf + e_lfanew + 24 + 28, 4);
    memcpy(&hdr_size, buf + e_lfanew + 24 + 60, 4);

    /* Copy PE headers */
    memcpy(g_mem + image_base, buf, hdr_size);

    /* Copy sections */
    uint32_t sec_off = e_lfanew + 24 + opt_size;
    for (int i = 0; i < n_sec; i++) {
        uint8_t *s = buf + sec_off + i * 40;
        uint32_t vsz, vaddr, rsz, roff;
        memcpy(&vsz, s + 8, 4);
        memcpy(&vaddr, s + 12, 4);
        memcpy(&rsz, s + 16, 4);
        memcpy(&roff, s + 20, 4);
        uint32_t copy_len = rsz < vsz ? rsz : vsz;
        if (roff + copy_len <= (uint32_t)sz) {
            memcpy(g_mem + image_base + vaddr, buf + roff, copy_len);
        }
    }
    free(buf);

    /* Populate IAT slots with synthetic Win32 HLE addresses */
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < MHA2_IMPORT_COUNT; i++) {
        mem_w32(g_imports[i].iat_va, HLE_WIN32_BASE + g_imports[i].id);
    }
    return 0;
}

/* ---- Call Dispatchers ---- */
void win32_dispatch(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t import_id) {
    if (win32_kernel_dispatch(c, import_id)) return;
    if (win32_user_gdi_dispatch(c, import_id)) return;
    if (win32_ddraw_dispatch(c, import_id)) return;
    if (win32_dinput_dispatch(c, import_id)) return;
    if (win32_dsound_dispatch(c, import_id)) return;
    if (win32_avi_dispatch(c, import_id)) return;

    const char *dll = (import_id < MHA2_IMPORT_COUNT) ? g_imports[import_id].dll : "?";
    const char *name = (import_id < MHA2_IMPORT_COUNT) ? g_imports[import_id].name : "?";
    fprintf(stderr, "UNIMPLEMENTED Win32 import #%u: %s!%s (ret=0x%08x)\n",
            import_id, dll, name, mem_r32(c->esp));
    stdcall_ret(c, 0, 0);
}

void com_dispatch(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t iface_id, uint32_t slot) {
    switch (iface_id) {
        case COM_IFACE_DDRAW:
        case COM_IFACE_DDRAW_SURFACE:
        case COM_IFACE_DDRAW_CLIPPER:
            com_ddraw_dispatch(c, iface_id, slot);
            return;
        case COM_IFACE_DINPUT:
        case COM_IFACE_DINPUT_DEV:
            com_dinput_dispatch(c, iface_id, slot);
            return;
        case COM_IFACE_DSOUND:
        case COM_IFACE_DSOUND_BUF:
            com_dsound_dispatch(c, iface_id, slot);
            return;
        case COM_IFACE_DSHOW_GRAPH:
        case COM_IFACE_DSHOW_CTRL:
        case COM_IFACE_DSHOW_EVENT:
        case COM_IFACE_DSHOW_VIDWIN:
        case COM_IFACE_DSHOW_BASVID:
            com_dshow_dispatch(c, iface_id, slot);
            return;
        default:
            fprintf(stderr, "FATAL: unknown COM iface %u slot %u\n", iface_id, slot);
            abort();
    }
}

void dispatch_call(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t addr) {
    if (addr >= HLE_WIN32_BASE) {
        win32_dispatch(c, addr - HLE_WIN32_BASE);
        return;
    }
    if (addr >= HLE_COM_BASE && addr < HLE_WIN32_BASE) {
        uint32_t rel = addr - HLE_COM_BASE;
        com_dispatch(c, rel >> 12, rel & 0xFFFu);
        return;
    }
    if (addr >= GUEST_TEXT_START && addr < GUEST_TEXT_END) {
        RecompFn fn = g_code_table[addr - GUEST_TEXT_START];
        if (fn) {
            fn(c);
            return;
        }
    }
    fprintf(stderr, "FATAL: indirect call to unlifted address 0x%08x (ret=0x%08x, esp=0x%08x)\n",
            addr, mem_r32(c->esp), c->esp);
    abort();
}

uint32_t guest_call_stdcall(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t fn_addr, int nargs, const uint32_t *args) {
    /* Save registers that caller expects preserved across Win32 API calls */
    uint32_t s_ebx = c->ebx, s_esi = c->esi, s_edi = c->edi, s_ebp = c->ebp;
    for (int i = nargs - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        push32(c, args[i]);
    }
    push32(c, HLE_RET_SENTINEL);
    dispatch_call(c, fn_addr);
    uint32_t ret = c->eax;
    c->ebx = s_ebx; c->esi = s_esi; c->edi = s_edi; c->ebp = s_ebp;
    return ret;
}

uint32_t guest_call_cdecl(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t fn_addr, int nargs, const uint32_t *args) {
    uint32_t s_ebx = c->ebx, s_esi = c->esi, s_edi = c->edi, s_ebp = c->ebp;
    for (int i = nargs - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        push32(c, args[i]);
    }
    push32(c, HLE_RET_SENTINEL);
    dispatch_call(c, fn_addr);
    c->esp += (uint32_t)nargs * 4u;
    uint32_t ret = c->eax;
    c->ebx = s_ebx; c->esi = s_esi; c->edi = s_edi; c->ebp = s_ebp;
    return ret;
}

```

`runtime/cpu.h`:

```h
#ifndef MHA2_CPU_H
#define MHA2_CPU_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <math.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

/* 1.5 GiB flat 32-bit guest address space (demand-paged via mmap; all VAs < 0x7FFFFFFF) */
#define GUEST_MEM_SIZE   (1536u * 1024u * 1024u)
#define GUEST_TEXT_START 0x00401000u
#define GUEST_TEXT_END   0x004fd000u
#define GUEST_TEXT_SIZE  (GUEST_TEXT_END - GUEST_TEXT_START)

/* Synthetic address spaces for HLE dispatch */
#define HLE_COM_BASE     0xFE000000u
#define HLE_WIN32_BASE   0xFF000000u
#define HLE_RET_SENTINEL 0xFD000000u

/* Reserved guest memory layout */
#define GUEST_COM_VTABLES_BASE 0x00800000u  /* 64 KB for COM vtables & static strings */
#define GUEST_TEB_BASE         0x00900000u  /* 64 KB for thread TEBs (4 KB per thread) */
#define GUEST_HEAP_BASE        0x01000000u  /* ~1.42 GB virtual guest heap & surface/audio buffers */
#define GUEST_HEAP_END         0x5A000000u
#define GUEST_STACK_BASE       0x5B000000u  /* 2 MB stacks per thread up to 0x5F800000 */
#define GUEST_STACK_SIZE       (2u * 1024u * 1024u)

extern uint8_t *g_mem;

typedef struct CpuCtx {
    uint32_t eax, ecx, edx, ebx, esp, ebp, esi, edi;
    uint32_t cf, zf, sf, of, pf, df;
    uint32_t teb_addr;
    uint32_t thread_id;

    /* x87 FPU */
    long double st[8];
    int fpu_top;
    uint16_t fpu_sw;
    uint16_t fpu_cw;
} CpuCtx;

typedef void (*RecompFn)(CpuCtx *ctx);

extern RecompFn g_code_table[GUEST_TEXT_SIZE];

void dispatch_call(CpuCtx *ctx, uint32_t addr);
void win32_dispatch(CpuCtx *ctx, uint32_t import_id);
void com_dispatch(CpuCtx *ctx, uint32_t iface_id, uint32_t slot);
uint32_t guest_call_stdcall(CpuCtx *ctx, uint32_t fn_addr, int nargs, const uint32_t *args);
uint32_t guest_call_cdecl(CpuCtx *ctx, uint32_t fn_addr, int nargs, const uint32_t *args);

/* Memory accessors */
static inline void *mem_ptr(uint32_t addr) {
    return (void *)(g_mem + addr);
}

static inline uint8_t mem_r8(uint32_t addr) {
    return g_mem[addr];
}
static inline uint16_t mem_r16(uint32_t addr) {
    uint16_t v;
    memcpy(&v, g_mem + addr, 2);
    return v;
}
static inline uint32_t mem_r32(uint32_t addr) {
    uint32_t v;
    memcpy(&v, g_mem + addr, 4);
    return v;
}
static inline uint64_t mem_r64(uint32_t addr) {
    uint64_t v;
    memcpy(&v, g_mem + addr, 8);
    return v;
}
static inline float mem_rf32(uint32_t addr) {
    float v;
    memcpy(&v, g_mem + addr, 4);
    return v;
}
static inline double mem_rf64(uint32_t addr) {
    double v;
    memcpy(&v, g_mem + addr, 8);
    return v;
}
static inline long double mem_rf80(uint32_t addr) {
    long double v = 0.0L;
    memcpy(&v, g_mem + addr, 10);
    return v;
}

static inline void mem_w8(uint32_t addr, uint8_t v) {
    g_mem[addr] = v;
}
static inline void mem_w16(uint32_t addr, uint16_t v) {
    memcpy(g_mem + addr, &v, 2);
}
static inline void mem_w32(uint32_t addr, uint32_t v) {
    memcpy(g_mem + addr, &v, 4);
}
static inline void mem_w64(uint32_t addr, uint64_t v) {
    memcpy(g_mem + addr, &v, 8);
}
static inline void mem_wf32(uint32_t addr, float v) {
    memcpy(g_mem + addr, &v, 4);
}
static inline void mem_wf64(uint32_t addr, double v) {
    memcpy(g_mem + addr, &v, 8);
}
static inline void mem_wf80(uint32_t addr, long double v) {
    memcpy(g_mem + addr, &v, 10);
}

/* Push / Pop */
static inline void push32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t v) {
    c->esp -= 4;
    mem_w32(c->esp, v);
}
static inline uint32_t pop32(CpuCtx *c) {
    uint32_t v = mem_r32(c->esp);
    c->esp += 4;
    return v;
}
static inline void push16(CpuCtx *c, uint16_t v) {
    c->esp -= 2;
    mem_w16(c->esp, v);
}
static inline uint16_t pop16(CpuCtx *c) {
    uint16_t v = mem_r16(c->esp);
    c->esp += 2;
    return v;
}

/* Sub-register accessors */
static inline uint8_t r8_lo(uint32_t r) { return (uint8_t)(r & 0xFFu); }
static inline uint8_t r8_hi(uint32_t r) { return (uint8_t)((r >> 8) & 0xFFu); }
static inline uint16_t r16(uint32_t r)  { return (uint16_t)(r & 0xFFFFu); }
static inline void w8_lo(uint32_t *r, uint8_t v) { *r = (*r & ~0xFFu) | (uint32_t)v; }
static inline void w8_hi(uint32_t *r, uint8_t v) { *r = (*r & ~0xFF00u) | ((uint32_t)v << 8); }
static inline void w16(uint32_t *r, uint16_t v)  { *r = (*r & ~0xFFFFu) | (uint32_t)v; }

/* Parity helper */
static inline uint32_t parity8(uint32_t v) {
    return (uint32_t)(!__builtin_parity(v & 0xFFu));
}

/* ALU helpers */
static inline uint8_t alu_add8(CpuCtx *c, uint8_t a, uint8_t b) {
    uint32_t r = (uint32_t)a + (uint32_t)b;
    uint8_t res = (uint8_t)r;
    c->cf = (r >> 8) & 1u;
    c->of = (((a ^ res) & (b ^ res)) >> 7) & 1u;
    c->sf = (res >> 7) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint16_t alu_add16(CpuCtx *c, uint16_t a, uint16_t b) {
    uint32_t r = (uint32_t)a + (uint32_t)b;
    uint16_t res = (uint16_t)r;
    c->cf = (r >> 16) & 1u;
    c->of = (((a ^ res) & (b ^ res)) >> 15) & 1u;
    c->sf = (res >> 15) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint32_t alu_add32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t a, uint32_t b) {
    uint64_t r = (uint64_t)a + (uint64_t)b;
    uint32_t res = (uint32_t)r;
    c->cf = (uint32_t)((r >> 32) & 1u);
    c->of = (((a ^ res) & (b ^ res)) >> 31) & 1u;
    c->sf = (res >> 31) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}

static inline uint8_t alu_adc8(CpuCtx *c, uint8_t a, uint8_t b) {
    uint32_t r = (uint32_t)a + (uint32_t)b + (c->cf & 1u);
    uint8_t res = (uint8_t)r;
    c->cf = (r >> 8) & 1u;
    c->of = (((a ^ res) & (b ^ res)) >> 7) & 1u;
    c->sf = (res >> 7) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint16_t alu_adc16(CpuCtx *c, uint16_t a, uint16_t b) {
    uint32_t r = (uint32_t)a + (uint32_t)b + (c->cf & 1u);
    uint16_t res = (uint16_t)r;
    c->cf = (r >> 16) & 1u;
    c->of = (((a ^ res) & (b ^ res)) >> 15) & 1u;
    c->sf = (res >> 15) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint32_t alu_adc32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t a, uint32_t b) {
    uint64_t r = (uint64_t)a + (uint64_t)b + (uint64_t)(c->cf & 1u);
    uint32_t res = (uint32_t)r;
    c->cf = (uint32_t)((r >> 32) & 1u);
    c->of = (((a ^ res) & (b ^ res)) >> 31) & 1u;
    c->sf = (res >> 31) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}

static inline uint8_t alu_sub8(CpuCtx *c, uint8_t a, uint8_t b) {
    uint8_t res = (uint8_t)(a - b);
    c->cf = (a < b);
    c->of = (((a ^ b) & (a ^ res)) >> 7) & 1u;
    c->sf = (res >> 7) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint16_t alu_sub16(CpuCtx *c, uint16_t a, uint16_t b) {
    uint16_t res = (uint16_t)(a - b);
    c->cf = (a < b);
    c->of = (((a ^ b) & (a ^ res)) >> 15) & 1u;
    c->sf = (res >> 15) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint32_t alu_sub32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t a, uint32_t b) {
    uint32_t res = a - b;
    c->cf = (a < b);
    c->of = (((a ^ b) & (a ^ res)) >> 31) & 1u;
    c->sf = (res >> 31) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}

static inline uint8_t alu_sbb8(CpuCtx *c, uint8_t a, uint8_t b) {
    uint32_t cin = c->cf & 1u;
    uint32_t r = (uint32_t)a - (uint32_t)b - cin;
    uint8_t res = (uint8_t)r;
    c->cf = (r >> 31) & 1u;
    c->of = (((a ^ b) & (a ^ res)) >> 7) & 1u;
    c->sf = (res >> 7) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint16_t alu_sbb16(CpuCtx *c, uint16_t a, uint16_t b) {
    uint32_t cin = c->cf & 1u;
    uint32_t r = (uint32_t)a - (uint32_t)b - cin;
    uint16_t res = (uint16_t)r;
    c->cf = (r >> 31) & 1u;
    c->of = (((a ^ b) & (a ^ res)) >> 15) & 1u;
    c->sf = (res >> 15) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint32_t alu_sbb32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t a, uint32_t b) {
    uint64_t cin = c->cf & 1u;
    uint64_t r = (uint64_t)a - (uint64_t)b - cin;
    uint32_t res = (uint32_t)r;
    c->cf = (uint32_t)((r >> 63) & 1u);
    c->of = (((a ^ b) & (a ^ res)) >> 31) & 1u;
    c->sf = (res >> 31) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}

static inline uint8_t alu_inc8(CpuCtx *c, uint8_t a) {
    uint8_t res = (uint8_t)(a + 1u);
    c->of = (a == 0x7Fu);
    c->sf = (res >> 7) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint16_t alu_inc16(CpuCtx *c, uint16_t a) {
    uint16_t res = (uint16_t)(a + 1u);
    c->of = (a == 0x7FFFu);
    c->sf = (res >> 15) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint32_t alu_inc32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t a) {
    uint32_t res = a + 1u;
    c->of = (a == 0x7FFFFFFFu);
    c->sf = (res >> 31) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}

static inline uint8_t alu_dec8(CpuCtx *c, uint8_t a) {
    uint8_t res = (uint8_t)(a - 1u);
    c->of = (a == 0x80u);
    c->sf = (res >> 7) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint16_t alu_dec16(CpuCtx *c, uint16_t a) {
    uint16_t res = (uint16_t)(a - 1u);
    c->of = (a == 0x8000u);
    c->sf = (res >> 15) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint32_t alu_dec32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t a) {
    uint32_t res = a - 1u;
    c->of = (a == 0x80000000u);
    c->sf = (res >> 31) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}

static inline uint8_t alu_neg8(CpuCtx *c, uint8_t a) {
    uint8_t res = (uint8_t)(0u - a);
    c->cf = (a != 0);
    c->of = (a == 0x80u);
    c->sf = (res >> 7) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint16_t alu_neg16(CpuCtx *c, uint16_t a) {
    uint16_t res = (uint16_t)(0u - a);
    c->cf = (a != 0);
    c->of = (a == 0x8000u);
    c->sf = (res >> 15) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint32_t alu_neg32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t a) {
    uint32_t res = 0u - a;
    c->cf = (a != 0);
    c->of = (a == 0x80000000u);
    c->sf = (res >> 31) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}

static inline uint8_t alu_logic8(CpuCtx *c, uint8_t res) {
    c->cf = 0; c->of = 0;
    c->sf = (res >> 7) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint16_t alu_logic16(CpuCtx *c, uint16_t res) {
    c->cf = 0; c->of = 0;
    c->sf = (res >> 15) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint32_t alu_logic32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t res) {
    c->cf = 0; c->of = 0;
    c->sf = (res >> 31) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}

/* Shifts & Rotates */
static inline uint8_t alu_shl8(CpuCtx *c, uint8_t a, uint32_t cnt) {
    cnt &= 31u;
    if (cnt == 0) return a;
    uint8_t res = (cnt < 8) ? (uint8_t)(a << cnt) : 0;
    c->cf = (cnt <= 8) ? ((a >> (8 - cnt)) & 1u) : 0;
    if (cnt == 1) c->of = ((res >> 7) & 1u) ^ c->cf;
    c->sf = (res >> 7) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint16_t alu_shl16(CpuCtx *c, uint16_t a, uint32_t cnt) {
    cnt &= 31u;
    if (cnt == 0) return a;
    uint16_t res = (cnt < 16) ? (uint16_t)(a << cnt) : 0;
    c->cf = (cnt <= 16) ? ((a >> (16 - cnt)) & 1u) : 0;
    if (cnt == 1) c->of = ((res >> 15) & 1u) ^ c->cf;
    c->sf = (res >> 15) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint32_t alu_shl32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t a, uint32_t cnt) {
    cnt &= 31u;
    if (cnt == 0) return a;
    uint32_t res = a << cnt;
    c->cf = (a >> (32 - cnt)) & 1u;
    if (cnt == 1) c->of = ((res >> 31) & 1u) ^ c->cf;
    c->sf = (res >> 31) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}

static inline uint8_t alu_shr8(CpuCtx *c, uint8_t a, uint32_t cnt) {
    cnt &= 31u;
    if (cnt == 0) return a;
    uint8_t res = (cnt < 8) ? (uint8_t)(a >> cnt) : 0;
    c->cf = (cnt <= 8) ? ((a >> (cnt - 1)) & 1u) : 0;
    if (cnt == 1) c->of = (a >> 7) & 1u;
    c->sf = (res >> 7) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint16_t alu_shr16(CpuCtx *c, uint16_t a, uint32_t cnt) {
    cnt &= 31u;
    if (cnt == 0) return a;
    uint16_t res = (cnt < 16) ? (uint16_t)(a >> cnt) : 0;
    c->cf = (cnt <= 16) ? ((a >> (cnt - 1)) & 1u) : 0;
    if (cnt == 1) c->of = (a >> 15) & 1u;
    c->sf = (res >> 15) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint32_t alu_shr32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t a, uint32_t cnt) {
    cnt &= 31u;
    if (cnt == 0) return a;
    uint32_t res = a >> cnt;
    c->cf = (a >> (cnt - 1)) & 1u;
    if (cnt == 1) c->of = (a >> 31) & 1u;
    c->sf = (res >> 31) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}

static inline uint8_t alu_sar8(CpuCtx *c, uint8_t a, uint32_t cnt) {
    cnt &= 31u;
    if (cnt == 0) return a;
    int8_t sa = (int8_t)a;
    uint8_t res = (cnt < 8) ? (uint8_t)(sa >> cnt) : (uint8_t)(sa >> 7);
    c->cf = (cnt < 8) ? (((uint32_t)sa >> (cnt - 1)) & 1u) : ((a >> 7) & 1u);
    if (cnt == 1) c->of = 0;
    c->sf = (res >> 7) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint16_t alu_sar16(CpuCtx *c, uint16_t a, uint32_t cnt) {
    cnt &= 31u;
    if (cnt == 0) return a;
    int16_t sa = (int16_t)a;
    uint16_t res = (cnt < 16) ? (uint16_t)(sa >> cnt) : (uint16_t)(sa >> 15);
    c->cf = (cnt < 16) ? (((uint32_t)sa >> (cnt - 1)) & 1u) : ((a >> 15) & 1u);
    if (cnt == 1) c->of = 0;
    c->sf = (res >> 15) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint32_t alu_sar32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t a, uint32_t cnt) {
    cnt &= 31u;
    if (cnt == 0) return a;
    int32_t sa = (int32_t)a;
    uint32_t res = (uint32_t)(sa >> cnt);
    c->cf = (a >> (cnt - 1)) & 1u;
    if (cnt == 1) c->of = 0;
    c->sf = (res >> 31) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}

static inline uint32_t alu_rol32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t a, uint32_t cnt) {
    cnt &= 31u;
    if (cnt == 0) return a;
    uint32_t res = (a << cnt) | (a >> (32 - cnt));
    c->cf = res & 1u;
    if (cnt == 1) c->of = ((res >> 31) & 1u) ^ c->cf;
    return res;
}
static inline uint32_t alu_ror32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t a, uint32_t cnt) {
    cnt &= 31u;
    if (cnt == 0) return a;
    uint32_t res = (a >> cnt) | (a << (32 - cnt));
    c->cf = (res >> 31) & 1u;
    if (cnt == 1) c->of = ((res >> 31) ^ (res >> 30)) & 1u;
    return res;
}
static inline uint8_t alu_rol8(CpuCtx *c, uint8_t a, uint32_t cnt) {
    cnt &= 31u;
    if (cnt == 0) return a;
    uint32_t r = cnt & 7u;
    uint8_t res = r ? (uint8_t)((a << r) | (a >> (8u - r))) : a;
    c->cf = res & 1u;
    if (cnt == 1) c->of = ((res >> 7) & 1u) ^ c->cf;
    return res;
}
static inline uint8_t alu_ror8(CpuCtx *c, uint8_t a, uint32_t cnt) {
    cnt &= 31u;
    if (cnt == 0) return a;
    uint32_t r = cnt & 7u;
    uint8_t res = r ? (uint8_t)((a >> r) | (a << (8u - r))) : a;
    c->cf = (res >> 7) & 1u;
    if (cnt == 1) c->of = ((res >> 7) ^ (res >> 6)) & 1u;
    return res;
}
static inline uint16_t alu_rol16(CpuCtx *c, uint16_t a, uint32_t cnt) {
    cnt &= 31u;
    if (cnt == 0) return a;
    uint32_t r = cnt & 15u;
    uint16_t res = r ? (uint16_t)((a << r) | (a >> (16u - r))) : a;
    c->cf = res & 1u;
    if (cnt == 1) c->of = ((res >> 15) & 1u) ^ c->cf;
    return res;
}
static inline uint16_t alu_ror16(CpuCtx *c, uint16_t a, uint32_t cnt) {
    cnt &= 31u;
    if (cnt == 0) return a;
    uint32_t r = cnt & 15u;
    uint16_t res = r ? (uint16_t)((a >> r) | (a << (16u - r))) : a;
    c->cf = (res >> 15) & 1u;
    if (cnt == 1) c->of = ((res >> 15) ^ (res >> 14)) & 1u;
    return res;
}
static inline uint32_t alu_rcr32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t a, uint32_t cnt) {
    cnt &= 31u;
    if (cnt == 0) return a;
    uint32_t cf = c->cf & 1u;
    uint32_t res = a;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < cnt; i++) {
        uint32_t new_cf = res & 1u;
        res = (res >> 1) | (cf << 31);
        cf = new_cf;
    }
    c->cf = cf;
    if (cnt == 1) c->of = ((res >> 31) ^ (res >> 30)) & 1u;
    return res;
}
static inline uint32_t alu_rcl32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t a, uint32_t cnt) {
    cnt &= 31u;
    if (cnt == 0) return a;
    uint32_t cf = c->cf & 1u;
    uint32_t res = a;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < cnt; i++) {
        uint32_t new_cf = (res >> 31) & 1u;
        res = (res << 1) | cf;
        cf = new_cf;
    }
    c->cf = cf;
    if (cnt == 1) c->of = ((res >> 31) & 1u) ^ cf;
    return res;
}
static inline uint32_t alu_shld32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t dst, uint32_t src, uint32_t cnt) {
    cnt &= 31u;
    if (cnt == 0) return dst;
    uint32_t res = (dst << cnt) | (src >> (32 - cnt));
    c->cf = (dst >> (32 - cnt)) & 1u;
    if (cnt == 1) c->of = ((res >> 31) & 1u) ^ ((dst >> 31) & 1u);
    c->sf = (res >> 31) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}
static inline uint32_t alu_shrd32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t dst, uint32_t src, uint32_t cnt) {
    cnt &= 31u;
    if (cnt == 0) return dst;
    uint32_t res = (dst >> cnt) | (src << (32 - cnt));
    c->cf = (dst >> (cnt - 1)) & 1u;
    if (cnt == 1) c->of = ((res >> 31) & 1u) ^ ((dst >> 31) & 1u);
    c->sf = (res >> 31) & 1u;
    c->zf = (res == 0);
    c->pf = parity8(res);
    return res;
}

/* Mul / Div */
static inline void alu_mul8(CpuCtx *c, uint8_t b) {
    uint16_t r = (uint16_t)((uint8_t)c->eax) * (uint16_t)b;
    w16(&c->eax, r);
    c->cf = c->of = ((r >> 8) != 0);
}
static inline void alu_mul16(CpuCtx *c, uint16_t b) {
    uint32_t r = (uint32_t)((uint16_t)c->eax) * (uint32_t)b;
    w16(&c->eax, (uint16_t)r);
    w16(&c->edx, (uint16_t)(r >> 16));
    c->cf = c->of = ((r >> 16) != 0);
}
static inline void alu_mul32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t b) {
    uint64_t r = (uint64_t)c->eax * (uint64_t)b;
    c->eax = (uint32_t)r;
    c->edx = (uint32_t)(r >> 32);
    c->cf = c->of = (c->edx != 0);
}
static inline void alu_imul1_8(CpuCtx *c, uint8_t b) {
    int16_t r = (int16_t)(int8_t)c->eax * (int16_t)(int8_t)b;
    w16(&c->eax, (uint16_t)r);
    c->cf = c->of = (r != (int16_t)(int8_t)r);
}
static inline void alu_imul1_16(CpuCtx *c, uint16_t b) {
    int32_t r = (int32_t)(int16_t)c->eax * (int32_t)(int16_t)b;
    w16(&c->eax, (uint16_t)r);
    w16(&c->edx, (uint16_t)((uint32_t)r >> 16));
    c->cf = c->of = (r != (int32_t)(int16_t)r);
}
static inline void alu_imul1_32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t b) {
    int64_t r = (int64_t)(int32_t)c->eax * (int64_t)(int32_t)b;
    c->eax = (uint32_t)r;
    c->edx = (uint32_t)((uint64_t)r >> 32);
    c->cf = c->of = (r != (int64_t)(int32_t)r);
}
static inline uint16_t alu_imul2_16(CpuCtx *c, uint16_t a, uint16_t b) {
    int32_t r = (int32_t)(int16_t)a * (int32_t)(int16_t)b;
    c->cf = c->of = (r != (int32_t)(int16_t)r);
    return (uint16_t)r;
}
static inline uint32_t alu_imul2_32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t a, uint32_t b) {
    int64_t r = (int64_t)(int32_t)a * (int64_t)(int32_t)b;
    c->cf = c->of = (r != (int64_t)(int32_t)r);
    return (uint32_t)r;
}
static inline void alu_div8(CpuCtx *c, uint8_t b) {
    uint16_t num = (uint16_t)c->eax;
    uint8_t q = (uint8_t)(num / b);
    uint8_t r = (uint8_t)(num % b);
    w8_lo(&c->eax, q);
    w8_hi(&c->eax, r);
}
static inline void alu_div16(CpuCtx *c, uint16_t b) {
    uint32_t num = ((uint32_t)(uint16_t)c->edx << 16) | (uint16_t)c->eax;
    w16(&c->eax, (uint16_t)(num / b));
    w16(&c->edx, (uint16_t)(num % b));
}
static inline void alu_div32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t b) {
    uint64_t num = ((uint64_t)c->edx << 32) | c->eax;
    c->eax = (uint32_t)(num / b);
    c->edx = (uint32_t)(num % b);
}
static inline void alu_idiv8(CpuCtx *c, uint8_t b) {
    int16_t num = (int16_t)(uint16_t)c->eax;
    int8_t d = (int8_t)b;
    w8_lo(&c->eax, (uint8_t)(num / d));
    w8_hi(&c->eax, (uint8_t)(num % d));
}
static inline void alu_idiv16(CpuCtx *c, uint16_t b) {
    int32_t num = (int32_t)(((uint32_t)(uint16_t)c->edx << 16) | (uint16_t)c->eax);
    int16_t d = (int16_t)b;
    w16(&c->eax, (uint16_t)(num / d));
    w16(&c->edx, (uint16_t)(num % d));
}
static inline void alu_idiv32(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t b) {
    int64_t num = (int64_t)(((uint64_t)c->edx << 32) | c->eax);
    int32_t d = (int32_t)b;
    c->eax = (uint32_t)(num / d);
    c->edx = (uint32_t)(num % d);
}

/* x87 FPU helpers */
static inline long double *fpu_st_ptr(CpuCtx *c, int i) {
    return &c->st[(c->fpu_top + i) & 7];
}
static inline long double fpu_st(CpuCtx *c, int i) {
    return c->st[(c->fpu_top + i) & 7];
}
static inline void fpu_push(CpuCtx *c, long double v) {
    c->fpu_top = (c->fpu_top - 1) & 7;
    c->st[c->fpu_top] = v;
}
static inline long double fpu_pop(CpuCtx *c) {
    long double v = c->st[c->fpu_top];
    c->fpu_top = (c->fpu_top + 1) & 7;
    return v;
}
static inline void fpu_cmp(CpuCtx *c, long double a, long double b) {
    c->fpu_sw &= (uint16_t)~0x4500u; /* clear C3, C2, C0 */
    if (isnan(a) || isnan(b)) {
        c->fpu_sw |= 0x4500u; /* C3=1, C2=1, C0=1 */
    } else if (a > b) {
        /* C3=0, C2=0, C0=0 */
    } else if (a < b) {
        c->fpu_sw |= 0x0100u; /* C0=1 */
    } else {
        c->fpu_sw |= 0x4000u; /* C3=1 */
    }
}
static inline uint16_t fpu_get_sw(CpuCtx *c) {
    return (uint16_t)((c->fpu_sw & ~0x3800u) | ((c->fpu_top & 7) << 11));
}
static inline int64_t fpu_round_i64(CpuCtx *c, long double v) {
    uint16_t rc = (c->fpu_cw >> 10) & 3u;
    switch (rc) {
        case 0: return (int64_t)llrintl(v);   /* round to nearest */
        case 1: return (int64_t)floorl(v);    /* floor (-inf) */
        case 2: return (int64_t)ceill(v);     /* ceil (+inf) */
        case 3: return (int64_t)truncl(v);    /* trunc (toward 0) */
    }
    return (int64_t)v;
}
static inline long double fpu_round_ld(CpuCtx *c, long double v) {
    uint16_t rc = (c->fpu_cw >> 10) & 3u;
    switch (rc) {
        case 0: return rintl(v);
        case 1: return floorl(v);
        case 2: return ceill(v);
        case 3: return truncl(v);
    }
    return v;
}
static inline void fpu_save(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t addr) {
    memset(g_mem + addr, 0, 108);
    mem_w32(addr + 0, c->fpu_cw);
    mem_w32(addr + 4, fpu_get_sw(c));
    /* Save 8 doubles in slots 28..92 (custom compact layout matching fpu_rstor) */
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        double d = (double)fpu_st(c, i);
        mem_wf64(addr + 28 + i * 8, d);
    }
    /* Intel SDM: FSAVE/FNSAVE re-initializes the FPU after saving state */
    c->fpu_cw = 0x037Fu;
    c->fpu_sw = 0;
    c->fpu_top = 0;
}
static inline void fpu_rstor(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t addr) {
    c->fpu_cw = (uint16_t)mem_r32(addr + 0);
    c->fpu_sw = (uint16_t)mem_r32(addr + 4);
    c->fpu_top = (c->fpu_sw >> 11) & 7;
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        *fpu_st_ptr(c, i) = (long double)mem_rf64(addr + 28 + i * 8);
    }
}

/* String instructions */
static inline void str_movsb(CpuCtx *c) {
    int32_t step = c->df ? -1 : 1;
    mem_w8(c->edi, mem_r8(c->esi));
    c->esi += (uint32_t)step; c->edi += (uint32_t)step;
}
static inline void str_movsw(CpuCtx *c) {
    int32_t step = c->df ? -2 : 2;
    mem_w16(c->edi, mem_r16(c->esi));
    c->esi += (uint32_t)step; c->edi += (uint32_t)step;
}
static inline void str_movsd(CpuCtx *c) {
    int32_t step = c->df ? -4 : 4;
    mem_w32(c->edi, mem_r32(c->esi));
    c->esi += (uint32_t)step; c->edi += (uint32_t)step;
}
static inline void rep_movsb(CpuCtx *c) {
    if (!c->df && c->ecx > 0) {
        memmove(g_mem + c->edi, g_mem + c->esi, c->ecx);
        c->esi += c->ecx; c->edi += c->ecx; c->ecx = 0;
    } else {
        while (c->ecx) { str_movsb(c); c->ecx--; }
    }
}
static inline void rep_movsw(CpuCtx *c) {
    while (c->ecx) { str_movsw(c); c->ecx--; }
}
static inline void rep_movsd(CpuCtx *c) {
    if (!c->df && c->ecx > 0) {
        uint32_t n = c->ecx * 4u;
        memmove(g_mem + c->edi, g_mem + c->esi, n);
        c->esi += n; c->edi += n; c->ecx = 0;
    } else {
        while (c->ecx) { str_movsd(c); c->ecx--; }
    }
}
static inline void str_stosb(CpuCtx *c) {
    int32_t step = c->df ? -1 : 1;
    mem_w8(c->edi, (uint8_t)c->eax);
    c->edi += (uint32_t)step;
}
static inline void str_stosw(CpuCtx *c) {
    int32_t step = c->df ? -2 : 2;
    mem_w16(c->edi, (uint16_t)c->eax);
    c->edi += (uint32_t)step;
}
static inline void str_stosd(CpuCtx *c) {
    int32_t step = c->df ? -4 : 4;
    mem_w32(c->edi, c->eax);
    c->edi += (uint32_t)step;
}
static inline void rep_stosb(CpuCtx *c) {
    if (!c->df && c->ecx > 0) {
        memset(g_mem + c->edi, (uint8_t)c->eax, c->ecx);
        c->edi += c->ecx; c->ecx = 0;
    } else {
        while (c->ecx) { str_stosb(c); c->ecx--; }
    }
}
static inline void rep_stosw(CpuCtx *c) {
    while (c->ecx) { str_stosw(c); c->ecx--; }
}
static inline void rep_stosd(CpuCtx *c) {
    while (c->ecx) { str_stosd(c); c->ecx--; }
}
static inline void repe_cmpsb(CpuCtx *c) {
    int32_t step = c->df ? -1 : 1;
    while (c->ecx) {
        alu_sub8(c, mem_r8(c->esi), mem_r8(c->edi));
        c->esi += (uint32_t)step; c->edi += (uint32_t)step;
        c->ecx--;
        if (!c->zf) break;
    }
}
static inline void repe_cmpsw(CpuCtx *c) {
    int32_t step = c->df ? -2 : 2;
    while (c->ecx) {
        alu_sub16(c, mem_r16(c->esi), mem_r16(c->edi));
        c->esi += (uint32_t)step; c->edi += (uint32_t)step;
        c->ecx--;
        if (!c->zf) break;
    }
}
static inline void repe_cmpsd(CpuCtx *c) {
    int32_t step = c->df ? -4 : 4;
    while (c->ecx) {
        alu_sub32(c, mem_r32(c->esi), mem_r32(c->edi));
        c->esi += (uint32_t)step; c->edi += (uint32_t)step;
        c->ecx--;
        if (!c->zf) break;
    }
}
static inline void repne_scasb(CpuCtx *c) {
    int32_t step = c->df ? -1 : 1;
    while (c->ecx) {
        alu_sub8(c, (uint8_t)c->eax, mem_r8(c->edi));
        c->edi += (uint32_t)step;
        c->ecx--;
        if (c->zf) break;
    }
}
static inline void repe_scasb(CpuCtx *c) {
    int32_t step = c->df ? -1 : 1;
    while (c->ecx) {
        alu_sub8(c, (uint8_t)c->eax, mem_r8(c->edi));
        c->edi += (uint32_t)step;
        c->ecx--;
        if (!c->zf) break;
    }
}

/* SAHF */
static inline void alu_sahf(CpuCtx *c) {
    uint8_t ah = (uint8_t)((c->eax >> 8) & 0xFFu);
    c->sf = (ah >> 7) & 1u;
    c->zf = (ah >> 6) & 1u;
    c->pf = (ah >> 2) & 1u;
    c->cf = (ah >> 0) & 1u;
}
static inline void alu_lahf(CpuCtx *c) {
    uint8_t ah = (uint8_t)((c->sf << 7) | (c->zf << 6) | (c->pf << 2) | 0x02u | (c->cf & 1u));
    w8_hi(&c->eax, ah);
}

#endif /* MHA2_CPU_H */

```

`runtime/game_patches.c`:

```c
/*
 * Original 2005 game (mha2.exe) bugfixes & hot-path native replacements.
 *
 * Installed into g_code_table[] after init_code_table() so calls to these
 * addresses (emitted via g_code_table by recomp/lift.py's HOOKABLE_FUNCS)
 * execute clean, bug-free native C instead of the buggy/slow 2005 x86 code.
 */
#include "runtime.h"
#include <math.h>
#include <unistd.h>

/* 0x004443f0: void __fastcall MetaSpinLock::Lock(uint8_t *out_acquired)
 * Original bug: non-atomic DAT_0051b3b8++ with 200x Sleep(1) and forced
 * lock-steal (DAT_0051b3b8 = 0) while another thread is still in the CS.
 * Fix: true atomic CAS on DAT_0051b3b8 (-1 -> 0) with zero forced lock-steal. */
static void patch_004443f0_metalock(CpuCtx *c) {
    uint32_t tid = c->thread_id ? c->thread_id : 1000u;
    uint32_t out_acq = c->ecx;
    uint32_t *owner_ptr = (uint32_t *)(g_mem + 0x00527824u);
    int32_t  *lock_ptr  = (int32_t  *)(g_mem + 0x0051b3b8u);

    if (__atomic_load_n(owner_ptr, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) == tid) {
        if (out_acq) mem_w8(out_acq, 0);
        c->esp += 4u;
        return;
    }
    if (out_acq) mem_w8(out_acq, 1);
    for (;;) {
        int32_t expected = -1;
        if (__atomic_compare_exchange_n(lock_ptr, &expected, 0, 0,
                                        __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL, __ATOMIC_RELAXED)) {
            __atomic_store_n(owner_ptr, tid, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
            break;
        }
        usleep(200);
    }
    c->esp += 4u;
}

/* 0x00444230: int __thiscall ObjMutex::TryLock(void *this, uint32_t timeout_ms, uint32_t *out_status)
 * Original bug: uses the non-atomic meta-spinlock with forced lock-steal and 10ms Sleep polling.
 * Fix: atomic CAS directly on *(uint32_t *)(this + 4) with 1ms polling and zero lock-steal. */
static void patch_00444230_trylock(CpuCtx *c) {
    uint32_t this_va    = c->ecx;
    uint32_t timeout_ms = arg_u32(c, 0);
    uint32_t status_va  = arg_u32(c, 1);
    uint32_t tid        = c->thread_id ? c->thread_id : 1000u;

    if (status_va) mem_w32(status_va, 2u);
    if (!this_va || this_va >= GUEST_MEM_SIZE - 8u) {
        stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0u);
        return;
    }

    uint32_t *slot = (uint32_t *)(g_mem + this_va + 4u);
    if (__atomic_load_n(slot, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) == tid) {
        stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0u);
        return;
    }

    if (status_va) mem_w32(status_va, 3u);
    uint32_t expected = 0u;
    if (__atomic_compare_exchange_n(slot, &expected, tid, 0,
                                    __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL, __ATOMIC_RELAXED)) {
        stdcall_ret(c, 2, this_va);
        return;
    }

    uint32_t start = SDL_GetTicks();
    do {
        usleep(1000);
        expected = 0u;
        if (__atomic_compare_exchange_n(slot, &expected, tid, 0,
                                        __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL, __ATOMIC_RELAXED)) {
            if (status_va) mem_w32(status_va, 0u);
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, this_va);
            return;
        }
    } while ((uint32_t)(SDL_GetTicks() - start) < timeout_ms);

    if (status_va) mem_w32(status_va, 4u);
    stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0u);
}

/* 0x004a7a5e: double __cdecl ceil(double x)
 * Called inside the inner per-span loop of all 8 scaled RLE blitters (0x48dba0 et al.).
 * Replaces 40+ emulated x87/bit-mask instructions with native hardware ceil(). */
static void patch_004a7a5e_ceil(CpuCtx *c) {
    double x = mem_rf64(c->esp + 4u);
    fpu_push(c, (long double)ceil(x));
    c->esp += 4u;
}

/* 0x004a71cc: int64_t __ftol(void) (pops ST(0), returns truncated int64 in EDX:EAX)
 * Called inside all 8 scaled RLE blitters and across the entire engine.
 * Replaces 15 emulated x87 FPU-CW save/modify/restore instructions with native truncl(). */
static void patch_004a71cc_ftol(CpuCtx *c) {
    long double v = fpu_st(c, 0);
    fpu_pop(c);
    int64_t iv = (int64_t)truncl(v);
    c->eax = (uint32_t)(uint64_t)iv;
    c->edx = (uint32_t)((uint64_t)iv >> 32);
    c->esp += 4u;
}

static void install_patch(uint32_t va, RecompFn fn) {
    if (va >= GUEST_TEXT_START && va < GUEST_TEXT_START + GUEST_TEXT_SIZE) {
        g_code_table[va - GUEST_TEXT_START] = fn;
    }
}

void game_patches_install(void) {
    install_patch(0x004443f0u, patch_004443f0_metalock);
    install_patch(0x00444230u, patch_00444230_trylock);
    install_patch(0x004a7a5eu, patch_004a7a5e_ceil);
    install_patch(0x004a71ccu, patch_004a71cc_ftol);
}

/* ============================================================================
 * Read-Only Engine Clickable Hotspot & Scene-Graph Inspector (Spacebar QoL)
 * ============================================================================ */

static inline int hs_va_ok(uint32_t va, uint32_t sz) {
    return g_mem != NULL && va >= 0x00400000u && sz <= GUEST_MEM_SIZE && va <= GUEST_MEM_SIZE - sz;
}

static uint32_t hs_resolve_1d_bucket(uint32_t b0, uint32_t b1, uint32_t blo, uint32_t bhi, uint32_t g) {
    if ((int32_t)g < 0) return 0;
    if (g == 0u) return hs_va_ok(b0, 12u) ? b0 : 0u;
    if (g == 1u) return hs_va_ok(b1, 12u) ? b1 : 0u;

    uint32_t base2d = 0, slot = 0;
    if (g < 0x100u) {
        if ((g & 0xFFFF0000u) != 0u) return 0u;
        base2d = blo;
        slot = g;
    } else {
        if ((g & 0xFF0000FFu) != 0u) return 0u;
        base2d = bhi;
        slot = g >> 8;
    }
    if (!hs_va_ok(base2d, 12u)) return 0u;
    int32_t scnt = (int32_t)mem_r32(base2d);
    uint32_t sarr = mem_r32(base2d + 8u);
    if (scnt <= 0 || slot >= (uint32_t)scnt || sarr < 0x00400000u || sarr > GUEST_MEM_SIZE - 12u) {
        return 0u;
    }
    if (slot > (GUEST_MEM_SIZE - 12u - sarr) / 12u) return 0u;
    uint32_t b1d = sarr + slot * 12u;
    return hs_va_ok(b1d, 12u) ? b1d : 0u;
}

static void hs_resolve_id(uint32_t *g, int32_t *idx, int max_depth) {
    for (int d = 0; d < max_depth && ((*g & 0xF0000000u) == 0x80000000u); d++) {
        if (*idx < 0) {
            *g = 0u;
            *idx = -1;
            return;
        }
        uint32_t k = *g & 0x7FFFFFFFu;
        uint32_t b1d = hs_resolve_1d_bucket(0x00527760u, 0x0052776cu, 0x00527784u, 0x00527778u, k);
        if (!b1d) {
            *g = 0u;
            *idx = -1;
            return;
        }
        int32_t cnt = (int32_t)mem_r32(b1d);
        uint32_t data = mem_r32(b1d + 8u);
        if (cnt <= 0 || *idx >= cnt || data < 0x00400000u || data > GUEST_MEM_SIZE - 8u ||
            (uint32_t)(*idx) > (GUEST_MEM_SIZE - 8u - data) / 8u) {
            *g = 0u;
            *idx = -1;
            return;
        }
        uint32_t entry = data + (uint32_t)(*idx) * 8u;
        *g = mem_r32(entry);
        *idx = (int32_t)mem_r32(entry + 4u);
    }
}

static uint32_t hs_lookup_ptr_table(uint32_t b0, uint32_t b1, uint32_t blo, uint32_t bhi,
                                    uint32_t g, int32_t idx) {
    hs_resolve_id(&g, &idx, 2);
    if (idx < 0) return 0u;
    uint32_t b1d = hs_resolve_1d_bucket(b0, b1, blo, bhi, g);
    if (!b1d) return 0u;
    int32_t cnt = (int32_t)mem_r32(b1d);
    uint32_t data = mem_r32(b1d + 8u);
    if (cnt <= 0 || idx >= cnt || data < 0x00400000u || data > GUEST_MEM_SIZE - 4u ||
        (uint32_t)idx > (GUEST_MEM_SIZE - 4u - data) / 4u) {
        return 0u;
    }
    return mem_r32(data + (uint32_t)idx * 4u);
}

static uint32_t hs_get_props_off(uint32_t vt) {
    switch (vt) {
        case 0x005070f4u: return 0xa8u;  /* Hotspot */
        case 0x00507fc8u: return 0x214u; /* Walker */
        case 0x0050815cu: return 0x1bcu; /* Prop */
        case 0x004fe9e8u:
        case 0x004fef84u:
        case 0x00501a2cu:
        case 0x00501c2cu:
        case 0x005030d8u: return 0x1d8u; /* AnimObj */
        default:          return 0u;
    }
}

static inline void hs_read_prop(uint32_t obj, uint32_t props_off, int slot,
                                uint32_t *out_g, int32_t *out_idx) {
    uint32_t p = obj + props_off + (uint32_t)slot * 8u;
    *out_g = mem_r32(p);
    *out_idx = (int32_t)mem_r32(p + 4u);
}

static void hs_cp1252_to_utf8(uint32_t str_va, char *dst, size_t dst_sz) {
    dst[0] = '\0';
    if (!hs_va_ok(str_va, 1u) || dst_sz < 4u) return;

    char raw[240];
    size_t rlen = 0;
    while (rlen + 1u < sizeof(raw) && hs_va_ok(str_va + (uint32_t)rlen, 1u)) {
        uint8_t ch = mem_r8(str_va + (uint32_t)rlen);
        if (ch == 0u) break;
        raw[rlen++] = (char)ch;
    }
    raw[rlen] = '\0';

    size_t s0 = 0, s1 = rlen;
    while (s0 < s1 && ((uint8_t)raw[s0] == ' ' || (uint8_t)raw[s0] == '\t' ||
                       (uint8_t)raw[s0] == '\r' || (uint8_t)raw[s0] == '\n')) s0++;
    while (s1 > s0 && ((uint8_t)raw[s1 - 1u] == ' ' || (uint8_t)raw[s1 - 1u] == '\t' ||
                       (uint8_t)raw[s1 - 1u] == '\r' || (uint8_t)raw[s1 - 1u] == '\n')) s1--;

    size_t j = 0;
    for (size_t i = s0; i < s1 && j + 4u < dst_sz; i++) {
        uint8_t ch = (uint8_t)raw[i];
        if (ch < 0x80u) {
            dst[j++] = (char)ch;
            continue;
        }
        uint32_t cp = ch;
        if      (ch == 0x80u) cp = 0x20ACu;
        else if (ch == 0x82u) cp = 0x201Au;
        else if (ch == 0x84u) cp = 0x201Eu;
        else if (ch == 0x85u) cp = 0x2026u;
        else if (ch == 0x91u) cp = 0x2018u;
        else if (ch == 0x92u) cp = 0x2019u;
        else if (ch == 0x93u) cp = 0x201Cu;
        else if (ch == 0x94u) cp = 0x201Du;
        else if (ch == 0x96u) cp = 0x2013u;
        else if (ch == 0x97u) cp = 0x2014u;
        if (cp < 0x800u) {
            dst[j++] = (char)(0xC0u | (cp >> 6));
            dst[j++] = (char)(0x80u | (cp & 0x3Fu));
        } else {
            dst[j++] = (char)(0xE0u | (cp >> 12));
            dst[j++] = (char)(0x80u | ((cp >> 6) & 0x3Fu));
            dst[j++] = (char)(0x80u | (cp & 0x3Fu));
        }
    }
    dst[j] = '\0';
}

static void hs_lookup_label(uint32_t obj, uint32_t props_off, uint32_t hs,
                            char *dst, size_t dst_sz) {
    dst[0] = '\0';
    uint32_t sg = 0u;
    int32_t sidx = -1;

    hs_read_prop(obj, props_off, 1, &sg, &sidx);
    hs_resolve_id(&sg, &sidx, 3);

    if (sidx < 0) {
        uint32_t lg = 0u;
        int32_t lidx = -1;
        hs_read_prop(obj, props_off, 9, &lg, &lidx);
        uint32_t spr = hs_lookup_ptr_table(0x00527be8u, 0x00527bf4u, 0x00527c0cu, 0x00527c00u, lg, lidx);
        if (hs_va_ok(spr, 4u)) {
            uint32_t spoff = hs_get_props_off(mem_r32(spr));
            if (spoff && hs_va_ok(spr, spoff + 25u * 8u)) {
                hs_read_prop(spr, spoff, 1, &sg, &sidx);
                hs_resolve_id(&sg, &sidx, 3);
            }
        }
    }

    if (sidx < 0 && hs && hs != obj && hs_va_ok(hs, 0xa8u + 25u * 8u)) {
        hs_read_prop(hs, 0xa8u, 1, &sg, &sidx);
        hs_resolve_id(&sg, &sidx, 3);
    }

    if (sidx < 0) return;
    uint32_t sl = hs_lookup_ptr_table(0x005277f0u, 0x005277fcu, 0x00527814u, 0x00527808u, sg, sidx);
    if (!hs_va_ok(sl, 0x10u)) return;

    int32_t nlines = (int32_t)mem_r32(sl + 0x0cu);
    uint32_t arr = mem_r32(sl + 0x08u);
    if (nlines <= 0) return;
    if (nlines > 32) nlines = 32;
    if (!hs_va_ok(arr, (uint32_t)nlines * 4u)) return;

    for (int32_t i = 0; i < nlines; i++) {
        uint32_t line_va = mem_r32(arr + (uint32_t)i * 4u);
        if (!line_va) continue;
        hs_cp1252_to_utf8(line_va, dst, dst_sz);
        if (dst[0] != '\0') return;
    }
}

typedef struct HsWalkCtx {
    GameHotspot *out;
    int max_out;
    int count;
    struct {
        uint32_t g;
        int32_t  i;
        uint32_t hs_va;
        int      from_sprite;
    } keys[64];
    int32_t clip_l, clip_t, clip_r, clip_b;
    uint32_t skip_ui1, skip_ui2;
    uint32_t layer_mask;
    uint32_t player;
    uint32_t hotspots_ptr;
    int32_t  hotspot_count;
    int      is_location;
} HsWalkCtx;

static uint32_t hs_resolve_hotspot(const HsWalkCtx *ctx, uint32_t obj, uint32_t vt, uint32_t poff) {
    if (vt == 0x005070f4u) return obj;
    uint32_t hs = 0u;
    uint32_t g0 = 0u;
    int32_t i0 = -1;
    hs_read_prop(obj, poff, 0, &g0, &i0);
    hs_resolve_id(&g0, &i0, 2);
    if (i0 >= 0 && i0 < ctx->hotspot_count && ctx->hotspots_ptr &&
        hs_va_ok(ctx->hotspots_ptr + (uint32_t)i0 * 4u, 4u)) {
        hs = mem_r32(ctx->hotspots_ptr + (uint32_t)i0 * 4u);
    }
    if (!hs) {
        uint32_t g2 = 0u;
        int32_t i2 = -1;
        hs_read_prop(obj, poff, 2, &g2, &i2);
        hs = hs_lookup_ptr_table(0x00527af0u, 0x00527afcu, 0x00527b14u, 0x00527b08u, g2, i2);
    }
    if (hs && (!hs_va_ok(hs, 0xa8u + 25u * 8u) || mem_r32(hs) != 0x005070f4u)) {
        return 0u;
    }
    return hs;
}

static int hs_has_any_verb(uint32_t hs) {
    if (!hs || !hs_va_ok(hs, 0xa8u + 25u * 8u)) return 0;
    for (int slot = 10; slot < 25; slot++) {
        uint32_t vg = 0u;
        int32_t vi = -1;
        hs_read_prop(hs, 0xa8u, slot, &vg, &vi);
        hs_resolve_id(&vg, &vi, 2);
        if (vi >= 0) return 1;
    }
    return 0;
}

static int hs_prop_has_dialog(uint32_t obj, uint32_t poff) {
    if (!obj || !poff || !hs_va_ok(obj, poff + 25u * 8u)) return 0;
    for (int slot = 19; slot <= 20; slot++) {
        uint32_t dg = 0u;
        int32_t di = -1;
        hs_read_prop(obj, poff, slot, &dg, &di);
        if (hs_lookup_ptr_table(0x005282e0u, 0x005282ecu, 0x00528304u, 0x005282f8u, dg, di) != 0u) {
            return 1;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

static int hs_classify_kind(uint32_t obj, uint32_t vt, uint32_t poff, uint32_t hs) {
    if (hs && (int32_t)mem_r32(hs + 0x80u) > 0) return 1; /* Exit */
    if (vt == 0x00507fc8u) return 2; /* Walker NPC */
    uint32_t g3 = 0u;
    int32_t i3 = -1;
    hs_read_prop(obj, poff, 3, &g3, &i3);
    hs_resolve_id(&g3, &i3, 2);
    if (i3 == 3) return 2;
    if (hs && hs != obj) {
        hs_read_prop(hs, 0xa8u, 3, &g3, &i3);
        hs_resolve_id(&g3, &i3, 2);
        if (i3 == 3) return 2;
    }
    if (hs_prop_has_dialog(obj, poff) || (hs && hs != obj && hs_prop_has_dialog(hs, 0xa8u))) {
        return 2;
    }
    return 0;
}

static int hs_clip_and_merge(const HsWalkCtx *ctx, int32_t sl, int32_t st, int32_t sr, int32_t sb,
                             int32_t *ux0, int32_t *uy0, int32_t *ux1, int32_t *uy1,
                             int64_t *best_area, int32_t *best_cx, int32_t *best_cy) {
    if (sl < ctx->clip_l) sl = ctx->clip_l;
    if (st < ctx->clip_t) st = ctx->clip_t;
    if (sr > ctx->clip_r) sr = ctx->clip_r;
    if (sb > ctx->clip_b) sb = ctx->clip_b;
    if (sr <= sl || sb <= st) return 0;
    if (*ux1 <= *ux0) {
        *ux0 = sl; *uy0 = st; *ux1 = sr; *uy1 = sb;
    } else {
        if (sl < *ux0) *ux0 = sl;
        if (st < *uy0) *uy0 = st;
        if (sr > *ux1) *ux1 = sr;
        if (sb > *uy1) *uy1 = sb;
    }
    int64_t area = (int64_t)(sr - sl) * (int64_t)(sb - st);
    if (area > *best_area) {
        *best_area = area;
        *best_cx = (sl + sr) / 2;
        *best_cy = (st + sb) / 2;
    }
    return 1;
}

static int hs_compute_bounds(const HsWalkCtx *ctx, uint32_t obj, uint32_t vt,
                             int32_t abs_x, int32_t abs_y,
                             int *out_x0, int *out_y0, int *out_x1, int *out_y1,
                             int *out_ax, int *out_ay) {
    int is_scaled = (vt != 0x005070f4u) &&
                    !(mem_r32(obj + 0x58u) == 0u && mem_r32(obj + 0x5cu) == 0x3ff00000u);
    double sc = 1.0;
    if (is_scaled) {
        sc = mem_rf64(obj + 0x58u);
        if (!(sc > 0.01 && sc <= 20.0)) {
            is_scaled = 0;
            sc = 1.0;
        }
    }

    int32_t ux0 = 0, uy0 = 0, ux1 = 0, uy1 = 0;
    int64_t best_area = -1;
    int32_t best_cx = 0, best_cy = 0;

    uint32_t shape = mem_r32(obj + 0x54u);
    uint32_t shape_vt = hs_va_ok(shape, 0x20u) ? mem_r32(shape) : 0u;
    if (shape_vt == 0x00503d88u) {
        int32_t l = (int32_t)mem_r32(shape + 0x08u);
        int32_t t = (int32_t)mem_r32(shape + 0x0cu);
        int32_t r = (int32_t)mem_r32(shape + 0x10u);
        int32_t b = (int32_t)mem_r32(shape + 0x14u);
```
