
`runtime/hd_assets.c`:

```c
#ifndef _GNU_SOURCE
#define _GNU_SOURCE
#endif
#include "hd_assets.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <strings.h>
#include <dirent.h>
#include <pthread.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>

#include <libavformat/avformat.h>
#include <libavcodec/avcodec.h>
#include <libswscale/swscale.h>
#include <libavutil/imgutils.h>

#define MAX_HD_ASSETS 512
#define MAX_CACHE_ENTRIES 1024
#define HD_CACHE_MAGIC 0x31434448u /* "HDC1" */

typedef struct HdCacheDiskEntry {
    char rel_name[512];
    int64_t mtime;
    int64_t fsize;
    int32_t orig_w;
    int32_t orig_h;
    uint8_t thumb[HD_THUMB_BYTES];
} HdCacheDiskEntry;

static HdCacheDiskEntry *g_disk_cache = NULL;
static int g_disk_cache_count = 0;
static HdCacheDiskEntry *g_new_cache = NULL;
static int g_new_cache_count = 0;
static int g_cache_dirty = 0;

static const HdCacheDiskEntry *find_in_disk_cache(const char *rel, int64_t mtime, int64_t fsize) {
    for (int i = 0; i < g_disk_cache_count; i++) {
        if (g_disk_cache[i].mtime == mtime && g_disk_cache[i].fsize == fsize &&
            strcmp(g_disk_cache[i].rel_name, rel) == 0) {
            return &g_disk_cache[i];
        }
    }
    return NULL;
}

static void load_disk_cache(const char *hd_dir) {
    char path[1100];
    snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "%s/.hd_index.cache", hd_dir);
    FILE *f = fopen(path, "rb");
    if (!f) return;
    uint32_t magic = 0, count = 0;
    if (fread(&magic, 4, 1, f) == 1 && fread(&count, 4, 1, f) == 1 &&
        magic == HD_CACHE_MAGIC && count > 0 && count <= MAX_CACHE_ENTRIES) {
        g_disk_cache = (HdCacheDiskEntry *)malloc(count * sizeof(HdCacheDiskEntry));
        if (g_disk_cache && fread(g_disk_cache, sizeof(HdCacheDiskEntry), count, f) == count) {
            g_disk_cache_count = (int)count;
        } else {
            free(g_disk_cache);
            g_disk_cache = NULL;
        }
    }
    fclose(f);
}

static void save_disk_cache(const char *hd_dir) {
    if (!g_new_cache || g_new_cache_count <= 0) return;
    char path[1100];
    snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "%s/.hd_index.cache", hd_dir);
    FILE *f = fopen(path, "wb");
    if (!f) return;
    uint32_t magic = HD_CACHE_MAGIC;
    uint32_t count = (uint32_t)g_new_cache_count;
    fwrite(&magic, 4, 1, f);
    fwrite(&count, 4, 1, f);
    fwrite(g_new_cache, sizeof(HdCacheDiskEntry), count, f);
    fclose(f);
}

typedef struct HdAssetEntry {
    char full_path[1024];
    char rel_name[512];
    int orig_w;
    int orig_h;
    uint8_t thumb[HD_THUMB_BYTES];
    int thumb_valid;
    int thumb_luma_mean;
} HdAssetEntry;

static HdAssetEntry g_assets[MAX_HD_ASSETS];
static int g_asset_count = 0;

/* Single active decoded full-res RGBA cache (avoids holding hundreds of 4K PNGs in RAM) */
static int g_cached_id = -1;
static HdImageRGBA g_cached_img = { NULL, 0, 0 };

static int has_image_ext(const char *name) {
    const char *dot = strrchr(name, '.');
    if (!dot) return 0;
    return (strcasecmp(dot, ".png") == 0 ||
            strcasecmp(dot, ".webp") == 0 ||
            strcasecmp(dot, ".jpg") == 0 ||
            strcasecmp(dot, ".jpeg") == 0 ||
            strcasecmp(dot, ".bmp") == 0);
}

/* Decode an image file using FFmpeg libavformat+libavcodec+libswscale into RGBA
 * (if out_w/out_h == 0, keeps native resolution; else scales to out_w x out_h). */
static uint8_t *decode_image_ffmpeg(const char *path, int dst_w, int dst_h,
                                    enum AVPixelFormat dst_fmt, int *out_w, int *out_h) {
    AVFormatContext *fmt = NULL;
    if (avformat_open_input(&fmt, path, NULL, NULL) != 0) return NULL;
    if (avformat_find_stream_info(fmt, NULL) < 0) {
        avformat_close_input(&fmt);
        return NULL;
    }

    int vidx = -1;
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < fmt->nb_streams; i++) {
        if (fmt->streams[i]->codecpar->codec_type == AVMEDIA_TYPE_VIDEO) {
            vidx = (int)i;
            break;
        }
    }
    if (vidx < 0) {
        avformat_close_input(&fmt);
        return NULL;
    }

    const AVCodec *codec = avcodec_find_decoder(fmt->streams[vidx]->codecpar->codec_id);
    if (!codec) {
        avformat_close_input(&fmt);
        return NULL;
    }
    AVCodecContext *cc = avcodec_alloc_context3(codec);
    if (!cc || avcodec_parameters_to_context(cc, fmt->streams[vidx]->codecpar) < 0 ||
        avcodec_open2(cc, codec, NULL) < 0) {
        if (cc) avcodec_free_context(&cc);
        avformat_close_input(&fmt);
        return NULL;
    }

    AVPacket *pkt = av_packet_alloc();
    AVFrame  *frm = av_frame_alloc();
    uint8_t  *out_buf = NULL;

    while (av_read_frame(fmt, pkt) >= 0) {
        if (pkt->stream_index == vidx) {
            if (avcodec_send_packet(cc, pkt) == 0 && avcodec_receive_frame(cc, frm) == 0) {
                int tw = (dst_w > 0) ? dst_w : frm->width;
                int th = (dst_h > 0) ? dst_h : frm->height;
                int bpp = (dst_fmt == AV_PIX_FMT_RGBA) ? 4 : 3;
                out_buf = (uint8_t *)malloc((size_t)tw * (size_t)th * (size_t)bpp);
                if (out_buf) {
                    struct SwsContext *sws = sws_getContext(
                        frm->width, frm->height, (enum AVPixelFormat)frm->format,
                        tw, th, dst_fmt,
                        (dst_w > 0) ? SWS_AREA : SWS_LANCZOS, NULL, NULL, NULL);
                    if (sws) {
                        uint8_t *dst_data[4] = { out_buf, NULL, NULL, NULL };
                        int dst_linesize[4] = { tw * bpp, 0, 0, 0 };
                        sws_scale(sws, (const uint8_t *const *)frm->data, frm->linesize,
                                  0, frm->height, dst_data, dst_linesize);
                        sws_freeContext(sws);
                        if (out_w) *out_w = frm->width;
                        if (out_h) *out_h = frm->height;
                    } else {
                        free(out_buf);
                        out_buf = NULL;
                    }
                }
                av_packet_unref(pkt);
                break;
            }
        }
        av_packet_unref(pkt);
    }

    av_frame_free(&frm);
    av_packet_free(&pkt);
    avcodec_free_context(&cc);
    avformat_close_input(&fmt);
    return out_buf;
}

static void scan_dir_recursive(const char *root_dir, const char *rel_prefix) {
    char cur_dir[1024];
    if (rel_prefix && rel_prefix[0]) {
        snprintf(cur_dir, sizeof(cur_dir), "%s/%s", root_dir, rel_prefix);
    } else {
        snprintf(cur_dir, sizeof(cur_dir), "%s", root_dir);
    }

    DIR *d = opendir(cur_dir);
    if (!d) return;

    struct dirent *ent;
    while ((ent = readdir(d)) != NULL && g_asset_count < MAX_HD_ASSETS) {
        if (ent->d_name[0] == '.') continue;
        char full[1024], rel[512];
        int fn = snprintf(full, sizeof(full), "%s/%s", cur_dir, ent->d_name);
        if (fn < 0 || fn >= (int)sizeof(full)) continue;
        int rn;
        if (rel_prefix && rel_prefix[0]) {
            rn = snprintf(rel, sizeof(rel), "%s/%s", rel_prefix, ent->d_name);
        } else {
            rn = snprintf(rel, sizeof(rel), "%s", ent->d_name);
        }
        if (rn < 0 || rn >= (int)sizeof(rel)) continue;

        struct stat st;
        if (stat(full, &st) != 0) continue;
        if (S_ISDIR(st.st_mode)) {
            if ((!rel_prefix || !rel_prefix[0]) &&
                (strcmp(ent->d_name, "sprites") == 0 || strcmp(ent->d_name, "videos") == 0)) {
                continue;
            }
            scan_dir_recursive(root_dir, rel);
        } else if (S_ISREG(st.st_mode) && has_image_ext(ent->d_name)) {
            /* Skip optional 16:9 widescreen wing extensions (*_wide.webp / *_wide.png) from 4:3 matching */
            if (strstr(ent->d_name, "_wide.") != NULL) continue;
            int64_t mtime = (int64_t)st.st_mtime;
            int64_t fsize = (int64_t)st.st_size;
            int nw = 0, nh = 0;
            uint8_t thumb_buf[HD_THUMB_BYTES];
            const HdCacheDiskEntry *ce = find_in_disk_cache(rel, mtime, fsize);
            if (ce) {
                nw = ce->orig_w;
                nh = ce->orig_h;
                memcpy(thumb_buf, ce->thumb, HD_THUMB_BYTES);
            } else {
                uint8_t *thumb = decode_image_ffmpeg(full, HD_THUMB_W, HD_THUMB_H, AV_PIX_FMT_RGB24, &nw, &nh);
                if (!thumb) continue;
                memcpy(thumb_buf, thumb, HD_THUMB_BYTES);
                free(thumb);
                g_cache_dirty = 1;
            }

            if (g_new_cache && g_new_cache_count < MAX_CACHE_ENTRIES) {
                HdCacheDiskEntry *ne = &g_new_cache[g_new_cache_count++];
                memset(ne, 0, sizeof(*ne));
                snprintf(ne->rel_name, sizeof(ne->rel_name), "%s", rel);
                ne->mtime = mtime;
                ne->fsize = fsize;
                ne->orig_w = nw;
                ne->orig_h = nh;
                memcpy(ne->thumb, thumb_buf, HD_THUMB_BYTES);
            }

            /* Index both 16:9 gameplay rooms (>= 800x450) and 4:3 menus (>= 800x600) */
            if (nw < 800 || nh < 450) {
                continue;
            }

            HdAssetEntry *e = &g_assets[g_asset_count++];
            snprintf(e->full_path, sizeof(e->full_path), "%s", full);
            snprintf(e->rel_name, sizeof(e->rel_name), "%s", rel);
            e->orig_w = nw;
            e->orig_h = nh;
            memcpy(e->thumb, thumb_buf, HD_THUMB_BYTES);
            e->thumb_valid = 1;
            int sum = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < HD_THUMB_BYTES; i++) sum += thumb_buf[i];
            e->thumb_luma_mean = sum / HD_THUMB_BYTES;
        }
    }
    closedir(d);
}

static void hd_sprites_init(const char *hd_dir);
static void hd_sprites_shutdown(void);

int hd_assets_init(const char *hd_dir) {
    hd_assets_shutdown();
    if (!hd_dir || !hd_dir[0]) return 0;
    struct stat st;
    if (stat(hd_dir, &st) != 0 || !S_ISDIR(st.st_mode)) return 0;

    av_log_set_level(AV_LOG_QUIET);
    g_disk_cache = NULL;
    g_disk_cache_count = 0;
    g_new_cache = (HdCacheDiskEntry *)malloc(MAX_CACHE_ENTRIES * sizeof(HdCacheDiskEntry));
    g_new_cache_count = 0;
    g_cache_dirty = 0;

    load_disk_cache(hd_dir);
    scan_dir_recursive(hd_dir, "");
    if (g_cache_dirty || g_new_cache_count != g_disk_cache_count) {
        save_disk_cache(hd_dir);
    }
    free(g_disk_cache); g_disk_cache = NULL; g_disk_cache_count = 0;
    free(g_new_cache);  g_new_cache = NULL;  g_new_cache_count = 0;

    if (g_asset_count > 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "[HD] Indexed %d HD background replacement(s) from '%s'%s\n",
                g_asset_count, hd_dir, g_cache_dirty ? " (cache updated)" : " (cached)");
    }
    hd_sprites_init(hd_dir);
    return g_asset_count;
}

void hd_assets_shutdown(void) {
    hd_sprites_shutdown();
    if (g_cached_img.rgba) {
        free(g_cached_img.rgba);
        g_cached_img.rgba = NULL;
    }
    g_cached_img.width = 0;
    g_cached_img.height = 0;
    g_cached_id = -1;
    g_asset_count = 0;
}

int hd_assets_count(void) {
    return g_asset_count;
}

/* Compute fast 16x12 area-sampled RGB thumbnail of a guest surface (takes ~10 us). */
static void compute_surface_thumb(const void *pixels, int w, int h, int pitch, int bpp,
                                  uint8_t out_thumb[HD_THUMB_BYTES], int *out_mean) {
    const uint8_t *base = (const uint8_t *)pixels;
    int total_sum = 0;
    for (int ty = 0; ty < HD_THUMB_H; ty++) {
        int y0 = (ty * h) / HD_THUMB_H;
        int y1 = ((ty + 1) * h) / HD_THUMB_H;
        int ystep = (y1 - y0) > 8 ? ((y1 - y0) / 4) : 1;
        for (int tx = 0; tx < HD_THUMB_W; tx++) {
            int x0 = (tx * w) / HD_THUMB_W;
            int x1 = ((tx + 1) * w) / HD_THUMB_W;
            int xstep = (x1 - x0) > 8 ? ((x1 - x0) / 4) : 1;
            int sr = 0, sg = 0, sb = 0, cnt = 0;
            for (int y = y0; y < y1; y += ystep) {
                const uint8_t *row = base + y * pitch;
                for (int x = x0; x < x1; x += xstep) {
                    if (bpp == 32) {
                        uint32_t px;
                        memcpy(&px, row + x * 4, 4);
                        sr += (int)((px >> 16) & 0xFFu);
                        sg += (int)((px >> 8) & 0xFFu);
                        sb += (int)(px & 0xFFu);
                    } else {
                        uint16_t px;
                        memcpy(&px, row + x * 2, 2);
                        sr += (int)(((px >> 11) & 0x1Fu) * 255u / 31u);
                        sg += (int)(((px >> 5) & 0x3Fu) * 255u / 63u);
                        sb += (int)((px & 0x1Fu) * 255u / 31u);
                    }
                    cnt++;
                }
            }
            if (cnt <= 0) cnt = 1;
            int idx = (ty * HD_THUMB_W + tx) * 3;
            uint8_t r = (uint8_t)(sr / cnt);
            uint8_t g = (uint8_t)(sg / cnt);
            uint8_t b = (uint8_t)(sb / cnt);
            out_thumb[idx + 0] = r;
            out_thumb[idx + 1] = g;
            out_thumb[idx + 2] = b;
            total_sum += r + g + b;
        }
    }
    if (out_mean) *out_mean = total_sum / HD_THUMB_BYTES;
}

int hd_assets_match_pixels(const void *pixels, int w, int h, int pitch, int bpp) {
    if (g_asset_count <= 0 || !pixels || w < 640 || h < 440 || (bpp != 16 && bpp != 32)) {
        return -1;
    }
    uint8_t thumb[HD_THUMB_BYTES];
    int mean = 0;
    compute_surface_thumb(pixels, w, h, pitch, bpp, thumb, &mean);
    /* Reject all-black / blank surfaces */
    if (mean < 5) return -1;

    float src_aspect = (float)w / (float)h;
    int best_id = -1;
    int best_sad = 1 << 30;
    for (int i = 0; i < g_asset_count; i++) {
        const HdAssetEntry *e = &g_assets[i];
        if (!e->thumb_valid || e->orig_h <= 0) continue;
        float e_aspect = (float)e->orig_w / (float)e->orig_h;
        float diff = src_aspect - e_aspect;
        if (diff < -0.08f || diff > 0.08f) continue;
        int sad = 0;
        for (int k = 0; k < HD_THUMB_BYTES; k++) {
            int d = (int)thumb[k] - (int)e->thumb[k];
            sad += (d < 0) ? -d : d;
        }
        if (sad < best_sad) {
            best_sad = sad;
            best_id = i;
        }
    }

    /* Require tight per-channel average error (< 14 / 255) */
    if (best_id >= 0 && (best_sad / HD_THUMB_BYTES) < 14) {
        return best_id;
    }
    return -1;
}

const HdImageRGBA *hd_assets_get_rgba(int asset_id) {
    if (asset_id < 0 || asset_id >= g_asset_count) return NULL;
    if (g_cached_id == asset_id && g_cached_img.rgba) {
        return &g_cached_img;
    }
    if (g_cached_img.rgba) {
        free(g_cached_img.rgba);
        g_cached_img.rgba = NULL;
    }
    g_cached_id = -1;

    const HdAssetEntry *e = &g_assets[asset_id];
    int nw = 0, nh = 0;
    uint8_t *rgba = decode_image_ffmpeg(e->full_path, 0, 0, AV_PIX_FMT_RGBA, &nw, &nh);
    if (!rgba) return NULL;

    g_cached_img.rgba = rgba;
    g_cached_img.width = nw;
    g_cached_img.height = nh;
    g_cached_id = asset_id;
    fprintf(stderr, "[HD] Activated HD background '%s' (%dx%d)\n", e->rel_name, nw, nh);
    return &g_cached_img;
}

const char *hd_assets_get_name(int asset_id) {
    if (asset_id < 0 || asset_id >= g_asset_count) return "";
    return g_assets[asset_id].rel_name;
}

int hd_assets_decode_wide_rgba(int asset_id, HdImageRGBA *out) {
    if (!out || asset_id < 0 || asset_id >= g_asset_count) return -1;
    const char *base = g_assets[asset_id].full_path;
    const char *dot = strrchr(base, '.');
    if (!dot) return -1;
    size_t stem_len = (size_t)(dot - base);
    if (stem_len + 16 >= 1024) return -1;
    static const char *exts[] = { "webp", "png", "jpg" };
    struct stat st;
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        char wpath[1024];
        snprintf(wpath, sizeof(wpath), "%.*s_wide.%s", (int)stem_len, base, exts[i]);
        if (stat(wpath, &st) == 0 && S_ISREG(st.st_mode)) {
            int nw = 0, nh = 0;
            uint8_t *rgba = decode_image_ffmpeg(wpath, 0, 0, AV_PIX_FMT_RGBA, &nw, &nh);
            if (!rgba || nw <= 0 || nh <= 0) {
                free(rgba);
                return -1;
            }
            out->rgba = rgba;
            out->width = nw;
            out->height = nh;
            return 0;
        }
    }
    return -1;
}

/* ============================================================================
 * Native HD Foreground Sprites, Characters, Inventory & Cursors Indexer
 * ============================================================================ */

#define MAX_HD_SPRITES     16384
#define HD_SPR_TW          24
#define HD_SPR_TH          24
#define HD_SPR_TBYTES      (HD_SPR_TW * HD_SPR_TH * 4) /* 2304 bytes RGBA */
#define HD_SPR_CACHE_MAGIC 0x35534448u /* "HDS5" */
#define HD_SPR_BUCKETS     2048

typedef struct HdSprCacheDiskEntry {
    char rel_name[256];
    int64_t mtime;
    int64_t fsize;
    int32_t hd_w;
    int32_t hd_h;
    uint8_t thumb[HD_SPR_TBYTES];
} HdSprCacheDiskEntry;

typedef struct HdSpriteEntry {
    char full_path[512];
    char rel_name[256];
    int hd_w;
    int hd_h;
    uint8_t thumb[HD_SPR_TBYTES];
    int next_in_bucket;
} HdSpriteEntry;

static HdSpriteEntry *g_sprites = NULL;
static int g_sprite_count = 0;
static int g_spr_buckets[HD_SPR_BUCKETS];
static char g_spr_room_prefix[64] = "";
static size_t g_spr_room_prefix_len = 0;
static uint32_t g_spr_room_gen = 1;

/* ---- Background Room-Sprite Pre-Decoder Thread (eliminates first-frame micro-stutter) ---- */
#define PREDEC_SLOTS 96
typedef struct PredecEntry {
    int sprite_id;
    uint8_t *rgba;
    int w, h;
} PredecEntry;

static PredecEntry     g_predec[PREDEC_SLOTS];
static pthread_mutex_t g_predec_mtx = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER;
static pthread_cond_t  g_predec_cv  = PTHREAD_COND_INITIALIZER;
static pthread_t       g_predec_thr;
static int             g_predec_running = 0;
static int             g_predec_stop    = 0;
static uint32_t        g_predec_target_gen = 0;
static char            g_predec_target_prefix[64] = "";

static void predec_clear_locked(void) {
    for (int i = 0; i < PREDEC_SLOTS; i++) {
        free(g_predec[i].rgba);
        g_predec[i].rgba = NULL;
        g_predec[i].sprite_id = -1;
        g_predec[i].w = g_predec[i].h = 0;
    }
}

extern void runtime_unpin_current_thread(void);

static void *predec_worker_main(void *arg) {
    (void)arg;
    runtime_unpin_current_thread();
    uint32_t last_gen = 0;
    for (;;) {
        char pref[64];
        uint32_t cur_gen;
        pthread_mutex_lock(&g_predec_mtx);
        while (!g_predec_stop && g_predec_target_gen == last_gen) {
            pthread_cond_wait(&g_predec_cv, &g_predec_mtx);
        }
        if (g_predec_stop) {
            pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_predec_mtx);
            break;
        }
        cur_gen = g_predec_target_gen;
        snprintf(pref, sizeof(pref), "%s", g_predec_target_prefix);
        predec_clear_locked();
        pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_predec_mtx);

        last_gen = cur_gen;
        size_t plen = strlen(pref);
        if (plen == 0 || !g_sprites || g_sprite_count <= 0) continue;

        int slot = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < g_sprite_count && slot < PREDEC_SLOTS; i++) {
            pthread_mutex_lock(&g_predec_mtx);
            int abort_now = (g_predec_stop || g_predec_target_gen != cur_gen);
            pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_predec_mtx);
            if (abort_now) break;

            if (strncmp(g_sprites[i].rel_name, pref, plen) != 0) continue;
            if (g_sprites[i].hd_w > 4096 || g_sprites[i].hd_h > 4096) continue;
            int nw = 0, nh = 0;
            uint8_t *rgba = decode_image_ffmpeg(g_sprites[i].full_path, 0, 0, AV_PIX_FMT_RGBA, &nw, &nh);
            if (!rgba) continue;

            pthread_mutex_lock(&g_predec_mtx);
            if (g_predec_stop || g_predec_target_gen != cur_gen) {
                free(rgba);
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_predec_mtx);
                break;
            }
            g_predec[slot].sprite_id = i;
            g_predec[slot].rgba = rgba;
            g_predec[slot].w = nw;
            g_predec[slot].h = nh;
            slot++;
            pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_predec_mtx);
        }
    }
    return NULL;
}

void hd_sprites_set_room_prefix(const char *bg_rel_name) {
    char next_prefix[64] = "";
    if (bg_rel_name && bg_rel_name[0]) {
        /* Extract "<dir>/<room>/" from e.g. "l/006/img/objBg.png" -> "l/006/" */
        const char *s1 = strchr(bg_rel_name, '/');
        if (s1) {
            const char *s2 = strchr(s1 + 1, '/');
            if (s2) {
                size_t len = (size_t)(s2 - bg_rel_name + 1);
                if (len < sizeof(next_prefix)) {
                    memcpy(next_prefix, bg_rel_name, len);
                    next_prefix[len] = '\0';
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if (strcmp(g_spr_room_prefix, next_prefix) != 0) {
        snprintf(g_spr_room_prefix, sizeof(g_spr_room_prefix), "%s", next_prefix);
        g_spr_room_prefix_len = strlen(g_spr_room_prefix);
        g_spr_room_gen++;
        if (g_predec_running) {
            pthread_mutex_lock(&g_predec_mtx);
            snprintf(g_predec_target_prefix, sizeof(g_predec_target_prefix), "%s", next_prefix);
            g_predec_target_gen = g_spr_room_gen;
            pthread_cond_signal(&g_predec_cv);
            pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_predec_mtx);
        }
    }
}

uint32_t hd_sprites_get_room_gen(void) {
    return g_spr_room_gen;
}

static int sprite_allowed_in_active_room(const char *rel_name) {
    if (!rel_name) return 0;
    /* Global UI/cursors/inventory (g/) and Moorhuhn character animations (s/) are allowed in EVERY room! */
    if ((rel_name[0] == 'g' || rel_name[0] == 's') && rel_name[1] == '/') return 1;
    /* Split-background outdoor room layers (l/011, l/014, l/022, l/023, l/026, l/027, l/028, l/036)
       have no single 800x450 objBg surface, so they must ALWAYS be matchable across rooms — even when
       entering directly from another room whose prefix is still locked. */
    if (strstr(rel_name, "/objBgO") != NULL ||
        strstr(rel_name, "/objBgU") != NULL ||
        strstr(rel_name, "/mskBg.") != NULL ||
        strstr(rel_name, "/objWolken") != NULL) {
        return 1;
    }
    /* Room-specific sprites belong to the active room prefix (e.g. "l/006/", "x/001/") */
    if (g_spr_room_prefix_len > 0) {
        if (strncmp(rel_name, g_spr_room_prefix, g_spr_room_prefix_len) == 0) {
            return 1;
        }
        /* Starting rooms l/001, l/002, l/003, l/004 all share l/001/img/xobjBg.png! */
        if (strcmp(g_spr_room_prefix, "l/001/") == 0 &&
            (strncmp(rel_name, "l/002/", 6) == 0 ||
             strncmp(rel_name, "l/003/", 6) == 0 ||
             strncmp(rel_name, "l/004/", 6) == 0)) {
            return 1;
        }
        return 0;
    }
    return 0;
}

/* Fast hash lookup for sprite disk cache during startup */
static HdSprCacheDiskEntry *g_spr_dcache = NULL;
static int g_spr_dcache_count = 0;
static int *g_spr_dcache_head = NULL;
static int *g_spr_dcache_next = NULL;
static HdSprCacheDiskEntry *g_spr_ncache = NULL;
static int g_spr_ncache_count = 0;
static int g_spr_cache_dirty = 0;

static uint32_t hash_str_u32(const char *s) {
    uint32_t h = 2166136261u;
    while (*s) {
        h ^= (uint8_t)(*s++);
        h *= 16777619u;
    }
    return h;
}

static uint32_t hash_dims_u32(int w, int h) {
    uint32_t k = ((uint32_t)w * 2654435761u) ^ ((uint32_t)h * 40503u);
    return k & (HD_SPR_BUCKETS - 1);
}

static void compute_rgba_sprite_thumb(const uint8_t *rgba, int w, int h, uint8_t out[HD_SPR_TBYTES]) {
    /* Detect whether the sprite has an opaque tbl1 core (alpha >= 245).
       If yes, measure coverage & RGB on the opaque core so DirectDraw tbl1-only surfaces
       and composited tbl1+tbl2 4K HD PNGs produce identical thumbnails!
       If no (pure semi-transparent tbl2-only sprite like ghosts/glows), fall back to alpha >= 48. */
    int has_core = 0;
    int sy_step = h > 64 ? (h / 32) : 1;
    int sx_step = w > 64 ? (w / 32) : 1;
    for (int y = 0; y < h && !has_core; y += sy_step) {
        const uint8_t *row = rgba + (size_t)y * (size_t)w * 4u;
        for (int x = 0; x < w; x += sx_step) {
            if (row[(size_t)x * 4u + 3u] >= 245u) {
                has_core = 1;
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    /* Use 160u core threshold (instead of 245u) so 1px 4K Gaussian-AA silhouette rims on
       non-tbl2 props (invAbacus, invToteFledermaus, objVase, xobjKerze, objPortrait) stay
       matched to their 1-bit 1x originals while still rejecting low-alpha tbl2 edge halos. */
    uint8_t a_thr = has_core ? 160u : 48u;

    for (int ty = 0; ty < HD_SPR_TH; ty++) {
        int y0 = (ty * h) / HD_SPR_TH;
        int y1 = ((ty + 1) * h) / HD_SPR_TH;
        if (y1 <= y0) y1 = y0 + 1;
        int ystep = (y1 - y0) > 32 ? ((y1 - y0) / 16) : 1;
        for (int tx = 0; tx < HD_SPR_TW; tx++) {
            int x0 = (tx * w) / HD_SPR_TW;
            int x1 = ((tx + 1) * w) / HD_SPR_TW;
            if (x1 <= x0) x1 = x0 + 1;
            int xstep = (x1 - x0) > 32 ? ((x1 - x0) / 16) : 1;
            int sr = 0, sg = 0, sb = 0, ocnt = 0, acnt = 0, tcnt = 0;
            for (int y = y0; y < y1; y += ystep) {
                int cy = (y < h) ? y : (h - 1);
                const uint8_t *row = rgba + (size_t)cy * (size_t)w * 4u;
                for (int x = x0; x < x1; x += xstep) {
                    int cx = (x < w) ? x : (w - 1);
                    const uint8_t *p = row + (size_t)cx * 4u;
                    tcnt++;
                    if (p[3] >= a_thr) {
                        acnt++;
                        sr += p[0];
                        sg += p[1];
                        sb += p[2];
                        ocnt++;
                    }
                }
            }
            int idx = (ty * HD_SPR_TW + tx) * 4;
            if (ocnt > 0) {
                out[idx + 0] = (uint8_t)(sr / ocnt);
                out[idx + 1] = (uint8_t)(sg / ocnt);
                out[idx + 2] = (uint8_t)(sb / ocnt);
            } else {
                out[idx + 0] = 0;
                out[idx + 1] = 0;
                out[idx + 2] = 0;
            }
            out[idx + 3] = (uint8_t)((tcnt > 0) ? ((acnt * 255) / tcnt) : 0);
        }
    }
}

static void load_sprite_disk_cache(const char *hd_dir) {
    char path[1100];
    snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "%s/.hd_sprite_index.cache", hd_dir);
    FILE *f = fopen(path, "rb");
    if (!f) return;
    uint32_t magic = 0, count = 0;
    if (fread(&magic, 4, 1, f) == 1 && fread(&count, 4, 1, f) == 1 &&
        magic == HD_SPR_CACHE_MAGIC && count > 0 && count <= MAX_HD_SPRITES) {
        g_spr_dcache = (HdSprCacheDiskEntry *)malloc((size_t)count * sizeof(HdSprCacheDiskEntry));
        g_spr_dcache_head = (int *)malloc(4096 * sizeof(int));
        g_spr_dcache_next = (int *)malloc((size_t)count * sizeof(int));
        if (g_spr_dcache && g_spr_dcache_head && g_spr_dcache_next &&
            fread(g_spr_dcache, sizeof(HdSprCacheDiskEntry), count, f) == count) {
            g_spr_dcache_count = (int)count;
            for (int i = 0; i < 4096; i++) g_spr_dcache_head[i] = -1;
            for (int i = 0; i < g_spr_dcache_count; i++) {
                uint32_t b = hash_str_u32(g_spr_dcache[i].rel_name) & 4095u;
                g_spr_dcache_next[i] = g_spr_dcache_head[b];
                g_spr_dcache_head[b] = i;
            }
        } else {
            free(g_spr_dcache); g_spr_dcache = NULL;
            free(g_spr_dcache_head); g_spr_dcache_head = NULL;
            free(g_spr_dcache_next); g_spr_dcache_next = NULL;
        }
    }
    fclose(f);
}

static const HdSprCacheDiskEntry *find_sprite_in_dcache(const char *rel, int64_t mtime, int64_t fsize) {
    if (!g_spr_dcache || !g_spr_dcache_head) return NULL;
    uint32_t b = hash_str_u32(rel) & 4095u;
    for (int i = g_spr_dcache_head[b]; i >= 0; i = g_spr_dcache_next[i]) {
        if (g_spr_dcache[i].mtime == mtime && g_spr_dcache[i].fsize == fsize &&
            strcmp(g_spr_dcache[i].rel_name, rel) == 0) {
            return &g_spr_dcache[i];
        }
    }
    return NULL;
}

static void save_sprite_disk_cache(const char *hd_dir) {
    if (!g_spr_ncache || g_spr_ncache_count <= 0) return;
    char path[1100];
    snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "%s/.hd_sprite_index.cache", hd_dir);
    FILE *f = fopen(path, "wb");
    if (!f) return;
    uint32_t magic = HD_SPR_CACHE_MAGIC;
    uint32_t count = (uint32_t)g_spr_ncache_count;
    fwrite(&magic, 4, 1, f);
    fwrite(&count, 4, 1, f);
    fwrite(g_spr_ncache, sizeof(HdSprCacheDiskEntry), count, f);
    fclose(f);
}

static void scan_sprites_recursive(const char *root_dir, const char *rel_prefix) {
    char cur_dir[1024];
    if (rel_prefix && rel_prefix[0]) {
        snprintf(cur_dir, sizeof(cur_dir), "%s/%s", root_dir, rel_prefix);
    } else {
        snprintf(cur_dir, sizeof(cur_dir), "%s", root_dir);
    }

    DIR *d = opendir(cur_dir);
    if (!d) return;

    struct dirent *ent;
    while ((ent = readdir(d)) != NULL && g_sprite_count < MAX_HD_SPRITES) {
        if (ent->d_name[0] == '.') continue;
        char full[512], rel[256];
        int fn = snprintf(full, sizeof(full), "%s/%s", cur_dir, ent->d_name);
        if (fn < 0 || fn >= (int)sizeof(full)) continue;
        int rn;
        if (rel_prefix && rel_prefix[0]) {
            rn = snprintf(rel, sizeof(rel), "%s/%s", rel_prefix, ent->d_name);
        } else {
            rn = snprintf(rel, sizeof(rel), "%s", ent->d_name);
        }
        if (rn < 0 || rn >= (int)sizeof(rel)) continue;

        struct stat st;
        if (stat(full, &st) != 0) continue;
        if (S_ISDIR(st.st_mode)) {
            scan_sprites_recursive(root_dir, rel);
        } else if (S_ISREG(st.st_mode) && has_image_ext(ent->d_name)) {
            /* Skip optional 16:9 widescreen wing extensions (*_wide.webp / *_wide.png) from 4:3 matching */
            if (strstr(ent->d_name, "_wide.") != NULL) continue;
            int64_t mtime = (int64_t)st.st_mtime;
            int64_t fsize = (int64_t)st.st_size;
            int nw = 0, nh = 0;
            uint8_t tbuf[HD_SPR_TBYTES];
            const HdSprCacheDiskEntry *ce = find_sprite_in_dcache(rel, mtime, fsize);
            if (ce) {
                nw = ce->hd_w;
                nh = ce->hd_h;
                memcpy(tbuf, ce->thumb, HD_SPR_TBYTES);
            } else {
                uint8_t *rgba = decode_image_ffmpeg(full, 0, 0, AV_PIX_FMT_RGBA, &nw, &nh);
                if (!rgba || nw < 16 || nh < 16) {
                    free(rgba);
                    continue;
                }
                compute_rgba_sprite_thumb(rgba, nw, nh, tbuf);
                free(rgba);
                g_spr_cache_dirty = 1;
            }

            if (g_spr_ncache && g_spr_ncache_count < MAX_HD_SPRITES) {
                HdSprCacheDiskEntry *ne = &g_spr_ncache[g_spr_ncache_count++];
                memset(ne, 0, sizeof(*ne));
                snprintf(ne->rel_name, sizeof(ne->rel_name), "%s", rel);
                ne->mtime = mtime;
                ne->fsize = fsize;
                ne->hd_w = nw;
                ne->hd_h = nh;
                memcpy(ne->thumb, tbuf, HD_SPR_TBYTES);
            }

            int idx = g_sprite_count++;
            HdSpriteEntry *e = &g_sprites[idx];
            snprintf(e->full_path, sizeof(e->full_path), "%s", full);
            snprintf(e->rel_name, sizeof(e->rel_name), "%s", rel);
            e->hd_w = nw;
            e->hd_h = nh;
            memcpy(e->thumb, tbuf, HD_SPR_TBYTES);
            uint32_t b = hash_dims_u32(nw, nh);
            e->next_in_bucket = g_spr_buckets[b];
            g_spr_buckets[b] = idx;
        }
    }
    closedir(d);
}

static void hd_sprites_init(const char *hd_dir) {
    char spr_dir[1024];
    snprintf(spr_dir, sizeof(spr_dir), "%s/sprites", hd_dir);
    struct stat st;
    if (stat(spr_dir, &st) != 0 || !S_ISDIR(st.st_mode)) return;

    g_sprites = (HdSpriteEntry *)malloc(MAX_HD_SPRITES * sizeof(HdSpriteEntry));
    if (!g_sprites) return;
    g_sprite_count = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < HD_SPR_BUCKETS; i++) g_spr_buckets[i] = -1;

    g_spr_dcache = NULL;
    g_spr_dcache_count = 0;
    g_spr_dcache_head = NULL;
    g_spr_dcache_next = NULL;
    g_spr_ncache = (HdSprCacheDiskEntry *)malloc(MAX_HD_SPRITES * sizeof(HdSprCacheDiskEntry));
    g_spr_ncache_count = 0;
    g_spr_cache_dirty = 0;

    load_sprite_disk_cache(hd_dir);
    scan_sprites_recursive(spr_dir, "");
    if (g_spr_cache_dirty || g_spr_ncache_count != g_spr_dcache_count) {
        save_sprite_disk_cache(hd_dir);
    }

    free(g_spr_dcache); g_spr_dcache = NULL; g_spr_dcache_count = 0;
    free(g_spr_dcache_head); g_spr_dcache_head = NULL;
    free(g_spr_dcache_next); g_spr_dcache_next = NULL;
    free(g_spr_ncache); g_spr_ncache = NULL; g_spr_ncache_count = 0;

    if (g_sprite_count > 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "[HD] Indexed %d HD RGBA sprite(s) from '%s'%s\n",
                g_sprite_count, spr_dir, g_spr_cache_dirty ? " (cache updated)" : " (cached)");
        for (int i = 0; i < PREDEC_SLOTS; i++) {
            g_predec[i].sprite_id = -1;
            g_predec[i].rgba = NULL;
        }
        g_predec_stop = 0;
        if (pthread_create(&g_predec_thr, NULL, predec_worker_main, NULL) == 0) {
            g_predec_running = 1;
        }
    }
}

static void hd_sprites_shutdown(void) {
    if (g_predec_running) {
        pthread_mutex_lock(&g_predec_mtx);
        g_predec_stop = 1;
        pthread_cond_signal(&g_predec_cv);
        pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_predec_mtx);
        pthread_join(g_predec_thr, NULL);
        g_predec_running = 0;
        pthread_mutex_lock(&g_predec_mtx);
        predec_clear_locked();
        pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_predec_mtx);
    }
    free(g_sprites);
    g_sprites = NULL;
    g_sprite_count = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < HD_SPR_BUCKETS; i++) g_spr_buckets[i] = -1;
}

int hd_sprites_count(void) {
    return g_sprite_count;
}

static int match_sprite_thumb_internal(const uint8_t thumb[HD_SPR_TBYTES], int sw, int sh) {
    int target_scales[2] = { 4, 2 };
    int is_font_strip  = (sw >= 200 && sh <= 40);
    int is_small_prop  = (sw <= 64 || sh <= 64);
    int max_mean_alpha = is_small_prop ? 28 : 18;
    int max_cell_da    = is_small_prop ? 256 : 200;
    int max_mean_rgb   = is_font_strip ? 40 : (is_small_prop ? 22 : 18);
    int max_cell_drgb  = is_font_strip ? 112 : (is_small_prop ? 160 : 96);

    int probe_opaque = 0;
    int probe_faint  = 0;
    for (int c = 0; c < HD_SPR_TW * HD_SPR_TH; c++) {
        uint8_t a0 = thumb[c * 4 + 3];
        if (a0 >= 48) probe_opaque++;
        if (a0 >= 12) probe_faint++;
    }
    if (probe_opaque == 0 && probe_faint == 0) return -1;
    const int min_cell_a = (probe_opaque <= 2) ? 12 : 48;
    const int min_shared = (probe_opaque <= 2) ? 1 : 2;

    int best_id = -1;
    int best_score = 1 << 30;
    for (int si = 0; si < 2; si++) {
        int tw = sw * target_scales[si];
        int th = sh * target_scales[si];
        for (int idx = g_spr_buckets[hash_dims_u32(tw, th)]; idx >= 0; idx = g_sprites[idx].next_in_bucket) {
            const HdSpriteEntry *e = &g_sprites[idx];
            if (e->hd_w != tw || e->hd_h != th) continue;
            if (!sprite_allowed_in_active_room(e->rel_name)) continue;
            int alpha_sad = 0;
            int rgb_sad = 0;
            int shared_cells = 0;
            int cand_opaque = 0;
            int max_da = 0;
            int max_drgb = 0;
            for (int c = 0; c < HD_SPR_TW * HD_SPR_TH; c++) {
                int o = c * 4;
                int a0 = thumb[o + 3];
                int a1 = e->thumb[o + 3];
                if (a1 >= 48) cand_opaque++;
                int da = a0 - a1;
                if (da < 0) da = -da;
                alpha_sad += da;
                if (da > max_da) max_da = da;
                if (a0 >= min_cell_a && a1 >= min_cell_a) {
                    int dr = (int)thumb[o + 0] - (int)e->thumb[o + 0];
                    int dg = (int)thumb[o + 1] - (int)e->thumb[o + 1];
                    int db = (int)thumb[o + 2] - (int)e->thumb[o + 2];
                    int cdiff = ((dr < 0 ? -dr : dr) + (dg < 0 ? -dg : dg) + (db < 0 ? -db : db)) / 3;
                    rgb_sad += cdiff;
                    if (cdiff > max_drgb) max_drgb = cdiff;
                    shared_cells++;
                }
            }
            int mean_alpha = alpha_sad / (HD_SPR_TW * HD_SPR_TH);
            if (mean_alpha >= max_mean_alpha || max_da >= max_cell_da) continue;
            if (shared_cells < min_shared) continue;
            if (probe_opaque <= 2 && cand_opaque > 3) continue;
            int mean_rgb = rgb_sad / shared_cells;
            if (mean_rgb >= max_mean_rgb || max_drgb >= max_cell_drgb) continue;
            /* Un-truncated fixed-point SAD + peak-cell penalty so nearly-identical puzzle sprites
               (e.g. Safe rotary wheel digits 0-9, dice, cards, locks) never tie or mismatch! */
            int score = (rgb_sad * 256) / shared_cells +
                        (alpha_sad * 128) / (HD_SPR_TW * HD_SPR_TH) +
                        max_drgb * 4;
            if (score < best_score) {
                best_score = score;
                best_id = idx;
            }
        }
        if (best_id >= 0) break; /* prefer 4x over 2x if found */
    }
    return best_id;
}

static int has_candidate_for_dims(int sw, int sh) {
    int target_scales[2] = { 4, 2 };
    for (int si = 0; si < 2; si++) {
        int tw = sw * target_scales[si];
        int th = sh * target_scales[si];
        for (int idx = g_spr_buckets[hash_dims_u32(tw, th)]; idx >= 0; idx = g_sprites[idx].next_in_bucket) {
            if (g_sprites[idx].hd_w == tw && g_sprites[idx].hd_h == th &&
                sprite_allowed_in_active_room(g_sprites[idx].rel_name)) {
                return 1;
            }
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

int hd_sprites_find_strip_cell(int surf_w, int surf_h, int *out_cw, int *out_ch, int max_out) {
    if (g_sprite_count <= 0 || !g_sprites || surf_w < 16 || surf_h < 16 || !out_cw || !out_ch || max_out <= 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    int found = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < g_sprite_count; i++) {
        const HdSpriteEntry *e = &g_sprites[i];
        if (!sprite_allowed_in_active_room(e->rel_name)) continue;
        for (int scale = 4; scale >= 2; scale -= 2) {
            if ((e->hd_w % scale) != 0 || (e->hd_h % scale) != 0) continue;
            int cw = e->hd_w / scale;
            int ch = e->hd_h / scale;
            if (cw < 16 || ch < 16) continue;
            if ((cw < surf_w || ch < surf_h) && (surf_w % cw) == 0 && (surf_h % ch) == 0) {
                int dup = 0;
                for (int k = 0; k < found; k++) {
                    if (out_cw[k] == cw && out_ch[k] == ch) {
                        dup = 1;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if (!dup) {
                    out_cw[found] = cw;
                    out_ch[found] = ch;
                    found++;
                    if (found >= max_out) return found;
                }
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    return found;
}

int hd_sprites_match_rgba(const uint8_t *rgba, int w, int h) {
    if (g_sprite_count <= 0 || !g_sprites || !rgba || w < 16 || h < 16) return -1;
    if (!has_candidate_for_dims(w, h)) return -1;
    uint8_t thumb[HD_SPR_TBYTES];
    compute_rgba_sprite_thumb(rgba, w, h, thumb);
    return match_sprite_thumb_internal(thumb, w, h);
}

int hd_sprites_match_rect(const void *pixels, int surf_w, int surf_h, int pitch, int bpp,
                          int sx0, int sy0, int sw, int sh,
                          int use_ck, uint32_t ck_lo, uint32_t ck_hi) {
    if (g_sprite_count <= 0 || !g_sprites || !pixels) return -1;
    if (sw < 16 || sh < 16 || sx0 < 0 || sy0 < 0 || sx0 + sw > surf_w || sy0 + sh > surf_h) return -1;
    if (bpp != 16 && bpp != 32) return -1;
    if (!has_candidate_for_dims(sw, sh)) return -1;

    /* Compute 8x8 color-key-aware RGBA thumbnail of the source rect */
    uint8_t thumb[HD_SPR_TBYTES];
    const uint8_t *base = (const uint8_t *)pixels;
    int total_opaque = 0;
    for (int ty = 0; ty < HD_SPR_TH; ty++) {
        int y0 = sy0 + (ty * sh) / HD_SPR_TH;
        int y1 = sy0 + ((ty + 1) * sh) / HD_SPR_TH;
        if (y1 <= y0) y1 = y0 + 1;
        int ystep = (y1 - y0) > 32 ? ((y1 - y0) / 16) : 1;
        for (int tx = 0; tx < HD_SPR_TW; tx++) {
            int x0 = sx0 + (tx * sw) / HD_SPR_TW;
            int x1 = sx0 + ((tx + 1) * sw) / HD_SPR_TW;
            if (x1 <= x0) x1 = x0 + 1;
            int xstep = (x1 - x0) > 32 ? ((x1 - x0) / 16) : 1;
            int sr = 0, sg = 0, sb = 0, ocnt = 0, tcnt = 0;
            for (int y = y0; y < y1; y += ystep) {
                const uint8_t *row = base + y * pitch;
                for (int x = x0; x < x1; x += xstep) {
                    tcnt++;
                    if (bpp == 32) {
                        uint32_t px;
                        memcpy(&px, row + x * 4, 4);
                        uint32_t rgb = px & 0xFFFFFFu;
                        if (use_ck && rgb >= (ck_lo & 0xFFFFFFu) && rgb <= (ck_hi & 0xFFFFFFu)) continue;
                        sr += (int)((rgb >> 16) & 0xFFu);
                        sg += (int)((rgb >> 8) & 0xFFu);
                        sb += (int)(rgb & 0xFFu);
                        ocnt++;
                    } else {
                        uint16_t px;
                        memcpy(&px, row + x * 2, 2);
                        if (use_ck && px >= ck_lo && px <= ck_hi) continue;
                        sr += (int)(((px >> 11) & 0x1Fu) * 255u / 31u);
                        sg += (int)(((px >> 5) & 0x3Fu) * 255u / 63u);
                        sb += (int)((px & 0x1Fu) * 255u / 31u);
                        ocnt++;
                    }
                }
            }
            int tidx = (ty * HD_SPR_TW + tx) * 4;
            if (ocnt > 0) {
                thumb[tidx + 0] = (uint8_t)(sr / ocnt);
                thumb[tidx + 1] = (uint8_t)(sg / ocnt);
                thumb[tidx + 2] = (uint8_t)(sb / ocnt);
                total_opaque += ocnt;
            } else {
                thumb[tidx + 0] = 0;
                thumb[tidx + 1] = 0;
                thumb[tidx + 2] = 0;
            }
            thumb[tidx + 3] = (uint8_t)((tcnt > 0) ? ((ocnt * 255) / tcnt) : 0);
        }
    }
    if (total_opaque == 0) return -1;
    return match_sprite_thumb_internal(thumb, sw, sh);
}

int hd_sprites_decode_rgba(int sprite_id, HdImageRGBA *out) {
    if (!out || !g_sprites || sprite_id < 0 || sprite_id >= g_sprite_count) return -1;
    if (g_predec_running) {
        pthread_mutex_lock(&g_predec_mtx);
        for (int i = 0; i < PREDEC_SLOTS; i++) {
            if (g_predec[i].sprite_id == sprite_id && g_predec[i].rgba) {
                out->rgba = g_predec[i].rgba;
                out->width = g_predec[i].w;
                out->height = g_predec[i].h;
                g_predec[i].rgba = NULL;
                g_predec[i].sprite_id = -1;
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_predec_mtx);
                return 0;
            }
        }
        pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_predec_mtx);
    }
    int nw = 0, nh = 0;
    uint8_t *rgba = decode_image_ffmpeg(g_sprites[sprite_id].full_path, 0, 0, AV_PIX_FMT_RGBA, &nw, &nh);
    if (!rgba) return -1;
    out->rgba = rgba;
    out->width = nw;
    out->height = nh;
    return 0;
}

void hd_sprites_free_rgba(HdImageRGBA *img) {
    if (!img) return;
    free(img->rgba);
    img->rgba = NULL;
    img->width = 0;
    img->height = 0;
}

const char *hd_sprites_get_name(int sprite_id) {
    if (!g_sprites || sprite_id < 0 || sprite_id >= g_sprite_count) return "";
    return g_sprites[sprite_id].rel_name;
}

void hd_sprites_get_orig_dims(int sprite_id, int *out_w, int *out_h) {
    if (!g_sprites || sprite_id < 0 || sprite_id >= g_sprite_count) {
        if (out_w) *out_w = 0;
        if (out_h) *out_h = 0;
        return;
    }
    if (out_w) *out_w = g_sprites[sprite_id].hd_w;
    if (out_h) *out_h = g_sprites[sprite_id].hd_h;
}

```

`runtime/hd_assets.h`:

```h
#ifndef MHA2_HD_ASSETS_H
#define MHA2_HD_ASSETS_H

#include <stdint.h>

#define HD_THUMB_W 16
#define HD_THUMB_H 12
#define HD_THUMB_BYTES (HD_THUMB_W * HD_THUMB_H * 3)

typedef struct HdImageRGBA {
    uint8_t *rgba;
    int width;
    int height;
} HdImageRGBA;

/* Scan hd_dir recursively for .png/.jpg/.jpeg replacement assets.
 * Returns number of assets indexed. */
int hd_assets_init(const char *hd_dir);
void hd_assets_shutdown(void);
int hd_assets_count(void);

/* Match an 800x600 (or full-frame) guest surface against indexed HD assets.
 * Returns asset index >= 0 on match, or -1 if no match. */
int hd_assets_match_pixels(const void *pixels, int w, int h, int pitch, int bpp);

/* Load full-resolution RGBA pixels for asset index (cached LRU in memory).
 * Returns pointer valid until next hd_assets_get_rgba/shutdown call, or NULL. */
const HdImageRGBA *hd_assets_get_rgba(int asset_id);

/* Return relative asset name (e.g. "x/001/img/objBg.png") for HUD/toast display. */
const char *hd_assets_get_name(int asset_id);

/* Decode optional 16:9 widescreen extended sibling (*_wide.webp / *_wide.png) for 4:3 menus.
 * Returns 0 on success (caller frees via hd_sprites_free_rgba), or -1 if none exists. */
int hd_assets_decode_wide_rgba(int asset_id, HdImageRGBA *out);

/* ---- Native HD Foreground Sprites, Characters, Inventory & Cursors (hd_assets/sprites/) ---- */
int hd_sprites_count(void);
void hd_sprites_set_room_prefix(const char *bg_rel_name);
uint32_t hd_sprites_get_room_gen(void);

/* Match a color-keyed sprite frame rect inside a guest DirectDraw surface against indexed HD sprites.
 * Returns sprite index >= 0 on match, or -1 if no match. */
int hd_sprites_match_rect(const void *pixels, int surf_w, int surf_h, int pitch, int bpp,
                          int sx0, int sy0, int sw, int sh,
                          int use_ck, uint32_t ck_lo, uint32_t ck_hi);

/* Match a decoded 1x RGBA buffer (e.g. from the engine's software IT3 RLE blitter) against indexed HD sprites. */
int hd_sprites_match_rgba(const uint8_t *rgba, int w, int h);

/* Decode full-resolution RGBA pixels for a sprite index (returns newly allocated buffer; caller frees
 * via hd_sprites_free_rgba, or NULL on error). */
int hd_sprites_decode_rgba(int sprite_id, HdImageRGBA *out);
void hd_sprites_free_rgba(HdImageRGBA *img);
const char *hd_sprites_get_name(int sprite_id);
void hd_sprites_get_orig_dims(int sprite_id, int *out_w, int *out_h);

/* Discover distinct candidate frame cell sizes (cw, ch) for a multi-frame strip surface
 * from active-room indexed sprites (returns count stored in out_cw/out_ch, <= max_out). */
int hd_sprites_find_strip_cell(int surf_w, int surf_h, int *out_cw, int *out_ch, int max_out);

#endif /* MHA2_HD_ASSETS_H */

```

`runtime/main.c`:

```c
#ifndef _GNU_SOURCE
#define _GNU_SOURCE
#endif
#include "runtime.h"
#include "gl_renderer.h"
#include "hd_assets.h"
#include "imports.h"
#include "funcs.h"
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sched.h>

static cpu_set_t g_orig_cpu_mask;
static int g_has_orig_cpu_mask = 0;

void runtime_unpin_current_thread(void) {
    if (g_has_orig_cpu_mask) {
        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(g_orig_cpu_mask), &g_orig_cpu_mask);
    }
}

static int file_exists(const char *p) {
    struct stat st;
    return stat(p, &st) == 0;
}

static void print_help(const char *prog) {
    printf("Moorhuhn Adventure 2: Der Fluch des Goldes — Native Linux Remastered Engine\n");
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n\n", prog);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  --game-dir <dir>         Directory containing mha2.dat / mha2.exe (default: game_files)\n");
    printf("  -f, --fullscreen         Start in borderless fullscreen\n");
    printf("  --width <W> --height <H> Initial window size (default: 1280x800)\n");
    printf("  --aspect <mode>          Widescreen mode: auto (default), 16:10, 16:9, 4:3\n");
    printf("  --no-ambient-bars        Use pure black sidebars on 4:3 menus instead of ambient blur\n");
    printf("  --shader <name>          Upscaler: bicubic (default), lanczos, edge, bilinear, nearest, crt\n");
    printf("  --vsync <mode>           Frame pacing: adaptive (default), on, off\n");
    printf("  --fps <N>                FPS cap when --vsync off (default: 240)\n");
    printf("  --16bit                  Use original 16-bit RGB565 instead of 32-bit True Color\n");
    printf("  --hd-dir <dir>           Directory containing HD PNG replacements (default: hd_assets)\n");
    printf("  --no-hd-assets           Disable HD PNG background override\n");
    printf("  --no-hd-sprites          Disable HD RGBA foreground sprite override\n");
    printf("  --no-hd-text             Disable native monitor-DPI vector text rendering\n");
    printf("  --no-hw-cursor           Disable high-refresh decoupled cursor rendering\n");
    printf("  --no-bloom               Disable emissive bloom post-processing\n");
    printf("  --grade <mode>           Color grading: original, remastered (default), warm, daylight\n");
    printf("  --no-transitions         Disable smooth room crossfade transitions\n");
    printf("  --hud                    Show live performance/upscaler HUD on startup\n");
    printf("  --debug                  Verbose Win32/DirectX logging\n\n");
    printf("In-Game Hotkeys:\n");
    printf("  F1                       Open/Close Interactive Graphics & Settings Menu\n");
    printf("  F2                       Toggle Split-Screen Before / After Comparison\n");
    printf("  F3                       Toggle live stats HUD\n");
    printf("  F4                       Cycle VSync / high-refresh-rate mode\n");
    printf("  F5                       Toggle Native HD Vector Text & Subtitles\n");
    printf("  F6                       Toggle HD Asset Pack Override\n");
    printf("  F7 / F8 / F9             Master volume down / up / mute\n");
    printf("  F10                      Toggle High-Refresh Decoupled Cursor\n");
    printf("  F11 or Alt+Enter         Toggle borderless fullscreen\n");
    printf("  F12                      Cycle Widescreen Aspect (Auto / 16:10 / 16:9 / 4:3)\n");
    printf("  Ctrl+L                   Open/Close Dialog & Clue History Log\n");
}

int main(int argc, char **argv) {
    /* Save original multi-core affinity for native worker threads (HD pre-decoder & audio),
       then pin guest threads to Core 0: original 2005 Win98/XP game uses non-atomic
       refcounts across main / loading / processing threads (SingleProcAffinity). */
    CPU_ZERO(&g_orig_cpu_mask);
    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(g_orig_cpu_mask), &g_orig_cpu_mask) == 0) {
        g_has_orig_cpu_mask = 1;
    }
    cpu_set_t mask;
    CPU_ZERO(&mask);
    CPU_SET(0, &mask);
    sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask);

    /* Load persistent user settings before applying CLI overrides */
    gl_config_load("mha2_settings.ini");

    const char *exe_override = NULL;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "--debug") == 0) {
            g_debug_log = 1;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--game-dir") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            snprintf(g_game_dir, sizeof(g_game_dir), "%s", argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--exe") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            exe_override = argv[++i];
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-f") == 0 || strcmp(argv[i], "--fullscreen") == 0) {
            g_cfg.start_fullscreen = 1;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--windowed") == 0) {
            g_cfg.start_fullscreen = 0;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--width") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            g_cfg.init_win_w = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--height") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            g_cfg.init_win_h = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--16bit") == 0) {
            g_cfg.truecolor_32bit = 0;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--hd-dir") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            snprintf(g_cfg.hd_assets_dir, sizeof(g_cfg.hd_assets_dir), "%s", argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--no-hd-assets") == 0) {
            g_cfg.hd_assets_enabled = 0;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--no-hd-sprites") == 0) {
            g_cfg.hd_sprites_enabled = 0;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--no-hd-text") == 0) {
            g_cfg.hd_vector_text = 0;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--no-hw-cursor") == 0) {
            g_cfg.decoupled_cursor = 0;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--no-ambient-bars") == 0) {
            g_cfg.ambient_bars = 0;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--no-bloom") == 0) {
            g_cfg.bloom_level = BLOOM_OFF;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--no-transitions") == 0) {
            g_cfg.scene_transitions = 0;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--grade") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            const char *g = argv[++i];
            if (strcmp(g, "original") == 0) g_cfg.color_grade = GRADE_ORIGINAL;
            else if (strcmp(g, "remastered") == 0) g_cfg.color_grade = GRADE_REMASTERED;
            else if (strcmp(g, "warm") == 0) g_cfg.color_grade = GRADE_WARM;
            else if (strcmp(g, "daylight") == 0) g_cfg.color_grade = GRADE_DAYLIGHT;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--aspect") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            const char *a = argv[++i];
            if (strcmp(a, "auto") == 0) g_cfg.aspect_mode = ASPECT_AUTO;
            else if (strcmp(a, "16:10") == 0 || strcmp(a, "1610") == 0) g_cfg.aspect_mode = ASPECT_16_10;
            else if (strcmp(a, "16:9") == 0 || strcmp(a, "169") == 0) g_cfg.aspect_mode = ASPECT_16_9;
            else if (strcmp(a, "4:3") == 0 || strcmp(a, "43") == 0) g_cfg.aspect_mode = ASPECT_4_3;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--hud") == 0) {
            g_cfg.show_hud = 1;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--fps") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            g_cfg.max_fps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--vsync") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            const char *m = argv[++i];
            if (strcmp(m, "adaptive") == 0) g_cfg.vsync_mode = VSYNC_ADAPTIVE;
            else if (strcmp(m, "on") == 0) g_cfg.vsync_mode = VSYNC_ON;
            else if (strcmp(m, "off") == 0) g_cfg.vsync_mode = VSYNC_UNCAPPED;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--shader") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            const char *s = argv[++i];
            if (strcmp(s, "bicubic") == 0) g_cfg.shader_mode = SHADER_BICUBIC_CAS;
            else if (strcmp(s, "lanczos") == 0) g_cfg.shader_mode = SHADER_LANCZOS_CAS;
            else if (strcmp(s, "edge") == 0) g_cfg.shader_mode = SHADER_EDGE_SMOOTH;
            else if (strcmp(s, "bilinear") == 0) g_cfg.shader_mode = SHADER_BILINEAR;
            else if (strcmp(s, "nearest") == 0) g_cfg.shader_mode = SHADER_NEAREST;
            else if (strcmp(s, "crt") == 0) g_cfg.shader_mode = SHADER_CRT;
            else if (strcmp(s, "fsr") == 0 || strcmp(s, "fsr1") == 0) g_cfg.shader_mode = SHADER_FSR1;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "--help") == 0 || strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            print_help(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        }
    }

    if (!file_exists(g_game_dir)) {
        if (file_exists("mha2.dat")) {
            snprintf(g_game_dir, sizeof(g_game_dir), ".");
        }
    }

    char exe_path[1280];
    if (exe_override) {
        snprintf(exe_path, sizeof(exe_path), "%s", exe_override);
    } else {
        snprintf(exe_path, sizeof(exe_path), "%s/mha2.exe", g_game_dir);
    }

    if (SDL_Init(SDL_INIT_VIDEO | SDL_INIT_AUDIO | SDL_INIT_TIMER | SDL_INIT_GAMECONTROLLER) != 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: SDL_Init failed: %s\n", SDL_GetError());
        return 1;
    }

    runtime_mem_init();
    if (runtime_load_pe(exe_path) != 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "ERROR: failed to load PE data from '%s'\n", exe_path);
        return 1;
    }

    init_code_table();
    game_patches_install();
    ddraw_install_rle_hooks();
    hd_assets_init(g_cfg.hd_assets_dir);
    win32_kernel_init();
    win32_user_gdi_init();
    win32_dsound_init();

    CpuCtx main_ctx;
    memset(&main_ctx, 0, sizeof(main_ctx));
    main_ctx.thread_id = guest_alloc_thread_slot(&main_ctx.teb_addr, &main_ctx.esp);
    main_ctx.fpu_cw = 0x037Fu;

    fprintf(stderr, "[MHA2] Launching Remastered v5 Engine (%s, %s, %s, HD-Bg:%d, HD-Sprites:%d, game_dir='%s')...\n",
            g_cfg.truecolor_32bit ? "32-bit TrueColor" : "16-bit RGB565",
            gl_shader_name(g_cfg.shader_mode),
            gl_vsync_name(g_cfg.vsync_mode),
            hd_assets_count(),
            hd_sprites_count(),
            g_game_dir);

    push32(&main_ctx, HLE_RET_SENTINEL);
    dispatch_call(&main_ctx, MHA2_ENTRY_VA);

    gl_config_save("mha2_settings.ini");
    win32_dsound_shutdown();
    gl_renderer_shutdown();
    hd_assets_shutdown();
    SDL_Quit();
    return 0;
}

```

`runtime/runtime.h`:

```h
#ifndef MHA2_RUNTIME_H
#define MHA2_RUNTIME_H

#include "cpu.h"
#include "gl_renderer.h"
#include <SDL2/SDL.h>
#include <SDL2/SDL_ttf.h>

/* COM Interface IDs for synthetic vtables (0xFE000000 | (iface << 12) | slot) */
enum ComIfaceId {
    COM_IFACE_DDRAW         = 1,
    COM_IFACE_DDRAW_SURFACE = 2,
    COM_IFACE_DDRAW_CLIPPER = 3,
    COM_IFACE_DINPUT        = 4,
    COM_IFACE_DINPUT_DEV    = 5,
    COM_IFACE_DSOUND        = 6,
    COM_IFACE_DSOUND_BUF    = 7,
    COM_IFACE_DSHOW_GRAPH   = 8,
    COM_IFACE_DSHOW_CTRL    = 9,
    COM_IFACE_DSHOW_EVENT   = 10,
    COM_IFACE_DSHOW_VIDWIN  = 11,
    COM_IFACE_DSHOW_BASVID  = 12,
};

/* Global game directory (e.g. "game_files") */
extern char g_game_dir[1024];
extern volatile int g_quit_requested;
extern int g_debug_log;

/* Memory & Guest Allocator (cpu.c) */
void runtime_mem_init(void);
int  runtime_load_pe(const char *exe_path);
void init_code_table(void);

uint32_t guest_alloc(uint32_t size);

uint32_t guest_alloc_size(uint32_t addr);

uint32_t com_create_obj(uint32_t iface_id, uint32_t num_slots, uint32_t handle_val);

/* User32 / GDI32 / SDL2 Window (win32_user_gdi.c) */

/* DirectDraw (win32_ddraw.c) */
typedef struct DDrawSurface {
    int in_use;
    int refcount;
    uint32_t obj_va;
    uint32_t width;
    uint32_t height;
    uint32_t bpp;
    uint32_t pitch;
    uint32_t caps;
    uint32_t pixels_va;
    uint32_t attached_handle; /* backbuffer handle for primary */
    int has_src_colorkey;
    uint32_t src_ck_low;
    uint32_t src_ck_high;
    int is_primary;
    int is_backbuffer;

    /* v3/v5 Remaster metadata */
    int hd_asset_id;          /* -2=unchecked, -1=none, >=0=HD background asset index */
    int known_cell_w;         /* locked uniform frame cell width for multi-frame IT3 strips */
    int known_cell_h;         /* locked uniform frame cell height for multi-frame IT3 strips */
    uint32_t cell_probe_room_gen;
    uint32_t sprite_cache_room_gen;
    int sprite_cache_count;
    struct {
        int sx0, sy0, sw, sh;
        int sprite_id;        /* -1=none, >=0=matched HD sprite index */
    } sprite_cache[96];
    GlTextDraw text_draws[MAX_SURFACE_TEXT_DRAWS];
    int num_text_draws;

    /* Cursor sprite auto-detection */
    int is_cursor;
    int cursor_track_count;
    int last_origin_x;
    int last_origin_y;
    int last_sx0;
    int last_sy0;
    int last_mouse_x;
    int last_mouse_y;
    int cursor_hot_x;
    int cursor_hot_y;
    int cand_hot_x;
    int cand_hot_y;
    int cursor_full_w;
    int cursor_full_h;
} DDrawSurface;

DDrawSurface *ddraw_get_surface(uint32_t handle);

void ddraw_present_surface(DDrawSurface *surf);

void ddraw_install_rle_hooks(void);
void game_patches_install(void);

typedef struct GameHotspot {
    int x, y;          /* marker anchor point in 800x600 screen coords (inside clickable region) */
    int x0, y0, x1, y1;/* clipped bounding box in 800x600 screen coords */
    int kind;          /* 0 = Object/Item, 1 = Exit, 2 = Character/NPC */
    char label[128];   /* UTF-8 hover name from engine string table (may be empty) */
} GameHotspot;
int  game_collect_room_hotspots(GameHotspot *out, int max_out);
int  win32_ddraw_dispatch(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t import_id);
void com_ddraw_dispatch(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t iface_id, uint32_t slot);

/* DirectInput (win32_dinput.c) */

/* DirectSound (win32_dsound.c) */

void com_dsound_dispatch(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t iface_id, uint32_t slot);

/* AVI / DirectShow / VFW (win32_avi.c) */
int  win32_avi_is_playing(void);
int  win32_avi_dispatch(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t import_id);
uint32_t com_dshow_create_graph(void);
void com_dshow_dispatch(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t iface_id, uint32_t slot);

/* Helper to read stdcall args on guest stack:
 * On entry to win32/COM function: [esp+0] = retaddr, [esp+4] = arg0, [esp+8] = arg1, ... */
static inline uint32_t arg_u32(CpuCtx *c, int idx) {
    return mem_r32(c->esp + 4u + (uint32_t)idx * 4u);
}
static inline int32_t arg_i32(CpuCtx *c, int idx) {
    return (int32_t)arg_u32(c, idx);
}
static inline const char *arg_cstr(CpuCtx *c, int idx) {
    uint32_t a = arg_u32(c, idx);
    return a ? (const char *)(g_mem + a) : NULL;
}
static inline void stdcall_ret(CpuCtx *c, int nargs, uint32_t retval) {
    c->eax = retval;
    c->esp += 4u + (uint32_t)nargs * 4u;
}
static inline void cdecl_ret(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t retval) {
    c->eax = retval;
    c->esp += 4u;
}

#endif /* MHA2_RUNTIME_H */

```

`runtime/win32_avi.c`:

```c
#include "runtime.h"
#include "imports.h"
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <libavformat/avformat.h>
#include <libavcodec/avcodec.h>
#include <libswscale/swscale.h>
#include <libavutil/imgutils.h>

typedef struct FfVideo {
    int in_use;
    AVFormatContext *fmt;
    AVCodecContext  *dec;
    struct SwsContext *sws;
    int vstream;
    int width;
    int height;
    double fps;
    int64_t total_frames;
    int64_t cur_frame;
    AVFrame *frame;
    AVPacket *pkt;
    uint8_t *rgb_buf;
    int rgb_stride;
    /* For AVIStreamGetFrame guest DIB */
    uint32_t dib_va;
    /* For DirectShow playback */
    int running;
    int eof;
    uint32_t start_ticks;
    uint32_t paused_elapsed;
} FfVideo;

#define MAX_VIDEOS 16
static FfVideo g_vids[MAX_VIDEOS];

static void ff_close(FfVideo *v) {
    if (!v || !v->in_use) return;
    if (v->sws) { sws_freeContext(v->sws); v->sws = NULL; }
    if (v->frame) { av_frame_free(&v->frame); }
    if (v->pkt) { av_packet_free(&v->pkt); }
    if (v->dec) { avcodec_free_context(&v->dec); }
    if (v->fmt) { avformat_close_input(&v->fmt); }
    if (v->rgb_buf) { free(v->rgb_buf); v->rgb_buf = NULL; }
    if (v->dib_va) { guest_free(v->dib_va); v->dib_va = 0; }
    v->in_use = 0;
}

static int ff_try_hd_video(const char *orig_path, char *out_path, size_t out_sz) {
    if (!g_cfg.hd_assets_enabled || !g_cfg.hd_assets_dir[0] || !orig_path) return 0;
    const char *slash = strrchr(orig_path, '/');
    const char *base = slash ? slash + 1 : orig_path;
    char stem[256];
    snprintf(stem, sizeof(stem), "%.255s", base);
    char *dot = strrchr(stem, '.');
    if (dot) *dot = '\0';
    if (!stem[0]) return 0;

    static const char *exts[] = { "mp4", "mkv", "webm", "avi" };
    struct stat st;
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        int n = snprintf(out_path, out_sz, "%s/videos/%s.%s", g_cfg.hd_assets_dir, stem, exts[i]);
        if (n > 0 && (size_t)n < out_sz && stat(out_path, &st) == 0 && S_ISREG(st.st_mode)) {
            return 1;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

static int ff_open(FfVideo *v, const char *win_path) {
    char lpath[1024];
    char hd_path[1024];
    int used_hd = 0;
    resolve_game_path(win_path, lpath, sizeof(lpath), 0);
    if (ff_try_hd_video(lpath, hd_path, sizeof(hd_path))) {
        memcpy(lpath, hd_path, sizeof(lpath));
        used_hd = 1;
    }
    memset(v, 0, sizeof(*v));
    if (avformat_open_input(&v->fmt, lpath, NULL, NULL) < 0) {
        if (used_hd) {
            /* Fall back to original AVI if HD file failed to open */
            resolve_game_path(win_path, lpath, sizeof(lpath), 0);
            used_hd = 0;
            if (avformat_open_input(&v->fmt, lpath, NULL, NULL) < 0) {
                fprintf(stderr, "[AVI] avformat_open_input failed for '%s' -> '%s'\n", win_path, lpath);
                return -1;
            }
        } else {
            fprintf(stderr, "[AVI] avformat_open_input failed for '%s' -> '%s'\n", win_path, lpath);
            return -1;
        }
    }
    if (avformat_find_stream_info(v->fmt, NULL) < 0) {
        avformat_close_input(&v->fmt);
        return -1;
    }
    v->vstream = av_find_best_stream(v->fmt, AVMEDIA_TYPE_VIDEO, -1, -1, NULL, 0);
    if (v->vstream < 0) {
        avformat_close_input(&v->fmt);
        return -1;
    }
    AVStream *st = v->fmt->streams[v->vstream];
    const AVCodec *codec = avcodec_find_decoder(st->codecpar->codec_id);
    if (!codec) {
        avformat_close_input(&v->fmt);
        return -1;
    }
    v->dec = avcodec_alloc_context3(codec);
    avcodec_parameters_to_context(v->dec, st->codecpar);
    if (avcodec_open2(v->dec, codec, NULL) < 0) {
        avcodec_free_context(&v->dec);
        avformat_close_input(&v->fmt);
        return -1;
    }
    v->width = v->dec->width;
    v->height = v->dec->height;
    AVRational r = av_guess_frame_rate(v->fmt, st, NULL);
    v->fps = (r.num > 0 && r.den > 0) ? av_q2d(r) : 25.0;
    if (st->nb_frames > 0) v->total_frames = st->nb_frames;
    else if (v->fmt->duration > 0) v->total_frames = (int64_t)((double)v->fmt->duration / AV_TIME_BASE * v->fps);
    else v->total_frames = 1000;

    v->frame = av_frame_alloc();
    v->pkt = av_packet_alloc();
    v->rgb_stride = v->width * 3;
    v->rgb_buf = (uint8_t *)calloc(1, (size_t)v->rgb_stride * (size_t)v->height);
    v->sws = sws_getContext(v->width, v->height, v->dec->pix_fmt,
                            v->width, v->height, AV_PIX_FMT_RGB24,
                            SWS_LANCZOS, NULL, NULL, NULL);
    if (!v->frame || !v->pkt || !v->rgb_buf || !v->sws) {
        v->in_use = 1;
        ff_close(v);
        return -1;
    }
    v->in_use = 1;
    v->cur_frame = -1;
    fprintf(stderr, "[AVI] Opened %s'%s' (%dx%d @ %.2f fps, ~%lld frames)\n",
            used_hd ? "HD cutscene " : "",
            lpath, v->width, v->height, v->fps, (long long)v->total_frames);
    return 0;
}

static int ff_decode_next_frame(FfVideo *v) {
    if (!v || !v->in_use || v->eof) return 0;
    int flushed = 0;
    int consec_err = 0;
    while (1) {
        int r = avcodec_receive_frame(v->dec, v->frame);
        if (r == 0) {
            uint8_t *dst[4] = { v->rgb_buf, NULL, NULL, NULL };
            int dst_linesize[4] = { v->rgb_stride, 0, 0, 0 };
            sws_scale(v->sws, (const uint8_t *const *)v->frame->data, v->frame->linesize,
                      0, v->height, dst, dst_linesize);
            v->cur_frame++;
            return 1;
        }
        if (r == AVERROR_EOF || r != AVERROR(EAGAIN)) {
            v->eof = 1;
            return 0;
        }
        if (av_read_frame(v->fmt, v->pkt) < 0) {
            if (!flushed) {
                flushed = 1;
                int sr = avcodec_send_packet(v->dec, NULL);
                if (sr == 0 || sr == AVERROR(EAGAIN)) {
                    continue;
                }
            }
            v->eof = 1;
            return 0;
        }
        if (v->pkt->stream_index == v->vstream) {
            int sr = avcodec_send_packet(v->dec, v->pkt);
            if (sr < 0 && sr != AVERROR(EAGAIN)) {
                if (++consec_err > 64) {
                    av_packet_unref(v->pkt);
                    v->eof = 1;
                    return 0;
                }
            } else {
                consec_err = 0;
            }
        }
        av_packet_unref(v->pkt);
    }
}

static FfVideo *alloc_vid(uint32_t *out_idx) {
    for (uint32_t i = 1; i < MAX_VIDEOS; i++) {
        if (!g_vids[i].in_use) {
            if (out_idx) *out_idx = i;
            return &g_vids[i];
        }
    }
    return NULL;
}

static FfVideo *get_vid(uint32_t idx) {
    if (idx >= 1 && idx < MAX_VIDEOS && g_vids[idx].in_use) return &g_vids[idx];
    return NULL;
}

int win32_avi_is_playing(void) {
    for (uint32_t i = 1; i < MAX_VIDEOS; i++) {
        if (!g_vids[i].in_use) continue;
        if (g_vids[i].dib_va != 0) return 1;              /* Active AVIFile/DrawDib playback */
        if (g_vids[i].running && !g_vids[i].eof) return 1;/* Active DirectShow playback */
    }
    return 0;
}

/* ---- DirectShow COM ---- */
static uint32_t g_dshow_vid_idx = 0;

uint32_t com_dshow_create_graph(void) {
    uint32_t idx = 0;
    FfVideo *v = alloc_vid(&idx);
    if (!v) return 0;
    memset(v, 0, sizeof(*v));
    v->in_use = 1;
    g_dshow_vid_idx = idx;
    return com_create_obj(COM_IFACE_DSHOW_GRAPH, 24, idx);
}

static void dshow_step_playback(FfVideo *v) {
    if (!v || !v->running || v->eof || !v->fmt) return;
    win32_pump_sdl_events();
    uint32_t elapsed = SDL_GetTicks() - v->start_ticks;
    int64_t target_frame = (int64_t)((double)elapsed * v->fps / 1000.0);
    int decoded = 0;
    while (v->cur_frame < target_frame && !v->eof) {
        if (!ff_decode_next_frame(v)) break;
        decoded = 1;
    }
    if (decoded) {
        ddraw_present_rgb24(v->rgb_buf, v->width, v->height, v->rgb_stride);
    }
}

void com_dshow_dispatch(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t iface_id, uint32_t slot) {
    uint32_t this_va = arg_u32(c, 0);
    uint32_t vid_idx = com_obj_handle(this_va);
    FfVideo *v = get_vid(vid_idx);

    if (iface_id == COM_IFACE_DSHOW_GRAPH) {
        switch (slot) {
            case 0: { /* QueryInterface(this, riid, ppv) */
                uint32_t iid_va = arg_u32(c, 1);
                uint32_t ppv = arg_u32(c, 2);
                uint32_t d1 = iid_va ? mem_r32(iid_va) : 0;
                uint32_t obj = 0;
                if (d1 == 0x56a868b1u) obj = com_create_obj(COM_IFACE_DSHOW_CTRL, 20, vid_idx);
                else if (d1 == 0x56a868b6u) obj = com_create_obj(COM_IFACE_DSHOW_EVENT, 20, vid_idx);
                else if (d1 == 0x56a868b4u) obj = com_create_obj(COM_IFACE_DSHOW_VIDWIN, 48, vid_idx);
                else if (d1 == 0x56a868b5u) obj = com_create_obj(COM_IFACE_DSHOW_BASVID, 48, vid_idx);
                else if (d1 == 0x56a868a9u) obj = this_va;
                if (ppv) mem_w32(ppv, obj);
                stdcall_ret(c, 3, obj ? 0u : 0x80004002u); return;
            }
            case 1: stdcall_ret(c, 1, 2); return;
            case 2: /* Release */
                if (v) ff_close(v);
                if (this_va) guest_free(this_va);
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
            case 13: { /* RenderFile(this, lpcwstrFile, lpcwstrPlayList) -> +0x34 */
                uint32_t wpath_va = arg_u32(c, 1);
                char apath[512];
                int i = 0;
                while (wpath_va && i < 511) {
                    uint16_t ch = mem_r16(wpath_va + (uint32_t)i * 2u);
                    if (!ch) break;
                    apath[i++] = (char)(ch < 128 ? ch : '?');
                }
                apath[i] = '\0';
                if (v) {
                    ff_close(v);
                    if (ff_open(v, apath) == 0) {
                        stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0); return;
                    }
                    v->in_use = 1;
                }
                stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0x80040218u /* VFW_E_CANNOT_RENDER */); return;
            }
            case 15: /* SetLogFile(this, hFile) -> +0x3c */
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            default:
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
        }
    }

    if (iface_id == COM_IFACE_DSHOW_CTRL) {
        /* IDispatch (slots 0..6) + Run(7), Pause(8), Stop(9), GetState(10) */
        switch (slot) {
            case 0: { uint32_t ppv = arg_u32(c, 2); if (ppv) mem_w32(ppv, this_va); stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0); return; }
            case 1: stdcall_ret(c, 1, 2); return;
            case 2:
                if (this_va) guest_free(this_va);
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
            case 7: /* Run(this) */
                if (v) {
                    if (!v->running) {
                        v->start_ticks = SDL_GetTicks() - v->paused_elapsed;
                        v->running = 1;
                    }
                    dshow_step_playback(v);
                }
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
            case 8: /* Pause(this) */
                if (v && v->running) {
                    v->paused_elapsed = SDL_GetTicks() - v->start_ticks;
                    v->running = 0;
                }
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
            case 9: /* Stop(this) */
                if (v) { v->running = 0; v->eof = 1; v->paused_elapsed = 0; }
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
            case 10: { /* GetState(this, msTimeout, pfs) */
                uint32_t pfs = arg_u32(c, 2);
                if (v) dshow_step_playback(v);
                if (pfs) mem_w32(pfs, (v && v->running && !v->eof) ? 2u : 0u);
                stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0); return;
            }
            default:
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
        }
    }

    if (iface_id == COM_IFACE_DSHOW_EVENT) {
        /* IMediaEventEx: IDispatch(0..6) + GetEventHandle(7), GetEvent(8), WaitForCompletion(9),
           CancelDefaultHandling(10), RestoreDefaultHandling(11), FreeEventParams(12),
           SetNotifyWindow(13), SetNotifyFlags(14), GetNotifyFlags(15) */
        switch (slot) {
            case 0: { uint32_t ppv = arg_u32(c, 2); if (ppv) mem_w32(ppv, this_va); stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0); return; }
            case 1: stdcall_ret(c, 1, 2); return;
            case 2:
                if (this_va) guest_free(this_va);
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
            case 7: { /* GetEventHandle(this, hEvent) */
                uint32_t ph = arg_u32(c, 1);
                if (ph) mem_w32(ph, 0);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            }
            case 8: { /* GetEvent(this, lEventCode, lParam1, lParam2, msTimeout) -> +0x20 */
                uint32_t pec = arg_u32(c, 1);
                uint32_t p1  = arg_u32(c, 2);
                uint32_t p2  = arg_u32(c, 3);
                if (v) dshow_step_playback(v);
                if (!v || v->eof) {
                    if (pec) mem_w32(pec, 1u /* EC_COMPLETE */);
                    if (p1) mem_w32(p1, 0);
                    if (p2) mem_w32(p2, 0);
                    stdcall_ret(c, 5, 0); return;
                }
                stdcall_ret(c, 5, 0x80004004u /* E_ABORT: no event yet */); return;
            }
            case 9: { /* WaitForCompletion(this, msTimeout, pEvCode) -> +0x24 */
                uint32_t ms = arg_u32(c, 1);
                uint32_t pec = arg_u32(c, 2);
                if (v) dshow_step_playback(v);
                if (!v || v->eof) {
                    if (pec) mem_w32(pec, 1u /* EC_COMPLETE */);
                    stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0); return;
                }
                if (ms > 0 && ms < 100) SDL_Delay(ms);
                else if (ms >= 100) SDL_Delay(16);
                if (v) dshow_step_playback(v);
                if (pec) mem_w32(pec, (v && v->eof) ? 1u : 0u);
                stdcall_ret(c, 3, (v && v->eof) ? 0u : 0x80004004u); return;
            }
            case 10: /* CancelDefaultHandling(this, lEvCode) */
            case 11: /* RestoreDefaultHandling(this, lEvCode) */
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            case 12: /* FreeEventParams(this, lEvCode, lParam1, lParam2) */
                stdcall_ret(c, 4, 0); return;
            case 13: /* SetNotifyWindow(this, hwnd, lMsg, lInstanceData) */
                stdcall_ret(c, 4, 0); return;
            case 14: /* SetNotifyFlags(this, lNoNotifyFlags) */
            case 15: /* GetNotifyFlags(this, lplNoNotifyFlags) */
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            default:
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
        }
    }

    if (iface_id == COM_IFACE_DSHOW_VIDWIN) {
        /* IVideoWindow vtable layout:
           0..2   IUnknown: QueryInterface(3), AddRef(1), Release(1)
           3..6   IDispatch: GetTypeInfoCount(2), GetTypeInfo(4), GetIDsOfNames(6), Invoke(9)
           7..36  1-param put_/get_ property methods (2 args each, e.g. +0x24 put_WindowStyle,
                  +0x34 put_AutoShow, +0x4c put_Visible, +0x74 put_Owner, +0x7c put_MessageDrain)
           37     +0x94 SetWindowForeground(this, Focus) [2 args]
           38     +0x98 NotifyOwnerMessage(this, hwnd, uMsg, wParam, lParam) [5 args]
           39     +0x9c SetWindowPosition(this, Left, Top, Width, Height) [5 args]
           40     +0xa0 GetWindowPosition(this, pLeft, pTop, pWidth, pHeight) [5 args]
           41     +0xa4 GetMinIdealImageSize(this, pWidth, pHeight) [3 args]
           42     +0xa8 GetMaxIdealImageSize(this, pWidth, pHeight) [3 args]
           43     +0xac GetRestorePosition(this, pLeft, pTop, pWidth, pHeight) [5 args]
           44..45 +0xb0/+0xb4 HideCursor / IsCursorHidden [2 args] */
        uint32_t vw = (v && v->width > 0) ? (uint32_t)v->width : 800u;
        uint32_t vh = (v && v->height > 0) ? (uint32_t)v->height : 600u;
        switch (slot) {
            case 0: { uint32_t ppv = arg_u32(c, 2); if (ppv) mem_w32(ppv, this_va); stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0); return; }
            case 1: stdcall_ret(c, 1, 2); return;
            case 2:
                if (this_va) guest_free(this_va);
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
            case 3: stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0x80004001u); return;
            case 4: stdcall_ret(c, 4, 0x80004001u); return;
            case 5: stdcall_ret(c, 6, 0x80004001u); return;
            case 6: stdcall_ret(c, 9, 0x80004001u); return;
            case 37: /* SetWindowForeground(this, Focus) */
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            case 38: /* NotifyOwnerMessage(this, hwnd, uMsg, wParam, lParam) */
            case 39: /* SetWindowPosition(this, Left, Top, Width, Height) */
                stdcall_ret(c, 5, 0); return;
            case 40: /* GetWindowPosition(this, pLeft, pTop, pWidth, pHeight) */
            case 43: { /* GetRestorePosition(this, pLeft, pTop, pWidth, pHeight) */
                uint32_t pl = arg_u32(c, 1), pt = arg_u32(c, 2), pw = arg_u32(c, 3), ph = arg_u32(c, 4);
                if (pl) mem_w32(pl, 0);
                if (pt) mem_w32(pt, 0);
                if (pw) mem_w32(pw, vw);
                if (ph) mem_w32(ph, vh);
                stdcall_ret(c, 5, 0); return;
            }
            case 41: /* GetMinIdealImageSize(this, pWidth, pHeight) */
            case 42: { /* GetMaxIdealImageSize(this, pWidth, pHeight) */
                uint32_t pw = arg_u32(c, 1), ph = arg_u32(c, 2);
                if (pw) mem_w32(pw, vw);
                if (ph) mem_w32(ph, vh);
                stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0); return;
            }
            default: /* All 1-param put_/get_ property methods take (this, val) = 2 args */
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
        }
    }

    if (iface_id == COM_IFACE_DSHOW_BASVID) {
        /* IBasicVideo vtable layout:
           0..2   IUnknown, 3..6 IDispatch
           10,11  +0x28/+0x2c get_VideoWidth / get_VideoHeight [2 args]
           13,15  get_SourceLeft / get_SourceWidth (+0x3c) [2 args]
           17,19  get_SourceTop / get_SourceHeight (+0x4c) [2 args]
           21,23  get_DestinationLeft / get_DestinationWidth (+0x5c) [2 args]
           25,27  get_DestinationTop / get_DestinationHeight (+0x6c) [2 args]
           28,29  SetSourcePosition / GetSourcePosition [5 args]
           30     SetDefaultSourcePosition [1 arg]
           31,32  SetDestinationPosition / GetDestinationPosition [5 args]
           33     SetDefaultDestinationPosition [1 arg]
```
