```c
                    DDrawSurface *back = ddraw_get_surface(s->attached_handle);
                    if (back) {
                        back->refcount++;
                        if (out) mem_w32(out, back->obj_va);
                        stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0); return;
                    }
                }
                if (out) mem_w32(out, 0);
                stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0x887600FFu); return;
            }
            case 15: { /* GetCaps(this, lpDDSCaps) */
                uint32_t caps_va = arg_u32(c, 1);
                if (caps_va && s) mem_w32(caps_va, s->caps);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            }
            case 17: { /* GetDC(this, lphDC) */
                uint32_t out = arg_u32(c, 1);
                uint32_t hdc = gdi_create_surface_dc(h);
                if (out) mem_w32(out, hdc);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, hdc ? 0u : 0x80004005u); return;
            }
            case 21: { /* GetPixelFormat(this, lpDDPixelFormat) */
                uint32_t pf = arg_u32(c, 1);
                if (pf && s) fill_pixelformat(pf, s->bpp);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            }
            case 22: { /* GetSurfaceDesc(this, lpDDSurfaceDesc) */
                uint32_t desc = arg_u32(c, 1);
                if (desc && s) fill_surf_desc(desc, s, 0);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            }
            case 24: /* IsLost(this) */
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
            case 25: { /* Lock(this, lpDestRect, lpDDSurfaceDesc, dwFlags, hEvent) */
                uint32_t drc = arg_u32(c, 1);
                uint32_t desc = arg_u32(c, 2);
                if (desc && s) {
                    if ((s->is_primary || s->is_backbuffer) && s->pixels_va && gl_renderer_is_active()) {
                        int slot = surf_spr_slot(s);
                        size_t need = (size_t)s->pitch * (size_t)s->height;
                        if (need > g_lock_snap_sz[slot]) {
                            uint8_t *nb = (uint8_t *)realloc(g_lock_snap[slot], need);
                            if (nb) {
                                g_lock_snap[slot] = nb;
                                g_lock_snap_sz[slot] = need;
                            }
                        }
                        if (g_lock_snap[slot] && g_lock_snap_sz[slot] >= need) {
                            memcpy(g_lock_snap[slot], g_mem + s->pixels_va, need);
                            g_lock_snap_valid[slot] = 1;
                        }
                    }
                    fill_surf_desc(desc, s, 1);
                    if (drc) {
                        int x0 = (int32_t)mem_r32(drc + 0);
                        int y0 = (int32_t)mem_r32(drc + 4);
                        uint32_t ptr = s->pixels_va + (uint32_t)y0 * s->pitch + (uint32_t)x0 * (s->bpp / 8u);
                        mem_w32(desc + 36, ptr);
                    }
                }
                stdcall_ret(c, 5, 0); return;
            }
            case 26: { /* ReleaseDC(this, hDC) */
                gdi_release_surface_dc(arg_u32(c, 1));
                if (s && s->is_primary) ddraw_present_surface(s);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            }
            case 27: /* Restore(this) */
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
            case 28: /* SetClipper(this, lpDDClipper) */
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            case 29: { /* SetColorKey(this, dwFlags, lpDDColorKey) */
                uint32_t flags = arg_u32(c, 1);
                uint32_t ck_va = arg_u32(c, 2);
                if (s && (flags & 8u /* DDCKEY_SRCBLT */)) {
                    if (ck_va) {
                        s->has_src_colorkey = 1;
                        s->src_ck_low = mem_r32(ck_va + 0);
                        s->src_ck_high = mem_r32(ck_va + 4);
                    } else {
                        s->has_src_colorkey = 0;
                    }
                    s->sprite_cache_count = 0;
                    s->known_cell_w = 0;
                    s->known_cell_h = 0;
                    s->cell_probe_room_gen = 0;
                }
                stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0); return;
            }
            case 32: /* Unlock(this, lpSurfaceData) */
                if (s) {
                    if (!s->is_primary && !s->is_backbuffer) {
                        s->hd_asset_id = -2; /* re-check HD match on next Blt */
                        s->sprite_cache_count = 0;
                        s->known_cell_w = 0;
                        s->known_cell_h = 0;
                        s->cell_probe_room_gen = 0;
                        s->num_text_draws = 0;
                    } else {
                        sync_screen_lock_diff_to_canvas(s);
                        if (s->is_primary) {
                            win32_pump_sdl_events();
                            ddraw_present_surface(s);
                        }
                    }
                }
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            default:
                fprintf(stderr, "[DDRAW] Unhandled IDirectDrawSurface slot %u\n", slot);
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
        }
    }
}

/* ============================================================================
 * Native 4K GPU Hooks for mha2.exe's Software IT3 RLE Sprite Blitters
 * (Intercepts Moorhuhn s/ animations, surf=0x0 character animations, scaled,
 *  mirrored, and alpha-blended IT3 sprites drawn directly into locked buffers)
 * ============================================================================ */

#define RLE_MATCH_CACHE_SIZE 1024
typedef struct RleMatchCacheEntry {
    uint32_t frame_va;
    uint32_t data_va;
    uint32_t data_sig;
    uint32_t room_gen;
    int cell_w;
    int cell_h;
    int sprite_id;
} RleMatchCacheEntry;

static RleMatchCacheEntry g_rle_cache[RLE_MATCH_CACHE_SIZE];
static uint8_t *g_rle_tmp_rgba = NULL;
static size_t g_rle_tmp_cap = 0;

static DDrawSurface *find_screen_surface_by_pixels(uint32_t pixels_va, int *out_ox, int *out_oy) {
    if (!pixels_va) return NULL;
    for (uint32_t i = 1; i < MAX_DD_SURFACES; i++) {
        DDrawSurface *s = &g_surfaces[i];
        if (!s->in_use || (!s->is_primary && !s->is_backbuffer) || !s->pixels_va) continue;
        uint32_t sz = s->pitch * s->height;
        if (pixels_va >= s->pixels_va && pixels_va < s->pixels_va + sz) {
            uint32_t off = pixels_va - s->pixels_va;
            int bps = (int)(s->bpp / 8u);
            if (out_oy) *out_oy = (s->pitch > 0) ? (int)(off / s->pitch) : 0;
            if (out_ox) *out_ox = (s->pitch > 0 && bps > 0) ? (int)((off % s->pitch) / (uint32_t)bps) : 0;
            return s;
        }
    }
    return NULL;
}

static int decode_rle_frame_into_rgba(uint32_t frame_va, int cell_w, int cell_h, uint8_t *rgba) {
    if (!frame_va || frame_va >= GUEST_MEM_SIZE - 0x30u) return 0;
    uint32_t rle_w = mem_r32(frame_va + 0x00u);
    uint32_t rle_h = mem_r32(frame_va + 0x04u);
    uint32_t bpp   = mem_r32(frame_va + 0x14u);
    uint32_t has_a = mem_r32(frame_va + 0x18u);
    uint32_t data_va = mem_r32(frame_va + 0x2cu);
    if (rle_w == 0 || rle_h == 0 || data_va < GUEST_HEAP_BASE || data_va >= GUEST_MEM_SIZE - 32u) return 0;
    if (bpp != 2u && bpp != 3u && bpp != 4u) return 0;

    uint32_t pos = data_va;
    int x = 0, y = 0;
    int max_y = (int)rle_h < cell_h ? (int)rle_h : cell_h;
    while (pos < GUEST_MEM_SIZE - 8u && y < max_y) {
        uint8_t b = g_mem[pos++];
        if (b == 0) break;
        if ((b & 0x80u) == 0) {
            x += (int)b;
        } else {
            int cnt = (int)(b & 0x7Fu);
            if (cnt == 0) {
                y++;
                x = 0;
                continue;
            }
            for (int i = 0; i < cnt; i++) {
                uint8_t r = 0, g = 0, bl = 0, a = 255;
                if (bpp == 4u) {
                    if (pos + 4u >= GUEST_MEM_SIZE) { y = max_y; break; }
                    bl = g_mem[pos + 0];
                    g  = g_mem[pos + 1];
                    r  = g_mem[pos + 2];
                    pos += 4u;
                } else if (bpp == 3u) {
                    if (pos + 3u >= GUEST_MEM_SIZE) { y = max_y; break; }
                    bl = g_mem[pos + 0];
                    g  = g_mem[pos + 1];
                    r  = g_mem[pos + 2];
                    pos += 3u;
                } else {
                    if (pos + 2u >= GUEST_MEM_SIZE) { y = max_y; break; }
                    uint16_t p16 = mem_r16(pos);
                    pos += 2u;
                    r  = (uint8_t)(((p16 >> 11) & 0x1Fu) * 255u / 31u);
                    g  = (uint8_t)(((p16 >> 5) & 0x3Fu) * 255u / 63u);
                    bl = (uint8_t)((p16 & 0x1Fu) * 255u / 31u);
                }
                if (has_a) {
                    if (pos >= GUEST_MEM_SIZE) { y = max_y; break; }
                    a = g_mem[pos++];
                }
                if (x >= 0 && x < cell_w && y >= 0 && y < cell_h) {
                    size_t di = ((size_t)y * (size_t)cell_w + (size_t)x) * 4u;
                    rgba[di + 0] = r;
                    rgba[di + 1] = g;
                    rgba[di + 2] = bl;
                    rgba[di + 3] = a;
                }
                x++;
            }
        }
    }
    return 1;
}

static int lookup_rle_both_sprite(uint32_t tbl1_va, uint32_t tbl2_va, int cell_w, int cell_h) {
    if (hd_sprites_count() <= 0 || cell_w < 16 || cell_h < 16 || cell_w > 2048 || cell_h > 2048) return -1;
    uint32_t primary_va = tbl1_va ? tbl1_va : tbl2_va;
    if (!primary_va || primary_va >= GUEST_MEM_SIZE - 0x30u) return -1;

    uint32_t rle_w = mem_r32(primary_va + 0x00u);
    uint32_t rle_h = mem_r32(primary_va + 0x04u);
    uint32_t bpp   = mem_r32(primary_va + 0x14u);
    uint32_t data_va = mem_r32(primary_va + 0x2cu);
    if (rle_w == 0 || rle_h == 0 || data_va < GUEST_HEAP_BASE || data_va >= GUEST_MEM_SIZE - 32u) return -1;
    if (bpp != 2u && bpp != 3u && bpp != 4u) return -1;

    uint32_t sig = rle_w * 73856093u ^ rle_h * 19349663u ^ bpp * 83492791u ^ (tbl2_va * 2654435761u);
    for (uint32_t k = 0; k < 16u; k++) {
        sig ^= (uint32_t)g_mem[data_va + k];
        sig *= 16777619u;
    }

    uint32_t rgen = hd_sprites_get_room_gen();
    uint32_t hidx = ((primary_va >> 3) ^ (data_va >> 4) ^ (uint32_t)(cell_w * 31 + cell_h)) & (RLE_MATCH_CACHE_SIZE - 1u);
    RleMatchCacheEntry *ce = &g_rle_cache[hidx];
    if (ce->frame_va == primary_va && ce->data_va == data_va && ce->data_sig == sig &&
        ce->cell_w == cell_w && ce->cell_h == cell_h && ce->room_gen == rgen) {
        return ce->sprite_id;
    }

    size_t need = (size_t)cell_w * (size_t)cell_h * 4u;
    if (need > g_rle_tmp_cap) {
        uint8_t *nb = (uint8_t *)realloc(g_rle_tmp_rgba, need);
        if (!nb) return -1;
        g_rle_tmp_rgba = nb;
        g_rle_tmp_cap = need;
    }
    memset(g_rle_tmp_rgba, 0, need);

    int ok = 0;
    if (tbl1_va) ok |= decode_rle_frame_into_rgba(tbl1_va, cell_w, cell_h, g_rle_tmp_rgba);
    if (tbl2_va) ok |= decode_rle_frame_into_rgba(tbl2_va, cell_w, cell_h, g_rle_tmp_rgba);
    if (!ok) return -1;

    int spr_id = hd_sprites_match_rgba(g_rle_tmp_rgba, cell_w, cell_h);
    ce->frame_va = primary_va;
    ce->data_va = data_va;
    ce->data_sig = sig;
    ce->room_gen = rgen;
    ce->cell_w = cell_w;
    ce->cell_h = cell_h;
    ce->sprite_id = spr_id;
    return spr_id;
}

static int lookup_rle_frame_sprite(uint32_t frame_va, int cell_w, int cell_h) {
    return lookup_rle_both_sprite(frame_va, 0, cell_w, cell_h);
}

static void sync_rect_into_lock_snap(DDrawSurface *s, int x0, int y0, int x1, int y1, int upload_diff_first) {
    int slot = surf_spr_slot(s);
    if (!g_lock_snap_valid[slot] || !g_lock_snap[slot]) return;
    int W = (int)s->width, H = (int)s->height;
    if (x0 < 0) x0 = 0;
    if (y0 < 0) y0 = 0;
    if (x1 > W) x1 = W;
    if (y1 > H) y1 = H;
    if (x1 <= x0 || y1 <= y0) return;

    int bps = (int)(s->bpp / 8u);
    int bw = x1 - x0, bh = y1 - y0;
    const uint8_t *cur_base = g_mem + s->pixels_va;
    uint8_t *old_base = g_lock_snap[slot];

    if (upload_diff_first) {
        uint32_t *patch = ensure_patch_rgba_buf((size_t)bw * (size_t)bh);
        if (patch) {
            int any = 0;
            for (int y = 0; y < bh; y++) {
                const uint8_t *crow = cur_base + (size_t)(y0 + y) * s->pitch;
                const uint8_t *orow = old_base + (size_t)(y0 + y) * s->pitch;
                uint32_t *drow = patch + (size_t)y * (size_t)bw;
                for (int x = 0; x < bw; x++) {
                    int sx = x0 + x;
                    if (memcmp(crow + sx * bps, orow + sx * bps, (size_t)bps) == 0) {
                        drow[x] = 0;
                    } else {
                        any = 1;
                        if (s->bpp == 32) {
                            uint32_t px;
                            memcpy(&px, crow + sx * 4, 4);
                            uint8_t r = (uint8_t)((px >> 16) & 0xFFu);
                            uint8_t g = (uint8_t)((px >> 8) & 0xFFu);
                            uint8_t b = (uint8_t)(px & 0xFFu);
                            drow[x] = (0xFFu << 24) | ((uint32_t)b << 16) | ((uint32_t)g << 8) | r;
                        } else {
                            uint16_t px;
                            memcpy(&px, crow + sx * 2, 2);
                            uint8_t r = (uint8_t)(((px >> 11) & 0x1Fu) * 255u / 31u);
                            uint8_t g = (uint8_t)(((px >> 5) & 0x3Fu) * 255u / 63u);
                            uint8_t b = (uint8_t)((px & 0x1Fu) * 255u / 31u);
                            drow[x] = (0xFFu << 24) | ((uint32_t)b << 16) | ((uint32_t)g << 8) | r;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            if (any) {
                gl_canvas_blit_rgba_patch(slot, W, H, patch, bw, bh, x0, y0, x1, y1, 1);
            }
        }
    }

    /* Update snapshot so Unlock() never overwrites this rect with 1x software pixels */
    size_t row_bytes = (size_t)bw * (size_t)bps;
    for (int y = y0; y < y1; y++) {
        memcpy(old_base + (size_t)y * s->pitch + (size_t)x0 * (size_t)bps,
               cur_base + (size_t)y * s->pitch + (size_t)x0 * (size_t)bps,
               row_bytes);
    }
}

static RecompFn g_orig_rle_fn[8];
static RecompFn g_orig_rle_both_fn = NULL;
static int g_in_rle_both = 0;
static const uint32_t g_rle_addrs[8] = {
    0x0048dba0u, 0x0048e260u,
    0x0048fc10u, 0x004902d0u,
    0x00491c70u, 0x00492310u,
    0x00493dd0u, 0x00494480u,
};

/* Master hook on Blitter::BlitBoth (0x00494b80, __thiscall(desc, tbl1, tbl2)):
   1) When tbl1 != NULL (e.g. ALL of Moorhuhn s/001..s/008 & surf=0x0 animations),
      matches tbl1+tbl2 together ONCE against our composited 4K HD sprite and renders
      in one clean 4K pass — eliminating the blurry 1x tbl2 dashed border completely!
   2) When tbl1 == NULL && tbl2 != NULL (DirectDraw-backed IT3 sprites & ALL 5 cursors
      whose tbl1 was already drawn via BltFast), suppresses the redundant blurry 1x tbl2
      ring whenever tbl1 was already drawn as our decoupled 4K cursor or 4K HD sprite! */
static void hook_rle_both(CpuCtx *c) {
    if (!g_orig_rle_both_fn) return;
    if (g_font_ctx.active && g_cfg.hd_vector_text) {
        g_orig_rle_both_fn(c);
        return;
    }

    uint32_t desc_va = mem_r32(c->esp + 4u);
    uint32_t tbl1_va = mem_r32(c->esp + 8u);
    uint32_t tbl2_va = mem_r32(c->esp + 12u);

    uint32_t dst_pix_va = 0, clip_va = 0;
    int dst_x = 0, dst_y = 0, cell_w = 0, cell_h = 0;
    int flip_x = 0, flip_y = 0;
    double scale = 1.0;
    uint32_t alpha_u = 256u;
    int clip_x0 = 0, clip_y0 = 0, clip_x1 = 0, clip_y1 = 0;
    int valid_desc = 0;
    if (desc_va && desc_va < GUEST_MEM_SIZE - 0x60u && (tbl1_va || tbl2_va)) {
        dst_pix_va = mem_r32(desc_va + 0x00u);
        dst_x      = (int32_t)mem_r32(desc_va + 0x08u);
        dst_y      = (int32_t)mem_r32(desc_va + 0x0cu);
        cell_w     = (int32_t)mem_r32(desc_va + 0x10u);
        cell_h     = (int32_t)mem_r32(desc_va + 0x14u);
        clip_va    = mem_r32(desc_va + 0x18u);
        flip_x     = (int)g_mem[desc_va + 0x20u];
        flip_y     = (int)g_mem[desc_va + 0x21u];
        scale      = mem_rf64(desc_va + 0x28u);
        int raw_a  = (int32_t)mem_r32(desc_va + 0x30u);
        alpha_u    = (raw_a > 0 && raw_a < 255) ? (uint32_t)raw_a : 256u;
        if (clip_va && clip_va < GUEST_MEM_SIZE - 16u) {
            clip_x0 = (int32_t)mem_r32(clip_va + 0u);
            clip_y0 = (int32_t)mem_r32(clip_va + 4u);
            clip_x1 = (int32_t)mem_r32(clip_va + 8u);
            clip_y1 = (int32_t)mem_r32(clip_va + 12u);
        }
        valid_desc = (scale >= 0.001 && cell_w > 0 && cell_h > 0);
    }

    int prev_in = g_in_rle_both;
    g_in_rle_both = 1;
    g_orig_rle_both_fn(c);
    g_in_rle_both = prev_in;

    if (!valid_desc || !gl_renderer_is_active()) return;
    int base_ox = 0, base_oy = 0;
    DDrawSurface *s = find_screen_surface_by_pixels(dst_pix_va, &base_ox, &base_oy);
    if (!s) return;

    int scaled_w = (scale == 1.0) ? cell_w : (int)((double)cell_w * scale + 0.5);
    int scaled_h = (scale == 1.0) ? cell_h : (int)((double)cell_h * scale + 0.5);
    if (scaled_w <= 0 || scaled_h <= 0) return;

    int ux0 = base_ox + dst_x;
    int uy0 = base_oy + dst_y;
    int dx0 = ux0, dy0 = uy0;
    int dx1 = ux0 + scaled_w, dy1 = uy0 + scaled_h;
    if (clip_va) {
        int cx0 = base_ox + clip_x0, cy0 = base_oy + clip_y0;
        int cx1 = base_ox + clip_x1, cy1 = base_oy + clip_y1;
        if (dx0 < cx0) dx0 = cx0;
        if (dy0 < cy0) dy0 = cy0;
        if (dx1 > cx1) dx1 = cx1;
        if (dy1 > cy1) dy1 = cy1;
    }
    if (dx0 < 0) dx0 = 0;
    if (dy0 < 0) dy0 = 0;
    if (dx1 > (int)s->width) dx1 = (int)s->width;
    if (dy1 > (int)s->height) dy1 = (int)s->height;
    if (dx1 <= dx0 || dy1 <= dy0) return;

    int slot = surf_spr_slot(s);

    /* Case A: tbl1 was already drawn via DirectDraw BltFast (cursor or HD prop) ->
       suppress the redundant 1x tbl2 semi-transparent ring so it never stamps blurry
       1x pixels onto the 4K canvas! */
    if (!tbl1_va && tbl2_va) {
        if (g_cfg.decoupled_cursor && g_cursor_drawn_this_frame &&
            ux0 == g_active_cursor_x0 && uy0 == g_active_cursor_y0 &&
            scaled_w == (g_active_cursor_x1 - g_active_cursor_x0) &&
            scaled_h == (g_active_cursor_y1 - g_active_cursor_y0)) {
            sync_rect_into_lock_snap(s, dx0, dy0, dx1, dy1, 0);
            return;
        }
        if (g_last_dd_hd_blit.valid && g_last_dd_hd_blit.slot == slot &&
            abs(ux0 - g_last_dd_hd_blit.ux0) <= 1 && abs(uy0 - g_last_dd_hd_blit.uy0) <= 1 &&
            abs(scaled_w - g_last_dd_hd_blit.fw) <= 1 && abs(scaled_h - g_last_dd_hd_blit.fh) <= 1) {
            g_last_dd_hd_blit.valid = 0;
            sync_rect_into_lock_snap(s, dx0, dy0, dx1, dy1, 0);
            return;
        }
    }

    int spr_id = (g_cfg.hd_sprites_enabled && hd_sprites_count() > 0)
                 ? lookup_rle_both_sprite(tbl1_va, tbl2_va, cell_w, cell_h) : -1;
    if (spr_id >= 0) {
        maybe_lock_split_room_from_sprite(spr_id);
        float fx0 = (float)(dx0 - ux0) / (float)scaled_w;
        float fy0 = (float)(dy0 - uy0) / (float)scaled_h;
        float fx1 = (float)(dx1 - ux0) / (float)scaled_w;
        float fy1 = (float)(dy1 - uy0) / (float)scaled_h;
        float u0 = flip_x ? (1.0f - fx0) : fx0;
        float u1 = flip_x ? (1.0f - fx1) : fx1;
        float v0 = flip_y ? (1.0f - fy0) : fy0;
        float v1 = flip_y ? (1.0f - fy1) : fy1;
        float alpha_f = (alpha_u >= 256u) ? 1.0f : ((float)alpha_u / 256.0f);
        gl_canvas_blit_hd_sprite(slot, (int)s->width, (int)s->height, spr_id,
                                 dx0, dy0, dx1, dy1, u0, v0, u1, v1, 1, alpha_f);
        sync_rect_into_lock_snap(s, dx0, dy0, dx1, dy1, 0);
    } else {
        sync_rect_into_lock_snap(s, dx0, dy0, dx1, dy1, 1);
    }
}

static void hook_rle_common(CpuCtx *c, int idx, int is_alpha) {
    /* When inside BlitBoth (0x00494b80) or Font::DrawText (0x00486c40), let the software
       blitter update 1x RAM only — the outer hook handles the single clean 4K pass! */
    if (g_in_rle_both || (g_font_ctx.active && g_cfg.hd_vector_text)) {
        g_orig_rle_fn[idx](c);
        return;
    }

    uint32_t desc_va  = mem_r32(c->esp + 4u);
    uint32_t frame_va = mem_r32(c->esp + 8u);
    uint32_t alpha_u  = is_alpha ? mem_r32(c->esp + 12u) : 256u;

    /* Capture descriptor fields BEFORE orig_fn runs (0x48d7c0 may normalize scale to 1.0) */
    uint32_t dst_pix_va = 0, clip_va = 0;
    int dst_x = 0, dst_y = 0, cell_w = 0, cell_h = 0;
    int flip_x = 0, flip_y = 0;
    double scale = 1.0;
    int clip_x0 = 0, clip_y0 = 0, clip_x1 = 0, clip_y1 = 0;
    int valid_desc = 0;
    if (desc_va && desc_va < GUEST_MEM_SIZE - 0x60u && frame_va && frame_va < GUEST_MEM_SIZE - 0x30u) {
        dst_pix_va = mem_r32(desc_va + 0x00u);
        dst_x      = (int32_t)mem_r32(desc_va + 0x08u);
        dst_y      = (int32_t)mem_r32(desc_va + 0x0cu);
        cell_w     = (int32_t)mem_r32(desc_va + 0x10u);
        cell_h     = (int32_t)mem_r32(desc_va + 0x14u);
        clip_va    = mem_r32(desc_va + 0x18u);
        flip_x     = (int)g_mem[desc_va + 0x20u];
        flip_y     = (int)g_mem[desc_va + 0x21u];
        scale      = mem_rf64(desc_va + 0x28u);
        if (clip_va && clip_va < GUEST_MEM_SIZE - 16u) {
            clip_x0 = (int32_t)mem_r32(clip_va + 0u);
            clip_y0 = (int32_t)mem_r32(clip_va + 4u);
            clip_x1 = (int32_t)mem_r32(clip_va + 8u);
            clip_y1 = (int32_t)mem_r32(clip_va + 12u);
        }
        valid_desc = (scale >= 0.001 && cell_w > 0 && cell_h > 0);
    }

    /* Always execute the original software RLE blitter so 1x RAM stays 100% accurate */
    g_orig_rle_fn[idx](c);

    if (!valid_desc || alpha_u == 0 || !gl_renderer_is_active()) return;
    int base_ox = 0, base_oy = 0;
    DDrawSurface *s = find_screen_surface_by_pixels(dst_pix_va, &base_ox, &base_oy);
    if (!s) return;

    int scaled_w = (scale == 1.0) ? cell_w : (int)((double)cell_w * scale + 0.5);
    int scaled_h = (scale == 1.0) ? cell_h : (int)((double)cell_h * scale + 0.5);
    if (scaled_w <= 0 || scaled_h <= 0) return;

    int ux0 = base_ox + dst_x;
    int uy0 = base_oy + dst_y;
    int dx0 = ux0, dy0 = uy0;
    int dx1 = ux0 + scaled_w, dy1 = uy0 + scaled_h;
    if (clip_va) {
        int cx0 = base_ox + clip_x0, cy0 = base_oy + clip_y0;
        int cx1 = base_ox + clip_x1, cy1 = base_oy + clip_y1;
        if (dx0 < cx0) dx0 = cx0;
        if (dy0 < cy0) dy0 = cy0;
        if (dx1 > cx1) dx1 = cx1;
        if (dy1 > cy1) dy1 = cy1;
    }
    if (dx0 < 0) dx0 = 0;
    if (dy0 < 0) dy0 = 0;
    if (dx1 > (int)s->width) dx1 = (int)s->width;
    if (dy1 > (int)s->height) dy1 = (int)s->height;
    if (dx1 <= dx0 || dy1 <= dy0) return;

    int spr_id = (g_cfg.hd_sprites_enabled && hd_sprites_count() > 0)
                 ? lookup_rle_frame_sprite(frame_va, cell_w, cell_h) : -1;
    if (spr_id >= 0) {
        maybe_lock_split_room_from_sprite(spr_id);
        float fx0 = (float)(dx0 - ux0) / (float)scaled_w;
        float fy0 = (float)(dy0 - uy0) / (float)scaled_h;
        float fx1 = (float)(dx1 - ux0) / (float)scaled_w;
        float fy1 = (float)(dy1 - uy0) / (float)scaled_h;
        float u0 = flip_x ? (1.0f - fx0) : fx0;
        float u1 = flip_x ? (1.0f - fx1) : fx1;
        float v0 = flip_y ? (1.0f - fy0) : fy0;
        float v1 = flip_y ? (1.0f - fy1) : fy1;
        float alpha_f = (alpha_u >= 256u) ? 1.0f : ((float)alpha_u / 256.0f);
        gl_canvas_blit_hd_sprite(surf_spr_slot(s), (int)s->width, (int)s->height, spr_id,
                                 dx0, dy0, dx1, dy1, u0, v0, u1, v1, 1, alpha_f);
        sync_rect_into_lock_snap(s, dx0, dy0, dx1, dy1, 0);
    } else {
        sync_rect_into_lock_snap(s, dx0, dy0, dx1, dy1, 1);
    }
}

static void hook_rle_0(CpuCtx *c) { hook_rle_common(c, 0, 0); }
static void hook_rle_1(CpuCtx *c) { hook_rle_common(c, 1, 1); }
static void hook_rle_2(CpuCtx *c) { hook_rle_common(c, 2, 0); }
static void hook_rle_3(CpuCtx *c) { hook_rle_common(c, 3, 1); }
static void hook_rle_4(CpuCtx *c) { hook_rle_common(c, 4, 0); }
static void hook_rle_5(CpuCtx *c) { hook_rle_common(c, 5, 1); }
static void hook_rle_6(CpuCtx *c) { hook_rle_common(c, 6, 0); }
static void hook_rle_7(CpuCtx *c) { hook_rle_common(c, 7, 1); }

static RecompFn g_orig_font_draw_fn = NULL;

static void font_cp1252_to_utf8(const uint8_t *src, int len, char *dst, size_t dst_sz,
                                int *out_s0, int *out_s1) {
    int s0 = 0, s1 = len;
    while (s0 < s1 && src[s0] == ' ') s0++;
    while (s1 > s0 && (src[s1 - 1] == ' ' || src[s1 - 1] == '\0')) s1--;
    if (out_s0) *out_s0 = s0;
    if (out_s1) *out_s1 = s1;
    size_t j = 0;
    for (int i = s0; i < s1 && j + 4 < dst_sz; i++) {
        uint8_t ch = src[i];
        if (ch == 0) break;
        if (ch < 0x80u) {
            dst[j++] = (char)ch;
        } else {
            uint32_t cp = ch;
            if (ch == 0x80u) cp = 0x20ACu;
            else if (ch == 0x84u) cp = 0x201Eu;
            else if (ch == 0x85u) cp = 0x2026u;
            else if (ch == 0x91u) cp = 0x2018u;
            else if (ch == 0x92u) cp = 0x2019u;
            else if (ch == 0x93u) cp = 0x201Cu;
            else if (ch == 0x94u) cp = 0x201Du;
            else if (ch == 0x96u) cp = 0x2013u;
            else if (ch == 0x97u) cp = 0x2014u;
            if (cp < 0x800u) {
                dst[j++] = (char)(0xC0u | (cp >> 6));
                dst[j++] = (char)(0x80u | (cp & 0x3Fu));
            } else {
                dst[j++] = (char)(0xE0u | (cp >> 12));
                dst[j++] = (char)(0x80u | ((cp >> 6) & 0x3Fu));
                dst[j++] = (char)(0x80u | (cp & 0x3Fu));
            }
        }
    }
    dst[j] = '\0';
}

static void hook_font_draw_text(CpuCtx *c) {
    if (!g_orig_font_draw_fn) return;
    if (!g_cfg.hd_vector_text || !gl_renderer_is_active()) {
        g_orig_font_draw_fn(c);
        return;
    }

    uint32_t font_va = c->ecx;
    uint32_t clip_va = mem_r32(c->esp + 8u);
    uint32_t str_va  = mem_r32(c->esp + 12u);
    int draw_x       = (int32_t)mem_r32(c->esp + 16u);
    int draw_y       = (int32_t)mem_r32(c->esp + 20u);
    int str_len      = (int32_t)mem_r32(c->esp + 24u);

    if (!str_va || str_len <= 0 || str_va >= GUEST_MEM_SIZE - 4u) {
        g_orig_font_draw_fn(c);
        return;
    }
    if (str_len > 240) str_len = 240;

    int s0 = 0, s1 = 0;
    char utf8[256];
    font_cp1252_to_utf8(g_mem + str_va, str_len, utf8, sizeof(utf8), &s0, &s1);
    if (!utf8[0] || s1 <= s0) {
        g_orig_font_draw_fn(c);
        return;
    }

    int line_h = 16;
    if (font_va && font_va < GUEST_MEM_SIZE - 0x24u) {
        uint32_t fhdr_va = mem_r32(font_va + 0x1cu);
        if (fhdr_va && fhdr_va < GUEST_MEM_SIZE - 0x18u) {
            int h = (int32_t)mem_r32(fhdr_va + 0x04u);
            if (h >= 10 && h <= 64) line_h = h;
        }
    }

    /* Call Font::MeasureText (0x00486ad0) on a scratch CpuCtx copy to get the exact,
       dirty-rect-invariant unclipped pixel span [text_x0 .. text_x0 + text_w]! */
    int lead_w = 0, text_w = 0;
    if (font_va && g_code_table[0x00486ad0u - GUEST_TEXT_START]) {
        if (s0 > 0) {
            CpuCtx tmp = *c;
            tmp.ecx = font_va;
            uint32_t a[2] = { str_va, (uint32_t)s0 };
            lead_w = (int)guest_call_stdcall(&tmp, 0x00486ad0u, 2, a);
        }
        CpuCtx tmp = *c;
        tmp.ecx = font_va;
        uint32_t a[2] = { str_va + (uint32_t)s0, (uint32_t)(s1 - s0) };
        text_w = (int)guest_call_stdcall(&tmp, 0x00486ad0u, 2, a);
    }
    if (text_w <= 0) {
        g_orig_font_draw_fn(c);
        return;
    }

    int cx0 = 0, cy0 = 0, cx1 = (int)g_dd_width, cy1 = (int)g_dd_height;
    if (clip_va && clip_va < GUEST_MEM_SIZE - 16u) {
        cx0 = (int32_t)mem_r32(clip_va + 0u);
        cy0 = (int32_t)mem_r32(clip_va + 4u);
        cx1 = (int32_t)mem_r32(clip_va + 8u);
        cy1 = (int32_t)mem_r32(clip_va + 12u);
    }

    FontDrawCtx prev = g_font_ctx;
    g_font_ctx.active = 1;
    g_font_ctx.font_va = font_va;
    g_font_ctx.text_x0 = draw_x + lead_w;
    g_font_ctx.text_y0 = draw_y;
    g_font_ctx.text_w = text_w;
    g_font_ctx.text_h = line_h;
    g_font_ctx.clip_x0 = cx0;
    g_font_ctx.clip_y0 = cy0;
    g_font_ctx.clip_x1 = cx1;
    g_font_ctx.clip_y1 = cy1;
    snprintf(g_font_ctx.utf8, sizeof(g_font_ctx.utf8), "%s", utf8);

    g_orig_font_draw_fn(c);

    g_font_ctx = prev;
}

void ddraw_install_rle_hooks(void) {
    static const RecompFn hooks[8] = {
        hook_rle_0, hook_rle_1, hook_rle_2, hook_rle_3,
        hook_rle_4, hook_rle_5, hook_rle_6, hook_rle_7,
    };
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        uint32_t off = g_rle_addrs[i] - GUEST_TEXT_START;
        g_orig_rle_fn[i] = g_code_table[off];
        if (g_orig_rle_fn[i]) {
            g_code_table[off] = hooks[i];
        }
    }
    uint32_t both_off = 0x00494b80u - GUEST_TEXT_START;
    g_orig_rle_both_fn = g_code_table[both_off];
    if (g_orig_rle_both_fn) {
        g_code_table[both_off] = hook_rle_both;
    }
    uint32_t font_off = 0x00486c40u - GUEST_TEXT_START;
    g_orig_font_draw_fn = g_code_table[font_off];
    if (g_orig_font_draw_fn) {
        g_code_table[font_off] = hook_font_draw_text;
    }
}
```

`runtime/win32_dinput.c`:

```c
#include "runtime.h"
#include "imports.h"

typedef struct DiEvent {
    uint32_t ofs;
    uint32_t data;
    uint32_t ts;
    uint32_t seq;
} DiEvent;

#define DI_EV_CAP 512
static DiEvent g_di_q[DI_EV_CAP];
static int g_di_head = 0, g_di_tail = 0;
static uint32_t g_di_seq = 1;
static int g_rel_x = 0, g_rel_y = 0, g_rel_z = 0;
static uint8_t g_btn[4] = {0, 0, 0, 0};

static void di_push(uint32_t ofs, uint32_t data) {
    int next = (g_di_tail + 1) % DI_EV_CAP;
    if (next == g_di_head) g_di_head = (g_di_head + 1) % DI_EV_CAP;
    g_di_q[g_di_tail].ofs = ofs;
    g_di_q[g_di_tail].data = data;
    g_di_q[g_di_tail].ts = SDL_GetTicks();
    g_di_q[g_di_tail].seq = g_di_seq++;
    g_di_tail = next;
}

void dinput_push_mouse_move(int dx, int dy, int abs_x, int abs_y) {
    (void)abs_x; (void)abs_y;
    g_rel_x += dx;
    g_rel_y += dy;
    if (dx != 0) di_push(0u /* DIMOFS_X */, (uint32_t)(int32_t)dx);
    if (dy != 0) di_push(4u /* DIMOFS_Y */, (uint32_t)(int32_t)dy);
}

void dinput_push_mouse_button(int btn, int down) {
    if (btn < 0 || btn >= 4) return;
    g_btn[btn] = down ? 0x80u : 0x00u;
    di_push(12u + (uint32_t)btn /* DIMOFS_BUTTON0..3 */, down ? 0x80u : 0x00u);
}

void dinput_push_mouse_wheel(int dz) {
    g_rel_z += dz;
    if (dz != 0) di_push(8u /* DIMOFS_Z */, (uint32_t)(int32_t)dz);
}

int win32_dinput_dispatch(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t import_id) {
    if (import_id == IMP_DINPUT_DLL_DirectInputCreateA) {
        uint32_t ppdi = arg_u32(c, 2);
        uint32_t obj = com_create_obj(COM_IFACE_DINPUT, 12, 1);
        if (ppdi) mem_w32(ppdi, obj);
        if (g_debug_log) fprintf(stderr, "[DINPUT] DirectInputCreateA -> obj=0x%08x\n", obj);
        stdcall_ret(c, 4, 0);
        return 1;
    }
    return 0;
}

void com_dinput_dispatch(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t iface_id, uint32_t slot) {
    uint32_t this_va = arg_u32(c, 0);

    if (iface_id == COM_IFACE_DINPUT) {
        switch (slot) {
            case 0: { uint32_t ppv = arg_u32(c, 2); if (ppv) mem_w32(ppv, this_va); stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0); return; }
            case 1: stdcall_ret(c, 1, 2); return;
            case 2: stdcall_ret(c, 1, 1); return;
            case 3: { /* CreateDevice(this, rguid, lplpDirectInputDevice, pUnkOuter) */
                uint32_t out = arg_u32(c, 2);
                uint32_t dev = com_create_obj(COM_IFACE_DINPUT_DEV, 20, 1);
                if (out) mem_w32(out, dev);
                if (g_debug_log) fprintf(stderr, "[DINPUT] CreateDevice -> dev=0x%08x\n", dev);
                stdcall_ret(c, 4, 0); return;
            }
            case 4: { /* EnumDevices(this, dwDevType, lpCallback, pvRef, dwFlags) */
                uint32_t cb_va = arg_u32(c, 2);
                uint32_t ref   = arg_u32(c, 3);
                if (cb_va) {
                    /* DIDEVICEINSTANCEA is 580 bytes */
                    uint32_t inst = guest_alloc(580);
                    memset(g_mem + inst, 0, 580);
                    mem_w32(inst + 0, 580);
                    /* GUID_SysMouse */
                    static const uint8_t guid_mouse[16] = {
                        0xE0,0x2E,0x1D,0x6F, 0xD5,0xD5, 0xCF,0x11, 0xBF,0xC7, 0x44,0x45,0x53,0x54,0x00,0x00
                    };
                    memcpy(g_mem + inst + 4, guid_mouse, 16);
                    memcpy(g_mem + inst + 20, guid_mouse, 16);
                    mem_w32(inst + 36, 0x00000102u /* DIDEVTYPE_MOUSE | DIDEVTYPEMOUSE_TRADITIONAL */);
                    snprintf((char *)(g_mem + inst + 40), 260, "SDL2 Mouse");
                    snprintf((char *)(g_mem + inst + 300), 260, "SDL2 Mouse");
                    uint32_t a[2] = { inst, ref };
                    guest_call_stdcall(c, cb_va, 2, a);
                    guest_free(inst);
                }
                stdcall_ret(c, 5, 0); return;
            }
            default:
                fprintf(stderr, "[DINPUT] Unhandled IDirectInput slot %u\n", slot);
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
        }
    }

    if (iface_id == COM_IFACE_DINPUT_DEV) {
        switch (slot) {
            case 0: { uint32_t ppv = arg_u32(c, 2); if (ppv) mem_w32(ppv, this_va); stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0); return; }
            case 1: stdcall_ret(c, 1, 2); return;
            case 2: stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
            case 3: { /* GetCapabilities(this, lpDIDevCaps) */
                uint32_t caps = arg_u32(c, 1);
                if (caps) {
                    uint32_t sz = mem_r32(caps);
                    if (sz > 4 && sz <= 64) memset(g_mem + caps + 4, 0, sz - 4);
                    mem_w32(caps + 4, 1); /* DIDC_ATTACHED */
                    mem_w32(caps + 8, 2); /* DIDEVTYPE_MOUSE */
                    mem_w32(caps + 12, 3);/* dwAxes */
                    mem_w32(caps + 16, 3);/* dwButtons */
                }
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            }
            case 5: /* GetProperty(this, rguidProp, pdiph) */
                stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0); return;
            case 6: /* SetProperty(this, rguidProp, pdiph) */
                stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0); return;
            case 7: /* Acquire(this) */
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
            case 8: /* Unacquire(this) */
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
            case 9: { /* GetDeviceState(this, cbData, lpvData) */
                win32_pump_sdl_events();
                uint32_t cb = arg_u32(c, 1);
                uint32_t dst = arg_u32(c, 2);
                if (dst && cb >= 16) {
                    mem_w32(dst + 0, (uint32_t)(int32_t)g_rel_x);
                    mem_w32(dst + 4, (uint32_t)(int32_t)g_rel_y);
                    mem_w32(dst + 8, (uint32_t)(int32_t)g_rel_z);
                    g_rel_x = g_rel_y = g_rel_z = 0;
                    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) mem_w8(dst + 12u + (uint32_t)i, g_btn[i]);
                }
                stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0); return;
            }
            case 10: { /* GetDeviceData(this, cbObjectData, rgdod, pdwInOut, dwFlags) */
                win32_pump_sdl_events();
                uint32_t cb = arg_u32(c, 1);
                uint32_t rgdod = arg_u32(c, 2);
                uint32_t pdw = arg_u32(c, 3);
                uint32_t flags = arg_u32(c, 4);
                uint32_t max_n = pdw ? mem_r32(pdw) : 0;
                uint32_t count = 0;
                int peek = (flags & 1u /* DIGDD_PEEK */) != 0;
                int idx = g_di_head;
                while (idx != g_di_tail && count < max_n) {
                    if (rgdod && cb >= 16) {
                        uint32_t dst = rgdod + count * cb;
                        mem_w32(dst + 0, g_di_q[idx].ofs);
                        mem_w32(dst + 4, g_di_q[idx].data);
                        mem_w32(dst + 8, g_di_q[idx].ts);
                        mem_w32(dst + 12, g_di_q[idx].seq);
                    }
                    idx = (idx + 1) % DI_EV_CAP;
                    count++;
                }
                if (!peek) g_di_head = idx;
                if (pdw) mem_w32(pdw, count);
                stdcall_ret(c, 5, 0); return;
            }
            case 11: /* SetDataFormat(this, lpdf) */
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            case 12: /* SetEventNotification(this, hEvent) */
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            case 13: /* SetCooperativeLevel(this, hwnd, dwFlags) */
                stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0); return;
            case 17: /* Poll(this) */
                win32_pump_sdl_events();
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
            default:
                fprintf(stderr, "[DINPUT] Unhandled IDirectInputDevice slot %u\n", slot);
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
        }
    }
}
```

`runtime/win32_dsound.c`:

```c
#include "runtime.h"
#include "gl_renderer.h"
#include "imports.h"
#include <pthread.h>

typedef struct DSoundData {
    int in_use;
    int refcount;
    uint32_t va;
    uint32_t size;
} DSoundData;

typedef struct DSoundBuf {
    int in_use;
    int refcount;
    uint32_t obj_va;
    int data_id;          /* index into g_ds_data[] (refcounted shared PCM) */
    uint32_t data_va;     /* cached guest-accessible PCM ring buffer VA */
    uint32_t size;        /* buffer size in bytes */
    uint32_t freq;        /* sample rate Hz */
    uint16_t channels;    /* 1 or 2 */
    uint16_t bits;        /* 8 or 16 */
    uint32_t block_align; /* channels * (bits/8) */

    int playing;
    int looping;
    double play_frame;    /* fractional frame index */
    int32_t volume_mb;    /* DirectSound volume: 0 (max) .. -10000 (mute) */
    int32_t pan_mb;       /* DirectSound pan: -10000 (left) .. 0 .. +10000 (right) */
    float gain_l;
    float gain_r;
    float cur_gain_l;     /* smoothed gain to prevent pops/clicks */
    float cur_gain_r;
} DSoundBuf;

#define MAX_DS_BUFS 256
#define MIX_RATE    48000

static DSoundData g_ds_data[MAX_DS_BUFS];
static DSoundBuf g_ds_bufs[MAX_DS_BUFS];
static int g_ds_obj_refcount = 0;
static SDL_AudioDeviceID g_audio_dev = 0;
static pthread_mutex_t g_ds_mtx = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER;

static int alloc_ds_data(uint32_t size) {
    for (int i = 1; i < MAX_DS_BUFS; i++) {
        if (!g_ds_data[i].in_use) {
            uint32_t va = guest_alloc(size + 64u);
            if (!va) return 0;
            g_ds_data[i].in_use = 1;
            g_ds_data[i].refcount = 1;
            g_ds_data[i].va = va;
            g_ds_data[i].size = size;
            return i;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

static void release_ds_data(int data_id) {
    if (data_id < 1 || data_id >= MAX_DS_BUFS) return;
    DSoundData *d = &g_ds_data[data_id];
    if (!d->in_use) return;
    d->refcount--;
    if (d->refcount <= 0) {
        if (d->va) guest_free(d->va);
        memset(d, 0, sizeof(*d));
    }
}

/* ---- Real-Time Mastering Biquad EQ (Low-Shelf + High-Shelf) + M/S Stereo Widener ---- */
typedef struct Biquad {
    float b0, b1, b2, a1, a2;
    float z1, z2;
} Biquad;

static Biquad g_eq_ls_l, g_eq_ls_r, g_eq_hs_l, g_eq_hs_r;
static int g_eq_last_mode = -1;

static void biquad_set_shelf(Biquad *q, int high_shelf, float freq_hz, float gain_db) {
    float A = powf(10.0f, gain_db / 40.0f);
    float w0 = 2.0f * 3.14159265358979323846f * freq_hz / (float)MIX_RATE;
    float cw = cosf(w0);
    float sw = sinf(w0);
    float alpha = sw * 0.70710678f; /* S = 1.0 smooth shelf slope */
    float two_sqrtA_alpha = 2.0f * sqrtf(A) * alpha;
    float b0, b1, b2, a0, a1, a2;
    if (!high_shelf) {
        b0 =    A * ((A + 1.0f) - (A - 1.0f) * cw + two_sqrtA_alpha);
        b1 =  2.0f * A * ((A - 1.0f) - (A + 1.0f) * cw);
        b2 =    A * ((A + 1.0f) - (A - 1.0f) * cw - two_sqrtA_alpha);
        a0 =        (A + 1.0f) + (A - 1.0f) * cw + two_sqrtA_alpha;
        a1 = -2.0f * ((A - 1.0f) + (A + 1.0f) * cw);
        a2 =        (A + 1.0f) + (A - 1.0f) * cw - two_sqrtA_alpha;
    } else {
        b0 =    A * ((A + 1.0f) + (A - 1.0f) * cw + two_sqrtA_alpha);
        b1 = -2.0f * A * ((A - 1.0f) + (A + 1.0f) * cw);
        b2 =    A * ((A + 1.0f) + (A - 1.0f) * cw - two_sqrtA_alpha);
        a0 =        (A + 1.0f) - (A - 1.0f) * cw + two_sqrtA_alpha;
        a1 =  2.0f * ((A - 1.0f) - (A + 1.0f) * cw);
        a2 =        (A + 1.0f) - (A - 1.0f) * cw - two_sqrtA_alpha;
    }
    float inv = 1.0f / a0;
    q->b0 = b0 * inv; q->b1 = b1 * inv; q->b2 = b2 * inv;
    q->a1 = a1 * inv; q->a2 = a2 * inv;
}

static inline float biquad_step(Biquad *q, float x) {
    float y = q->b0 * x + q->z1;
    q->z1 = q->b1 * x - q->a1 * y + q->z2;
    q->z2 = q->b2 * x - q->a2 * y;
    return y;
}

static void update_master_eq(int mode) {
    if (mode == g_eq_last_mode) return;
    g_eq_last_mode = mode;
    float ls_db = (mode == EQ_STUDIO) ? 2.2f : 3.0f;
    float hs_db = (mode == EQ_STUDIO) ? 4.2f : 2.2f;
    float hs_hz = (mode == EQ_STUDIO) ? 6200.0f : 7200.0f;
    biquad_set_shelf(&g_eq_ls_l, 0, 115.0f, ls_db);
    biquad_set_shelf(&g_eq_ls_r, 0, 115.0f, ls_db);
    biquad_set_shelf(&g_eq_hs_l, 1, hs_hz, hs_db);
    biquad_set_shelf(&g_eq_hs_r, 1, hs_hz, hs_db);
}

static void update_gains(DSoundBuf *b) {
    float vol = (b->volume_mb <= -10000) ? 0.0f : powf(10.0f, (float)b->volume_mb / 2000.0f);
    if (vol > 1.0f) vol = 1.0f;
    float gl = vol, gr = vol;
    if (b->pan_mb < 0) {
        gr *= powf(10.0f, (float)b->pan_mb / 2000.0f);
    } else if (b->pan_mb > 0) {
        gl *= powf(10.0f, -(float)b->pan_mb / 2000.0f);
    }
    b->gain_l = gl;
    b->gain_r = gr;
}

static inline float read_sample(const uint8_t *base, uint32_t total_frames, int64_t frame,
                                int ch_idx, uint16_t channels, uint16_t bits, int looping) {
    if (frame < 0) frame = looping ? (frame + total_frames) : 0;
    if (frame >= (int64_t)total_frames) frame = looping ? (frame % total_frames) : (int64_t)(total_frames - 1);
    uint32_t idx = (uint32_t)frame;
    if (bits == 16) {
        int16_t s;
        memcpy(&s, base + (idx * channels + (uint32_t)ch_idx) * 2u, 2);
        return (float)s / 32768.0f;
    } else {
        uint8_t u = base[idx * channels + (uint32_t)ch_idx];
        return ((float)u - 128.0f) / 128.0f;
    }
}

/* 4-point Catmull-Rom cubic interpolation for artifact-free 22kHz -> 48kHz upsampling */
static inline float sample_cubic(const uint8_t *base, uint32_t total_frames, double pos,
                                 int ch_idx, uint16_t channels, uint16_t bits, int looping) {
    int64_t i = (int64_t)floor(pos);
    float t = (float)(pos - (double)i);
    float y0 = read_sample(base, total_frames, i - 1, ch_idx, channels, bits, looping);
    float y1 = read_sample(base, total_frames, i,     ch_idx, channels, bits, looping);
    float y2 = read_sample(base, total_frames, i + 1, ch_idx, channels, bits, looping);
    float y3 = read_sample(base, total_frames, i + 2, ch_idx, channels, bits, looping);
    float a0 = -0.5f * y0 + 1.5f * y1 - 1.5f * y2 + 0.5f * y3;
    float a1 =        y0 - 2.5f * y1 + 2.0f * y2 - 0.5f * y3;
    float a2 = -0.5f * y0             + 0.5f * y2;
    float a3 = y1;
    return ((a0 * t + a1) * t + a2) * t + a3;
}

/* Subtle Schroeder Stereo Castle Reverb (4 lowpass-damped comb filters + 2 allpass diffusers)
   for warm spatial ambience on 2005 music & SFX — zero heap allocations */
#define RV_COMBS 4
#define RV_APS   2
static const int k_comb_len_l[RV_COMBS] = { 1617, 1791, 1987, 2179 };
static const int k_comb_len_r[RV_COMBS] = { 1649, 1823, 2017, 2213 };
static const int k_ap_len_l[RV_APS]     = { 347, 557 };
static const int k_ap_len_r[RV_APS]     = { 373, 587 };
static float g_comb_l[RV_COMBS][2300];
static float g_comb_r[RV_COMBS][2300];
static int   g_comb_idx_l[RV_COMBS], g_comb_idx_r[RV_COMBS];
static float g_comb_lp_l[RV_COMBS],  g_comb_lp_r[RV_COMBS];
static float g_ap_l[RV_APS][640];
static float g_ap_r[RV_APS][640];
static int   g_ap_idx_l[RV_APS], g_ap_idx_r[RV_APS];
static float g_cur_master = 1.0f;

static void SDLCALL dsound_audio_callback(void *userdata, Uint8 *stream, int len) {
    (void)userdata;
    static int audio_unpinned = 0;
    if (!audio_unpinned) {
        audio_unpinned = 1;
        runtime_unpin_current_thread();
    }
    float *out = (float *)stream;
    int nframes = len / 8; /* stereo float32 */
    memset(out, 0, (size_t)len);

    int muted = __atomic_load_n(&g_cfg.muted, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
    float master_vol = 1.0f;
    __atomic_load(&g_cfg.master_volume, &master_vol, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
    float target_master = muted ? 0.0f : master_vol;

    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ds_mtx);
    for (int i = 1; i < MAX_DS_BUFS; i++) {
        DSoundBuf *b = &g_ds_bufs[i];
        if (!b->in_use || !b->playing || !b->data_va || b->block_align == 0) continue;
        uint32_t total_frames = b->size / b->block_align;
        if (total_frames == 0) continue;
        const uint8_t *base = g_mem + b->data_va;
        double step = (double)b->freq / (double)MIX_RATE;
        double pos = b->play_frame;
        float gl = b->cur_gain_l;
        float gr = b->cur_gain_r;
        float tgl = b->gain_l;
        float tgr = b->gain_r;

        for (int f = 0; f < nframes; f++) {
            if (pos >= (double)total_frames) {
                if (b->looping) {
                    pos = fmod(pos, (double)total_frames);
                } else {
                    b->playing = 0;
                    pos = 0.0;
                    break;
                }
            }
            /* Smooth gain ramp (~5ms) to eliminate clicks */
            gl += (tgl - gl) * 0.015f;
            gr += (tgr - gr) * 0.015f;
            float sl = sample_cubic(base, total_frames, pos, 0, b->channels, b->bits, b->looping);
            float sr = (b->channels >= 2)
                ? sample_cubic(base, total_frames, pos, 1, b->channels, b->bits, b->looping)
                : sl;
            out[f * 2 + 0] += sl * gl;
            out[f * 2 + 1] += sr * gr;
            pos += step;
        }
        b->play_frame = pos;
        b->cur_gain_l = gl;
        b->cur_gain_r = gr;
    }
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ds_mtx);

    int rev_mode = __atomic_load_n(&g_cfg.audio_reverb, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
    float rev_wet = (rev_mode == REVERB_RICH) ? 0.26f : ((rev_mode == REVERB_SUBTLE) ? 0.13f : 0.0f);
    float rev_fb  = (rev_mode == REVERB_RICH) ? 0.78f : 0.70f;
    float damp    = 0.35f;

    int eq_mode = __atomic_load_n(&g_cfg.audio_eq, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
    if (eq_mode != EQ_OFF) update_master_eq(eq_mode);
    float stereo_width = (eq_mode == EQ_STUDIO) ? 1.24f : ((eq_mode == EQ_WARM) ? 1.16f : 1.0f);

    for (int f = 0; f < nframes; f++) {
        /* Smooth master volume / mute anti-pop ramping */
        g_cur_master += (target_master - g_cur_master) * 0.004f;
        float dl = out[f * 2 + 0] * g_cur_master;
        float dr = out[f * 2 + 1] * g_cur_master;

        if (eq_mode != EQ_OFF) {
            dl = biquad_step(&g_eq_hs_l, biquad_step(&g_eq_ls_l, dl));
            dr = biquad_step(&g_eq_hs_r, biquad_step(&g_eq_ls_r, dr));
            float mid  = 0.5f * (dl + dr);
            float side = 0.5f * (dl - dr) * stereo_width;
            dl = mid + side;
            dr = mid - side;
        }

        if (rev_wet > 0.001f) {
            float inp = (dl + dr) * 0.25f;
            float wl = 0.0f, wr = 0.0f;
            for (int c = 0; c < RV_COMBS; c++) {
                int il = g_comb_idx_l[c];
                float yl = g_comb_l[c][il];
                g_comb_lp_l[c] = yl * (1.0f - damp) + g_comb_lp_l[c] * damp;
                g_comb_l[c][il] = inp + g_comb_lp_l[c] * rev_fb;
                if (++il >= k_comb_len_l[c]) il = 0;
                g_comb_idx_l[c] = il;
                wl += yl;

                int ir = g_comb_idx_r[c];
                float yr = g_comb_r[c][ir];
                g_comb_lp_r[c] = yr * (1.0f - damp) + g_comb_lp_r[c] * damp;
                g_comb_r[c][ir] = inp + g_comb_lp_r[c] * rev_fb;
                if (++ir >= k_comb_len_r[c]) ir = 0;
                g_comb_idx_r[c] = ir;
                wr += yr;
            }
            for (int a = 0; a < RV_APS; a++) {
                int il = g_ap_idx_l[a];
                float bl = g_ap_l[a][il];
                float ol = -wl + bl;
                g_ap_l[a][il] = wl + bl * 0.5f;
                if (++il >= k_ap_len_l[a]) il = 0;
                g_ap_idx_l[a] = il;
                wl = ol;

                int ir = g_ap_idx_r[a];
                float br = g_ap_r[a][ir];
                float or_ = -wr + br;
                g_ap_r[a][ir] = wr + br * 0.5f;
                if (++ir >= k_ap_len_r[a]) ir = 0;
                g_ap_idx_r[a] = ir;
                wr = or_;
            }
            dl = dl * (1.0f - rev_wet * 0.35f) + wl * rev_wet;
            dr = dr * (1.0f - rev_wet * 0.35f) + wr * rev_wet;
        }

        /* Gentle soft-clipping limiter */
        if (dl > 0.85f || dl < -0.85f) dl = tanhf(dl);
        if (dr > 0.85f || dr < -0.85f) dr = tanhf(dr);
        out[f * 2 + 0] = dl;
        out[f * 2 + 1] = dr;
    }
}

void win32_dsound_init(void) {
    SDL_AudioSpec want, have;
    memset(&want, 0, sizeof(want));
    want.freq = MIX_RATE;
    want.format = AUDIO_F32SYS;
    want.channels = 2;
    want.samples = 512; /* ~10.6 ms low-latency buffer */
    want.callback = dsound_audio_callback;
    g_audio_dev = SDL_OpenAudioDevice(NULL, 0, &want, &have, 0);
    if (g_audio_dev != 0) {
        SDL_PauseAudioDevice(g_audio_dev, 0);
    } else {
        fprintf(stderr, "WARN: SDL_OpenAudioDevice failed: %s (continuing muted)\n", SDL_GetError());
    }
}

void win32_dsound_shutdown(void) {
    if (g_audio_dev != 0) {
        SDL_CloseAudioDevice(g_audio_dev);
        g_audio_dev = 0;
    }
}

static DSoundBuf *get_buf(uint32_t h) {
    if (h >= 1 && h < MAX_DS_BUFS && g_ds_bufs[h].in_use) return &g_ds_bufs[h];
    return NULL;
}

int win32_dsound_dispatch(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t import_id) {
    if (import_id == IMP_DSOUND_DLL_Ordinal_1) {
        /* DirectSoundCreate(lpGuid, ppDS, pUnkOuter) */
        uint32_t ppds = arg_u32(c, 1);
        uint32_t obj = com_create_obj(COM_IFACE_DSOUND, 16, 1);
        g_ds_obj_refcount = 1;
        if (ppds) mem_w32(ppds, obj);
        if (g_debug_log) fprintf(stderr, "[DSOUND] DirectSoundCreate -> obj=0x%08x\n", obj);
        stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0);
        return 1;
    }
    return 0;
}

void com_dsound_dispatch(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t iface_id, uint32_t slot) {
    uint32_t this_va = arg_u32(c, 0);

    if (iface_id == COM_IFACE_DSOUND) {
        switch (slot) {
            case 0: { uint32_t ppv = arg_u32(c, 2); if (ppv) mem_w32(ppv, this_va); stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0); return; }
            case 1: stdcall_ret(c, 1, (uint32_t)(++g_ds_obj_refcount)); return;
            case 2: {
                uint32_t rc = 0;
                if (g_ds_obj_refcount > 0) {
                    g_ds_obj_refcount--;
                    rc = (uint32_t)g_ds_obj_refcount;
                    if (g_ds_obj_refcount == 0 && this_va) guest_free(this_va);
                }
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, rc); return;
            }
            case 3: { /* CreateSoundBuffer(this, lpcDSBufferDesc, lplpDirectSoundBuffer, pUnkOuter) */
                uint32_t desc_va = arg_u32(c, 1);
                uint32_t out_va  = arg_u32(c, 2);
                uint32_t flags   = mem_r32(desc_va + 4);
                uint32_t bsz     = mem_r32(desc_va + 8);
                uint32_t wfx_va  = mem_r32(desc_va + 16);
                int is_primary   = (flags & 1u) != 0;

                pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ds_mtx);
                uint32_t h = 0;
                for (uint32_t i = 1; i < MAX_DS_BUFS; i++) {
                    if (!g_ds_bufs[i].in_use) { h = i; break; }
                }
                if (h) {
                    DSoundBuf *b = &g_ds_bufs[h];
                    memset(b, 0, sizeof(*b));
                    b->in_use = 1;
                    b->refcount = 1;
                    b->channels = wfx_va ? mem_r16(wfx_va + 2) : 2;
                    b->freq = wfx_va ? mem_r32(wfx_va + 4) : 22050;
                    b->bits = wfx_va ? mem_r16(wfx_va + 14) : 16;
                    if (b->channels == 0) b->channels = 2;
                    if (b->freq == 0) b->freq = 22050;
                    if (b->bits != 8 && b->bits != 16) b->bits = 16;
                    b->block_align = b->channels * (b->bits / 8u);
                    b->size = (bsz > 0) ? bsz : (is_primary ? 16384u : 4096u);
                    b->data_id = alloc_ds_data(b->size);
                    if (!b->data_id) {
                        memset(b, 0, sizeof(*b));
                        h = 0;
                        if (out_va) mem_w32(out_va, 0);
                    } else {
                        b->data_va = g_ds_data[b->data_id].va;
                        memset(g_mem + b->data_va, b->bits == 8 ? 0x80 : 0, b->size);
                        b->volume_mb = 0;
                        b->pan_mb = 0;
                        update_gains(b);
                        b->cur_gain_l = b->gain_l;
                        b->cur_gain_r = b->gain_r;
                        b->obj_va = com_create_obj(COM_IFACE_DSOUND_BUF, 24, h);
                        if (!b->obj_va) {
                            release_ds_data(b->data_id);
                            memset(b, 0, sizeof(*b));
                            h = 0;
                        }
                        if (out_va) mem_w32(out_va, h ? b->obj_va : 0);
                    }
                } else {
                    if (out_va) mem_w32(out_va, 0);
                }
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ds_mtx);
                stdcall_ret(c, 4, h ? 0u : 0x8007000Eu); return;
            }
            case 4: { /* GetCaps(this, lpDSCaps) */
                uint32_t caps = arg_u32(c, 1);
                if (caps) {
                    uint32_t sz = mem_r32(caps);
                    if (sz > 4 && sz <= 128) memset(g_mem + caps + 4, 0, sz - 4);
                    mem_w32(caps + 4, 0x00000FFFu);
                    mem_w32(caps + 8, 8000);
                    mem_w32(caps + 12, 48000);
                    mem_w32(caps + 16, 1);
                    mem_w32(caps + 20, 64);
                    mem_w32(caps + 24, 64);
                    mem_w32(caps + 28, 64);
                }
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            }
            case 5: { /* DuplicateSoundBuffer(this, lpDsbOriginal, lplpDsbDuplicate) */
                uint32_t orig_obj = arg_u32(c, 1);
                uint32_t out_va   = arg_u32(c, 2);
                pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ds_mtx);
                DSoundBuf *orig = get_buf(com_obj_handle(orig_obj));
                uint32_t h = 0;
                if (orig) {
                    for (uint32_t i = 1; i < MAX_DS_BUFS; i++) {
                        if (!g_ds_bufs[i].in_use) { h = i; break; }
                    }
                    if (h) {
                        DSoundBuf *b = &g_ds_bufs[h];
                        *b = *orig;
                        b->refcount = 1;
                        b->playing = 0;
                        b->play_frame = 0.0;
                        if (b->data_id >= 1 && b->data_id < MAX_DS_BUFS && g_ds_data[b->data_id].in_use) {
                            g_ds_data[b->data_id].refcount++;
                        }
                        b->obj_va = com_create_obj(COM_IFACE_DSOUND_BUF, 24, h);
                        if (!b->obj_va) {
                            release_ds_data(b->data_id);
                            memset(b, 0, sizeof(*b));
                            h = 0;
                        }
                        if (out_va) mem_w32(out_va, h ? b->obj_va : 0);
                    }
                }
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ds_mtx);
                stdcall_ret(c, 3, h ? 0u : 0x80070057u); return;
            }
            case 6: /* SetCooperativeLevel(this, hwnd, dwLevel) */
                stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0); return;
            case 10: /* Initialize(this, pcGuid) */
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            default:
                fprintf(stderr, "[DSOUND] Unhandled IDirectSound slot %u\n", slot);
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
        }
    }

    if (iface_id == COM_IFACE_DSOUND_BUF) {
        uint32_t h = com_obj_handle(this_va);
        switch (slot) {
            case 0: { uint32_t ppv = arg_u32(c, 2); if (ppv) mem_w32(ppv, this_va); stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0); return; }
            case 1: {
                pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ds_mtx);
                DSoundBuf *b = get_buf(h);
                uint32_t rc = b ? (uint32_t)(++b->refcount) : 1u;
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ds_mtx);
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, rc); return;
            }
            case 2: {
                pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ds_mtx);
                DSoundBuf *b = get_buf(h);
                uint32_t rc = 0;
                if (b) {
                    b->refcount--;
                    rc = (uint32_t)b->refcount;
                    if (b->refcount <= 0) {
                        b->playing = 0;
                        release_ds_data(b->data_id);
                        b->data_id = 0;
                        b->data_va = 0;
                        b->in_use = 0;
                        guest_free(this_va);
                    }
                }
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ds_mtx);
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, rc); return;
            }
            case 4: { /* GetCurrentPosition(this, lpdwPlay, lpdwWrite) */
                uint32_t pplay = arg_u32(c, 1);
                uint32_t pwrite = arg_u32(c, 2);
                pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ds_mtx);
                DSoundBuf *b = get_buf(h);
                if (b && b->size > 0) {
                    uint32_t byte_pos = ((uint32_t)b->play_frame * b->block_align) % b->size;
                    uint32_t write_pos = (byte_pos + 2048u) % b->size;
                    if (pplay) mem_w32(pplay, byte_pos);
                    if (pwrite) mem_w32(pwrite, write_pos);
                }
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ds_mtx);
                stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0); return;
            }
            case 5: { /* GetFormat(this, lpwfx, dwSize, lpdwWritten) */
                uint32_t wfx = arg_u32(c, 1);
                uint32_t wr  = arg_u32(c, 3);
                pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ds_mtx);
                DSoundBuf *b = get_buf(h);
                if (b && wfx) {
                    mem_w16(wfx + 0, 1); /* WAVE_FORMAT_PCM */
                    mem_w16(wfx + 2, b->channels);
                    mem_w32(wfx + 4, b->freq);
                    mem_w32(wfx + 8, b->freq * b->block_align);
                    mem_w16(wfx + 12, (uint16_t)b->block_align);
                    mem_w16(wfx + 14, b->bits);
                    mem_w16(wfx + 16, 0);
                }
                if (wr) mem_w32(wr, 18);
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ds_mtx);
                stdcall_ret(c, 4, 0); return;
            }
            case 6: { /* GetVolume */
                uint32_t out = arg_u32(c, 1);
                pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ds_mtx);
                DSoundBuf *b = get_buf(h);
                if (b && out) mem_w32(out, (uint32_t)b->volume_mb);
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ds_mtx);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            }
            case 7: { /* GetPan */
                uint32_t out = arg_u32(c, 1);
                pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ds_mtx);
                DSoundBuf *b = get_buf(h);
                if (b && out) mem_w32(out, (uint32_t)b->pan_mb);
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ds_mtx);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            }
            case 8: { /* GetFrequency */
                uint32_t out = arg_u32(c, 1);
                pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ds_mtx);
                DSoundBuf *b = get_buf(h);
                if (b && out) mem_w32(out, b->freq);
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ds_mtx);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            }
            case 9: { /* GetStatus(this, lpdwStatus) */
                uint32_t out = arg_u32(c, 1);
                pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ds_mtx);
                DSoundBuf *b = get_buf(h);
                uint32_t st = 0;
                if (b && b->playing) {
                    st |= 1u; /* DSBSTATUS_PLAYING */
                    if (b->looping) st |= 4u; /* DSBSTATUS_LOOPING */
                }
                if (out) mem_w32(out, st);
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ds_mtx);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            }
            case 11: { /* Lock(this, dwWriteCursor, dwWriteBytes, lplpvAudioPtr1, lpdwAudioBytes1, lplpvAudioPtr2, lpdwAudioBytes2, dwFlags) */
                uint32_t cursor = arg_u32(c, 1);
                uint32_t bytes  = arg_u32(c, 2);
                uint32_t pp1 = arg_u32(c, 3), pl1 = arg_u32(c, 4);
                uint32_t pp2 = arg_u32(c, 5), pl2 = arg_u32(c, 6);
                uint32_t flags = arg_u32(c, 7);
                pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ds_mtx);
                DSoundBuf *b = get_buf(h);
                if (b && b->data_va && b->size > 0) {
                    if (flags & 2u /* DSBLOCK_ENTIREBUFFER */) { cursor = 0; bytes = b->size; }
                    if (flags & 1u /* DSBLOCK_FROMWRITECURSOR */) {
                        cursor = ((uint32_t)b->play_frame * b->block_align) % b->size;
                    }
                    cursor %= b->size;
                    if (bytes > b->size) bytes = b->size;
                    uint32_t first = b->size - cursor;
                    if (first >= bytes) {
                        if (pp1) mem_w32(pp1, b->data_va + cursor);
                        if (pl1) mem_w32(pl1, bytes);
                        if (pp2) mem_w32(pp2, 0);
                        if (pl2) mem_w32(pl2, 0);
                    } else {
                        if (pp1) mem_w32(pp1, b->data_va + cursor);
                        if (pl1) mem_w32(pl1, first);
                        if (pp2) mem_w32(pp2, b->data_va);
                        if (pl2) mem_w32(pl2, bytes - first);
                    }
                }
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ds_mtx);
                stdcall_ret(c, 8, 0); return;
            }
            case 12: { /* Play(this, dwReserved1, dwReserved2, dwFlags) */
                uint32_t flags = arg_u32(c, 3);
                pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ds_mtx);
                DSoundBuf *b = get_buf(h);
                if (b) {
                    b->playing = 1;
                    b->looping = (flags & 1u) ? 1 : 0;
                }
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ds_mtx);
                stdcall_ret(c, 4, 0); return;
            }
            case 13: { /* SetCurrentPosition(this, dwNewPosition) */
                uint32_t pos = arg_u32(c, 1);
                pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ds_mtx);
                DSoundBuf *b = get_buf(h);
                if (b && b->block_align > 0 && b->size > 0) {
                    b->play_frame = (double)((pos % b->size) / b->block_align);
                }
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ds_mtx);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            }
            case 14: /* SetFormat(this, lpcfxFormat) */
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            case 15: { /* SetVolume(this, lVolume) */
                int32_t vol = arg_i32(c, 1);
                pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ds_mtx);
                DSoundBuf *b = get_buf(h);
                if (b) { b->volume_mb = vol; update_gains(b); }
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ds_mtx);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            }
            case 16: { /* SetPan(this, lPan) */
                int32_t pan = arg_i32(c, 1);
                pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ds_mtx);
                DSoundBuf *b = get_buf(h);
                if (b) { b->pan_mb = pan; update_gains(b); }
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ds_mtx);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            }
            case 17: { /* SetFrequency(this, dwFrequency) */
                uint32_t freq = arg_u32(c, 1);
                pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ds_mtx);
                DSoundBuf *b = get_buf(h);
                if (b && freq >= 100 && freq <= 192000) b->freq = freq;
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ds_mtx);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return;
            }
            case 18: { /* Stop(this) */
                pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ds_mtx);
                DSoundBuf *b = get_buf(h);
                if (b) b->playing = 0;
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ds_mtx);
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
            }
            case 19: /* Unlock(this, ptr1, len1, ptr2, len2) */
                stdcall_ret(c, 5, 0); return;
            case 20: /* Restore(this) */
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
            default:
                fprintf(stderr, "[DSOUND] Unhandled IDirectSoundBuffer slot %u\n", slot);
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return;
        }
    }
}

```

`runtime/win32_kernel.c`:

```c
#ifndef _GNU_SOURCE
#define _GNU_SOURCE
#endif
#include "runtime.h"
#include "gl_renderer.h"
#include "hd_assets.h"
#include "imports.h"
#include <pthread.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <dirent.h>
#include <time.h>
#include <errno.h>
#include <ctype.h>
#include <fnmatch.h>

static __thread uint32_t tl_last_error = 0;

void win32_set_last_error(uint32_t err) { tl_last_error = err; }
uint32_t win32_get_last_error(void) { return tl_last_error; }

/* ---- Case-insensitive path resolution into g_game_dir ---- */
static int find_ci_component(const char *dir, const char *name, char *matched, size_t msz) {
    DIR *d = opendir(dir[0] ? dir : ".");
    if (!d) return 0;
    struct dirent *de;
    while ((de = readdir(d)) != NULL) {
        if (strcasecmp(de->d_name, name) == 0) {
            snprintf(matched, msz, "%s", de->d_name);
            closedir(d);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    closedir(d);
    return 0;
}

void resolve_game_path(const char *win_path, char *out_linux, size_t out_sz, int for_write) {
    char clean[1024];
    size_t j = 0;
    const char *p = win_path ? win_path : "";

    /* Strip leading drive letter like C:\ */
    if (isalpha((unsigned char)p[0]) && p[1] == ':') {
        p += 2;
        if (*p == '\\' || *p == '/') p++;
    }
    while (*p == '\\' || *p == '/') p++;
    /* Strip leading .\ or ./ */
    while (p[0] == '.' && (p[1] == '\\' || p[1] == '/')) p += 2;

    for (; *p && j + 1 < sizeof(clean); p++) {
        clean[j++] = (*p == '\\') ? '/' : *p;
    }
    clean[j] = '\0';

    /* If clean already starts with g_game_dir/, avoid double-prefixing */
    char cur[1024];
    size_t gdl = strlen(g_game_dir);
    if (strncmp(clean, g_game_dir, gdl) == 0 && (clean[gdl] == '/' || clean[gdl] == '\0')) {
        snprintf(cur, sizeof(cur), "%s", clean);
        snprintf(out_linux, out_sz, "%s", cur);
        return;
    }

    snprintf(cur, sizeof(cur), "%s", g_game_dir);
    char tmp[1024];
    snprintf(tmp, sizeof(tmp), "%s", clean);
    char *saveptr = NULL;
    char *tok = strtok_r(tmp, "/", &saveptr);
    while (tok) {
        if (strcmp(tok, ".") == 0) {
            tok = strtok_r(NULL, "/", &saveptr);
            continue;
        }
        char matched[256];
        char next_path[1400];
        if (find_ci_component(cur, tok, matched, sizeof(matched))) {
            snprintf(next_path, sizeof(next_path), "%s/%s", cur, matched);
        } else {
            if (for_write) {
                /* Ensure parent directory exists */
                mkdir(cur, 0755);
            }
            snprintf(next_path, sizeof(next_path), "%s/%s", cur, tok);
        }
        snprintf(cur, sizeof(cur), "%.*s", (int)(sizeof(cur) - 1), next_path);
        tok = strtok_r(NULL, "/", &saveptr);
    }
    snprintf(out_linux, out_sz, "%s", cur);
}

/* ---- Handle Table ---- */
enum HandleType {
    HT_NONE = 0,
    HT_FILE,
    HT_EVENT,
    HT_SEMAPHORE,
    HT_MUTEX,
    HT_THREAD,
    HT_FIND,
};

typedef struct FindState {
    char dir_path[1024];
    char pattern[256];
    DIR *dir;
} FindState;

typedef struct SyncObj {
    pthread_mutex_t mtx;
    pthread_cond_t  cond;
    int refcount;
    int manual_reset;
    int signaled;
    int count;
    int max_count;
    int owner_tid;
    int lock_depth;
} SyncObj;

static void sync_unref(SyncObj *so) {
    if (!so) return;
    if (__atomic_sub_fetch(&so->refcount, 1, __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL) == 0) {
        pthread_mutex_destroy(&so->mtx);
        pthread_cond_destroy(&so->cond);
        free(so);
    }
}

typedef struct ThreadObj {
    pthread_t pt;
    pthread_mutex_t mtx;
    pthread_cond_t cond;
    int refcount;
    uint32_t entry_va;
    uint32_t param_va;
    uint32_t teb_va;
    uint32_t stack_top;
    uint32_t tid;
    int suspended;
    int finished;
    uint32_t exit_code;
} ThreadObj;

static void thread_unref(ThreadObj *to) {
    if (!to) return;
    if (__atomic_sub_fetch(&to->refcount, 1, __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL) == 0) {
        if (to->tid) guest_free_thread_slot(to->tid);
        pthread_mutex_destroy(&to->mtx);
        pthread_cond_destroy(&to->cond);
        free(to);
    }
}

typedef struct WinHandle {
    enum HandleType type;
    int fd;
    SyncObj *sync;
    ThreadObj *thr;
    FindState *find;
} WinHandle;

#define MAX_HANDLES 4096
static WinHandle g_handles[MAX_HANDLES];
static pthread_mutex_t g_hdl_mtx = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER;

static uint32_t alloc_handle(enum HandleType t) {
    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_hdl_mtx);
    for (uint32_t i = 0x100; i < MAX_HANDLES; i++) {
        if (g_handles[i].type == HT_NONE) {
            memset(&g_handles[i], 0, sizeof(WinHandle));
            g_handles[i].type = t;
            g_handles[i].fd = -1;
            pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_hdl_mtx);
            return i;
        }
    }
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_hdl_mtx);
    return 0;
}

static WinHandle *get_handle(uint32_t h) {
    if (h >= MAX_HANDLES || g_handles[h].type == HT_NONE) return NULL;
    return &g_handles[h];
}

/* ---- Critical Sections ---- */
#define MAX_CS 1024
static pthread_mutex_t g_cs_pool[MAX_CS];
static int g_cs_used[MAX_CS];
static pthread_mutex_t g_cs_alloc_mtx = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER;

static pthread_mutex_t *get_or_init_cs(uint32_t cs_va) {
    uint32_t id = mem_r32(cs_va);
    if (id >= 1 && id < MAX_CS && g_cs_used[id]) {
        return &g_cs_pool[id];
    }
    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_cs_alloc_mtx);
    id = mem_r32(cs_va);
    if (id >= 1 && id < MAX_CS && g_cs_used[id]) {
        pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_cs_alloc_mtx);
        return &g_cs_pool[id];
    }
    for (uint32_t i = 1; i < MAX_CS; i++) {
        if (!g_cs_used[i]) {
            pthread_mutexattr_t attr;
            pthread_mutexattr_init(&attr);
            pthread_mutexattr_settype(&attr, PTHREAD_MUTEX_RECURSIVE);
            pthread_mutex_init(&g_cs_pool[i], &attr);
            pthread_mutexattr_destroy(&attr);
            g_cs_used[i] = 1;
            mem_w32(cs_va, i);
            pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_cs_alloc_mtx);
            return &g_cs_pool[i];
        }
    }
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_cs_alloc_mtx);
    return NULL;
}

static __thread ThreadObj *tl_thread_obj = NULL;

/* ---- Thread entry trampoline ---- */
static void *guest_thread_main(void *arg) {
    ThreadObj *to = (ThreadObj *)arg;
    tl_thread_obj = to;
    pthread_mutex_lock(&to->mtx);
    while (to->suspended) {
        pthread_cond_wait(&to->cond, &to->mtx);
    }
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&to->mtx);

    CpuCtx ctx;
    memset(&ctx, 0, sizeof(ctx));
    ctx.esp = to->stack_top;
    ctx.teb_addr = to->teb_va;
    ctx.thread_id = to->tid;
    ctx.fpu_cw = 0x037Fu;

    uint32_t a[1] = { to->param_va };
    uint32_t ret = guest_call_stdcall(&ctx, to->entry_va, 1, a);

    pthread_mutex_lock(&to->mtx);
    to->exit_code = ret;
    to->finished = 1;
    pthread_cond_broadcast(&to->cond);
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&to->mtx);
    tl_thread_obj = NULL;
    thread_unref(to);
    return NULL;
}

/* ---- Timing helpers ---- */
static uint64_t g_start_ns = 0;

static uint64_t now_ns(void) {
    struct timespec ts;
    clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &ts);
    return (uint64_t)ts.tv_sec * 1000000000ull + (uint64_t)ts.tv_nsec;
}

static pthread_mutex_t g_vclock_mtx = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER;
static uint64_t g_vclock_last_real = 0;
static uint64_t g_vclock_acc_ns = 0;

static uint64_t guest_elapsed_ns(void) {
    uint64_t real = now_ns();
    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_vclock_mtx);
    if (g_vclock_last_real == 0) {
        g_vclock_last_real = g_start_ns ? g_start_ns : real;
    }
    if (real > g_vclock_last_real) {
        uint64_t delta = real - g_vclock_last_real;
        float mult = gl_renderer_get_speed_mult();
        if (mult != 1.0f) {
            g_vclock_acc_ns += (uint64_t)((double)delta * (double)mult);
        } else {
            g_vclock_acc_ns += delta;
        }
        g_vclock_last_real = real;
    }
    uint64_t res = g_vclock_acc_ns;
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_vclock_mtx);
    return res;
}

static void make_abs_timeout(struct timespec *ts, uint32_t ms) {
    clock_gettime(CLOCK_REALTIME, ts);
    ts->tv_sec += ms / 1000u;
    ts->tv_nsec += (long)(ms % 1000u) * 1000000L;
    if (ts->tv_nsec >= 1000000000L) {
        ts->tv_sec += 1;
        ts->tv_nsec -= 1000000000L;
    }
}

/* ---- Static guest strings for CRT ---- */
static uint32_t g_cmdline_va = 0;
static uint32_t g_env_a_va = 0;
static uint32_t g_env_w_va = 0;
static uint32_t g_tls_next = 1;
static pthread_mutex_t g_tls_mtx = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER;

void win32_kernel_init(void) {
    g_start_ns = now_ns();
    g_cmdline_va = guest_alloc_cstr("mha2.exe");
    static const char env_a[] = "PATH=C:\\WINDOWS\0TEMP=.\0\0";
    g_env_a_va = guest_alloc_static(env_a, sizeof(env_a));
    uint16_t env_w[64];
    size_t n = sizeof(env_a);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) env_w[i] = (uint8_t)env_a[i];
    g_env_w_va = guest_alloc_static(env_w, (uint32_t)(n * 2));

    /* Standard handles 1, 2, 3 */
    g_handles[1].type = HT_FILE; g_handles[1].fd = 0;
    g_handles[2].type = HT_FILE; g_handles[2].fd = 1;
    g_handles[3].type = HT_FILE; g_handles[3].fd = 2;
}

static void fill_find_data(uint32_t dst_va, const char *full_path, const char *name) {
    memset(g_mem + dst_va, 0, 320);
    struct stat st;
    uint32_t attrs = 0x80; /* FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL */
    uint64_t fsz = 0;
    if (stat(full_path, &st) == 0) {
        if (S_ISDIR(st.st_mode)) attrs = 0x10;
        fsz = (uint64_t)st.st_size;
        uint64_t ft = ((uint64_t)st.st_mtime + 11644473600ull) * 10000000ull;
        mem_w32(dst_va + 4, (uint32_t)ft);
        mem_w32(dst_va + 8, (uint32_t)(ft >> 32));
        mem_w32(dst_va + 12, (uint32_t)ft);
        mem_w32(dst_va + 16, (uint32_t)(ft >> 32));
        mem_w32(dst_va + 20, (uint32_t)ft);
        mem_w32(dst_va + 24, (uint32_t)(ft >> 32));
    }
    mem_w32(dst_va + 0, attrs);
    mem_w32(dst_va + 28, (uint32_t)(fsz >> 32));
    mem_w32(dst_va + 32, (uint32_t)fsz);
    snprintf((char *)(g_mem + dst_va + 44), 260, "%s", name);
}

static int find_next_match(FindState *fs, uint32_t dst_va) {
    struct dirent *de;
    while ((de = readdir(fs->dir)) != NULL) {
        if (fnmatch(fs->pattern, de->d_name, FNM_CASEFOLD) == 0) {
            char full[1280];
            snprintf(full, sizeof(full), "%s/%s", fs->dir_path, de->d_name);
            fill_find_data(dst_va, full, de->d_name);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

int win32_kernel_dispatch(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t import_id) {
    switch (import_id) {
        /* ---- Memory / Heap ---- */
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_HeapCreate:
            stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0x00100001u); return 1;
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_HeapDestroy:
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 1); return 1;
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_HeapAlloc: {
            uint32_t flags = arg_u32(c, 1);
            uint32_t sz = arg_u32(c, 2);
            uint32_t p = guest_alloc(sz);
            if (p && (flags & 0x08u)) memset(g_mem + p, 0, sz);
            stdcall_ret(c, 3, p); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_HeapReAlloc: {
            uint32_t flags = arg_u32(c, 1);
            uint32_t ptr = arg_u32(c, 2);
            uint32_t sz = arg_u32(c, 3);
            uint32_t old_sz = guest_alloc_size(ptr);
            uint32_t p = guest_realloc(ptr, sz);
            if (p && (flags & 0x08u) && sz > old_sz) {
                memset(g_mem + p + old_sz, 0, sz - old_sz);
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 4, p); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_HeapFree:
            guest_free(arg_u32(c, 2));
            stdcall_ret(c, 3, 1); return 1;
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_HeapSize:
            stdcall_ret(c, 3, guest_alloc_size(arg_u32(c, 2))); return 1;
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_VirtualAlloc: {
            uint32_t addr = arg_u32(c, 0);
            uint32_t sz = arg_u32(c, 1);
            uint32_t p = addr ? addr : guest_alloc(sz);
            if (p && !addr) memset(g_mem + p, 0, sz);
            stdcall_ret(c, 4, p); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_VirtualFree: {
            uint32_t addr = arg_u32(c, 0);
            uint32_t type = arg_u32(c, 2);
            if (type & 0x8000u) guest_free(addr);
            stdcall_ret(c, 3, 1); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_IsBadReadPtr:
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_IsBadWritePtr:
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_IsBadCodePtr: {
            uint32_t p = arg_u32(c, 0);
            int nargs = (import_id == IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_IsBadCodePtr) ? 1 : 2;
            stdcall_ret(c, nargs, (p == 0 || p >= GUEST_MEM_SIZE) ? 1 : 0);
            return 1;
        }

        /* ---- Process / Module / Env / Version ---- */
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetVersion:
            stdcall_ret(c, 0, 0x0A280105u); return 1; /* WinXP 5.1.2600 */
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetVersionExA: {
            uint32_t p = arg_u32(c, 0);
            if (p) {
                uint32_t sz = mem_r32(p);
                memset(g_mem + p + 4, 0, (sz > 4 && sz <= 256) ? sz - 4 : 144);
                mem_w32(p + 4, 5);    /* Major */
                mem_w32(p + 8, 1);    /* Minor */
                mem_w32(p + 12, 2600);/* Build */
                mem_w32(p + 16, 2);   /* VER_PLATFORM_WIN32_NT */
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 1); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetCommandLineA:
            stdcall_ret(c, 0, g_cmdline_va); return 1;
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetStartupInfoA: {
            uint32_t p = arg_u32(c, 0);
            if (p) {
                memset(g_mem + p, 0, 68);
                mem_w32(p + 0, 68);
                mem_w32(p + 44, 1);  /* STARTF_USESHOWWINDOW */
                mem_w16(p + 48, 1);  /* SW_SHOWNORMAL */
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetModuleHandleA:
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0x00400000u); return 1;
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetModuleFileNameA: {
            uint32_t buf = arg_u32(c, 1);
            uint32_t bsz = arg_u32(c, 2);
            const char *nm = "C:\\mha2\\mha2.exe";
            uint32_t len = (uint32_t)snprintf((char *)(g_mem + buf), bsz, "%s", nm);
            stdcall_ret(c, 3, len); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetCurrentProcess:
            stdcall_ret(c, 0, 0xFFFFFFFFu); return 1;
```
