```c
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetCurrentThreadId:
            stdcall_ret(c, 0, c->thread_id ? c->thread_id : 1000u); return 1;
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetEnvironmentStrings:
            stdcall_ret(c, 0, g_env_a_va); return 1;
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetEnvironmentStringsW:
            stdcall_ret(c, 0, g_env_w_va); return 1;
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_FreeEnvironmentStringsA:
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_FreeEnvironmentStringsW:
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 1); return 1;
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetEnvironmentVariableA:
            stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0); return 1;
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_SetEnvironmentVariableA:
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, 1); return 1;
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_SetHandleCount:
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, arg_u32(c, 0)); return 1;
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetStdHandle: {
            uint32_t which = arg_u32(c, 0);
            uint32_t h = (which == (uint32_t)-10) ? 1 : (which == (uint32_t)-11) ? 2 : 3;
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, h); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_SetStdHandle:
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, 1); return 1;
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_LoadLibraryA: {
            const char *name = arg_cstr(c, 0);
            if (g_debug_log) fprintf(stderr, "[KERNEL32] LoadLibraryA(%s)\n", name ? name : "NULL");
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0x00400000u); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetProcAddress: {
            const char *name = arg_cstr(c, 1);
            if (g_debug_log) fprintf(stderr, "[KERNEL32] GetProcAddress(%s)\n", name ? name : "NULL");
            /* Check if name matches any known import */
            if (name && arg_u32(c, 1) > 0xFFFFu) {
                for (uint32_t i = 0; i < MHA2_IMPORT_COUNT; i++) {
                    if (strcmp(g_imports[i].name, name) == 0) {
                        stdcall_ret(c, 2, HLE_WIN32_BASE + g_imports[i].id);
                        return 1;
                    }
                }
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_ExitProcess:
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_TerminateProcess: {
            uint32_t code = (import_id == IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_TerminateProcess)
                            ? arg_u32(c, 1) : arg_u32(c, 0);
            win32_dsound_shutdown();
            hd_assets_shutdown();
            /* OpenGL/SDL teardown must only run from the thread owning the GL context */
            if (!gl_renderer_is_active() || gl_renderer_is_gl_thread()) {
                gl_renderer_shutdown();
                SDL_Quit();
            }
            exit((int)code);
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_OutputDebugStringA: {
            const char *s = arg_cstr(c, 0);
            if (s && g_debug_log) fprintf(stderr, "[DBG] %s", s);
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetLastError:
            stdcall_ret(c, 0, win32_get_last_error()); return 1;
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_SetLastError:
            win32_set_last_error(arg_u32(c, 0));
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return 1;
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_SetUnhandledExceptionFilter:
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return 1;
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_UnhandledExceptionFilter:
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 1); return 1;
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_RaiseException:
            fprintf(stderr, "FATAL: guest RaiseException(code=0x%08x)\n", arg_u32(c, 0));
            abort();
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_RtlUnwind:
            stdcall_ret(c, 4, 0); return 1;

        /* ---- Atomics & TLS ---- */
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_InterlockedIncrement: {
            uint32_t p = arg_u32(c, 0);
            int32_t v = __atomic_add_fetch((int32_t *)(g_mem + p), 1, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST);
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, (uint32_t)v); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_InterlockedDecrement: {
            uint32_t p = arg_u32(c, 0);
            int32_t v = __atomic_sub_fetch((int32_t *)(g_mem + p), 1, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST);
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, (uint32_t)v); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_TlsAlloc: {
            pthread_mutex_lock(&g_tls_mtx);
            uint32_t idx = g_tls_next++;
            pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_tls_mtx);
            stdcall_ret(c, 0, idx); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_TlsGetValue: {
            uint32_t idx = arg_u32(c, 0);
            uint32_t val = (idx < 64 && c->teb_addr) ? mem_r32(c->teb_addr + 0xE10u + idx * 4u) : 0;
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, val); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_TlsSetValue: {
            uint32_t idx = arg_u32(c, 0);
            uint32_t val = arg_u32(c, 1);
            if (idx < 64 && c->teb_addr) mem_w32(c->teb_addr + 0xE10u + idx * 4u, val);
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, 1); return 1;
        }

        /* ---- Critical Sections ---- */
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_InitializeCriticalSection: {
            uint32_t cs = arg_u32(c, 0);
            mem_w32(cs, 0);
            get_or_init_cs(cs);
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_EnterCriticalSection: {
            pthread_mutex_t *m = get_or_init_cs(arg_u32(c, 0));
            if (m) pthread_mutex_lock(m);
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_LeaveCriticalSection: {
            pthread_mutex_t *m = get_or_init_cs(arg_u32(c, 0));
            if (m) pthread_mutex_unlock(m);
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_DeleteCriticalSection: {
            uint32_t cs = arg_u32(c, 0);
            pthread_mutex_lock(&g_cs_alloc_mtx);
            uint32_t id = mem_r32(cs);
            if (id >= 1 && id < MAX_CS && g_cs_used[id]) {
                pthread_mutex_destroy(&g_cs_pool[id]);
                g_cs_used[id] = 0;
                mem_w32(cs, 0);
            }
            pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_cs_alloc_mtx);
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return 1;
        }

        /* ---- Threads & Sync ---- */
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_CreateThread: {
            uint32_t entry = arg_u32(c, 2);
            uint32_t param = arg_u32(c, 3);
            uint32_t flags = arg_u32(c, 4);
            uint32_t tid_ptr = arg_u32(c, 5);

            uint32_t h = alloc_handle(HT_THREAD);
            if (!h) {
                win32_set_last_error(8u /* ERROR_NOT_ENOUGH_MEMORY */);
                stdcall_ret(c, 6, 0); return 1;
            }
            ThreadObj *to = (ThreadObj *)calloc(1, sizeof(ThreadObj));
            if (!to) {
                pthread_mutex_lock(&g_hdl_mtx);
                g_handles[h].type = HT_NONE;
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_hdl_mtx);
                stdcall_ret(c, 6, 0); return 1;
            }
            pthread_mutex_init(&to->mtx, NULL);
            pthread_cond_init(&to->cond, NULL);
            to->refcount = 2; /* 1 for handle, 1 for guest_thread_main */
            to->entry_va = entry;
            to->param_va = param;
            to->suspended = (flags & 0x4u) ? 1 : 0;
            to->tid = guest_alloc_thread_slot(&to->teb_va, &to->stack_top);
            if (!to->tid) {
                pthread_mutex_destroy(&to->mtx);
                pthread_cond_destroy(&to->cond);
                free(to);
                pthread_mutex_lock(&g_hdl_mtx);
                g_handles[h].type = HT_NONE;
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_hdl_mtx);
                stdcall_ret(c, 6, 0); return 1;
            }
            pthread_mutex_lock(&g_hdl_mtx);
            g_handles[h].thr = to;
            pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_hdl_mtx);
            if (tid_ptr) mem_w32(tid_ptr, to->tid);
            if (pthread_create(&to->pt, NULL, guest_thread_main, to) != 0) {
                pthread_mutex_lock(&g_hdl_mtx);
                g_handles[h].thr = NULL;
                g_handles[h].type = HT_NONE;
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_hdl_mtx);
                guest_free_thread_slot(to->tid);
                pthread_mutex_destroy(&to->mtx);
                pthread_cond_destroy(&to->cond);
                free(to);
                stdcall_ret(c, 6, 0); return 1;
            }
            if (g_debug_log) fprintf(stderr, "[KERNEL32] CreateThread(entry=0x%08x, param=0x%08x, flags=0x%x) -> h=0x%x tid=%u\n", entry, param, flags, h, to->tid);
            stdcall_ret(c, 6, h); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_ResumeThread: {
            uint32_t h = arg_u32(c, 0);
            pthread_mutex_lock(&g_hdl_mtx);
            WinHandle *wh = get_handle(h);
            ThreadObj *to = (wh && wh->type == HT_THREAD) ? wh->thr : NULL;
            if (to) __atomic_add_fetch(&to->refcount, 1, __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL);
            pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_hdl_mtx);
            uint32_t prev = 0;
            if (to) {
                pthread_mutex_lock(&to->mtx);
                prev = (uint32_t)to->suspended;
                to->suspended = 0;
                pthread_cond_broadcast(&to->cond);
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&to->mtx);
                thread_unref(to);
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, prev); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetExitCodeThread: {
            uint32_t h = arg_u32(c, 0);
            uint32_t out = arg_u32(c, 1);
            pthread_mutex_lock(&g_hdl_mtx);
            WinHandle *wh = get_handle(h);
            ThreadObj *to = (wh && wh->type == HT_THREAD) ? wh->thr : NULL;
            if (to) __atomic_add_fetch(&to->refcount, 1, __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL);
            pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_hdl_mtx);
            if (to && out) {
                pthread_mutex_lock(&to->mtx);
                uint32_t code = to->finished ? to->exit_code : 259u /* STILL_ACTIVE */;
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&to->mtx);
                mem_w32(out, code);
                thread_unref(to);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 1); return 1;
            }
            if (to) thread_unref(to);
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_ExitThread: {
            uint32_t code = arg_u32(c, 0);
            if (tl_thread_obj) {
                ThreadObj *to = tl_thread_obj;
                tl_thread_obj = NULL;
                pthread_mutex_lock(&to->mtx);
                to->exit_code = code;
                to->finished = 1;
                pthread_cond_broadcast(&to->cond);
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&to->mtx);
                thread_unref(to);
            }
            pthread_exit(NULL);
            return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_CreateEventA: {
            uint32_t h = alloc_handle(HT_EVENT);
            if (!h) { stdcall_ret(c, 4, 0); return 1; }
            SyncObj *so = (SyncObj *)calloc(1, sizeof(SyncObj));
            pthread_mutex_init(&so->mtx, NULL);
            pthread_cond_init(&so->cond, NULL);
            so->refcount = 1;
            so->manual_reset = arg_u32(c, 1) ? 1 : 0;
            so->signaled = arg_u32(c, 2) ? 1 : 0;
            g_handles[h].sync = so;
            stdcall_ret(c, 4, h); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_ResetEvent: {
            pthread_mutex_lock(&g_hdl_mtx);
            WinHandle *wh = get_handle(arg_u32(c, 0));
            SyncObj *so = (wh && wh->type == HT_EVENT) ? wh->sync : NULL;
            if (so) __atomic_add_fetch(&so->refcount, 1, __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL);
            pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_hdl_mtx);
            if (so) {
                pthread_mutex_lock(&so->mtx);
                so->signaled = 0;
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&so->mtx);
                sync_unref(so);
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 1); return 1;
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_CreateSemaphoreA: {
            uint32_t h = alloc_handle(HT_SEMAPHORE);
            if (!h) { stdcall_ret(c, 4, 0); return 1; }
            SyncObj *so = (SyncObj *)calloc(1, sizeof(SyncObj));
            pthread_mutex_init(&so->mtx, NULL);
            pthread_cond_init(&so->cond, NULL);
            so->refcount = 1;
            so->count = arg_i32(c, 1);
            so->max_count = arg_i32(c, 2);
            g_handles[h].sync = so;
            stdcall_ret(c, 4, h); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_ReleaseSemaphore: {
            pthread_mutex_lock(&g_hdl_mtx);
            WinHandle *wh = get_handle(arg_u32(c, 0));
            SyncObj *so = (wh && wh->type == HT_SEMAPHORE) ? wh->sync : NULL;
            if (so) __atomic_add_fetch(&so->refcount, 1, __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL);
            pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_hdl_mtx);
            int32_t rel = arg_i32(c, 1);
            uint32_t prev_ptr = arg_u32(c, 2);
            if (so) {
                pthread_mutex_lock(&so->mtx);
                if (prev_ptr) mem_w32(prev_ptr, (uint32_t)so->count);
                so->count += rel;
                if (so->count > so->max_count) so->count = so->max_count;
                pthread_cond_broadcast(&so->cond);
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&so->mtx);
                sync_unref(so);
                stdcall_ret(c, 3, 1); return 1;
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_CreateMutexA: {
            uint32_t h = alloc_handle(HT_MUTEX);
            if (!h) { stdcall_ret(c, 3, 0); return 1; }
            SyncObj *so = (SyncObj *)calloc(1, sizeof(SyncObj));
            pthread_mutex_init(&so->mtx, NULL);
            pthread_cond_init(&so->cond, NULL);
            so->refcount = 1;
            if (arg_u32(c, 1)) {
                so->owner_tid = (int)(c->thread_id ? c->thread_id : 1000u);
                so->lock_depth = 1;
            }
            g_handles[h].sync = so;
            stdcall_ret(c, 3, h); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_ReleaseMutex: {
            pthread_mutex_lock(&g_hdl_mtx);
            WinHandle *wh = get_handle(arg_u32(c, 0));
            SyncObj *so = (wh && wh->type == HT_MUTEX) ? wh->sync : NULL;
            if (so) __atomic_add_fetch(&so->refcount, 1, __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL);
            pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_hdl_mtx);
            if (so) {
                pthread_mutex_lock(&so->mtx);
                if (so->lock_depth > 0) {
                    so->lock_depth--;
                    if (so->lock_depth == 0) {
                        so->owner_tid = 0;
                        pthread_cond_signal(&so->cond);
                    }
                }
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&so->mtx);
                sync_unref(so);
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 1); return 1;
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_WaitForSingleObject: {
            uint32_t h = arg_u32(c, 0);
            uint32_t ms = arg_u32(c, 1);
            pthread_mutex_lock(&g_hdl_mtx);
            WinHandle *wh = get_handle(h);
            enum HandleType htype = wh ? wh->type : HT_NONE;
            SyncObj *so = (wh && (htype == HT_EVENT || htype == HT_SEMAPHORE || htype == HT_MUTEX)) ? wh->sync : NULL;
            ThreadObj *to = (wh && htype == HT_THREAD) ? wh->thr : NULL;
            if (so) __atomic_add_fetch(&so->refcount, 1, __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL);
            if (to) __atomic_add_fetch(&to->refcount, 1, __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL);
            pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_hdl_mtx);
            if (htype == HT_NONE) { stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0xFFFFFFFFu); return 1; }

            if (htype == HT_THREAD && to) {
                pthread_mutex_lock(&to->mtx);
                uint32_t res = 0;
                if (!to->finished) {
                    if (ms == 0) res = 0x102u;
                    else if (ms == 0xFFFFFFFFu) {
                        while (!to->finished) pthread_cond_wait(&to->cond, &to->mtx);
                    } else {
                        struct timespec ts;
                        make_abs_timeout(&ts, ms);
                        while (!to->finished) {
                            if (pthread_cond_timedwait(&to->cond, &to->mtx, &ts) == ETIMEDOUT) {
                                res = 0x102u; break;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&to->mtx);
                thread_unref(to);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, res); return 1;
            }
            if (htype == HT_SEMAPHORE && so) {
                pthread_mutex_lock(&so->mtx);
                uint32_t res = 0;
                if (so->count <= 0) {
                    if (ms == 0) res = 0x102u;
                    else if (ms == 0xFFFFFFFFu) {
                        while (so->count <= 0) pthread_cond_wait(&so->cond, &so->mtx);
                    } else {
                        struct timespec ts;
                        make_abs_timeout(&ts, ms);
                        while (so->count <= 0) {
                            if (pthread_cond_timedwait(&so->cond, &so->mtx, &ts) == ETIMEDOUT) {
                                res = 0x102u; break;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                if (res == 0 && so->count > 0) so->count--;
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&so->mtx);
                sync_unref(so);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, res); return 1;
            }
            if (htype == HT_EVENT && so) {
                pthread_mutex_lock(&so->mtx);
                uint32_t res = 0;
                if (!so->signaled) {
                    if (ms == 0) res = 0x102u;
                    else if (ms == 0xFFFFFFFFu) {
                        while (!so->signaled) pthread_cond_wait(&so->cond, &so->mtx);
                    } else {
                        struct timespec ts;
                        make_abs_timeout(&ts, ms);
                        while (!so->signaled) {
                            if (pthread_cond_timedwait(&so->cond, &so->mtx, &ts) == ETIMEDOUT) {
                                res = 0x102u; break;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                if (res == 0 && !so->manual_reset) so->signaled = 0;
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&so->mtx);
                sync_unref(so);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, res); return 1;
            }
            if (htype == HT_MUTEX && so) {
                int mytid = (int)(c->thread_id ? c->thread_id : 1000u);
                pthread_mutex_lock(&so->mtx);
                uint32_t res = 0;
                if (so->owner_tid != 0 && so->owner_tid != mytid) {
                    if (ms == 0) res = 0x102u;
                    else if (ms == 0xFFFFFFFFu) {
                        while (so->owner_tid != 0 && so->owner_tid != mytid)
                            pthread_cond_wait(&so->cond, &so->mtx);
                    } else {
                        struct timespec ts;
                        make_abs_timeout(&ts, ms);
                        while (so->owner_tid != 0 && so->owner_tid != mytid) {
                            if (pthread_cond_timedwait(&so->cond, &so->mtx, &ts) == ETIMEDOUT) {
                                res = 0x102u; break;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                if (res == 0) {
                    so->owner_tid = mytid;
                    so->lock_depth++;
                }
                pthread_mutex_unlock(&so->mtx);
                sync_unref(so);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, res); return 1;
            }
            if (so) sync_unref(so);
            if (to) thread_unref(to);
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return 1;
        }

        /* ---- Timers ---- */
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetTickCount: {
            uint64_t ms = guest_elapsed_ns() / 1000000ull;
            stdcall_ret(c, 0, (uint32_t)(ms + 1000u)); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_QueryPerformanceFrequency: {
            uint32_t p = arg_u32(c, 0);
            if (p) mem_w64(p, 1000000000ull);
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 1); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_QueryPerformanceCounter: {
            uint32_t p = arg_u32(c, 0);
            if (p) mem_w64(p, guest_elapsed_ns());
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 1); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_Sleep: {
            uint32_t ret_va = mem_r32(c->esp);
            uint32_t ms = arg_u32(c, 0);
            /* Original game bugfix (0x00453cd0): loading screen sleeps (250 - p1 - p2) ms
               every tick (50..250ms artificial stall). Cap to 4ms for snappy room loads. */
            if (ret_va == 0x00453f87u && ms > 4u) {
                ms = 4u;
            }
            if (ms == 0 || (ms <= 4 && c->thread_id == 1000u && gl_renderer_is_active())) {
                /* High-refresh-rate frame pacing is handled smoothly by OpenGL VSync / pacer */
                sched_yield();
            } else {
                usleep(ms * 1000u);
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return 1;
        }

        /* ---- File I/O ---- */
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_CreateFileA: {
            const char *fname = arg_cstr(c, 0);
            uint32_t access = arg_u32(c, 1);
            uint32_t disp = arg_u32(c, 4);
            int write_mode = (access & 0x40000000u) != 0;
            int read_mode  = (access & 0x80000000u) != 0;
            int oflags = (read_mode && write_mode) ? O_RDWR : (write_mode ? O_WRONLY : O_RDONLY);
            if (disp == 1) oflags |= O_CREAT | O_EXCL;        /* CREATE_NEW */
            else if (disp == 2) oflags |= O_CREAT | O_TRUNC;  /* CREATE_ALWAYS */
            else if (disp == 4) oflags |= O_CREAT;            /* OPEN_ALWAYS */
            else if (disp == 5) oflags |= O_TRUNC;            /* TRUNCATE_EXISTING */

            char lpath[1024];
            resolve_game_path(fname, lpath, sizeof(lpath), write_mode || (oflags & O_CREAT));
            int fd = open(lpath, oflags, 0644);
            if (g_debug_log) fprintf(stderr, "[FILE] CreateFileA('%s' -> '%s', acc=0x%x, disp=%u) -> fd=%d\n", fname ? fname : "", lpath, access, disp, fd);
            if (fd < 0) {
                win32_set_last_error(errno == ENOENT ? 2u : 5u);
                stdcall_ret(c, 7, 0xFFFFFFFFu); return 1;
            }
            uint32_t h = alloc_handle(HT_FILE);
            if (!h) {
                close(fd);
                win32_set_last_error(4u /* ERROR_TOO_MANY_OPEN_FILES */);
                stdcall_ret(c, 7, 0xFFFFFFFFu); return 1;
            }
            g_handles[h].fd = fd;
            win32_set_last_error(0);
            stdcall_ret(c, 7, h); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_ReadFile: {
            uint32_t ret_va = mem_r32(c->esp);
            WinHandle *wh = get_handle(arg_u32(c, 0));
            uint32_t buf = arg_u32(c, 1);
            uint32_t n = arg_u32(c, 2);
            uint32_t out_n = arg_u32(c, 3);
            if (!wh || wh->type != HT_FILE || wh->fd < 0) { stdcall_ret(c, 5, 0); return 1; }
            ssize_t r = read(wh->fd, g_mem + buf, n);
            if (r < 0) { stdcall_ret(c, 5, 0); return 1; }
            if (out_n) mem_w32(out_n, (uint32_t)r);
            /* Original game bugfix (0x0044a200): savegame/encrypted loader loops forever
               on truncated files because it never checks bytesRead == 0 at EOF. */
            if (r == 0 && n > 0 && (ret_va == 0x0044a286u || ret_va == 0x0044a2aeu)) {
                stdcall_ret(c, 5, 0); return 1;
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 5, 1); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_WriteFile: {
            WinHandle *wh = get_handle(arg_u32(c, 0));
            uint32_t buf = arg_u32(c, 1);
            uint32_t n = arg_u32(c, 2);
            uint32_t out_n = arg_u32(c, 3);
            if (!wh || wh->type != HT_FILE || wh->fd < 0) { stdcall_ret(c, 5, 0); return 1; }
            ssize_t w = write(wh->fd, g_mem + buf, n);
            if (w < 0) { stdcall_ret(c, 5, 0); return 1; }
            if (out_n) mem_w32(out_n, (uint32_t)w);
            stdcall_ret(c, 5, 1); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_SetFilePointer: {
            WinHandle *wh = get_handle(arg_u32(c, 0));
            int32_t dist = arg_i32(c, 1);
            uint32_t high_ptr = arg_u32(c, 2);
            uint32_t method = arg_u32(c, 3);
            if (!wh || wh->type != HT_FILE || wh->fd < 0) { stdcall_ret(c, 4, 0xFFFFFFFFu); return 1; }
            off_t off = dist;
            if (high_ptr) off |= ((off_t)(int32_t)mem_r32(high_ptr)) << 32;
            int whence = (method == 0) ? SEEK_SET : (method == 1) ? SEEK_CUR : SEEK_END;
            off_t res = lseek(wh->fd, off, whence);
            if (res < 0) { stdcall_ret(c, 4, 0xFFFFFFFFu); return 1; }
            if (high_ptr) mem_w32(high_ptr, (uint32_t)((uint64_t)res >> 32));
            stdcall_ret(c, 4, (uint32_t)res); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_SetEndOfFile: {
            WinHandle *wh = get_handle(arg_u32(c, 0));
            if (wh && wh->type == HT_FILE && wh->fd >= 0) {
                off_t cur = lseek(wh->fd, 0, SEEK_CUR);
                if (cur >= 0 && ftruncate(wh->fd, cur) == 0) {
                    stdcall_ret(c, 1, 1); return 1;
                }
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetFileSize: {
            uint32_t ret_va = mem_r32(c->esp);
            WinHandle *wh = get_handle(arg_u32(c, 0));
            uint32_t high_ptr = arg_u32(c, 1);
            if (!wh || wh->type != HT_FILE || wh->fd < 0) {
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, (ret_va == 0x0044a236u) ? 0u : 0xFFFFFFFFu); return 1;
            }
            struct stat st;
            if (fstat(wh->fd, &st) != 0) {
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, (ret_va == 0x0044a236u) ? 0u : 0xFFFFFFFFu); return 1;
            }
            /* Original game bugfix (0x0044a200): reject absurd sizes before operator_new(size+0x20) */
            if (ret_va == 0x0044a236u && (st.st_size < 0 || st.st_size > 0x08000000)) {
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0u); return 1;
            }
            if (high_ptr) mem_w32(high_ptr, (uint32_t)((uint64_t)st.st_size >> 32));
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, (uint32_t)st.st_size); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetFileType: {
            uint32_t h = arg_u32(c, 0);
            if (h <= 3) { stdcall_ret(c, 1, 2u /* FILE_TYPE_CHAR */); return 1; }
            WinHandle *wh = get_handle(h);
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, (wh && wh->type == HT_FILE) ? 1u /* FILE_TYPE_DISK */ : 0u);
            return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_FlushFileBuffers: {
            WinHandle *wh = get_handle(arg_u32(c, 0));
            if (wh && wh->type == HT_FILE && wh->fd >= 3) fsync(wh->fd);
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 1); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_CloseHandle: {
            uint32_t h = arg_u32(c, 0);
            pthread_mutex_lock(&g_hdl_mtx);
            WinHandle *wh = get_handle(h);
            int ok = 0;
            int fd_to_close = -1;
            SyncObj *so_to_unref = NULL;
            ThreadObj *to_detach = NULL;
            if (wh && h >= 0x100) {
                ok = 1;
                if (wh->type == HT_FILE && wh->fd >= 0) fd_to_close = wh->fd;
                so_to_unref = wh->sync;
                to_detach = wh->thr;
                wh->type = HT_NONE;
                wh->sync = NULL;
                wh->thr = NULL;
                wh->fd = -1;
            }
            pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_hdl_mtx);
            if (fd_to_close >= 0) close(fd_to_close);
            if (so_to_unref) sync_unref(so_to_unref);
            if (to_detach) {
                pthread_detach(to_detach->pt);
                thread_unref(to_detach);
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, ok ? 1u : 0u); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetFileAttributesA: {
            const char *fn = arg_cstr(c, 0);
            char lpath[1024];
            resolve_game_path(fn, lpath, sizeof(lpath), 0);
            struct stat st;
            if (stat(lpath, &st) != 0) {
                win32_set_last_error(2);
                stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0xFFFFFFFFu); return 1;
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, S_ISDIR(st.st_mode) ? 0x10u : 0x80u); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_CreateDirectoryA: {
            const char *fn = arg_cstr(c, 0);
            char lpath[1024];
            resolve_game_path(fn, lpath, sizeof(lpath), 1);
            int r = mkdir(lpath, 0755);
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, (r == 0 || errno == EEXIST) ? 1 : 0); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetCurrentDirectoryA: {
            uint32_t bsz = arg_u32(c, 0);
            uint32_t buf = arg_u32(c, 1);
            const char *cwd = "C:\\mha2";
            uint32_t len = (uint32_t)snprintf((char *)(g_mem + buf), bsz, "%s", cwd);
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, len); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetFullPathNameA: {
            const char *fn = arg_cstr(c, 0);
            uint32_t bsz = arg_u32(c, 1);
            uint32_t buf = arg_u32(c, 2);
            uint32_t fpart = arg_u32(c, 3);
            uint32_t len = (uint32_t)snprintf((char *)(g_mem + buf), bsz, "%s", fn ? fn : "");
            if (fpart) {
                const char *s = (const char *)(g_mem + buf);
                const char *sl = strrchr(s, '\\');
                const char *sl2 = strrchr(s, '/');
                if (sl2 > sl) sl = sl2;
                mem_w32(fpart, sl ? (buf + (uint32_t)(sl - s) + 1u) : buf);
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 4, len); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetDriveTypeA:
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 3u /* DRIVE_FIXED */); return 1;
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_CompareFileTime: {
            uint64_t a = mem_r64(arg_u32(c, 0));
            uint64_t b = mem_r64(arg_u32(c, 1));
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, (a < b) ? 0xFFFFFFFFu : (a > b ? 1u : 0u)); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_FindFirstFileA: {
            const char *pat = arg_cstr(c, 0);
            uint32_t dst = arg_u32(c, 1);
            char clean[1024];
            size_t j = 0;
            for (const char *p = pat ? pat : ""; *p && j + 1 < sizeof(clean); p++)
                clean[j++] = (*p == '\\') ? '/' : *p;
            clean[j] = '\0';
            char *sl = strrchr(clean, '/');
            char dir_win[1024], mask[256];
            if (sl) {
                *sl = '\0';
                snprintf(dir_win, sizeof(dir_win), "%s", clean);
                snprintf(mask, sizeof(mask), "%s", sl + 1);
            } else {
                dir_win[0] = '\0';
                snprintf(mask, sizeof(mask), "%.*s", (int)(sizeof(mask) - 1), clean);
            }
            char ldir[1024];
            resolve_game_path(dir_win, ldir, sizeof(ldir), 0);
            DIR *d = opendir(ldir);
            if (!d) {
                win32_set_last_error(2);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0xFFFFFFFFu); return 1;
            }
            FindState *fs = (FindState *)calloc(1, sizeof(FindState));
            snprintf(fs->dir_path, sizeof(fs->dir_path), "%s", ldir);
            snprintf(fs->pattern, sizeof(fs->pattern), "%s", mask);
            fs->dir = d;
            if (!find_next_match(fs, dst)) {
                closedir(d); free(fs);
                win32_set_last_error(2);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0xFFFFFFFFu); return 1;
            }
            /* Original game bugfix (0x00447820): startup data\g directory probe jumps past
               FindClose when FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY (0x10) is set, leaking DIR* forever. */
            uint32_t ret_va = mem_r32(c->esp);
            if (ret_va >= 0x00447820u && ret_va < 0x00448600u && dst && (mem_r32(dst) & 0x10u)) {
                closedir(d); free(fs);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0x000000FEu); return 1;
            }
            uint32_t h = alloc_handle(HT_FIND);
            if (!h) {
                closedir(d); free(fs);
                win32_set_last_error(4u);
                stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0xFFFFFFFFu); return 1;
            }
            g_handles[h].find = fs;
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, h); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_FindNextFileA: {
            WinHandle *wh = get_handle(arg_u32(c, 0));
            uint32_t dst = arg_u32(c, 1);
            if (!wh || wh->type != HT_FIND || !wh->find) { stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0); return 1; }
            int ok = find_next_match(wh->find, dst);
            if (!ok) win32_set_last_error(18); /* ERROR_NO_MORE_FILES */
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, ok ? 1u : 0u); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_FindClose: {
            uint32_t h = arg_u32(c, 0);
            FindState *fs = NULL;
            pthread_mutex_lock(&g_hdl_mtx);
            WinHandle *wh = get_handle(h);
            if (wh && wh->type == HT_FIND && wh->find) {
                fs = wh->find;
                wh->find = NULL;
                wh->type = HT_NONE;
            }
            pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_hdl_mtx);
            if (fs) {
                if (fs->dir) closedir(fs->dir);
                free(fs);
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 1); return 1;
        }

        /* ---- Codepage / Locale / String Conversion ---- */
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetACP:
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetOEMCP:
            stdcall_ret(c, 0, 1252u); return 1;
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetCPInfo: {
            uint32_t p = arg_u32(c, 1);
            if (p) {
                memset(g_mem + p, 0, 20);
                mem_w32(p + 0, 1);
                mem_w8(p + 4, '?');
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, 1); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_MultiByteToWideChar: {
            uint32_t src_va = arg_u32(c, 2);
            int32_t  src_len = arg_i32(c, 3);
            uint32_t dst_va = arg_u32(c, 4);
            int32_t  dst_len = arg_i32(c, 5);
            const uint8_t *s = g_mem + src_va;
            int32_t n = (src_len < 0) ? (int32_t)strlen((const char *)s) + 1 : src_len;
            if (dst_len == 0) { stdcall_ret(c, 6, (uint32_t)n); return 1; }
            int32_t m = n < dst_len ? n : dst_len;
            for (int32_t i = 0; i < m; i++) mem_w16(dst_va + (uint32_t)i * 2u, s[i]);
            stdcall_ret(c, 6, (uint32_t)m); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_WideCharToMultiByte: {
            uint32_t src_va = arg_u32(c, 2);
            int32_t  src_len = arg_i32(c, 3);
            uint32_t dst_va = arg_u32(c, 4);
            int32_t  dst_len = arg_i32(c, 5);
            int32_t n = src_len;
            if (n < 0) {
                n = 0;
                while (mem_r16(src_va + (uint32_t)n * 2u) != 0) n++;
                n++;
            }
            if (dst_len == 0) { stdcall_ret(c, 8, (uint32_t)n); return 1; }
            int32_t m = n < dst_len ? n : dst_len;
            for (int32_t i = 0; i < m; i++) {
                uint16_t ch = mem_r16(src_va + (uint32_t)i * 2u);
                mem_w8(dst_va + (uint32_t)i, ch < 256 ? (uint8_t)ch : '?');
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 8, (uint32_t)m); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetStringTypeA: {
            uint32_t src_va = arg_u32(c, 2);
            int32_t  n = arg_i32(c, 3);
            uint32_t dst_va = arg_u32(c, 4);
            if (n < 0) n = (int32_t)strlen((const char *)(g_mem + src_va)) + 1;
            for (int32_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                unsigned char ch = g_mem[src_va + i];
                uint16_t t = 0;
                if (isupper(ch)) t |= 0x0001;
                if (islower(ch)) t |= 0x0002;
                if (isdigit(ch)) t |= 0x0004;
                if (isspace(ch)) t |= 0x0008;
                if (ispunct(ch)) t |= 0x0010;
                if (iscntrl(ch)) t |= 0x0020;
                if (ch == ' ' || ch == '\t') t |= 0x0040;
                if (isxdigit(ch)) t |= 0x0080;
                if (isalpha(ch)) t |= 0x0100;
                mem_w16(dst_va + (uint32_t)i * 2u, t);
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 5, 1); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_GetStringTypeW: {
            uint32_t src_va = arg_u32(c, 1);
            int32_t  n = arg_i32(c, 2);
            uint32_t dst_va = arg_u32(c, 3);
            for (int32_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                uint16_t ch = mem_r16(src_va + (uint32_t)i * 2u);
                uint16_t t = 0;
                if (ch < 128) {
                    if (isupper(ch)) t |= 0x0001;
                    if (islower(ch)) t |= 0x0002;
                    if (isdigit(ch)) t |= 0x0004;
                    if (isspace(ch)) t |= 0x0008;
                    if (ispunct(ch)) t |= 0x0010;
                    if (iscntrl(ch)) t |= 0x0020;
                    if (ch == ' ' || ch == '\t') t |= 0x0040;
                    if (isxdigit(ch)) t |= 0x0080;
                    if (isalpha(ch)) t |= 0x0100;
                }
                mem_w16(dst_va + (uint32_t)i * 2u, t);
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 4, 1); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_LCMapStringA: {
            uint32_t flags = arg_u32(c, 1);
            uint32_t src = arg_u32(c, 2);
            int32_t  sn = arg_i32(c, 3);
            uint32_t dst = arg_u32(c, 4);
            int32_t  dn = arg_i32(c, 5);
            if (sn < 0) sn = (int32_t)strlen((const char *)(g_mem + src)) + 1;
            if (dn == 0) { stdcall_ret(c, 6, (uint32_t)sn); return 1; }
            int32_t m = sn < dn ? sn : dn;
            for (int32_t i = 0; i < m; i++) {
                unsigned char ch = g_mem[src + i];
                if (flags & 0x100u) ch = (unsigned char)tolower(ch);
                else if (flags & 0x200u) ch = (unsigned char)toupper(ch);
                g_mem[dst + i] = ch;
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 6, (uint32_t)m); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_LCMapStringW: {
            uint32_t flags = arg_u32(c, 1);
            uint32_t src = arg_u32(c, 2);
            int32_t  sn = arg_i32(c, 3);
            uint32_t dst = arg_u32(c, 4);
            int32_t  dn = arg_i32(c, 5);
            if (sn < 0) { sn = 0; while (mem_r16(src + (uint32_t)sn * 2u)) sn++; sn++; }
            if (dn == 0) { stdcall_ret(c, 6, (uint32_t)sn); return 1; }
            int32_t m = sn < dn ? sn : dn;
            for (int32_t i = 0; i < m; i++) {
                uint16_t ch = mem_r16(src + (uint32_t)i * 2u);
                if (ch < 128) {
                    if (flags & 0x100u) ch = (uint16_t)tolower(ch);
                    else if (flags & 0x200u) ch = (uint16_t)toupper(ch);
                }
                mem_w16(dst + (uint32_t)i * 2u, ch);
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 6, (uint32_t)m); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_CompareStringA: {
            uint32_t flags = arg_u32(c, 1);
            const char *s1 = arg_cstr(c, 2);
            int32_t n1 = arg_i32(c, 3);
            const char *s2 = arg_cstr(c, 4);
            int32_t n2 = arg_i32(c, 5);
            if (n1 < 0) n1 = (int32_t)strlen(s1);
            if (n2 < 0) n2 = (int32_t)strlen(s2);
            int m = n1 < n2 ? n1 : n2;
            int cmp = (flags & 1u) ? strncasecmp(s1, s2, (size_t)m) : strncmp(s1, s2, (size_t)m);
            if (cmp == 0) cmp = n1 - n2;
            stdcall_ret(c, 6, cmp < 0 ? 1u : (cmp > 0 ? 3u : 2u)); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_KERNEL32_DLL_CompareStringW: {
            uint32_t flags = arg_u32(c, 1);
            uint32_t s1 = arg_u32(c, 2);
            int32_t n1 = arg_i32(c, 3);
            uint32_t s2 = arg_u32(c, 4);
            int32_t n2 = arg_i32(c, 5);
            if (n1 < 0) { n1 = 0; while (mem_r16(s1 + (uint32_t)n1 * 2u)) n1++; }
            if (n2 < 0) { n2 = 0; while (mem_r16(s2 + (uint32_t)n2 * 2u)) n2++; }
            int m = n1 < n2 ? n1 : n2;
            int cmp = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
                uint16_t a = mem_r16(s1 + (uint32_t)i * 2u);
                uint16_t b = mem_r16(s2 + (uint32_t)i * 2u);
                if (flags & 1u) { if (a < 128) a = (uint16_t)tolower(a); if (b < 128) b = (uint16_t)tolower(b); }
                if (a != b) { cmp = (int)a - (int)b; break; }
            }
            if (cmp == 0) cmp = n1 - n2;
            stdcall_ret(c, 6, cmp < 0 ? 1u : (cmp > 0 ? 3u : 2u)); return 1;
        }

        /* ---- SHELL32 & OLE32 ---- */
        case IMP_SHELL32_DLL_ShellExecuteA: {
            const char *file = arg_cstr(c, 2);
            fprintf(stderr, "[SHELL32] ShellExecuteA(%s)\n", file ? file : "");
            stdcall_ret(c, 6, 33u); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_OLE32_DLL_CoInitialize:
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return 1;
        case IMP_OLE32_DLL_CoUninitialize:
            stdcall_ret(c, 0, 0); return 1;
        case IMP_OLE32_DLL_CoCreateInstance: {
            uint32_t clsid_va = arg_u32(c, 0);
            uint32_t ppv_va   = arg_u32(c, 4);
            uint32_t d1 = mem_r32(clsid_va);
            if (g_debug_log) fprintf(stderr, "[OLE32] CoCreateInstance(clsid.d1=0x%08x)\n", d1);
            if (d1 == 0xe436ebb3u) {
                /* CLSID_FilterGraph -> DirectShow AVI player */
                uint32_t obj = com_dshow_create_graph();
                if (ppv_va) mem_w32(ppv_va, obj);
                stdcall_ret(c, 5, 0); return 1;
            }
            /* For CLSID_DirectSound8 (0x3901cc3f) and CLSID_OverlayMixer (0xcd8743a1),
               return E_NOINTERFACE so the game cleanly uses DirectSound 7 / standard render! */
            if (ppv_va) mem_w32(ppv_va, 0);
            stdcall_ret(c, 5, 0x80040154u /* REGDB_E_CLASSNOTREG */); return 1;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

```

`runtime/win32_user_gdi.c`:

```c
#include "runtime.h"
#include "gl_renderer.h"
#include "imports.h"
#include <ctype.h>

SDL_Window   *g_sdl_window   = NULL;
SDL_Renderer *g_sdl_renderer = NULL;
int           g_win_w        = 800;
int           g_win_h        = 600;

static uint32_t g_wndproc_va  = 0;
static uint32_t g_main_hwnd   = 0x00010001u;
static uint32_t g_wnd_userdata = 0;

/* ---- Win32 Message Queue ---- */
typedef struct WinMsg {
    uint32_t hwnd;
    uint32_t msg;
    uint32_t wparam;
    uint32_t lparam;
    uint32_t time;
    int32_t  pt_x;
    int32_t  pt_y;
} WinMsg;

#define MSG_Q_CAP 512
static WinMsg g_msg_q[MSG_Q_CAP];
static int g_msg_head = 0, g_msg_tail = 0;
int g_mouse_x = 0, g_mouse_y = 0;
int g_os_cursor_hidden = 0;
static float g_mouse_fx = 0.0f, g_mouse_fy = 0.0f;

static void push_msg(uint32_t hwnd, uint32_t msg, uint32_t wp, uint32_t lp) {
    /* Coalesce consecutive WM_MOUSEMOVE events (just like real USER32) so
       500-1000Hz mice never overflow the original game's 9-msg/frame PeekMessage cap. */
    if (msg == 0x0200u && g_msg_head != g_msg_tail) {
        int prev = (g_msg_tail + MSG_Q_CAP - 1) % MSG_Q_CAP;
        if (g_msg_q[prev].msg == 0x0200u && g_msg_q[prev].hwnd == hwnd) {
            g_msg_q[prev].wparam = wp;
            g_msg_q[prev].lparam = lp;
            g_msg_q[prev].time = SDL_GetTicks();
            g_msg_q[prev].pt_x = g_mouse_x;
            g_msg_q[prev].pt_y = g_mouse_y;
            return;
        }
    }
    int next = (g_msg_tail + 1) % MSG_Q_CAP;
    if (next == g_msg_head) return; /* drop if full */
    WinMsg *m = &g_msg_q[g_msg_tail];
    m->hwnd = hwnd;
    m->msg = msg;
    m->wparam = wp;
    m->lparam = lp;
    m->time = SDL_GetTicks();
    m->pt_x = g_mouse_x;
    m->pt_y = g_mouse_y;
    g_msg_tail = next;
}

static int pop_msg(WinMsg *out, int remove) {
    if (g_msg_head == g_msg_tail) return 0;
    *out = g_msg_q[g_msg_head];
    if (remove) g_msg_head = (g_msg_head + 1) % MSG_Q_CAP;
    return 1;
}

static uint32_t sdl_key_to_vk(SDL_Keycode sym) {
    if (sym >= 'a' && sym <= 'z') return (uint32_t)(sym - 'a' + 'A');
    if (sym >= '0' && sym <= '9') return (uint32_t)sym;
    if (sym >= SDLK_F1 && sym <= SDLK_F12) return 0x70u + (uint32_t)(sym - SDLK_F1);
    switch (sym) {
        case SDLK_BACKSPACE: return 0x08;
        case SDLK_TAB:       return 0x09;
        case SDLK_RETURN:    return 0x0D;
        case SDLK_KP_ENTER:  return 0x0D;
        case SDLK_LSHIFT:
        case SDLK_RSHIFT:    return 0x10;
        case SDLK_LCTRL:
        case SDLK_RCTRL:     return 0x11;
        case SDLK_LALT:
        case SDLK_RALT:      return 0x12;
        case SDLK_PAUSE:     return 0x13;
        case SDLK_CAPSLOCK:  return 0x14;
        case SDLK_ESCAPE:    return 0x1B;
        case SDLK_SPACE:     return 0x20;
        case SDLK_PAGEUP:    return 0x21;
        case SDLK_PAGEDOWN:  return 0x22;
        case SDLK_END:       return 0x23;
        case SDLK_HOME:      return 0x24;
        case SDLK_LEFT:      return 0x25;
        case SDLK_UP:        return 0x26;
        case SDLK_RIGHT:     return 0x27;
        case SDLK_DOWN:      return 0x28;
        case SDLK_INSERT:    return 0x2D;
        case SDLK_DELETE:    return 0x2E;
        case SDLK_MINUS:     return 0xBD;
        case SDLK_EQUALS:    return 0xBB;
        case SDLK_PERIOD:    return 0xBE;
        case SDLK_COMMA:     return 0xBC;
        default: return 0;
    }
}

static SDL_GameController *g_gamepad = NULL;
static uint32_t g_gamepad_last_ms = 0;

static void gamepad_ensure_open(void) {
    if (g_gamepad && SDL_GameControllerGetAttached(g_gamepad)) return;
    if (g_gamepad) {
        SDL_GameControllerClose(g_gamepad);
        g_gamepad = NULL;
    }
    int n = SDL_NumJoysticks();
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        if (SDL_IsGameController(i)) {
            g_gamepad = SDL_GameControllerOpen(i);
            if (g_gamepad) {
                const char *nm = SDL_GameControllerName(g_gamepad);
                gl_renderer_show_toast("Controller Connected: %s", nm ? nm : "Gamepad");
                break;
            }
        }
    }
}

static void gamepad_update_cursor(void) {
    if (!g_gamepad || !SDL_GameControllerGetAttached(g_gamepad) || gl_menu_is_open()) return;
    uint32_t now = SDL_GetTicks();
    if (g_gamepad_last_ms == 0) { g_gamepad_last_ms = now; return; }
    uint32_t dt_ms = now - g_gamepad_last_ms;
    if (dt_ms < 4) return;
    if (dt_ms > 50) dt_ms = 50;
    g_gamepad_last_ms = now;

    float ax = (float)SDL_GameControllerGetAxis(g_gamepad, SDL_CONTROLLER_AXIS_LEFTX) / 32767.0f;
    float ay = (float)SDL_GameControllerGetAxis(g_gamepad, SDL_CONTROLLER_AXIS_LEFTY) / 32767.0f;
    float mag = sqrtf(ax * ax + ay * ay);
    if (mag < 0.18f) return;
    float norm = (mag - 0.18f) / 0.82f;
    if (norm > 1.0f) norm = 1.0f;
    float speed = (0.35f + 0.65f * norm * norm) * 460.0f * ((float)dt_ms * 0.001f);
    float nx = g_mouse_fx + (ax / mag) * speed;
    float ny = g_mouse_fy + (ay / mag) * speed;
    gl_renderer_clamp_gamepad_cursor(&nx, &ny);
    int gx = (int)(nx + 0.5f);
    int gy = (int)(ny + 0.5f);
    int dx = gx - g_mouse_x;
    int dy = gy - g_mouse_y;
    g_mouse_fx = nx; g_mouse_fy = ny;
    g_mouse_x = gx;  g_mouse_y = gy;
    gl_renderer_set_live_mouse(nx, ny, g_os_cursor_hidden);
    if (dx != 0 || dy != 0) {
        dinput_push_mouse_move(dx, dy, g_mouse_x, g_mouse_y);
        uint32_t lp = ((uint32_t)(uint16_t)g_mouse_y << 16) | (uint16_t)g_mouse_x;
        push_msg(g_main_hwnd, 0x0200u /* WM_MOUSEMOVE */, 0, lp);
    }
}

static uint32_t g_fast_walk_mask = 0; /* bit0: LShift, bit1: RShift, bit2: Pad Y, bit3: Pad LB */
static uint32_t g_hotspot_mask   = 0; /* bit0: Space, bit1: H, bit2: Pad X */

static void clear_held_qol_keys(void) {
    if (g_fast_walk_mask) {
        g_fast_walk_mask = 0;
        gl_renderer_set_fast_walk(0);
    }
    if (g_hotspot_mask) {
        g_hotspot_mask = 0;
        gl_renderer_set_hotspot_highlight(0);
    }
}

void win32_pump_sdl_events(void) {
    static int g_pad_init = 0;
    if (!g_pad_init) { g_pad_init = 1; gamepad_ensure_open(); }
    SDL_Event ev;
    while (SDL_PollEvent(&ev)) {
        if (ev.type == SDL_CONTROLLERDEVICEADDED) {
            gamepad_ensure_open();
            continue;
        }
        if (ev.type == SDL_CONTROLLERDEVICEREMOVED) {
            g_fast_walk_mask &= ~(4u | 8u);
            g_hotspot_mask   &= ~4u;
            gl_renderer_set_fast_walk(g_fast_walk_mask != 0);
            gl_renderer_set_hotspot_highlight(g_hotspot_mask != 0);
            if (g_gamepad && !SDL_GameControllerGetAttached(g_gamepad)) {
                SDL_GameControllerClose(g_gamepad);
                g_gamepad = NULL;
                gl_renderer_show_toast("Controller Disconnected");
            }
            continue;
        }
        if (ev.type == SDL_CONTROLLERBUTTONDOWN || ev.type == SDL_CONTROLLERBUTTONUP) {
            int down = (ev.type == SDL_CONTROLLERBUTTONDOWN);
            Uint8 btn = ev.cbutton.button;
            if (down && btn == SDL_CONTROLLER_BUTTON_BACK) {
                clear_held_qol_keys();
                gl_menu_toggle();
                continue;
            }
            if (gl_menu_is_open()) {
                if (down) {
                    SDL_Event synth;
                    memset(&synth, 0, sizeof(synth));
                    synth.type = SDL_KEYDOWN;
                    if (btn == SDL_CONTROLLER_BUTTON_DPAD_UP) synth.key.keysym.sym = SDLK_UP;
                    else if (btn == SDL_CONTROLLER_BUTTON_DPAD_DOWN) synth.key.keysym.sym = SDLK_DOWN;
                    else if (btn == SDL_CONTROLLER_BUTTON_DPAD_LEFT) synth.key.keysym.sym = SDLK_LEFT;
                    else if (btn == SDL_CONTROLLER_BUTTON_DPAD_RIGHT) synth.key.keysym.sym = SDLK_RIGHT;
                    else if (btn == SDL_CONTROLLER_BUTTON_A) synth.key.keysym.sym = SDLK_RETURN;
                    else if (btn == SDL_CONTROLLER_BUTTON_B || btn == SDL_CONTROLLER_BUTTON_START) synth.key.keysym.sym = SDLK_ESCAPE;
                    else if (btn == SDL_CONTROLLER_BUTTON_LEFTSHOULDER) synth.key.keysym.sym = SDLK_q;
                    else if (btn == SDL_CONTROLLER_BUTTON_RIGHTSHOULDER) synth.key.keysym.sym = SDLK_e;
                    if (synth.key.keysym.sym) gl_menu_handle_event(&synth);
                }
                continue;
            }
            uint32_t lp = ((uint32_t)(uint16_t)g_mouse_y << 16) | (uint16_t)g_mouse_x;
            if (btn == SDL_CONTROLLER_BUTTON_A) {
                dinput_push_mouse_button(0, down);
                push_msg(g_main_hwnd, down ? 0x0201u : 0x0202u, down ? 1u : 0u, lp);
            } else if (btn == SDL_CONTROLLER_BUTTON_B) {
                dinput_push_mouse_button(1, down);
                push_msg(g_main_hwnd, down ? 0x0204u : 0x0205u, down ? 2u : 0u, lp);
            } else if (btn == SDL_CONTROLLER_BUTTON_X) {
                if (down) g_hotspot_mask |= 4u; else g_hotspot_mask &= ~4u;
                gl_renderer_set_hotspot_highlight(g_hotspot_mask != 0);
            } else if (btn == SDL_CONTROLLER_BUTTON_Y) {
                if (down) g_fast_walk_mask |= 4u; else g_fast_walk_mask &= ~4u;
                gl_renderer_set_fast_walk(g_fast_walk_mask != 0);
            } else if (btn == SDL_CONTROLLER_BUTTON_LEFTSHOULDER) {
                if (down) g_fast_walk_mask |= 8u; else g_fast_walk_mask &= ~8u;
                gl_renderer_set_fast_walk(g_fast_walk_mask != 0);
            } else if (down && btn == SDL_CONTROLLER_BUTTON_RIGHTSHOULDER) {
                gl_renderer_toggle_compare_split();
            } else if (btn == SDL_CONTROLLER_BUTTON_START) {
                push_msg(g_main_hwnd, down ? 0x0100u : 0x0101u, 0x1Bu, down ? 1u : 0xC0000001u);
                if (down) push_msg(g_main_hwnd, 0x0102u, 0x1Bu, 1u);
            }
            continue;
        }
        if (ev.type == SDL_QUIT) {
            g_quit_requested = 1;
            push_msg(g_main_hwnd, 0x0010u /* WM_CLOSE */, 0, 0);
            push_msg(g_main_hwnd, 0x0012u /* WM_QUIT */, 0, 0);
            continue;
        }
        if (ev.type == SDL_KEYDOWN) {
            SDL_Keycode sym = ev.key.keysym.sym;
            if (sym == SDLK_F4 && (ev.key.keysym.mod & KMOD_ALT)) {
                g_quit_requested = 1;
                push_msg(g_main_hwnd, 0x0010u /* WM_CLOSE */, 0, 0);
                push_msg(g_main_hwnd, 0x0012u /* WM_QUIT */, 0, 0);
                continue;
            }
            if (sym == SDLK_F1) {
                clear_held_qol_keys();
                gl_menu_toggle();
                continue;
            }
            if (sym == SDLK_F11 || (sym == SDLK_RETURN && (ev.key.keysym.mod & KMOD_ALT))) {
                gl_renderer_toggle_fullscreen();
                continue;
            }
        }
        if (gl_menu_is_open()) {
            if (gl_menu_handle_event(&ev)) {
                continue;
            }
            /* Block all game keyboard/mouse input while F1 graphics menu is open */
            if (ev.type == SDL_KEYDOWN || ev.type == SDL_KEYUP || ev.type == SDL_TEXTINPUT ||
                ev.type == SDL_MOUSEMOTION || ev.type == SDL_MOUSEBUTTONDOWN ||
                ev.type == SDL_MOUSEBUTTONUP || ev.type == SDL_MOUSEWHEEL) {
                continue;
            }
        }
        if (gl_renderer_is_dialog_log_open()) {
            if (ev.type == SDL_KEYDOWN &&
                ((ev.key.keysym.sym == SDLK_l && (ev.key.keysym.mod & KMOD_CTRL)) ||
                 ev.key.keysym.sym == SDLK_ESCAPE)) {
                gl_renderer_toggle_dialog_log();
                continue;
            }
            if (ev.type == SDL_MOUSEBUTTONDOWN) {
                gl_renderer_toggle_dialog_log();
                continue;
            }
            if (ev.type == SDL_KEYDOWN || ev.type == SDL_KEYUP || ev.type == SDL_TEXTINPUT ||
                ev.type == SDL_MOUSEMOTION || ev.type == SDL_MOUSEBUTTONUP || ev.type == SDL_MOUSEWHEEL) {
                continue;
            }
        }
        switch (ev.type) {
            case SDL_KEYDOWN: {
                SDL_Keycode sym = ev.key.keysym.sym;
                if (sym == SDLK_l && (ev.key.keysym.mod & KMOD_CTRL)) {
                    clear_held_qol_keys();
                    gl_renderer_toggle_dialog_log();
                    break;
                }
                if (sym == SDLK_F2) { gl_renderer_toggle_compare_split(); break; }
                if (sym == SDLK_PRINTSCREEN || (sym == SDLK_p && (ev.key.keysym.mod & KMOD_CTRL))) {
                    gl_renderer_request_screenshot();
                    break;
                }
                if (sym == SDLK_LSHIFT) {
                    g_fast_walk_mask |= 1u;
                    gl_renderer_set_fast_walk(g_fast_walk_mask != 0);
                } else if (sym == SDLK_RSHIFT) {
                    g_fast_walk_mask |= 2u;
                    gl_renderer_set_fast_walk(g_fast_walk_mask != 0);
                }
                if (sym == SDLK_SPACE) {
                    g_hotspot_mask |= 1u;
                    gl_renderer_set_hotspot_highlight(g_hotspot_mask != 0);
                } else if (sym == SDLK_h) {
                    g_hotspot_mask |= 2u;
                    gl_renderer_set_hotspot_highlight(g_hotspot_mask != 0);
                }
                if (sym == SDLK_F3) { gl_renderer_toggle_hud(); break; }
                if (sym == SDLK_F4) { gl_renderer_cycle_vsync(); break; }
                if (sym == SDLK_F5) { gl_renderer_toggle_hd_text(); break; }
                if (sym == SDLK_F6) { gl_renderer_toggle_hd_assets(); break; }
                if (sym == SDLK_F10) { gl_renderer_toggle_decoupled_cursor(); break; }
                if (sym == SDLK_F12) { gl_renderer_cycle_aspect(); break; }
                if (sym == SDLK_F7 || sym == SDLK_F8) {
                    float vol = g_cfg.master_volume + ((sym == SDLK_F8) ? 0.1f : -0.1f);
                    if (vol < 0.0f) vol = 0.0f;
                    if (vol > 1.5f) vol = 1.5f;
                    __atomic_store(&g_cfg.master_volume, &vol, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
                    __atomic_store_n(&g_cfg.muted, 0, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
                    gl_config_save(NULL);
                    gl_renderer_show_toast("Volume: %d%%", (int)(vol * 100.0f + 0.5f));
                    break;
                }
                if (sym == SDLK_F9) {
                    int muted = !g_cfg.muted;
                    __atomic_store_n(&g_cfg.muted, muted, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
                    gl_config_save(NULL);
                    gl_renderer_show_toast("Audio: %s", muted ? "Muted" : "Unmuted");
                    break;
                }
                uint32_t vk = sdl_key_to_vk(sym);
                if (vk) {
                    push_msg(g_main_hwnd, 0x0100u /* WM_KEYDOWN */, vk, 1);
                    if (vk == 0x08 || vk == 0x0D || vk == 0x1B || vk == 0x09) {
                        push_msg(g_main_hwnd, 0x0102u /* WM_CHAR */, vk, 1);
                    }
                }
                break;
            }
            case SDL_KEYUP: {
                SDL_Keycode sym = ev.key.keysym.sym;
                if (sym == SDLK_LSHIFT) {
                    g_fast_walk_mask &= ~1u;
                    gl_renderer_set_fast_walk(g_fast_walk_mask != 0);
                } else if (sym == SDLK_RSHIFT) {
                    g_fast_walk_mask &= ~2u;
                    gl_renderer_set_fast_walk(g_fast_walk_mask != 0);
                }
                if (sym == SDLK_SPACE) {
                    g_hotspot_mask &= ~1u;
                    gl_renderer_set_hotspot_highlight(g_hotspot_mask != 0);
                } else if (sym == SDLK_h) {
                    g_hotspot_mask &= ~2u;
                    gl_renderer_set_hotspot_highlight(g_hotspot_mask != 0);
                }
                uint32_t vk = sdl_key_to_vk(sym);
                if (vk) push_msg(g_main_hwnd, 0x0101u /* WM_KEYUP */, vk, 0xC0000001u);
                break;
            }
            case SDL_TEXTINPUT: {
                const uint8_t *p = (const uint8_t *)ev.text.text;
                while (*p) {
                    uint32_t cp = 0;
                    if (*p < 0x80) { cp = *p++; }
                    else if ((*p & 0xE0) == 0xC0 && p[1]) {
                        cp = ((*p & 0x1Fu) << 6) | (p[1] & 0x3Fu);
                        p += 2;
                    } else { p++; continue; }
                    if (cp >= 0x20 && cp <= 0xFF) {
                        push_msg(g_main_hwnd, 0x0102u /* WM_CHAR */, cp, 1);
                    }
                }
                break;
            }
            case SDL_MOUSEMOTION: {
                float fx, fy;
                gl_window_to_game_coords_f(ev.motion.x, ev.motion.y, &fx, &fy);
                int gx = (int)(fx + 0.5f);
                int gy = (int)(fy + 0.5f);
                int dx = gx - g_mouse_x;
                int dy = gy - g_mouse_y;
                g_mouse_fx = fx;
                g_mouse_fy = fy;
                g_mouse_x = gx;
                g_mouse_y = gy;
                gl_renderer_set_live_mouse(fx, fy, g_os_cursor_hidden);
                dinput_push_mouse_move(dx, dy, g_mouse_x, g_mouse_y);
                uint32_t lp = ((uint32_t)(uint16_t)g_mouse_y << 16) | (uint16_t)g_mouse_x;
                push_msg(g_main_hwnd, 0x0200u /* WM_MOUSEMOVE */, 0, lp);
                break;
            }
            case SDL_MOUSEBUTTONDOWN:
            case SDL_MOUSEBUTTONUP: {
                int down = (ev.type == SDL_MOUSEBUTTONDOWN);
                float fx, fy;
                gl_window_to_game_coords_f(ev.button.x, ev.button.y, &fx, &fy);
                int gx = (int)(fx + 0.5f);
                int gy = (int)(fy + 0.5f);
                g_mouse_fx = fx;
                g_mouse_fy = fy;
                g_mouse_x = gx;
                g_mouse_y = gy;
                gl_renderer_set_live_mouse(fx, fy, g_os_cursor_hidden);
                uint32_t lp = ((uint32_t)(uint16_t)g_mouse_y << 16) | (uint16_t)g_mouse_x;
                if (ev.button.button == SDL_BUTTON_LEFT) {
                    dinput_push_mouse_button(0, down);
                    push_msg(g_main_hwnd, down ? 0x0201u : 0x0202u, down ? 1u : 0u, lp);
                } else if (ev.button.button == SDL_BUTTON_RIGHT) {
                    dinput_push_mouse_button(1, down);
                    push_msg(g_main_hwnd, down ? 0x0204u : 0x0205u, down ? 2u : 0u, lp);
                } else if (ev.button.button == SDL_BUTTON_MIDDLE) {
                    dinput_push_mouse_button(2, down);
                    push_msg(g_main_hwnd, down ? 0x0207u : 0x0208u, down ? 16u : 0u, lp);
                }
                break;
            }
            case SDL_MOUSEWHEEL: {
                int dz = ev.wheel.y * 120;
                dinput_push_mouse_wheel(dz);
                uint32_t wp = ((uint32_t)(uint16_t)(int16_t)dz) << 16;
                uint32_t lp = ((uint32_t)(uint16_t)g_mouse_y << 16) | (uint16_t)g_mouse_x;
                push_msg(g_main_hwnd, 0x020Au /* WM_MOUSEWHEEL */, wp, lp);
                break;
            }
            case SDL_WINDOWEVENT:
                if (ev.window.event == SDL_WINDOWEVENT_FOCUS_GAINED) {
                    push_msg(g_main_hwnd, 0x001Cu /* WM_ACTIVATEAPP */, 1, 0);
                    push_msg(g_main_hwnd, 0x0006u /* WM_ACTIVATE */, 1, 0);
                } else if (ev.window.event == SDL_WINDOWEVENT_FOCUS_LOST) {
                    clear_held_qol_keys();
                    push_msg(g_main_hwnd, 0x0006u /* WM_ACTIVATE */, 0, 0);
                    push_msg(g_main_hwnd, 0x001Cu /* WM_ACTIVATEAPP */, 0, 0);
                } else if (ev.window.event == SDL_WINDOWEVENT_RESIZED ||
                           ev.window.event == SDL_WINDOWEVENT_SIZE_CHANGED ||
                           ev.window.event == SDL_WINDOWEVENT_EXPOSED) {
                    gl_renderer_redraw_if_needed(1);
                }
                break;
        }
    }
    gamepad_update_cursor();
    gl_renderer_redraw_if_needed(0);
}

/* ---- GDI Objects & SDL_ttf Font Engine ---- */
typedef struct GdiFont {
    int in_use;
    TTF_Font *ttf;
    int height;
    int weight;
    char face[64];
} GdiFont;

typedef struct GdiDC {
    int in_use;
    uint32_t surface_handle; /* 0 = memory/screen DC, >0 = DDrawSurface handle */
    uint32_t font_handle;
    uint32_t text_color;     /* COLORREF 0x00BBGGRR */
    uint32_t bk_color;       /* COLORREF 0x00BBGGRR */
    uint32_t bk_mode;        /* 1 = TRANSPARENT, 2 = OPAQUE */
} GdiDC;

#define MAX_GDI_FONTS 256
#define MAX_GDI_DCS   256
static GdiFont g_fonts[MAX_GDI_FONTS];
static GdiDC   g_dcs[MAX_GDI_DCS];
static char    g_ttf_path[1024] = "";

static void find_system_ttf(void) {
    const char *env = getenv("MHA2_FONT_PATH");
    if (env && env[0]) {
        FILE *f = fopen(env, "rb");
        if (f) { fclose(f); snprintf(g_ttf_path, sizeof(g_ttf_path), "%s", env); return; }
    }
    const char *candidates[] = {
        "fonts/DejaVuSans.ttf",
        "/run/current-system/sw/share/X11/fonts/DejaVuSans.ttf",
        "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans.ttf",
        "/usr/share/fonts/dejavu-sans-fonts/DejaVuSans.ttf",
        "/usr/share/fonts/TTF/DejaVuSans.ttf",
        "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/liberation/LiberationSans-Regular.ttf",
        NULL
    };
    for (int i = 0; candidates[i]; i++) {
        FILE *f = fopen(candidates[i], "rb");
        if (f) { fclose(f); snprintf(g_ttf_path, sizeof(g_ttf_path), "%s", candidates[i]); return; }
    }
}

static TTF_Font *get_dc_font(GdiDC *dc) {
    uint32_t fh = dc ? dc->font_handle : 0;
    if (fh >= 0x2000 && fh < 0x2000 + MAX_GDI_FONTS && g_fonts[fh - 0x2000].in_use) {
        return g_fonts[fh - 0x2000].ttf;
    }
    /* Lazily open default 16px font in slot 0 */
    if (!g_fonts[0].in_use && g_ttf_path[0]) {
        g_fonts[0].ttf = TTF_OpenFont(g_ttf_path, 14);
        g_fonts[0].height = 16;
        g_fonts[0].in_use = 1;
    }
    return g_fonts[0].ttf;
}

static void cp1252_to_utf8(const uint8_t *src, int len, char *dst, size_t dst_sz) {
    size_t j = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < len && j + 4 < dst_sz; i++) {
        uint8_t ch = src[i];
        if (ch == 0) break;
        if (ch < 0x80) {
            dst[j++] = (char)ch;
        } else {
            /* Standard ISO-8859-1 / Windows-1252 range 0xA0..0xFF (umlauts äöüßÄÖÜ etc.) */
            uint32_t cp = ch;
            if (ch == 0x80) cp = 0x20AC;
            else if (ch == 0x84) cp = 0x201E;
            else if (ch == 0x93) cp = 0x201C;
            else if (ch == 0x94) cp = 0x201D;
            else if (ch == 0x96) cp = 0x2013;
            else if (ch == 0x97) cp = 0x2014;
            if (cp < 0x800) {
                dst[j++] = (char)(0xC0 | (cp >> 6));
                dst[j++] = (char)(0x80 | (cp & 0x3F));
            } else {
                dst[j++] = (char)(0xE0 | (cp >> 12));
                dst[j++] = (char)(0x80 | ((cp >> 6) & 0x3F));
                dst[j++] = (char)(0x80 | (cp & 0x3F));
            }
        }
    }
    dst[j] = '\0';
}

uint32_t gdi_create_surface_dc(uint32_t surface_handle) {
    for (int i = 1; i < MAX_GDI_DCS; i++) {
        if (!g_dcs[i].in_use) {
            g_dcs[i].in_use = 1;
            g_dcs[i].surface_handle = surface_handle;
            g_dcs[i].font_handle = 0;
            g_dcs[i].text_color = 0x00FFFFFFu;
            g_dcs[i].bk_color = 0x00000000u;
            g_dcs[i].bk_mode = 2; /* OPAQUE */
            return 0x3000u + (uint32_t)i;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

void gdi_release_surface_dc(uint32_t hdc) {
    if (hdc >= 0x3001 && hdc < 0x3000 + MAX_GDI_DCS) {
        g_dcs[hdc - 0x3000].in_use = 0;
    }
}

static GdiDC *get_dc(uint32_t hdc) {
    if (hdc >= 0x3001 && hdc < 0x3000 + MAX_GDI_DCS && g_dcs[hdc - 0x3000].in_use) {
        return &g_dcs[hdc - 0x3000];
    }
    return &g_dcs[0];
}

void win32_user_gdi_init(void) {
    if (TTF_Init() != 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "WARN: TTF_Init failed: %s\n", TTF_GetError());
    }
    find_system_ttf();
    g_dcs[0].in_use = 1;
    g_dcs[0].text_color = 0x00FFFFFFu;
    g_dcs[0].bk_color = 0x00000000u;
    g_dcs[0].bk_mode = 2;
}

static void ensure_sdl_window(const char *title, int w, int h) {
    if (w <= 0) w = 800;
    if (h <= 0) h = 600;
    g_win_w = w;
    g_win_h = h;
    if (!g_sdl_window) {
        gl_renderer_init(title, w, h);
    }
}

int win32_user_gdi_dispatch(CpuCtx *c, uint32_t import_id) {
    switch (import_id) {
        /* ---- USER32 ---- */
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_RegisterClassExA: {
            uint32_t wc = arg_u32(c, 0);
            if (wc) {
                g_wndproc_va = mem_r32(wc + 8);
                if (g_debug_log) fprintf(stderr, "[USER32] RegisterClassExA(wndproc=0x%08x)\n", g_wndproc_va);
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0xC001u); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_CreateWindowExA: {
            const char *title = arg_cstr(c, 2);
            int w = arg_i32(c, 6);
            int h = arg_i32(c, 7);
            if (w <= 0 || w > 4096) w = 800;
            if (h <= 0 || h > 4096) h = 600;
            ensure_sdl_window(title, w, h);
            if (g_debug_log) fprintf(stderr, "[USER32] CreateWindowExA('%s', %dx%d)\n", title ? title : "", w, h);
            /* Send WM_CREATE */
            if (g_wndproc_va) {
                uint32_t a[4] = { g_main_hwnd, 0x0001u, 0, 0 };
                guest_call_stdcall(c, g_wndproc_va, 4, a);
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 12, g_main_hwnd); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_DestroyWindow: {
            uint32_t hwnd = arg_u32(c, 0);
            if (hwnd && g_wndproc_va) {
                uint32_t a[4] = { hwnd, 0x0002u /* WM_DESTROY */, 0, 0 };
                guest_call_stdcall(c, g_wndproc_va, 4, a);
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 1); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_ShowWindow:
            if (arg_i32(c, 1) == 0 && g_sdl_window) {
                SDL_HideWindow(g_sdl_window);
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, 1); return 1;
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_UpdateWindow:
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 1); return 1;
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_ValidateRect:
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, 1); return 1;
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_SetWindowPos:
            stdcall_ret(c, 7, 1); return 1;
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_SetForegroundWindow:
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_SetActiveWindow:
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_SetFocus:
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, g_main_hwnd); return 1;
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_GetParent:
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return 1;
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_SetWindowLongA: {
            int idx = arg_i32(c, 1);
            uint32_t val = arg_u32(c, 2);
            uint32_t prev = 0;
            if (idx == -21 /* GWL_USERDATA */) { prev = g_wnd_userdata; g_wnd_userdata = val; }
            else if (idx == -4 /* GWL_WNDPROC */) { prev = g_wndproc_va; g_wndproc_va = val; }
            stdcall_ret(c, 3, prev); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_GetClientRect: {
            uint32_t rc = arg_u32(c, 1);
            if (rc) {
                mem_w32(rc + 0, 0);
                mem_w32(rc + 4, 0);
                mem_w32(rc + 8, (uint32_t)g_win_w);
                mem_w32(rc + 12, (uint32_t)g_win_h);
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, 1); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_GetWindowPlacement: {
            uint32_t wp = arg_u32(c, 1);
            if (wp) {
                memset(g_mem + wp, 0, 44);
                mem_w32(wp + 0, 44);
                mem_w32(wp + 8, 1);
                mem_w32(wp + 28, 0);
                mem_w32(wp + 32, 0);
                mem_w32(wp + 36, (uint32_t)g_win_w);
                mem_w32(wp + 40, (uint32_t)g_win_h);
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, 1); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_SetWindowPlacement:
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, 1); return 1;
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_ClientToScreen:
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, 1); return 1;
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_LoadIconA:
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_LoadCursorA:
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, 0x00020001u); return 1;
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_ShowCursor: {
            int show = arg_i32(c, 0);
            g_os_cursor_hidden = show ? 0 : 1;
            if (show) gl_renderer_clear_cursor();
            gl_renderer_set_live_mouse(g_mouse_fx, g_mouse_fy, g_os_cursor_hidden);
            SDL_ShowCursor(show ? SDL_ENABLE : SDL_DISABLE);
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, show ? 0u : 0xFFFFFFFFu); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_SetCursorPos:
            g_mouse_x = arg_i32(c, 0);
            g_mouse_y = arg_i32(c, 1);
            g_mouse_fx = (float)g_mouse_x;
            g_mouse_fy = (float)g_mouse_y;
            gl_renderer_set_live_mouse(g_mouse_fx, g_mouse_fy, g_os_cursor_hidden);
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, 1); return 1;
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_GetSystemMetrics: {
            int idx = arg_i32(c, 0);
            uint32_t val = 0;
            if (idx == 0) val = (uint32_t)g_win_w;
            else if (idx == 1) val = (uint32_t)g_win_h;
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, val); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_GetDC:
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, gdi_create_surface_dc(0)); return 1;
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_ReleaseDC:
            gdi_release_surface_dc(arg_u32(c, 1));
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, 1); return 1;
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_PostQuitMessage:
            g_quit_requested = 1;
            push_msg(g_main_hwnd, 0x0012u, arg_u32(c, 0), 0);
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return 1;
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_PeekMessageA: {
            win32_pump_sdl_events();
            uint32_t msg_va = arg_u32(c, 0);
            uint32_t remove = arg_u32(c, 4) & 1u;
            WinMsg m;
            if (pop_msg(&m, (int)remove)) {
                mem_w32(msg_va + 0, m.hwnd);
                mem_w32(msg_va + 4, m.msg);
                mem_w32(msg_va + 8, m.wparam);
                mem_w32(msg_va + 12, m.lparam);
                mem_w32(msg_va + 16, m.time);
                mem_w32(msg_va + 20, (uint32_t)m.pt_x);
                mem_w32(msg_va + 24, (uint32_t)m.pt_y);
                stdcall_ret(c, 5, 1); return 1;
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 5, 0); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_TranslateMessage:
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 1); return 1;
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_DispatchMessageA: {
            uint32_t msg_va = arg_u32(c, 0);
            uint32_t ret = 0;
            if (msg_va && g_wndproc_va) {
                uint32_t a[4] = {
                    mem_r32(msg_va + 0),
                    mem_r32(msg_va + 4),
                    mem_r32(msg_va + 8),
                    mem_r32(msg_va + 12),
                };
                ret = guest_call_stdcall(c, g_wndproc_va, 4, a);
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, ret); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_SendMessageA: {
            uint32_t hwnd = arg_u32(c, 0);
            uint32_t msg  = arg_u32(c, 1);
            uint32_t wp   = arg_u32(c, 2);
            uint32_t lp   = arg_u32(c, 3);
            uint32_t ret = 0;
            if (hwnd == g_main_hwnd && g_wndproc_va) {
                uint32_t a[4] = { hwnd, msg, wp, lp };
                ret = guest_call_stdcall(c, g_wndproc_va, 4, a);
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 4, ret); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_DefWindowProcA: {
            uint32_t msg = arg_u32(c, 1);
            if (msg == 0x0010u /* WM_CLOSE */) {
                g_quit_requested = 1;
                push_msg(g_main_hwnd, 0x0012u, 0, 0);
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 4, 0); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_USER32_DLL_MessageBoxA: {
            const char *text = arg_cstr(c, 1);
            const char *cap  = arg_cstr(c, 2);
            fprintf(stderr, "[MessageBoxA] %s: %s\n", cap ? cap : "", text ? text : "");
            stdcall_ret(c, 4, 1u /* IDOK */); return 1;
        }

        /* ---- GDI32 ---- */
        case IMP_GDI32_DLL_GetStockObject:
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0x4000u + arg_u32(c, 0)); return 1;
        case IMP_GDI32_DLL_CreateCompatibleDC:
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, gdi_create_surface_dc(0)); return 1;
        case IMP_GDI32_DLL_DeleteDC:
            gdi_release_surface_dc(arg_u32(c, 0));
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 1); return 1;
        case IMP_GDI32_DLL_DeleteObject: {
            uint32_t h = arg_u32(c, 0);
            if (h >= 0x2001 && h < 0x2000 + MAX_GDI_FONTS) {
                GdiFont *f = &g_fonts[h - 0x2000];
                if (f->in_use) {
                    if (f->ttf) TTF_CloseFont(f->ttf);
                    f->ttf = NULL;
                    f->in_use = 0;
                }
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 1); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_GDI32_DLL_SelectObject: {
            GdiDC *dc = get_dc(arg_u32(c, 0));
            uint32_t obj = arg_u32(c, 1);
            uint32_t prev = dc->font_handle;
            if (obj >= 0x2000 && obj < 0x2000 + MAX_GDI_FONTS) {
                dc->font_handle = obj;
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, prev); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_GDI32_DLL_SetTextColor: {
            GdiDC *dc = get_dc(arg_u32(c, 0));
            uint32_t prev = dc->text_color;
            dc->text_color = arg_u32(c, 1);
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, prev); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_GDI32_DLL_GetTextColor:
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, get_dc(arg_u32(c, 0))->text_color); return 1;
        case IMP_GDI32_DLL_SetBkColor: {
            GdiDC *dc = get_dc(arg_u32(c, 0));
            uint32_t prev = dc->bk_color;
            dc->bk_color = arg_u32(c, 1);
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, prev); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_GDI32_DLL_GetBkColor:
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, get_dc(arg_u32(c, 0))->bk_color); return 1;
        case IMP_GDI32_DLL_SetBkMode: {
            GdiDC *dc = get_dc(arg_u32(c, 0));
            uint32_t prev = dc->bk_mode;
            dc->bk_mode = arg_u32(c, 1);
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, prev); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_GDI32_DLL_GetBkMode:
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, get_dc(arg_u32(c, 0))->bk_mode); return 1;

        case IMP_GDI32_DLL_EnumFontFamiliesA: {
            uint32_t cb_va = arg_u32(c, 2);
            uint32_t lparam = arg_u32(c, 3);
            static const char *font_names[] = {
                "Arial", "Times New Roman", "Courier New", "Verdana",
                "Tahoma", "Comic Sans MS", "MS Sans Serif", "Trebuchet MS", NULL
            };
            uint32_t elf_va = guest_alloc(128);
            uint32_t ntm_va = guest_alloc(128);
            int res = 1;
            for (int i = 0; font_names[i] && cb_va; i++) {
                memset(g_mem + elf_va, 0, 128);
                memset(g_mem + ntm_va, 0, 128);
                mem_w32(elf_va + 0, 16); /* lfHeight */
                mem_w32(elf_va + 16, 400); /* lfWeight */
                snprintf((char *)(g_mem + elf_va + 28), 32, "%s", font_names[i]);
                mem_w32(ntm_va + 0, 16); /* tmHeight */
                mem_w32(ntm_va + 4, 13); /* tmAscent */
                mem_w32(ntm_va + 8, 3);  /* tmDescent */
                mem_w32(ntm_va + 20, 8); /* tmAveCharWidth */
                mem_w32(ntm_va + 24, 14);/* tmMaxCharWidth */
                uint32_t a[4] = { elf_va, ntm_va, 4u /* TRUETYPE_FONTTYPE */, lparam };
                res = (int)guest_call_stdcall(c, cb_va, 4, a);
                if (res == 0) break;
            }
            guest_free(elf_va);
            guest_free(ntm_va);
            stdcall_ret(c, 4, (uint32_t)res); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_GDI32_DLL_CreateFontIndirectA: {
            uint32_t lf_va = arg_u32(c, 0);
            int32_t lf_h = lf_va ? (int32_t)mem_r32(lf_va + 0) : 16;
            int32_t lf_w = lf_va ? (int32_t)mem_r32(lf_va + 16) : 400;
            const char *face = lf_va ? (const char *)(g_mem + lf_va + 28) : "Arial";
            int ptsz = lf_h < 0 ? -lf_h : (lf_h > 0 ? lf_h : 16);
            if (ptsz < 6) ptsz = 6;
            if (ptsz > 128) ptsz = 128;
            /* Scale slightly to match Windows GDI pixel height */
            int ttf_sz = (ptsz * 85) / 100;
            if (ttf_sz < 6) ttf_sz = 6;

            for (int i = 1; i < MAX_GDI_FONTS; i++) {
                if (!g_fonts[i].in_use) {
                    g_fonts[i].in_use = 1;
                    g_fonts[i].height = ptsz;
                    g_fonts[i].weight = lf_w;
                    snprintf(g_fonts[i].face, sizeof(g_fonts[i].face), "%s", face);
                    g_fonts[i].ttf = g_ttf_path[0] ? TTF_OpenFont(g_ttf_path, ttf_sz) : NULL;
                    if (g_fonts[i].ttf && lf_w >= 600) {
                        TTF_SetFontStyle(g_fonts[i].ttf, TTF_STYLE_BOLD);
                    }
                    stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0x2000u + (uint32_t)i); return 1;
                }
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 1, 0); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_GDI32_DLL_GetTextMetricsA: {
            GdiDC *dc = get_dc(arg_u32(c, 0));
            uint32_t tm_va = arg_u32(c, 1);
            TTF_Font *f = get_dc_font(dc);
            if (tm_va) {
                memset(g_mem + tm_va, 0, 56);
                int h = f ? TTF_FontHeight(f) : 16;
                int asc = f ? TTF_FontAscent(f) : 13;
                int desc = f ? -TTF_FontDescent(f) : 3;
                if (desc < 0) desc = 0;
                int w = 8;
                if (f) TTF_SizeUTF8(f, "n", &w, NULL);
                mem_w32(tm_va + 0, (uint32_t)h);
                mem_w32(tm_va + 4, (uint32_t)asc);
                mem_w32(tm_va + 8, (uint32_t)desc);
                mem_w32(tm_va + 20, (uint32_t)w);
                mem_w32(tm_va + 24, (uint32_t)(w * 2));
                mem_w32(tm_va + 28, 400);
                mem_w8(tm_va + 48, 32);
                mem_w8(tm_va + 49, 255);
                mem_w8(tm_va + 50, '?');
                mem_w8(tm_va + 51, ' ');
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 2, 1); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_GDI32_DLL_GetTextExtentPoint32A: {
            GdiDC *dc = get_dc(arg_u32(c, 0));
            uint32_t str_va = arg_u32(c, 1);
            int32_t len = arg_i32(c, 2);
            uint32_t sz_va = arg_u32(c, 3);
            TTF_Font *f = get_dc_font(dc);
            char u8[1024];
            if (len < 0) len = 0;
            if (len > 500) len = 500;
```
