```c
            cp1252_to_utf8(g_mem + str_va, len, u8, sizeof(u8));
            int w = len * 8, h = 16;
            if (f) {
                if (u8[0]) TTF_SizeUTF8(f, u8, &w, &h);
                else { w = 0; h = TTF_FontHeight(f); }
            }
            if (sz_va) {
                mem_w32(sz_va + 0, (uint32_t)w);
                mem_w32(sz_va + 4, (uint32_t)h);
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 4, 1); return 1;
        }
        case IMP_GDI32_DLL_TextOutA: {
            GdiDC *dc = get_dc(arg_u32(c, 0));
            int32_t x = arg_i32(c, 1);
            int32_t y = arg_i32(c, 2);
            uint32_t str_va = arg_u32(c, 3);
            int32_t len = arg_i32(c, 4);
            TTF_Font *f = get_dc_font(dc);
            DDrawSurface *ds = dc->surface_handle ? ddraw_get_surface(dc->surface_handle) : NULL;
            if (f && ds && ds->pixels_va && len > 0) {
                char u8[1024];
                if (len > 500) len = 500;
                cp1252_to_utf8(g_mem + str_va, len, u8, sizeof(u8));
                if (u8[0]) {
                    /* Record vector text draw for Native Monitor-DPI OpenGL rendering (F5) */
                    GdiFont *gf = (dc->font_handle >= 0x2001 && dc->font_handle < 0x2000 + MAX_GDI_FONTS &&
                                   g_fonts[dc->font_handle - 0x2000].in_use)
                        ? &g_fonts[dc->font_handle - 0x2000] : NULL;
                    GlTextDraw td;
                    memset(&td, 0, sizeof(td));
                    td.x = x;
                    td.y = y;
                    td.clip_x0 = 0;
                    td.clip_y0 = 0;
                    td.clip_x1 = (int)ds->width;
                    td.clip_y1 = (int)ds->height;
                    td.font_height = gf ? gf->height : 16;
                    td.font_weight = gf ? gf->weight : 400;
                    td.text_color = dc->text_color;
                    td.bk_color = dc->bk_color;
                    td.bk_mode = (int)dc->bk_mode;
                    snprintf(td.utf8, sizeof(td.utf8), "%.255s", u8);
                    ddraw_surface_add_text(ds, &td);

                    /* If Native HD Vector Text is active, skip blurry 800x600 software rasterization */
                    if (g_cfg.hd_vector_text && gl_renderer_is_active()) {
                        stdcall_ret(c, 5, 1); return 1;
                    }

                    SDL_Color fg = {
                        (uint8_t)(dc->text_color & 0xFF),
                        (uint8_t)((dc->text_color >> 8) & 0xFF),
                        (uint8_t)((dc->text_color >> 16) & 0xFF),
                        255
                    };
                    SDL_Surface *ts = TTF_RenderUTF8_Blended(f, u8, fg);
                    if (ts) {
                        SDL_Surface *rgba = SDL_ConvertSurfaceFormat(ts, SDL_PIXELFORMAT_ARGB8888, 0);
                        SDL_FreeSurface(ts);
                        if (rgba) {
                            uint8_t bg_r = (uint8_t)(dc->bk_color & 0xFF);
                            uint8_t bg_g = (uint8_t)((dc->bk_color >> 8) & 0xFF);
                            uint8_t bg_b = (uint8_t)((dc->bk_color >> 16) & 0xFF);
                            for (int sy = 0; sy < rgba->h; sy++) {
                                int dy = y + sy;
                                if (dy < 0 || dy >= (int)ds->height) continue;
                                const uint32_t *srow = (const uint32_t *)((const uint8_t *)rgba->pixels + sy * rgba->pitch);
                                uint8_t *drow = g_mem + ds->pixels_va + (uint32_t)dy * ds->pitch;
                                for (int sx = 0; sx < rgba->w; sx++) {
                                    int dx = x + sx;
                                    if (dx < 0 || dx >= (int)ds->width) continue;
                                    uint32_t px = srow[sx];
                                    uint32_t a = px >> 24;
                                    uint32_t sr = (px >> 16) & 0xFF;
                                    uint32_t sg = (px >> 8) & 0xFF;
                                    uint32_t sb = px & 0xFF;
                                    if (dc->bk_mode == 2 /* OPAQUE */) {
                                        sr = (sr * a + bg_r * (255 - a)) / 255;
                                        sg = (sg * a + bg_g * (255 - a)) / 255;
                                        sb = (sb * a + bg_b * (255 - a)) / 255;
                                        a = 255;
                                    }
                                    if (a == 0) continue;
                                    if (ds->bpp == 16) {
                                        uint16_t dp;
                                        memcpy(&dp, drow + dx * 2, 2);
                                        uint32_t dr = ((dp >> 11) & 0x1F) * 255 / 31;
                                        uint32_t dg = ((dp >> 5) & 0x3F) * 255 / 63;
                                        uint32_t db = (dp & 0x1F) * 255 / 31;
                                        uint32_t rr = (sr * a + dr * (255 - a)) / 255;
                                        uint32_t rg = (sg * a + dg * (255 - a)) / 255;
                                        uint32_t rb = (sb * a + db * (255 - a)) / 255;
                                        uint16_t out = (uint16_t)(((rr >> 3) << 11) | ((rg >> 2) << 5) | (rb >> 3));
                                        memcpy(drow + dx * 2, &out, 2);
                                    } else if (ds->bpp == 32) {
                                        uint32_t dp;
                                        memcpy(&dp, drow + dx * 4, 4);
                                        uint32_t dr = (dp >> 16) & 0xFF;
                                        uint32_t dg = (dp >> 8) & 0xFF;
                                        uint32_t db = dp & 0xFF;
                                        uint32_t rr = (sr * a + dr * (255 - a)) / 255;
                                        uint32_t rg = (sg * a + dg * (255 - a)) / 255;
                                        uint32_t rb = (sb * a + db * (255 - a)) / 255;
                                        uint32_t out = 0xFF000000u | (rr << 16) | (rg << 8) | rb;
                                        memcpy(drow + dx * 4, &out, 4);
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            SDL_FreeSurface(rgba);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            stdcall_ret(c, 5, 1); return 1;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}
```

```nix
{ pkgs ? import <nixpkgs> {} }:

pkgs.mkShell {
  buildInputs = with pkgs; [
    gcc
    gnumake
    pkg-config
    SDL2
    SDL2_ttf
    ffmpeg
    libGL
    dejavu_fonts
    (python3.withPackages (ps: [ ps.capstone ]))
  ];

  shellHook = ''
    export MHA2_FONT_PATH="${pkgs.dejavu_fonts}/share/fonts/truetype/DejaVuSans.ttf"
    export MHA2_GAME_FONT_PATH="${pkgs.dejavu_fonts}/share/fonts/truetype/DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf"
  '';
}
```

`tools/Containerfile.portable`:

```portable
FROM docker.io/library/ubuntu:22.04

ENV DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive

RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
    build-essential \
    pkg-config \
    nasm \
    yasm \
    curl \
    xz-utils \
    ca-certificates \
    patchelf \
    libsdl2-dev \
    libsdl2-ttf-dev \
    libgl-dev \
    zlib1g-dev \
    fonts-dejavu-core \
    binutils \
  && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

# Build minimal static FFmpeg 6.1.2 (local files, video/audio decoders used by MHA2, SIMD enabled)
RUN curl -fsSL https://ffmpeg.org/releases/ffmpeg-6.1.2.tar.xz -o /tmp/ffmpeg.tar.xz \
  && mkdir -p /tmp/ffmpeg-src \
  && tar -xf /tmp/ffmpeg.tar.xz -C /tmp/ffmpeg-src --strip-components=1 \
  && cd /tmp/ffmpeg-src \
  && ./configure \
       --prefix=/opt/ffmpeg-min \
       --enable-static --disable-shared --enable-pic \
       --disable-programs --disable-doc --disable-debug --disable-network \
       --disable-autodetect --disable-everything \
       --enable-pthreads --enable-zlib \
       --enable-protocol=file \
       --enable-demuxer=mov,avi,matroska \
       --enable-parser=h264,mpegvideo,mpeg4video,mjpeg \
       --enable-decoder=h264,mpeg1video,mpeg2video,mpeg4,cinepak,mjpeg,msvideo1,indeo3,indeo5,rawvideo,pcm_s16le,pcm_u8,mp3,aac \
       --enable-swscale \
  && make -j"$(nproc)" \
  && make install \
  && rm -rf /tmp/ffmpeg.tar.xz /tmp/ffmpeg-src

# PipeWire dev headers for SDL2 dlopen backend (separate layer after FFmpeg to preserve cache)
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
    libpipewire-0.3-dev \
  && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

# Build upstream SDL2 2.32.4 with dlopen backends enabled (no hard DT_NEEDED on Pulse/Wayland/PipeWire/GBM/libdecor)
RUN curl -fsSL https://github.com/libsdl-org/SDL/releases/download/release-2.32.4/SDL2-2.32.4.tar.gz -o /tmp/sdl2.tar.gz \
  && mkdir -p /tmp/sdl2-src \
  && tar -xf /tmp/sdl2.tar.gz -C /tmp/sdl2-src --strip-components=1 \
  && cd /tmp/sdl2-src \
  && ./configure \
       --prefix=/opt/sdl2-portable \
       --enable-shared --disable-static --disable-rpath \
       --enable-video-x11 --enable-x11-shared \
       --enable-video-wayland --enable-wayland-shared --enable-libdecor-shared \
       --enable-video-kmsdrm --enable-kmsdrm-shared \
       --enable-video-opengl \
       --enable-alsa --enable-alsa-shared \
       --enable-pulseaudio --enable-pulseaudio-shared \
       --enable-pipewire --enable-pipewire-shared \
       --disable-sndio --disable-jack --disable-esd --disable-arts --disable-nas --disable-oss \
       --disable-ibus --disable-fcitx --disable-video-vulkan \
  && make -j"$(nproc)" \
  && make install \
  && readelf -d /opt/sdl2-portable/lib/libSDL2-2.0.so.0 | grep NEEDED \
  && rm -rf /tmp/sdl2.tar.gz /tmp/sdl2-src

ENV PKG_CONFIG_PATH=/opt/sdl2-portable/lib/pkgconfig:/opt/ffmpeg-min/lib/pkgconfig

```

`tools/compress_hd_lossless.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Moorhuhn Adventure 2 — 100% Bit-Exact Lossless WebP Optimizer for hd_assets/
Converts all HD PNGs (<= 16383px) to lossless WebP with exact=True (preserving
anti-halo dilated RGB in alpha=0 pixels, guaranteed 0 max_pixel_diff) across all CPU cores.
"""
import concurrent.futures
import os
import sys
import time
from PIL import Image

Image.MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS = None


def convert_one(png_path: str):
    webp_path = png_path[:-4] + ".webp"
    tmp_path = webp_path + ".tmp.webp"
    try:
        with Image.open(png_path) as im:
            w, h = im.size
            if w > 16383 or h > 16383:
                return ("skip", 0, 0, None)
            old_sz = os.path.getsize(png_path)
            im.save(tmp_path, format="WEBP", lossless=True, exact=True, method=4)
            new_sz = os.path.getsize(tmp_path)
            if new_sz < old_sz:
                os.replace(tmp_path, webp_path)
                os.remove(png_path)
                return ("ok", old_sz, new_sz, None)
            else:
                os.remove(tmp_path)
                return ("kept_png", old_sz, old_sz, None)
    except Exception as e:
        return ("err", 0, 0, f"{png_path}: {e}")
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(tmp_path):
            try:
                os.remove(tmp_path)
            except OSError:
                pass


def main():
    root = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "hd_assets"
    pngs = []
    for r, _, files in os.walk(root):
        for f in files:
            if f.endswith(".png"):
                pngs.append(os.path.join(r, f))
    pngs.sort()
    print(f"[webp] Converting {len(pngs)} PNGs in {root}/ to 100% bit-exact lossless WebP...", flush=True)
    t0 = time.time()
    ok = kept = skip = err = 0
    old_total = new_total = 0
    workers = max(1, os.cpu_count() or 8)
    with concurrent.futures.ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=workers) as ex:
        for idx, (st, osz, nsz, err_msg) in enumerate(ex.map(convert_one, pngs, chunksize=16), 1):
            old_total += osz
            new_total += nsz
            if st == "ok": ok += 1
            elif st == "kept_png": kept += 1
            elif st == "skip": skip += 1
            else:
                err += 1
                if err_msg:
                    print(f"[webp] ERROR: {err_msg}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
            if idx % 1000 == 0 or idx == len(pngs):
                el = time.time() - t0
                print(f"  [{idx}/{len(pngs)}] ok={ok} kept={kept} skip={skip} err={err} "
                      f"({old_total/1e9:.2f}GB -> {new_total/1e9:.2f}GB, {el:.0f}s)", flush=True)

    # Clear disk caches so runtime re-indexes .webp paths cleanly
    for c in (".hd_index.cache", ".hd_sprite_index.cache"):
        cp = os.path.join(root, c)
        if os.path.exists(cp):
            os.remove(cp)
    saved_gb = (old_total - new_total) / 1e9
    pct = 100.0 * (1.0 - new_total / max(old_total, 1))
    print(f"[webp] Done in {time.time() - t0:.1f}s: saved {saved_gb:.2f} GB ({pct:.1f}% smaller), 0 quality loss!", flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/extract_assets.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Moorhuhn Adventure 2: Der Fluch des Goldes — mha2.dat Asset Extractor & HD Pack Helper.

Reverse-engineered virtual filesystem (VDrv) and image format (JPG-in-_img & IT3 RLE sprites).
Extracts room backgrounds, sprites (with full alpha transparency), audio, and scripts from
a user-supplied game_files/mha2.dat.

Usage:
  python3 tools/extract_assets.py --list
  python3 tools/extract_assets.py --backgrounds -o extracted_assets
  python3 tools/extract_assets.py --all -o extracted_assets
  python3 tools/extract_assets.py --filter x/001/ -o extracted_assets
"""
import argparse
import fnmatch
import os
import struct
import subprocess
import sys
import zlib


def descramble(data: bytes) -> bytes:
    """Exact port of FUN_004d9a00 (VDrv directory/entry header descrambler)."""
    b = bytearray(data)
    n = len(b)
    if n <= 0:
        return bytes(b)
    b[0] = (~b[0]) & 0xFF
    u10 = n - 1
    if u10 == 0:
        return bytes(b)

    # Pass 1
    u1 = 0
    u8 = (~u10) & 0xFFFFFFFF
    for i in range(u10):
        b[1 + i] ^= (u1 & 0xFF)
        rot = ((u8 & 1) << 1) | 5
        u8 >>= 1
        if u8 == 0:
            u8 = (~u10) & 0xFFFFFFFF
        u1 = ((((u1 >> rot) | (u1 << (32 - rot))) & 0xFFFFFFFF) + 1) & 0xFFFFFFFF
        if u1 == 0:
            u1 = 0x5A3C96E7

    # Pass 2
    half = u10 >> 1
    for i in range(half):
        x = b[1 + 2 * i]
        b[1 + 2 * i] = b[1 + 2 * i + 1] ^ 0xAA
        b[1 + 2 * i + 1] = x ^ 0x55

    # Pass 3
    u1 = 0
    u8 = u10
    for i in range(u10):
        b[1 + i] ^= (u1 & 0xFF)
        rot = (6 if (u8 & 1) else 0) + 11
        u8 >>= 1
        if u8 == 0:
            u8 = u10
        u1 = ((((u1 << rot) | (u1 >> (32 - rot))) & 0xFFFFFFFF) + 1) & 0xFFFFFFFF
        if u1 == 0:
            u1 = 0x5A3C96E7

    # Pass 4
    for i in range(half):
        x = b[1 + i]
        y = b[u10 - i]
        b[1 + i] = y ^ 0x0F
        b[u10 - i] = x ^ 0xF0

    return bytes(b)


def write_png_rgba(path: str, width: int, height: int, rgba: bytes):
    """Pure-stdlib RGBA PNG writer (zero external dependencies)."""
    def chunk(tag: bytes, payload: bytes) -> bytes:
        crc = zlib.crc32(tag + payload) & 0xFFFFFFFF
        return struct.pack(">I", len(payload)) + tag + payload + struct.pack(">I", crc)

    raw_rows = bytearray()
    stride = width * 4
    for y in range(height):
        raw_rows.append(0)  # Filter type 0 (None)
        raw_rows.extend(rgba[y * stride : (y + 1) * stride])

    ihdr = struct.pack(">IIBBBBB", width, height, 8, 6, 0, 0, 0)
    png = (
        b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n"
        + chunk(b"IHDR", ihdr)
        + chunk(b"IDAT", zlib.compress(bytes(raw_rows), 6))
        + chunk(b"IEND", b"")
    )
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "wb") as out:
        out.write(png)


class Mha2Archive:
    def __init__(self, dat_path: str):
        self.dat_path = dat_path
        self.f = open(dat_path, "rb")
        hdr = self.f.read(0x4C)
        if hdr[:4] != b"MHA2":
            raise RuntimeError(f"Not a valid MHA2 archive: {dat_path}")
        self.ver, self.fsize, self.idx_off = struct.unpack_from("<III", hdr, 0x40)
        self.entries = []
        self.f.seek(self.idx_off)
        self._read_tree("")

    def _read_tree(self, prefix: str):
        while self.f.tell() < self.fsize:
            h0 = self.f.read(16)
            if len(h0) < 16:
                break
            typ = struct.unpack_from("<I", h0, 0)[0]
            h1 = descramble(self.f.read(16))
            _, has_next, _, has_child = struct.unpack("<IIII", h1)
            if typ == 4:
                name = descramble(self.f.read(0x20)).split(b"\x00", 1)[0].decode("latin1")
                path = f"{prefix}/{name}" if prefix else name
                if has_child:
                    self._read_tree(path)
            elif typ == 3:
                payload = descramble(self.f.read(0x58))
                name = payload[:0x40].split(b"\x00", 1)[0].decode("latin1")
                flags, ft_lo, ft_hi, csz, usz, off = struct.unpack_from("<IIIIII", payload, 0x40)
                path = f"{prefix}/{name}" if prefix else name
                self.entries.append({
                    "path": path,
                    "flags": flags,
                    "csize": csz,
                    "usize": usz,
                    "offset": off,
                })
            else:
                break
            if has_next == 0:
                break

    def read_entry(self, entry) -> bytes:
        self.f.seek(entry["offset"])
        raw = self.f.read(entry["csize"])
        if entry["flags"] & 1:
            return zlib.decompress(raw)
        return raw

    def close(self):
        self.f.close()


def decode_it3_frame(raw: bytes, frame_off: int, palette, canvas_w: int = 0, canvas_h: int = 0):
    """Decode one IT3 RLE frame into (width, height, rgba_bytes).

    If canvas_w/canvas_h (from the IT3 t0 DirectDraw cell table) are provided and larger
    than the tight RLE bounding box (w, h), the frame is placed at top-left (0, 0) on a
    transparent (canvas_w x canvas_h) RGBA canvas, matching mha2.exe FUN_004de0d0 exactly.
    """
    w, h, colorkey, _px_cnt, _ctrl_cnt, bpp, has_alpha, _stride, _pad, _pal_cnt, _pal_off, data_off = (
        struct.unpack_from("<12I", raw, frame_off)
    )
    if w == 0 or h == 0 or data_off >= len(raw):
        return None
    out_w = canvas_w if canvas_w >= w else w
    out_h = canvas_h if canvas_h >= h else h
    out = bytearray(out_w * out_h * 4)
    pos = data_off
    x = 0
    y = 0
    n_raw = len(raw)
    while pos < n_raw and y < h:
        b = raw[pos]
        pos += 1
        if b == 0:
            break
        if (b & 0x80) == 0:
            x += b
        else:
            cnt = b & 0x7F
            if cnt == 0:
                y += 1
                x = 0
                continue
            for _ in range(cnt):
                if bpp == 1:
                    if pos >= n_raw:
                        break
                    idx = raw[pos]
                    pos += 1
                    rgb = palette[idx] if idx < len(palette) else 0
                    r = (rgb >> 16) & 0xFF
                    g = (rgb >> 8) & 0xFF
                    bl = rgb & 0xFF
                    if has_alpha:
                        a = raw[pos] if pos < n_raw else 255
                        pos += 1
                    else:
                        a = 255
                elif bpp == 2:
                    if pos + 2 > n_raw:
                        break
                    p16 = struct.unpack_from("<H", raw, pos)[0]
                    pos += 2
                    r = ((p16 >> 11) & 0x1F) * 255 // 31
                    g = ((p16 >> 5) & 0x3F) * 255 // 63
                    bl = (p16 & 0x1F) * 255 // 31
                    if has_alpha:
                        a = raw[pos] if pos < n_raw else 255
                        pos += 1
                    else:
                        a = 255
                elif bpp == 3:
                    if pos + 3 > n_raw:
                        break
                    bl, g, r = raw[pos], raw[pos + 1], raw[pos + 2]
                    pos += 3
                    if has_alpha:
                        a = raw[pos] if pos < n_raw else 255
                        pos += 1
                    else:
                        a = 255
                elif bpp == 4:
                    if pos + 4 > n_raw:
                        break
                    bl, g, r, a0 = raw[pos], raw[pos + 1], raw[pos + 2], raw[pos + 3]
                    pos += 4
                    if has_alpha:
                        a = raw[pos] if pos < n_raw else a0
                        pos += 1
                    else:
                        a = 255
                else:
                    return None
                if 0 <= x < out_w and 0 <= y < out_h:
                    di = (y * out_w + x) * 4
                    out[di] = r
                    out[di + 1] = g
                    out[di + 2] = bl
                    out[di + 3] = a
                x += 1
    return (out_w, out_h, bytes(out))


def extract_img_entry(raw: bytes, out_base: str, convert_jpg_to_png: bool = True, upscale: int = 1) -> int:
    """Extract a ._img container (JPEG background or IT3 multi-frame sprite). Returns count of files written."""
    if len(raw) < 0x50:
        return 0
    img_type = struct.unpack_from("<I", raw, 0)[0]
    written = 0
    if img_type == 0:
        # JPEG-backed image (room backgrounds, portraits, full-screen art)
        joff, jsz = struct.unpack_from("<II", raw, 0x40)
        if joff + jsz <= len(raw) and jsz > 4:
            jpg = raw[joff : joff + jsz]
            if convert_jpg_to_png or upscale > 1:
                png_path = out_base + ".png"
                os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(png_path), exist_ok=True)
                cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "mjpeg", "-i", "pipe:0"]
                if upscale > 1:
                    cmd += ["-vf", f"scale=iw*{upscale}:ih*{upscale}:flags=lanczos,unsharp=5:5:0.75:5:5:0.0"]
                cmd.append(png_path)
                p = subprocess.run(
                    cmd,
                    input=jpg,
                    stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                    stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
                )
                if p.returncode == 0:
                    written += 1
                else:
                    jpg_path = out_base + ".jpg"
                    with open(jpg_path, "wb") as out:
                        out.write(jpg)
                    written += 1
            else:
                jpg_path = out_base + ".jpg"
                os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(jpg_path), exist_ok=True)
                with open(jpg_path, "wb") as out:
                    out.write(jpg)
                written += 1
        return written

    if img_type == 1:
        # IT3 RLE sprite (1 or more frames)
        pal_off, pal_cnt = struct.unpack_from("<II", raw, 0x28)
        palette = []
        if pal_off and pal_cnt and pal_off + pal_cnt * 4 <= len(raw):
            palette = list(struct.unpack_from(f"<{pal_cnt}I", raw, pal_off))
        t0_off, t0_cnt, tbl1_off, tbl1_cnt = struct.unpack_from("<4I", raw, 0x38)
        if tbl1_off and tbl1_cnt:
            for fi in range(tbl1_cnt):
                foff = struct.unpack_from("<I", raw, tbl1_off + fi * 4)[0]
                if not foff:
                    continue
                cw, ch = 0, 0
                if t0_off and fi < t0_cnt and t0_off + (fi + 1) * 17 <= len(raw):
                    _, _, cw, ch = struct.unpack_from("<4I", raw, t0_off + fi * 17)
                dec = decode_it3_frame(raw, foff, palette, cw, ch)
                if dec:
                    w, h, rgba = dec
                    suffix = "" if tbl1_cnt == 1 else f"_f{fi:03d}"
                    write_png_rgba(out_base + suffix + ".png", w, h, rgba)
                    written += 1
    return written


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Extract assets from Moorhuhn Adventure 2 mha2.dat")
    ap.add_argument("--dat", default="game_files/mha2.dat", help="Path to mha2.dat")
    ap.add_argument("-o", "--out", default="extracted_assets", help="Output directory")
    ap.add_argument("--list", action="store_true", help="List archive entries and exit")
    ap.add_argument("--backgrounds", action="store_true", help="Extract all JPEG backgrounds (objBg + full images)")
    ap.add_argument("--images", action="store_true", help="Extract all ._img assets as PNGs")
    ap.add_argument("--all", action="store_true", help="Extract all files (images as PNG + raw audio/data)")
    ap.add_argument("--filter", default="*", help="Glob filter on VDrv path (e.g. 'x/001/*' or '*objBg*')")
    ap.add_argument("--keep-jpg", action="store_true", help="Write raw .jpg instead of converting JPEG backgrounds to .png")
    ap.add_argument("--upscale", type=int, default=1, choices=[1, 2, 3, 4],
                    help="Upscale extracted backgrounds by Nx (e.g. --upscale 2 for 1600x1200 starter hd_assets/)")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    arc = Mha2Archive(args.dat)
    try:
        matched = [e for e in arc.entries if fnmatch.fnmatch(e["path"], args.filter)]
        if args.list:
            for e in matched:
                comp = "ZLIB" if (e["flags"] & 1) else "RAW "
                print(f"{comp} {e['usize']:>9d}  {e['path']}")
            print(f"\nTotal: {len(matched)} / {len(arc.entries)} files")
            return

        if not (args.backgrounds or args.images or args.all):
            args.backgrounds = True

        n_files = 0
        for e in matched:
            path = e["path"]
            if path.endswith("._img"):
                raw = arc.read_entry(e)
                img_type = struct.unpack_from("<I", raw, 0)[0] if len(raw) >= 4 else -1
                if args.backgrounds and not (args.images or args.all):
                    if img_type != 0:
                        continue
                rel_no_ext = path[:-5]  # strip "._img"
                out_base = os.path.join(args.out, rel_no_ext)
                n_files += extract_img_entry(raw, out_base, convert_jpg_to_png=(not args.keep_jpg), upscale=args.upscale)
            elif args.all:
                raw = arc.read_entry(e)
                dst = os.path.join(args.out, path)
                os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dst), exist_ok=True)
                with open(dst, "wb") as out:
                    out.write(raw)
                n_files += 1
        print(f"[DONE] Extracted {n_files} files into '{args.out}'.")
    finally:
        arc.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

`tools/extract_game.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Standalone legal-copy extractor for Moorhuhn Adventure 2: Der Fluch des Goldes.

Extracts game files directly from either:
  1) An original Wise installer executable (Setup.EXE), OR
  2) A raw CD-ROM .bin (Mode 1/2352) or standard .iso image containing Setup.EXE.

Zero copyrighted data is included in this repository — users supply their own
original disc/installer.
"""
import argparse
import os
import re
import struct
import sys
import zlib


def extract_setup_from_iso_or_bin(image_path, out_setup_path):
    """Locate and extract Der_Fluch_des_Goldes/Setup/Setup.EXE from an ISO9660 .iso or Mode 1/2352 .bin."""
    size = os.path.getsize(image_path)
    with open(image_path, "rb") as f:
        # Detect Mode 1/2352 vs standard 2048-byte ISO
        f.seek(16 * 2352 + 16)
        sig_bin = f.read(6)
        f.seek(16 * 2048)
        sig_iso = f.read(6)

        if sig_bin == b"\x01CD001":
            sector_size, user_off = 2352, 16
        elif sig_iso == b"\x01CD001":
            sector_size, user_off = 2048, 0
        else:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Not a recognized ISO9660 .iso or Mode 1/2352 .bin image: {image_path}")

        def read_sectors(lba, nbytes):
            out = bytearray()
            rem = nbytes
            cur = lba
            while rem > 0:
                f.seek(cur * sector_size + user_off)
                chunk = f.read(min(2048, rem))
                if not chunk:
                    break
                out.extend(chunk)
                rem -= len(chunk)
                cur += 1
            return bytes(out)

        pvd = read_sectors(16, 2048)
        root_rec = pvd[156:156 + 34]
        root_lba = struct.unpack_from("<I", root_rec, 2)[0]
        root_len = struct.unpack_from("<I", root_rec, 10)[0]

        def walk_dir(lba, length, prefix=""):
            data = read_sectors(lba, length)
            pos = 0
            while pos < len(data):
                rec_len = data[pos]
                if rec_len == 0:
                    pos = ((pos // 2048) + 1) * 2048
                    continue
                rec = data[pos:pos + rec_len]
                pos += rec_len
                elba = struct.unpack_from("<I", rec, 2)[0]
                elen = struct.unpack_from("<I", rec, 10)[0]
                flags = rec[25]
                nlen = rec[32]
                name_raw = rec[33:33 + nlen]
                if name_raw in (b"\x00", b"\x01"):
                    continue
                name = name_raw.decode("latin1").split(";")[0]
                full = f"{prefix}/{name}" if prefix else name
                if flags & 2:
                    found = walk_dir(elba, elen, full)
                    if found:
                        return found
                else:
                    low = full.lower()
                    if low.endswith("setup.exe") and ("fluch" in low or "gold" in low or "mha2" in low or elen > 100_000_000):
                        return (full, elba, elen)
            return None

        hit = walk_dir(root_lba, root_len)
        if not hit:
            raise RuntimeError("Could not find Moorhuhn Adventure 2 Setup.EXE inside disc image.")
        full, elba, elen = hit
        print(f"[ISO] Found {full} ({elen:,} bytes at LBA {elba}), extracting...")
        with open(out_setup_path, "wb") as out:
            rem = elen
            cur = elba
            while rem > 0:
                f.seek(cur * sector_size + user_off)
                chunk = f.read(min(2048, rem))
                out.write(chunk)
                rem -= len(chunk)
                cur += 1
    return out_setup_path


def find_pe_overlay(data):
    """Return file offset where PE sections end (Wise payload overlay)."""
    e_lfanew = struct.unpack_from("<I", data, 0x3C)[0]
    n_sec = struct.unpack_from("<H", data, e_lfanew + 6)[0]
    opt_sz = struct.unpack_from("<H", data, e_lfanew + 20)[0]
    sec_off = e_lfanew + 24 + opt_sz
    end = 0
    for i in range(n_sec):
        raw_sz, raw_ptr = struct.unpack_from("<II", data, sec_off + i * 40 + 16)
        end = max(end, raw_ptr + raw_sz)
    return end


def extract_wise_setup(setup_path, out_dir):
    """Extract all game files from a Wise Setup.EXE with full CRC-32 verification."""
    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
    with open(setup_path, "rb") as f:
        header = f.read(1024 * 1024)
        pe_end = find_pe_overlay(header)

        # Locate Wise W32INST deflate stream near pe_end
        f.seek(pe_end)
        probe = f.read(2 * 1024 * 1024)

        # Scan for the Wise script (raw deflate stream containing %MAINDIR%)
        script_bytes = None
        data_base = None
        for off in range(0, min(len(probe), 131072)):
            try:
                d = zlib.decompressobj(-15)
                out = d.decompress(probe[off:off + 65536])
                if b"%MAINDIR%" in out and (b"mha2.exe" in out.lower() or b"mha2.dat" in out.lower()):
                    # Decompress full script
                    d2 = zlib.decompressobj(-15)
                    script_bytes = d2.decompress(probe[off:])
                    data_base = pe_end + off + (len(probe[off:]) - len(d2.unused_data))
                    break
            except zlib.error:
                continue

        if script_bytes is None:
            # Fallback: scan Wise streams starting after Wise DLLs
            pos = pe_end
            # Find Wise header marker (starts with 0x0000 or DLL name)
            idx = probe.find(b"W32INST")
            if idx >= 0:
                pos = pe_end + idx
            for off in range(0, len(probe) - 16):
                if probe[off:off + 2] in (b"\xec\xbd", b"\xed\xbd", b"\x78\x9c", b"\x78\xda"):
                    for wbits in (-15, 15):
                        try:
                            d = zlib.decompressobj(wbits)
                            out = d.decompress(probe[off:off + 262144])
                            if b"%MAINDIR%" in out and b"mha2" in out.lower():
                                d2 = zlib.decompressobj(wbits)
                                script_bytes = d2.decompress(probe[off:])
                                data_base = pe_end + off + (len(probe[off:]) - len(d2.unused_data))
                                break
                        except zlib.error:
                            pass
                    if script_bytes is not None:
                        break

        if script_bytes is None:
            raise RuntimeError("Could not locate Wise installation script in Setup.EXE.")

        # Parse Wise InstallFile opcodes (0x00) referencing %MAINDIR%\<filename>
        # Record layout before "%MAINDIR%\\":
        #   opcode(1)=0x00, flags(2), def_start(4), def_end(4), date(2), time(2), uncomp_size(4), ... crc32(4)
        # More reliably: search for every "%MAINDIR%\\" entry and read the preceding struct!
        # Wise opcode 0x00 format (40 bytes header before dest path):
        #   +0: 0x00
        #   +1: flags (2 bytes)
        #   +3: def_start (u32), +7: def_end (u32), +11: date (u16), +13: time (u16),
        #   +15: uncomp_size (u32), +19..38: 20 bytes zeros/version, +39: crc32 (u32) -> actually offset varies slightly.
        # Auto-calibrate base_delta using the first valid file (e.g. mha2.exe or readme)!
        entries = []
        for m in re.finditer(rb"%MAINDIR%\\([^\x00]+)\x00", script_bytes):
            rel = m.group(1).decode("latin1").replace("\\", "/")
            # Find the opcode header 40 bytes before "%MAINDIR%\\"
            hdr_pos = m.start() - 40
            if hdr_pos < 0:
                continue
            def_start, def_end = struct.unpack_from("<II", script_bytes, hdr_pos + 2)
            uncomp_sz = struct.unpack_from("<I", script_bytes, hdr_pos + 14)[0]
            crc32_exp = struct.unpack_from("<I", script_bytes, hdr_pos + 36)[0]
            if def_end > def_start and uncomp_sz > 0:
                entries.append((rel, def_start, def_end, uncomp_sz, crc32_exp))

        if not entries:
            raise RuntimeError("Parsed Wise script, but found no %MAINDIR% file entries.")

        # Calibrate exact Wise stream base offset using the first small entry
        file_size = os.path.getsize(setup_path)
        base_off = None
        cal_entry = min(entries, key=lambda e: e[2] - e[1])
        _, c_start, c_end, c_usz, c_crc = cal_entry
        c_len = c_end - c_start
        # Search around pe_end..pe_end+2MB for where def_start lands with valid deflate + CRC match
        f.seek(0)
        search_limit = min(file_size, pe_end + 4 * 1024 * 1024)
        for cand_base in range(pe_end, search_limit):
            if cand_base + c_end > file_size:
                break
            f.seek(cand_base + c_start)
            comp = f.read(c_len)
            try:
                raw = zlib.decompress(comp, -15)
                if len(raw) == c_usz and (zlib.crc32(raw) & 0xFFFFFFFF) == c_crc:
                    base_off = cand_base
                    break
            except zlib.error:
                continue

        if base_off is None:
            raise RuntimeError("Could not calibrate Wise stream base offset.")

        print(f"[WISE] Calibrated payload base at 0x{base_off:x}; extracting {len(entries)} files to '{out_dir}'...")
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(out_dir, "mha2.sav"), exist_ok=True)
        for rel, dstart, dend, usz, crc_exp in entries:
            dst = os.path.join(out_dir, rel)
            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dst), exist_ok=True)
            f.seek(base_off + dstart)
            comp = f.read(dend - dstart)
            raw = zlib.decompress(comp, -15)
            crc_got = zlib.crc32(raw) & 0xFFFFFFFF
            if len(raw) != usz or crc_got != crc_exp:
                raise RuntimeError(f"CRC/size mismatch on {rel}: got {len(raw)}/{crc_got:08x}, expected {usz}/{crc_exp:08x}")
            with open(dst, "wb") as out_f:
                out_f.write(raw)
            print(f"  OK  {rel:<30s} {len(raw):>11,d} bytes  CRC32={crc_got:08x}")


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Extract Moorhuhn Adventure 2 game files from Setup.EXE or CD .bin/.iso")
    ap.add_argument("source", help="Path to Setup.EXE or CD-ROM .bin/.iso image")
    ap.add_argument("-o", "--out", default="game_files", help="Output directory (default: game_files)")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    src = args.source
    tmp_setup = None
    if src.lower().endswith((".bin", ".iso")):
        tmp_setup = os.path.join(args.out, "_Setup_tmp.EXE")
        os.makedirs(args.out, exist_ok=True)
        extract_setup_from_iso_or_bin(src, tmp_setup)
        src = tmp_setup

    try:
        extract_wise_setup(src, args.out)
    finally:
        if tmp_setup and os.path.exists(tmp_setup):
            os.remove(tmp_setup)
    print(f"[DONE] Game files ready in '{args.out}'.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash

    SRC_SPR="$ROOT_DIR/hd_assets/sprites/s/001/img/walkS_f000.png"
```

`tools/package_portable_linux.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Builds a self-contained portable Linux x86_64 bundle for any modern Linux distro
# (Ubuntu 22.04+, Fedora 39+, Debian 12+, Arch, Linux Mint 21+, SteamOS, NixOS, etc.)
set -euo pipefail

MAKE_ARCHIVE=1
for arg in "$@"; do
    case "$arg" in
        --no-archive) MAKE_ARCHIVE=0 ;;
        *)
            echo "Usage: $0 [--no-archive]" >&2
            exit 2
            ;;
    esac
done

ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
DIST_NAME="Moorhuhn_Adventure2_Remastered_Linux_x86_64"
STAGE="$ROOT/work/dist/$DIST_NAME"
ARCHIVE="$ROOT/work/dist/${DIST_NAME}.tar.gz"
CONTAINERFILE="$ROOT/tools/Containerfile.portable"

if [[ ! -f "$ROOT/recomp/gen/dispatch.c" ]]; then
    echo "Error: $ROOT/recomp/gen/dispatch.c is missing; run 'make lift' first." >&2
    exit 1
fi

if command -v podman >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    CTR=podman
    CTR_RUN_ARGS=()
elif command -v docker >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    CTR=docker
    CTR_RUN_ARGS=(--user "$(id -u):$(id -g)")
else
    echo "Error: podman or docker is required to build the portable Linux bundle." >&2
    exit 1
fi

CF_HASH="$(sha256sum "$CONTAINERFILE" | awk '{print $1}')"
IMAGE_TAGGED="localhost/mha2-portable-builder:ubuntu2204-${CF_HASH:0:12}"
IMAGE_LATEST="localhost/mha2-portable-builder:ubuntu2204"

# NixOS `/` (/var/tmp) may be a small tmpfs; keep container image staging under work/tmp
mkdir -p "$ROOT/work/tmp"
export TMPDIR="$ROOT/work/tmp"

if ! "$CTR" image exists "$IMAGE_TAGGED" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "[pack] Building portable builder image $IMAGE_TAGGED ..."
    "$CTR" build -f "$CONTAINERFILE" -t "$IMAGE_TAGGED" -t "$IMAGE_LATEST" "$ROOT/tools"
else
    echo "[pack] Reusing cached builder image $IMAGE_TAGGED"
fi

echo "[pack] Staging portable Linux x86_64 bundle in $STAGE"
rm -rf "$STAGE"
mkdir -p "$STAGE/lib" "$STAGE/fonts" "$STAGE/game_files/mha2.sav" "$ROOT/work/build_portable"

# Build binary, collect transitive non-core deps, patch ELF headers, bundle fonts & verify ABI
"$CTR" run --rm "${CTR_RUN_ARGS[@]}" -v "$ROOT:/src" -w /src "$IMAGE_TAGGED" bash -euo pipefail -c '
    DIST_NAME="'"$DIST_NAME"'"
    STAGE="/src/work/dist/$DIST_NAME"
    export PKG_CONFIG_PATH=/opt/sdl2-portable/lib/pkgconfig:/opt/ffmpeg-min/lib/pkgconfig

    # Force recompile of runtime objects against /opt/sdl2-portable headers while preserving gen_*.o cache
    rm -f work/build_portable/rt_*.o work/build_portable/mha2_linux

    SDL_CFLAGS="$(pkg-config --cflags sdl2 SDL2_ttf gl) $(pkg-config --cflags libavformat libavcodec libswscale libavutil)"
    SDL_LIBS="-Wl,-rpath,/opt/sdl2-portable/lib $(pkg-config --libs sdl2 SDL2_ttf gl) $(pkg-config --static --libs libavformat libavcodec libswscale libavutil)"

    make -j"$(nproc)" \
        BUILD_DIR=work/build_portable \
        TARGET=work/build_portable/mha2_linux \
        SDL_CFLAGS="$SDL_CFLAGS" \
        SDL_LIBS="$SDL_LIBS"

    cp -f work/build_portable/mha2_linux "$STAGE/mha2_linux"
    chmod 755 "$STAGE/mha2_linux"

    sdl2_resolved="$(ldd "$STAGE/mha2_linux" | awk "/libSDL2-2\.0\.so\.0 =>/ {print \$3}")"
    if [[ "$sdl2_resolved" != "/opt/sdl2-portable/lib/libSDL2-2.0.so.0" ]]; then
        echo "ERROR: Expected libSDL2-2.0.so.0 from /opt/sdl2-portable/lib, got '\''$sdl2_resolved'\''" >&2
        exit 1
    fi

    EXCLUDE_REGEX="^(linux-vdso|ld-linux|libc\.so|libm\.so|libmvec\.so|libpthread\.so|libdl\.so|librt\.so|libresolv\.so|libnss_|libz\.so|libGL\.so|libGLX\.so|libGLdispatch\.so|libEGL\.so|libOpenGL\.so|libvulkan\.so|libdrm\.so|libgbm\.so|libX11|libXext|libXau|libXdmcp|libxcb|libwayland|libasound|libpulse|libpipewire|libdbus|libsystemd|libudev|libstdc\+\+\.so|libgcc_s\.so)"

    ldd "$STAGE/mha2_linux" 2>/dev/null | awk "/=> \// {print \$3}" | while read -r so; do
        base="$(basename "$so")"
        if [[ "$base" =~ $EXCLUDE_REGEX ]]; then
            continue
        fi
        if [[ ! -f "$STAGE/lib/$base" ]]; then
            cp -L "$so" "$STAGE/lib/$base"
            chmod 755 "$STAGE/lib/$base"
        fi
    done

    patchelf --set-interpreter /lib64/ld-linux-x86-64.so.2 --set-rpath "\$ORIGIN/lib" "$STAGE/mha2_linux"
    for so in "$STAGE"/lib/*.so*; do
        [[ -f "$so" ]] || continue
        patchelf --set-rpath "\$ORIGIN" "$so"
    done

    for fn in DejaVuSans.ttf DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf DejaVuSerif-Bold.ttf; do
        src="/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/$fn"
        if [[ ! -s "$src" ]]; then
            echo "ERROR: Missing font $src inside container!" >&2
            exit 1
        fi
        cp -L "$src" "$STAGE/fonts/$fn"
    done

    echo "[pack] Patched binary + $(ls -1 "$STAGE/lib" | wc -l) bundled libraries"

    # Strict portability & ABI verification (must resolve purely via ELF RUNPATH without LD_LIBRARY_PATH)
    if ldd "$STAGE/mha2_linux" | grep -q "not found"; then
        echo "ERROR: Unresolved shared library dependency without LD_LIBRARY_PATH:" >&2
        ldd "$STAGE/mha2_linux" >&2
        exit 1
    fi
    for req_lib in libSDL2-2.0.so.0 libSDL2_ttf-2.0.so.0; do
        resolved="$(ldd "$STAGE/mha2_linux" | awk -v lib="$req_lib" "\$1 == lib {print \$3}")"
        if [[ "$resolved" != "$STAGE/lib/$req_lib" ]]; then
            echo "ERROR: Expected $req_lib to resolve from $STAGE/lib/$req_lib via RUNPATH, got '\''$resolved'\''" >&2
            exit 1
        fi
    done

    for elf in "$STAGE/mha2_linux" "$STAGE"/lib/*.so*; do
        [[ -f "$elf" ]] || continue
        if grep -qa "/nix/store" "$elf"; then
            echo "ERROR: Found /nix/store reference in $elf" >&2
            exit 1
        fi
        bad_ver="$(readelf -V "$elf" 2>/dev/null | grep -oE "GLIBC_2\.(3[6-9]|[4-9][0-9])|GLIBCXX_3\.4\.(3[1-9]|[4-9][0-9])|CXXABI_1\.3\.(1[4-9]|[2-9][0-9])" || true)"
        if [[ -n "$bad_ver" ]]; then
            echo "ERROR: Incompatible ABI symbol version in $(basename "$elf"): $bad_ver" >&2
            exit 1
        fi
        bad_need="$(readelf -d "$elf" 2>/dev/null | grep "NEEDED" | grep -E "libvulkan\.so|libpipewire|libpulse|libwayland|libEGL|libgbm|libdecor" || true)"
        if [[ -n "$bad_need" ]]; then
            echo "ERROR: Forbidden hard DT_NEEDED in $(basename "$elf"): $bad_need" >&2
            exit 1
        fi
    done

    echo "[pack] ABI & portability verification passed!"
'

# Copy original game files (without personal saves)
cp "$ROOT/game_files/mha2.dat" "$ROOT/game_files/mha2.exe" "$STAGE/game_files/"
cp "$ROOT/game_files/"*.avi "$STAGE/game_files/"

# Copy complete 4K hd_assets (backgrounds, sprites, cursors, 4K MP4 videos, caches)
cp -a --reflink=auto "$ROOT/hd_assets" "$STAGE/hd_assets"

# Write portable launcher script (rely on ELF $ORIGIN/lib RUNPATH so host GPU/audio libs never conflict)
cat > "$STAGE/PLAY.sh" << 'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
cd "$DIR"
export MHA2_FONT_PATH="${MHA2_FONT_PATH:-$DIR/fonts/DejaVuSans.ttf}"
export MHA2_GAME_FONT_PATH="${MHA2_GAME_FONT_PATH:-$DIR/fonts/DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf}"
if [[ ! -f "$DIR/mha2_settings.ini" && $# -eq 0 ]]; then
    exec "$DIR/mha2_linux" --fullscreen
fi
exec "$DIR/mha2_linux" "$@"
EOF
chmod 755 "$STAGE/PLAY.sh"

# Write quick-start guide
cat > "$STAGE/README_PLAY.txt" << 'EOF'
Moorhuhn Adventure 2: Der Fluch des Goldes — Remastered v5 (Linux x86_64)
========================================================================

HOW TO PLAY:
  Double-click PLAY.sh or run in a terminal:
    ./PLAY.sh

  Windowed mode:
    ./PLAY.sh --windowed

CONTROLS & SHORTCUTS:
  Left Click     Interact / Walk
  Right Click    Cycle action cursor (Look / Take / Talk / Use)
  F1             Open Remaster v5 Graphics, Post-FX & Audio Settings Menu
  F2             Live Split-Screen Before / After (2005 vs 4K Remaster)
  Space or H     Hold to highlight interactive hotspots in the room
  Shift          Hold for 2x fast-walk speed
  Ctrl + P       Save a clean 4K PNG screenshot (saved to screenshots/)
  Alt + Enter    Toggle Fullscreen / Windowed
  F3             Toggle FPS / Remaster HUD
  F7 / F8 / F9   Volume Down / Up / Mute
  Gamepad        Left Stick = Cursor, A = Click, B = Right-Click, X = Hotspots, Start = F1 Menu

REQUIREMENTS:
  64-bit Linux (Ubuntu 22.04+, Fedora 39+, Debian 12+, Arch, Mint 21+, SteamOS, etc.)
  with OpenGL 3.3+ graphics drivers.
EOF

if [[ "$MAKE_ARCHIVE" -eq 1 ]]; then
    echo "[pack] Creating archive $ARCHIVE ..."
    GZIP=-1 tar -czf "$ARCHIVE" -C "$ROOT/work/dist" "$DIST_NAME"
    ls -lh "$ARCHIVE"
else
    echo "[pack] Skipping .tar.gz creation (--no-archive)"
fi
echo "[pack] Done!"
```

`tools/polish_cursors.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Moorhuhn Adventure 2 — Vector-Clean SDF Cursor Polisher
Replaces the bumpy 2005 tbl2 outer fringe on all cursor frames (mcArrow, mcLook,
mcTake, mcTalk, mcUse, cuExit, cuHelp) with a mathematically smooth signed-distance-field (SDF) contour,
clean interior RGB dilation, and a subtle modern soft drop shadow — while preserving 100%
of the original iconic cursor artwork and HDS5 matching.
"""
import glob
import os
import shutil
import cv2
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image


def polish_cursor(im: Image.Image) -> Image.Image:
    rgba = np.asarray(im.convert("RGBA"), dtype=np.uint8)
    h, w = rgba.shape[:2]
    rgb = rgba[:, :, :3].astype(np.float32)
    alpha = rgba[:, :, 3]

    # 1. Build clean core binary mask from opaque interior (eliminates bumpy tbl2 fringe)
    core = (alpha >= 175).astype(np.uint8)
    # Remove any isolated 1-2px detached noise specks
    num_l, labels, stats, _ = cv2.connectedComponentsWithStats(core, connectivity=8)
    clean_core = np.zeros_like(core)
    for lbl in range(1, num_l):
        if stats[lbl, cv2.CC_STAT_AREA] >= 36:
            clean_core[labels == lbl] = 1

    # 2. Signed Distance Field (positive inside, negative outside), smoothed for vector-like curvature
    dist_in = cv2.distanceTransform(clean_core, cv2.DIST_L2, 5)
    dist_out = cv2.distanceTransform(1 - clean_core, cv2.DIST_L2, 5)
    sdf = dist_in - dist_out
    sdf_smooth = cv2.GaussianBlur(sdf, (0, 0), sigmaX=2.2, sigmaY=2.2)

    # Anti-aliased 1.8px crisp vector edge from smoothed SDF (slightly expanded by +0.6px to preserve original fullness)
    new_alpha = np.clip((sdf_smooth + 0.8) / 1.8 + 0.5, 0.0, 1.0)

    # 3. Dilate clean opaque interior RGB outward so the smooth rim has zero grey/muddy fringe
    dil_rgb = rgb.copy()
    mask = (alpha >= 210) & (clean_core > 0)
    for _ in range(12):
        if mask.all():
            break
        k = np.ones((3, 3), np.float32)
        m_f = mask.astype(np.float32)
        s_m = cv2.filter2D(m_f, -1, k, borderType=cv2.BORDER_REPLICATE)
        fill = (~mask) & (s_m > 0.5)
        if not fill.any():
            break
        for c in range(3):
            s_c = cv2.filter2D(dil_rgb[:, :, c] * m_f, -1, k, borderType=cv2.BORDER_REPLICATE)
            dil_rgb[:, :, c][fill] = s_c[fill] / s_m[fill]
        mask[fill] = True

    # 4. Subtle clean modern drop shadow (offset +2px,+2px, soft blur) behind the crisp cursor
    sh_alpha = np.zeros((h, w), dtype=np.float32)
    sh_alpha[2:, 2:] = new_alpha[:-2, :-2]
    sh_alpha = cv2.GaussianBlur(sh_alpha, (0, 0), sigmaX=2.4, sigmaY=2.4) * 0.42

    # Porter-Duff "cursor OVER drop shadow"
    out_a = new_alpha + sh_alpha * (1.0 - new_alpha)
    out_rgb = np.zeros_like(dil_rgb)
    valid = out_a > 1e-4
    for c in range(3):
        # Shadow RGB is dark charcoal (12, 10, 16)
        sh_c = 12.0
        out_rgb[:, :, c][valid] = (
            dil_rgb[:, :, c][valid] * new_alpha[valid]
            + sh_c * sh_alpha[valid] * (1.0 - new_alpha[valid])
        ) / out_a[valid]

    out = np.dstack([
        np.clip(out_rgb + 0.5, 0, 255).astype(np.uint8),
        np.clip(out_a * 255.0 + 0.5, 0, 255).astype(np.uint8),
    ])
    return Image.fromarray(out, "RGBA")


def main():
    patterns = (
        "hd_assets/sprites/g/img/mc*.webp",
        "hd_assets/sprites/g/img/mc*.png",
        "hd_assets/sprites/g/img/cu*.webp",
        "hd_assets/sprites/g/img/cu*.png",
    )
    files = sorted({p for pat in patterns for p in glob.glob(pat)})
    backup_dir = "work/cursors_pre_sdf_backup"
    os.makedirs(backup_dir, exist_ok=True)
    for p in files:
        bk = os.path.join(backup_dir, os.path.basename(p))
        if not os.path.exists(bk):
            shutil.copy2(p, bk)
        with Image.open(bk) as im:
            pol = polish_cursor(im)
        if p.endswith(".webp"):
            pol.save(p, format="WEBP", lossless=True, exact=True, quality=100, method=6)
        else:
            pol.save(p, compress_level=3)
        print(f"[cursor] Polished {p} ({pol.size})")
    cache = "hd_assets/.hd_sprite_index.cache"
    if os.path.exists(cache):
        os.remove(cache)
    print(f"[cursor] Done ({len(files)} cursor frames polished, HDS5 cache cleared).")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/remaster_bg_diffusion.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Moorhuhn Adventure 2 - Generative 4K Room Background Remaster (SDXL + ControlNet-Tile)
Produces true modern-game 4K PBR-like textures with zero baked-in 2005 jaggies while
locking 100% pixel alignment to interactive hotspots and foreground props.

Usage:
  LD_LIBRARY_PATH="$(dirname $(find /nix/store -maxdepth 3 -name libstdc++.so.6 2>/dev/null | head -n1)):/run/opengl-driver/lib" \
    work/diffusion_venv/bin/python tools/remaster_bg_diffusion.py \
      --samples work/diffusion_samples \
      hd_assets/l/001/img/xobjBg.png hd_assets/l/005/img/objBg.png hd_assets/l/010/img/objBg.png
"""
import argparse
import os
import sys
import time
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
import cv2
import torch
from diffusers import (
    ControlNetModel,
    StableDiffusionXLControlNetImg2ImgPipeline,
    DPMSolverMultistepScheduler,
    AutoencoderKL,
)

BASE_MODEL = "RunDiffusion/Juggernaut-XL-v9"
CONTROLNET_MODEL = "xinsir/controlnet-tile-sdxl-1.0"

PROMPT = (
    "modern AAA stylized 3D point-and-click adventure game environment, Unreal Engine 5 render, "
    "crisp physically based materials, fine wood grain, carved weathered stone, rich woven fabric, "
    "clean smooth anti-aliased edges, intricate sharp 4K textures, rich cinematic lighting, "
    "subtle raytraced ambient occlusion, faithful remaster"
)
NEG_PROMPT = (
    "blurry, low resolution, jpeg artifacts, aliasing, jagged staircase edges, pixelated, "
    "muddy, smeary oil paint, plastic sheen, text, watermark, signature, extra objects, "
    "changed layout, distorted geometry, noisy grain"
)


def match_lowfreq_color(remastered: Image.Image, reference: Image.Image, sigma: float = 96.0, mix: float = 0.65) -> Image.Image:
    """Keep the remastered room's exact global lighting & color temperature aligned with
    the original so foreground sprites (Moorhuhn, props, doors) blend seamlessly."""
    rem = np.asarray(remastered, dtype=np.float32)
    ref = np.asarray(reference.resize(remastered.size, Image.LANCZOS), dtype=np.float32)

    # Compute low-frequency illumination delta in Lab space
    rem_lab = cv2.cvtColor(rem / 255.0, cv2.COLOR_RGB2LAB)
    ref_lab = cv2.cvtColor(ref / 255.0, cv2.COLOR_RGB2LAB)

    k = int(sigma * 3) | 1
    rem_low = cv2.GaussianBlur(rem_lab, (k, k), sigma)
    ref_low = cv2.GaussianBlur(ref_lab, (k, k), sigma)

    out_lab = rem_lab + (ref_low - rem_low) * mix
    out_lab[..., 0] = np.clip(out_lab[..., 0], 0.0, 100.0)
    out_lab[..., 1] = np.clip(out_lab[..., 1], -128.0, 127.0)
    out_lab[..., 2] = np.clip(out_lab[..., 2], -128.0, 127.0)

    out_rgb = cv2.cvtColor(out_lab, cv2.COLOR_LAB2RGB)
    return Image.fromarray(np.clip(out_rgb * 255.0, 0, 255).astype(np.uint8))


def load_pipeline():
    print(f"[diffusion] Loading ControlNet: {CONTROLNET_MODEL}", flush=True)
    controlnet = ControlNetModel.from_pretrained(
        CONTROLNET_MODEL,
        torch_dtype=torch.float16,
    )
    print(f"[diffusion] Loading SDXL base:  {BASE_MODEL}", flush=True)
    pipe = StableDiffusionXLControlNetImg2ImgPipeline.from_pretrained(
        BASE_MODEL,
        controlnet=controlnet,
        torch_dtype=torch.float16,
        variant="fp16",
        use_safetensors=True,
    )
    pipe.scheduler = DPMSolverMultistepScheduler.from_config(
        pipe.scheduler.config,
        use_karras_sigmas=True,
        algorithm_type="dpmsolver++",
    )
    pipe.vae.enable_tiling()
    pipe.vae.enable_slicing()
    pipe.enable_model_cpu_offload()
    return pipe


def make_feather_mask(w: int, h: int, overlap_l: int, overlap_r: int, overlap_t: int, overlap_b: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Cosine-feathered 2D weight mask for seamless tile blending."""
    wx = np.ones(w, dtype=np.float32)
    wy = np.ones(h, dtype=np.float32)
    if overlap_l > 0:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, np.pi * 0.5, overlap_l, dtype=np.float32)
        wx[:overlap_l] = np.sin(t) ** 2
    if overlap_r > 0:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, np.pi * 0.5, overlap_r, dtype=np.float32)
        wx[-overlap_r:] = np.cos(t) ** 2
    if overlap_t > 0:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, np.pi * 0.5, overlap_t, dtype=np.float32)
        wy[:overlap_t] = np.sin(t) ** 2
    if overlap_b > 0:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, np.pi * 0.5, overlap_b, dtype=np.float32)
        wy[-overlap_b:] = np.cos(t) ** 2
    return np.outer(wy, wx)[..., None]


def remaster_image(pipe, src_img: Image.Image, seed: int = 42,
                   s1_strength: float = 0.46, s2_strength: float = 0.36) -> Image.Image:
    orig_w, orig_h = src_img.size

    # Stage 1: Global pass at 1600x896 (exact 2x of 800x450, divisible by 8)
    # Eliminates baked-in 2005 1x jaggies & synthesizes coherent modern PBR materials.
    s1_w = ((orig_w // 2) // 8) * 8
    s1_h = ((orig_h // 2) // 8) * 8
    s1_in = src_img.resize((s1_w, s1_h), Image.LANCZOS)

    gen = torch.Generator(device="cuda").manual_seed(seed)
    print(f"  [stage 1] Global pass {s1_w}x{s1_h} (strength={s1_strength})...", flush=True)
    t0 = time.time()
    s1_out = pipe(
        prompt=PROMPT,
        negative_prompt=NEG_PROMPT,
        image=s1_in,
        control_image=s1_in,
        strength=s1_strength,
        controlnet_conditioning_scale=0.72,
        num_inference_steps=32,
        guidance_scale=6.5,
        generator=gen,
    ).images[0]
    print(f"  [stage 1] Done in {time.time() - t0:.1f}s", flush=True)

    # Stage 2: Seamless 3x2 tiled 4K micro-detail pass at SDXL's native ~1152x1024 sweet spot
    s2_base = s1_out.resize((orig_w, orig_h), Image.LANCZOS)
    ctrl_full = Image.blend(src_img.resize((orig_w, orig_h), Image.LANCZOS), s2_base, 0.65)

    overlap = 224
    # 3 columns across 3200px (~1200px each), 2 rows across 1800px (~1016px each)
    x_ranges = [
        (0, 1216),
        (992, 2208),
        (1984, orig_w),
    ]
    y_ranges = [
        (0, 1016),
        (792, orig_h),
    ]

    accum = np.zeros((orig_h, orig_w, 3), dtype=np.float32)
    weight = np.zeros((orig_h, orig_w, 1), dtype=np.float32)

    total_tiles = len(x_ranges) * len(y_ranges)
    tile_idx = 0
    for yi, (y0, y1) in enumerate(y_ranges):
        for xi, (x0, x1) in enumerate(x_ranges):
            tile_idx += 1
            tw, th = x1 - x0, y1 - y0
            rw = (tw // 8) * 8
            rh = (th // 8) * 8
            tile_in = s2_base.crop((x0, y0, x1, y1))
            tile_ctrl = ctrl_full.crop((x0, y0, x1, y1))
            if (rw, rh) != (tw, th):
                tile_in = tile_in.resize((rw, rh), Image.LANCZOS)
                tile_ctrl = tile_ctrl.resize((rw, rh), Image.LANCZOS)

            t1 = time.time()
            print(f"  [stage 2] Tile {tile_idx}/{total_tiles} ({rw}x{rh}, strength={s2_strength})...", flush=True)
            tgen = torch.Generator(device="cuda").manual_seed(seed + tile_idx)
            tile_out = pipe(
                prompt=PROMPT,
                negative_prompt=NEG_PROMPT,
                image=tile_in,
                control_image=tile_ctrl,
                strength=s2_strength,
                controlnet_conditioning_scale=0.76,
                num_inference_steps=30,
                guidance_scale=6.5,
                generator=tgen,
            ).images[0]
            if tile_out.size != (tw, th):
                tile_out = tile_out.resize((tw, th), Image.LANCZOS)
            print(f"  [stage 2] Tile {tile_idx}/{total_tiles} done in {time.time() - t1:.1f}s", flush=True)

            ov_l = overlap if xi > 0 else 0
            ov_r = overlap if xi < len(x_ranges) - 1 else 0
            ov_t = overlap if yi > 0 else 0
            ov_b = overlap if yi < len(y_ranges) - 1 else 0
            mask = make_feather_mask(tw, th, ov_l, ov_r, ov_t, ov_b)

            accum[y0:y1, x0:x1] += np.asarray(tile_out, dtype=np.float32) * mask
            weight[y0:y1, x0:x1] += mask

    blended = np.clip(accum / np.maximum(weight, 1e-6), 0, 255).astype(np.uint8)
    out_img = Image.fromarray(blended)
    out_img = match_lowfreq_color(out_img, src_img, sigma=96.0, mix=0.65)
    return out_img


def draw_label(im: Image.Image, text: str, xy=(24, 20)):
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
    try:
        font = ImageFont.truetype("/run/current-system/sw/share/X11/fonts/DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf", 34)
    except Exception:
        font = ImageFont.load_default()
    bbox = draw.textbbox(xy, text, font=font)
    pad = 12
    draw.rectangle((bbox[0] - pad, bbox[1] - pad, bbox[2] + pad, bbox[3] + pad), fill=(15, 18, 28))
    draw.text(xy, text, fill=(255, 224, 120), font=font)


def save_comparisons(before: Image.Image, after: Image.Image, out_dir: str, tag: str):
    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
    # 1. Full remastered 4K output
    after.save(os.path.join(out_dir, f"{tag}_after_4k.png"), compress_level=3)

    # 2. Full-room 50/50 vertical split comparison (1920x1080 for quick full-screen viewing)
    w, h = 1920, 1080
    b_rs = before.resize((w, h), Image.LANCZOS)
    a_rs = after.resize((w, h), Image.LANCZOS)
    split = Image.new("RGB", (w, h))
    split.paste(b_rs.crop((0, 0, w // 2, h)), (0, 0))
    split.paste(a_rs.crop((w // 2, 0, w, h)), (w // 2, 0))
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(split)
    draw.line([(w // 2, 0), (w // 2, h)], fill=(255, 215, 64), width=4)
    draw_label(split, "BEFORE: GAN 4x (NMKD-Siax)", (24, 20))
    draw_label(split, "AFTER: SDXL ControlNet-Tile 4K", (w // 2 + 24, 20))
    split.save(os.path.join(out_dir, f"{tag}_split.png"))

    # 3. Side-by-side full room (top = BEFORE, bottom = AFTER) at 1600x1800
    sw, sh = 1600, 900
    stack = Image.new("RGB", (sw, sh * 2 + 6), (255, 215, 64))
    b_st = before.resize((sw, sh), Image.LANCZOS)
    a_st = after.resize((sw, sh), Image.LANCZOS)
    draw_label(b_st, f"BEFORE (GAN 4x) - {tag}", (20, 16))
    draw_label(a_st, f"AFTER (SDXL ControlNet-Tile 4K) - {tag}", (20, 16))
    stack.paste(b_st, (0, 0))
    stack.paste(a_st, (0, sh + 6))
    stack.save(os.path.join(out_dir, f"{tag}_full_compare.png"))

    # 4. Native 1:1 4K detail crops (3 regions: top-center, mid-right, bottom-left)
    bw, bh = before.size
    cw, ch = 720, 540
    crops = [
        ("Detail 1 (Top Center)", (bw // 2 - cw // 2, 0)),
        ("Detail 2 (Mid Right)",  (int(bw * 0.62), int(bh * 0.25))),
        ("Detail 3 (Bottom Left)", (int(bw * 0.08), int(bh * 0.58))),
    ]
    sheet = Image.new("RGB", (cw * 2 + 8, (ch + 8) * len(crops)), (25, 28, 38))
    for i, (name, (cx, cy)) in enumerate(crops):
        cx = max(0, min(cx, bw - cw))
        cy = max(0, min(cy, bh - ch))
        cb = before.crop((cx, cy, cx + cw, cy + ch))
        ca = after.crop((cx, cy, cx + cw, cy + ch))
        draw_label(cb, f"BEFORE 1:1 - {name}", (12, 10))
        draw_label(ca, f"AFTER 1:1 - {name}", (12, 10))
        y = i * (ch + 8)
        sheet.paste(cb, (0, y))
        sheet.paste(ca, (cw + 8, y))
    sheet.save(os.path.join(out_dir, f"{tag}_zoom_1to1.png"))


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--samples", required=True, help="Output directory for before/after comparisons")
    ap.add_argument("--s1-strength", type=float, default=0.46)
    ap.add_argument("--s2-strength", type=float, default=0.36)
    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    ap.add_argument("inputs", nargs="+", help="Input 4K room background PNGs")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    pipe = load_pipeline()
    for p in args.inputs:
        room = os.path.basename(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(p)))
        base = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(p))[0]
        tag = f"room_{room}_{base}"
        print(f"\n[remaster] Processing {p} -> {tag}", flush=True)
        src = Image.open(p).convert("RGB")
        out = remaster_image(pipe, src, seed=args.seed,
                             s1_strength=args.s1_strength, s2_strength=args.s2_strength)
        save_comparisons(src, out, args.samples, tag)
        print(f"[remaster] Saved comparisons for {tag} in {args.samples}/", flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
