`tools/remaster_videos_4k.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Moorhuhn Adventure 2 — True 4K FMV Cutscene Remaster (4x-ClearRealityV1 Streaming GPU Pipeline)
Streams frames directly from ffmpeg -> RTX GPU (4x-ClearRealityV1 FP16) -> ffmpeg H.264 MP4
at native 3200x2400 (4x of 800x600) with zero temp-frame disk bloat!
"""
import argparse
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import numpy as np
import torch
import spandrel
import spandrel_extra_arches

spandrel_extra_arches.install()


def probe_video(path: str):
    p = subprocess.run(
        ["ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-select_streams", "v:0",
         "-show_entries", "stream=width,height,r_frame_rate,nb_frames",
         "-of", "csv=p=0", path],
        stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, text=True, check=True,
    )
    parts = p.stdout.strip().split(",")
    w, h = int(parts[0]), int(parts[1])
    fps = parts[2]
    nb = int(parts[3]) if len(parts) > 3 and parts[3].isdigit() else 0
    return w, h, fps, nb


@torch.inference_mode()
def remaster_video(desc, src_avi: str, dst_mp4: str, batch_size: int = 4, device: str = "cuda", dtype=torch.float16):
    w, h, fps, nb = probe_video(src_avi)
    scale = desc.scale
    ow, oh = w * scale, h * scale
    print(f"\n[video] {src_avi} ({w}x{h} @ {fps} fps, ~{nb} frames) -> {dst_mp4} ({ow}x{oh})", flush=True)

    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dst_mp4), exist_ok=True)
    tmp_mp4 = dst_mp4 + ".tmp.mp4"

    dec = subprocess.Popen(
        ["ffmpeg", "-v", "error", "-i", src_avi,
         "-f", "rawvideo", "-pix_fmt", "rgb24", "pipe:1"],
        stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
    )
    enc = subprocess.Popen(
        ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-v", "error",
         "-f", "rawvideo", "-pix_fmt", "rgb24", "-s", f"{ow}x{oh}", "-r", fps, "-i", "pipe:0",
         "-i", src_avi,
         "-map", "0:v:0", "-map", "1:a?",
         "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "medium", "-crf", "17", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p",
         "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "192k", "-movflags", "+faststart",
         tmp_mp4],
        stdin=subprocess.PIPE,
    )

    frame_bytes = w * h * 3
    done = 0
    t0 = time.time()
    batch = []

    def flush_batch(frames_list):
        nonlocal done
        arr = np.stack(frames_list, axis=0)  # (B, H, W, 3) uint8
        ten = torch.from_numpy(arr).to(device=device, dtype=dtype).permute(0, 3, 1, 2) / 255.0
        pred = desc(ten).clamp_(0.0, 1.0)
        out = (pred.permute(0, 2, 3, 1) * 255.0 + 0.5).to(torch.uint8).cpu().numpy()
        enc.stdin.write(out.tobytes())
        done += len(frames_list)

    exc = None
    try:
        while True:
            buf = dec.stdout.read(frame_bytes)
            if not buf or len(buf) < frame_bytes:
                break
            batch.append(np.frombuffer(buf, dtype=np.uint8).reshape(h, w, 3))
            if len(batch) >= batch_size:
                flush_batch(batch)
                batch.clear()
                if done % 200 == 0:
                    el = time.time() - t0
                    rate = done / max(el, 1e-3)
                    eta = (nb - done) / max(rate, 1e-3) if nb > done else 0
                    print(f"  [{done}/{nb or '?'}] {rate:.1f} fps ({el:.0f}s elapsed, ~{eta:.0f}s left)", flush=True)
        if batch:
            flush_batch(batch)
            batch.clear()
    except Exception as e:
        exc = e
        raise
    finally:
        if dec.stdout:
            dec.stdout.close()
        if enc.stdin:
            try:
                enc.stdin.close()
            except BrokenPipeError:
                pass
        dec_ret = dec.wait()
        enc_ret = enc.wait()
        if exc is not None or dec_ret != 0 or enc_ret != 0 or done == 0:
            if os.path.exists(tmp_mp4):
                try:
                    os.remove(tmp_mp4)
                except OSError:
                    pass
        if exc is None:
            if dec_ret != 0:
                raise RuntimeError(f"ffmpeg decoder failed with exit code {dec_ret}")
            if enc_ret != 0:
                raise RuntimeError(f"ffmpeg encoder failed with exit code {enc_ret}")
            if done == 0:
                raise RuntimeError(f"decoded 0 frames from {src_avi}")

    os.replace(tmp_mp4, dst_mp4)
    el = time.time() - t0
    sz_mb = os.path.getsize(dst_mp4) / (1024 * 1024)
    print(f"[video] Done: {dst_mp4} ({done} frames, {sz_mb:.1f} MB) in {el:.1f}s ({done/max(el,1e-3):.1f} fps)", flush=True)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--model", default="tools/models/sr/4x-ClearRealityV1.safetensors")
    ap.add_argument("--out-dir", default="hd_assets/videos")
    ap.add_argument("--batch", type=int, default=4)
    ap.add_argument("--only", nargs="*", default=["mha2il", "mha2ol", "mha2sl"])
    args = ap.parse_args()

    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    dtype = torch.float16 if device == "cuda" else torch.float32
    dtype_label = "FP16" if dtype == torch.float16 else "FP32"
    print(f"[video] Loading {args.model} in {dtype_label} on {device}...", flush=True)
    desc = spandrel.ModelLoader().load_from_file(args.model)
    desc.to(device=device, dtype=dtype).eval()

    for name in args.only:
        src = os.path.join("game_files", f"{name}.avi")
        dst = os.path.join(args.out_dir, f"{name}.mp4")
        if not os.path.exists(src):
            print(f"[video] Missing {src}, skipping", flush=True)
            continue
        remaster_video(desc, src, dst, batch_size=args.batch, device=device, dtype=dtype)

    # Symlink low-bitrate/50fps variants (ih/oh/sh) -> 4K master MP4s so every cutscene path uses 4K!
    for alias, target in (("mha2ih.mp4", "mha2il.mp4"), ("mha2oh.mp4", "mha2ol.mp4"), ("mha2sh.mp4", "mha2sl.mp4")):
        apth = os.path.join(args.out_dir, alias)
        tpth = os.path.join(args.out_dir, target)
        if os.path.exists(tpth):
            if os.path.lexists(apth):
                os.remove(apth)
            os.symlink(target, apth)
    print("[video] All 4K cutscenes ready!", flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/upscale_ai.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Moorhuhn Adventure 2 — AI Neural 4x Super-Resolution Pack Builder (Real-ESRGAN Vulkan).

Extracts original 1x assets from game_files/mha2.dat and batch-upscales them on your
GPU via realesrgan-ncnn-vulkan into hd_assets/ (4K backgrounds + 4x RGBA sprites).

Usage:
  python3 tools/upscale_ai.py --backgrounds             # 4x neural 4K backgrounds (realesrgan-x4plus)
  python3 tools/upscale_ai.py --sprites                 # 4x neural RGBA sprites (realesrgan-x4plus-anime)
  python3 tools/upscale_ai.py --all                     # Both 4K backgrounds + 4x RGBA sprites
  python3 tools/upscale_ai.py --all --sprites-scope all # Include all 8,660 animation frames too
"""
import argparse
import os
import re
import shutil
import struct
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from extract_assets import Mha2Archive, decode_it3_frame, write_png_rgba


def find_upscaler_cmd():
    for bin_name, nix_pkg in (
        ("upscayl-bin", "nixpkgs#upscayl-ncnn"),
        ("realesrgan-ncnn-vulkan", "nixpkgs#realesrgan-ncnn-vulkan"),
    ):
        exe = shutil.which(bin_name)
        if exe:
            return [exe]
    if shutil.which("nix"):
        return ["nix", "shell", "nixpkgs#upscayl-ncnn", "-c", "upscayl-bin"]
    raise RuntimeError("upscayl-bin / realesrgan-ncnn-vulkan not found (and 'nix' is not in PATH).")


def ensure_custom_model(model_dir, model_name):
    """Ensure custom NCNN .param/.bin model exists in model_dir (auto-downloads from Upscayl if missing)."""
    builtin = {"realesrgan-x4plus", "realesrgan-x4plus-anime", "realesr-animevideov3"}
    if model_name in builtin:
        return None
    os.makedirs(model_dir, exist_ok=True)
    param_p = os.path.join(model_dir, f"{model_name}.param")
    bin_p = os.path.join(model_dir, f"{model_name}.bin")
    if os.path.exists(param_p) and os.path.exists(bin_p):
        return model_dir

    # Official Upscayl open-source NCNN model mirrors
    bases = [
        "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/upscayl/custom-models/main/models",
        "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/upscayl/upscayl/main/resources/models",
    ]
    for base in bases:
        p1 = subprocess.run(["curl", "-fsSL", f"{base}/{model_name}.param", "-o", param_p])
        p2 = subprocess.run(["curl", "-fsSL", f"{base}/{model_name}.bin", "-o", bin_p])
        if p1.returncode == 0 and p2.returncode == 0 and os.path.getsize(bin_p) > 1024:
            print(f"[MODEL] Downloaded NCNN model '{model_name}' into '{model_dir}'.", flush=True)
            return model_dir
    raise RuntimeError(f"Could not find or download model '{model_name}' in '{model_dir}'.")


# Backwards-compatible alias for tools/upscale_videos.py
find_realesrgan_cmd = find_upscaler_cmd


def detect_best_gpu(cmd_prefix):
    """Pick discrete NVIDIA/AMD Vulkan GPU over integrated Intel/llvmpipe."""
    try:
        p = subprocess.run(
            cmd_prefix + ["-i", "/nonexistent_probe_file.png", "-o", "/tmp/probe_out.png", "-v"],
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True, timeout=20
        )
        out = p.stdout or ""
    except Exception:
        return "auto"

    gpus = re.findall(r"\[(\d+)\s+([^\]]+)\]", out)
    seen = {}
    for gid, name in gpus:
        if gid not in seen:
            seen[gid] = name.strip()
    if not seen:
        return "auto"

    # Prefer NVIDIA, then AMD/Radeon, then first non-llvmpipe
    for pat in ("NVIDIA", "GeForce", "RTX", "Radeon", "AMD"):
        for gid, name in seen.items():
            if pat.lower() in name.lower():
                print(f"[GPU] Selected Vulkan device {gid}: {name}")
                return gid
    for gid, name in seen.items():
        if "llvmpipe" not in name.lower():
            print(f"[GPU] Selected Vulkan device {gid}: {name}")
            return gid
    return "auto"


def png_dimensions(path):
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        hdr = f.read(24)
        if len(hdr) >= 24 and hdr[:8] == b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n":
            return struct.unpack(">II", hdr[16:24])
    return (0, 0)


def run_realesrgan_batch(cmd_prefix, in_dir, out_dir, model, scale, gpu_id, threads, chunk_label,
                         timeout_s=300, model_dir=None):
    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
    cmd = cmd_prefix + [
        "-i", in_dir,
        "-o", out_dir,
        "-n", model,
        "-s", str(scale),
        "-t", "256",
        "-f", "png",
        "-j", threads,
    ]
    if model_dir:
        cmd += ["-m", model_dir]
    if gpu_id != "auto":
        cmd += ["-g", str(gpu_id)]
    t0 = time.time()
    print(f"[AI] Upscaling {chunk_label} ({model} {scale}x, gpu={gpu_id}, threads={threads})...", flush=True)
    try:
        p = subprocess.run(cmd, timeout=timeout_s, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True)
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        print(f"[WARN] Batch timed out after {timeout_s}s ({chunk_label}); keeping completed outputs & retrying/falling back.", flush=True)
        return False
    if p.returncode != 0:
        print(f"[WARN] upscaler returned {p.returncode} on {chunk_label}; keeping completed outputs.", flush=True)
        return False
    dt = time.time() - t0
    print(f"[AI] Finished {chunk_label} in {dt:.1f}s.", flush=True)
    return True


def upscale_backgrounds(arc, out_dir, cmd_prefix, gpu_id, model, scale, threads,
                        min_w=640, min_h=440, model_dir=None):
    stage_root = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="mha2_bg_ai_")
    in_dir = os.path.join(stage_root, "in")
    out_stage = os.path.join(stage_root, "out")
    os.makedirs(in_dir, exist_ok=True)
    mapping = []

    try:
        print("[BG] Extracting 1x original backgrounds from mha2.dat...")
        idx = 0
        for e in arc.entries:
            path = e["path"]
            if not path.endswith("._img"):
                continue
            raw = arc.read_entry(e)
            if len(raw) < 0x50:
                continue
            img_type = struct.unpack_from("<I", raw, 0)[0]
            if img_type != 0:
                continue
            joff, jsz = struct.unpack_from("<II", raw, 0x40)
            if joff + jsz > len(raw) or jsz <= 4:
                continue
            jpg = raw[joff : joff + jsz]
            rel_png = path[:-5] + ".png"
            stage_png = os.path.join(in_dir, f"bg_{idx:05d}.png")
            p = subprocess.run(
                ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "mjpeg", "-i", "pipe:0", stage_png],
                input=jpg, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.PIPE
            )
            if p.returncode != 0 or not os.path.exists(stage_png):
                continue
            w, h = png_dimensions(stage_png)
            if w < min_w or h < min_h:
                os.remove(stage_png)
                continue
            mapping.append((f"bg_{idx:05d}.png", rel_png, w, h))
            idx += 1

        print(f"[BG] Staged {len(mapping)} full backgrounds (>= {min_w}x{min_h}) for {scale}x neural upscaling.")
        if not mapping:
            return 0

        run_realesrgan_batch(
            cmd_prefix, in_dir, out_stage, model, scale, gpu_id, threads,
            f"{len(mapping)} backgrounds", timeout_s=900, model_dir=model_dir
        )

        # Remove stale background cache so the engine rebuilds it cleanly
        cache_file = os.path.join(out_dir, ".hd_index.cache")
        if os.path.exists(cache_file):
            os.remove(cache_file)

        installed = 0
        for fname, rel_png, ow, oh in mapping:
            src_p = os.path.join(out_stage, fname)
            if not os.path.exists(src_p):
                continue
            dst_p = os.path.join(out_dir, rel_png)
            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dst_p), exist_ok=True)
            shutil.move(src_p, dst_p)
            installed += 1
        print(f"[BG] Installed {installed} {scale}x neural backgrounds into '{out_dir}'.")
        return installed
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(stage_root, ignore_errors=True)


def upscale_jpeg_props(arc, out_dir, cmd_prefix, gpu_id, bg_model, spr_model, scale, threads,
                       bg_model_dir=None, spr_model_dir=None):
    """Upscale ALL remaining img_type==0 JPEG assets (split outdoor rooms objBgO/objBgU/objWolken,
    doors, mirrors, Death/Golem strips, minigame art, savegame thumbnails) into hd_assets/sprites/
    so 100.0% of all 348 JPEG images in mha2.dat are high-definition!"""
    spr_out_root = os.path.join(out_dir, "sprites")
    os.makedirs(spr_out_root, exist_ok=True)
    stage_root = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="mha2_jprop_ai_")
    bg_in = os.path.join(stage_root, "bg_in")
    bg_out = os.path.join(stage_root, "bg_out")
    pr_in = os.path.join(stage_root, "pr_in")
    pr_out = os.path.join(stage_root, "pr_out")
    os.makedirs(bg_in, exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs(pr_in, exist_ok=True)
    bg_map = []
    pr_map = []
    tall_2x = []

    try:
        print("[JPROP] Extracting all remaining 1x JPEG props, split rooms & thumbnails from mha2.dat...", flush=True)
        idx = 0
        for e in arc.entries:
            path = e["path"]
            if not path.endswith("._img"):
                continue
            raw = arc.read_entry(e)
            if len(raw) < 0x50 or struct.unpack_from("<I", raw, 0)[0] != 0:
                continue
            joff, jsz = struct.unpack_from("<II", raw, 0x40)
            if joff + jsz > len(raw) or jsz <= 4:
                continue
            jpg = raw[joff : joff + jsz]
            rel_png = path[:-5] + ".png"
            tmp_png = os.path.join(stage_root, f"tmp_{idx:05d}.png")
            p = subprocess.run(
                ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "mjpeg", "-i", "pipe:0", tmp_png],
                input=jpg, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.PIPE
            )
            if p.returncode != 0 or not os.path.exists(tmp_png):
                continue
            w, h = png_dimensions(tmp_png)
            # Skip full backgrounds already handled by upscale_backgrounds (>= 640x440)
            if w >= 640 and h >= 440:
                os.remove(tmp_png)
                continue
            if w < 16 or h < 16:
                os.remove(tmp_png)
                continue

            fname = f"jp_{idx:05d}.png"
            idx += 1
            # Cap ultra-tall animation strips so 4x height stays <= 16384 (OpenGL texture limit)
            if h * scale > 16384 or w * scale > 16384:
                tall_2x.append((tmp_png, rel_png, w, h))
                continue

            low = path.lower()
            if "objbg" in low or "objwolken" in low or "objbruecke" in low:
                shutil.move(tmp_png, os.path.join(bg_in, fname))
                bg_map.append((fname, rel_png))
            else:
                shutil.move(tmp_png, os.path.join(pr_in, fname))
                pr_map.append((fname, rel_png))

        installed = 0
        if bg_map:
            run_realesrgan_batch(
                cmd_prefix, bg_in, bg_out, bg_model, scale, gpu_id, threads,
                f"{len(bg_map)} split-room JPEG backgrounds", timeout_s=300, model_dir=bg_model_dir
            )
            for fname, rel_png in bg_map:
                src_p = os.path.join(bg_out, fname)
                if os.path.exists(src_p) and os.path.getsize(src_p) > 0:
                    dst_p = os.path.join(spr_out_root, rel_png)
                    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dst_p), exist_ok=True)
                    shutil.move(src_p, dst_p)
                    installed += 1

        if pr_map:
            run_realesrgan_batch(
                cmd_prefix, pr_in, pr_out, spr_model, scale, gpu_id, threads,
                f"{len(pr_map)} JPEG props/doors/thumbnails", timeout_s=300, model_dir=spr_model_dir
            )
            for fname, rel_png in pr_map:
                src_p = os.path.join(pr_out, fname)
                if os.path.exists(src_p) and os.path.getsize(src_p) > 0:
                    dst_p = os.path.join(spr_out_root, rel_png)
                    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dst_p), exist_ok=True)
                    shutil.move(src_p, dst_p)
                    installed += 1

        if tall_2x:
            t_in = os.path.join(stage_root, "tall_in")
            t_out = os.path.join(stage_root, "tall_out")
            os.makedirs(t_in, exist_ok=True)
            t_map = []
            for ti, (tmp_p, rel_png, ow, oh) in enumerate(tall_2x):
                fn = f"t_{ti:04d}.png"
                shutil.move(tmp_p, os.path.join(t_in, fn))
                t_map.append((fn, rel_png, ow, oh))
            run_realesrgan_batch(
                cmd_prefix, t_in, t_out, spr_model, 4, gpu_id, threads,
                f"{len(t_map)} tall JPEG strips (downscaled to 2x)", timeout_s=300, model_dir=spr_model_dir
            )
            for fn, rel_png, ow, oh in t_map:
                src_p = os.path.join(t_out, fn)
                dst_p = os.path.join(spr_out_root, rel_png)
                os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dst_p), exist_ok=True)
                if os.path.exists(src_p) and os.path.getsize(src_p) > 0 and ow * 2 <= 16384 and oh * 2 <= 16384:
                    subprocess.run(
                        ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-i", src_p,
                         "-vf", f"scale={ow*2}:{oh*2}:flags=lanczos", dst_p],
                        stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL
                    )
                    if os.path.exists(dst_p):
                        installed += 1

        cache_file = os.path.join(out_dir, ".hd_sprite_index.cache")
        if os.path.exists(cache_file):
            os.remove(cache_file)
        print(f"[JPROP] Installed {installed} HD JPEG props/split-rooms/thumbnails into '{spr_out_root}'.", flush=True)
        return installed
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(stage_root, ignore_errors=True)


def should_include_sprite(vpath, frame_cnt, scope):
    if scope == "all":
        return True
    # 'core' scope: all single-frame sprites (buttons, inventory, static props),
    # all cursors (g/img/mc*), all inventory icons (inv*), and key character/menu sprites (<= 16 frames)
    low = vpath.lower()
    if "/mc" in low or "/inv" in low or low.startswith("x/") or low.startswith("g/"):
        return True
    if frame_cnt <= 16:
        return True
    return False


def upscale_sprites(arc, out_dir, cmd_prefix, gpu_id, model, scale, threads,
                    scope="all", batch_size=300, model_dir=None, force=False):
    spr_out_root = os.path.join(out_dir, "sprites")
    os.makedirs(spr_out_root, exist_ok=True)
    stage_root = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="mha2_spr_ai_")
    installed = 0

    try:
        print(f"[SPR] Extracting 1x RGBA IT3 sprites (scope={scope}, force={force}) from mha2.dat...", flush=True)
        batch_idx = 0
        cur_batch = []

        def flush_batch(items, bnum):
            nonlocal installed
            if not items:
                return
            in_dir = os.path.join(stage_root, f"in_{bnum:03d}")
            out_stage = os.path.join(stage_root, f"out_{bnum:03d}")
            run_realesrgan_batch(
                cmd_prefix, in_dir, out_stage, model, scale, gpu_id, threads,
                f"sprite batch #{bnum + 1} ({len(items)} sprites)", timeout_s=180, model_dir=model_dir
            )
            missing = []
            for fname, rel_png in items:
                src_p = os.path.join(out_stage, fname)
                dst_p = os.path.join(spr_out_root, rel_png)
                if os.path.exists(src_p) and os.path.getsize(src_p) > 0:
                    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dst_p), exist_ok=True)
                    shutil.move(src_p, dst_p)
                    installed += 1
                else:
                    missing.append((fname, rel_png))
            # Retry any missing files once in a clean sub-batch, then Lanczos fallback if still missing
            if missing:
                retry_in = os.path.join(stage_root, f"rin_{bnum:03d}")
                retry_out = os.path.join(stage_root, f"rout_{bnum:03d}")
                os.makedirs(retry_in, exist_ok=True)
                for fname, _ in missing:
                    shutil.copy2(os.path.join(in_dir, fname), os.path.join(retry_in, fname))
                run_realesrgan_batch(
                    cmd_prefix, retry_in, retry_out, model, scale, gpu_id, "1:1:1",
                    f"sprite retry #{bnum + 1} ({len(missing)} sprites)", timeout_s=120, model_dir=model_dir
                )
                for fname, rel_png in missing:
                    src_p = os.path.join(retry_out, fname)
                    dst_p = os.path.join(spr_out_root, rel_png)
                    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dst_p), exist_ok=True)
                    if os.path.exists(src_p) and os.path.getsize(src_p) > 0:
                        shutil.move(src_p, dst_p)
                        installed += 1
                    else:
                        # Clean Lanczos 4x fallback so zero sprites are skipped
                        subprocess.run(
                            ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-i", os.path.join(in_dir, fname),
                             "-vf", f"scale=iw*{scale}:ih*{scale}:flags=lanczos", dst_p],
                            stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL
                        )
                        if os.path.exists(dst_p):
                            installed += 1
                shutil.rmtree(retry_in, ignore_errors=True)
                shutil.rmtree(retry_out, ignore_errors=True)
            shutil.rmtree(in_dir, ignore_errors=True)
            shutil.rmtree(out_stage, ignore_errors=True)

        cur_in_dir = os.path.join(stage_root, f"in_{batch_idx:03d}")
        os.makedirs(cur_in_dir, exist_ok=True)
        local_i = 0

        for e in arc.entries:
            path = e["path"]
            if not path.endswith("._img"):
                continue
            raw = arc.read_entry(e)
            if len(raw) < 0x50:
                continue
            img_type = struct.unpack_from("<I", raw, 0)[0]
            if img_type != 1:
                continue
            pal_off, pal_cnt = struct.unpack_from("<II", raw, 0x28)
            palette = list(struct.unpack_from(f"<{pal_cnt}I", raw, pal_off)) if (pal_off and pal_cnt and pal_off + pal_cnt * 4 <= len(raw)) else []
            t0_off, t0_cnt, tbl_off, tbl_cnt = struct.unpack_from("<4I", raw, 0x38)
            if not tbl_off or not tbl_cnt:
                continue

            # Composite bitmap font atlases (fntWhite/fntGray/fntYellow/fntOtherSpeakers/fntBig/objFont)
            # into a single full-surface RGBA sheet matching mha2.exe's DirectDraw surface layout!
            if "/fnt" in path.lower() or path.endswith("/objFont._img"):
                _, _, sw, sh = struct.unpack_from("<4I", raw, 0)
                if sw > 0 and sh > 0:
                    rel_png = path[:-5] + ".png"
                    dst_existing = os.path.join(spr_out_root, rel_png)
                    if not force and os.path.exists(dst_existing):
                        ew, eh = png_dimensions(dst_existing)
                        if ew == sw * scale and eh == sh * scale:
                            installed += 1
                            continue
                    canvas = bytearray(sw * sh * 4)
                    for fi in range(min(t0_cnt, tbl_cnt)):
                        foff = struct.unpack_from("<I", raw, tbl_off + fi * 4)[0]
                        if not foff:
                            continue
                        cx, cy, cw, ch = struct.unpack_from("<4I", raw, t0_off + fi * 17)
                        dec = decode_it3_frame(raw, foff, palette, cw, ch)
                        if not dec:
                            continue
                        fw, fh, rgba = dec
                        for y in range(fh):
                            dy = cy + y
                            if dy < 0 or dy >= sh:
                                continue
                            for x in range(fw):
                                dx = cx + x
                                if dx < 0 or dx >= sw:
                                    continue
                                si = (y * fw + x) * 4
                                if rgba[si + 3] > 0:
                                    di = (dy * sw + dx) * 4
                                    canvas[di : di + 4] = rgba[si : si + 4]
                    fname = f"s_{local_i:05d}.png"
                    write_png_rgba(os.path.join(cur_in_dir, fname), sw, sh, bytes(canvas))
                    cur_batch.append((fname, rel_png))
                    local_i += 1
                    if len(cur_batch) >= batch_size:
                        flush_batch(cur_batch, batch_idx)
                        batch_idx += 1
                        cur_batch = []
                        local_i = 0
                continue
            if not should_include_sprite(path, tbl_cnt, scope):
                continue

            rel_base = path[:-5]
            for fi in range(tbl_cnt):
                foff = struct.unpack_from("<I", raw, tbl_off + fi * 4)[0]
                if not foff:
                    continue
                cw, ch = 0, 0
                if t0_off and fi < t0_cnt and t0_off + (fi + 1) * 17 <= len(raw):
                    _, _, cw, ch = struct.unpack_from("<4I", raw, t0_off + fi * 17)
                dec = decode_it3_frame(raw, foff, palette, cw, ch)
                if not dec:
                    continue
                w, h, rgba = dec
                if w < 16 or h < 16:
                    continue
                suffix = "" if tbl_cnt == 1 else f"_f{fi:03d}"
                rel_png = rel_base + suffix + ".png"
                dst_existing = os.path.join(spr_out_root, rel_png)
                if not force and os.path.exists(dst_existing):
                    ew, eh = png_dimensions(dst_existing)
                    if ew == w * scale and eh == h * scale:
                        installed += 1
                        continue
                fname = f"s_{local_i:05d}.png"
                write_png_rgba(os.path.join(cur_in_dir, fname), w, h, rgba)
                cur_batch.append((fname, rel_png))
                local_i += 1
                if len(cur_batch) >= batch_size:
                    flush_batch(cur_batch, batch_idx)
                    batch_idx += 1
                    cur_batch = []
                    local_i = 0
                    cur_in_dir = os.path.join(stage_root, f"in_{batch_idx:03d}")
                    os.makedirs(cur_in_dir, exist_ok=True)

        flush_batch(cur_batch, batch_idx)

        cache_file = os.path.join(out_dir, ".hd_sprite_index.cache")
        if os.path.exists(cache_file):
            os.remove(cache_file)
        print(f"[SPR] Installed {installed} {scale}x neural RGBA sprites into '{spr_out_root}'.")
        return installed
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(stage_root, ignore_errors=True)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Build 4x Real-ESRGAN AI Neural HD Pack for Moorhuhn Adventure 2")
    ap.add_argument("--dat", default="game_files/mha2.dat", help="Path to mha2.dat")
    ap.add_argument("-o", "--out", default="hd_assets", help="Target HD pack directory (default: hd_assets)")
    ap.add_argument("--backgrounds", action="store_true", help="AI-upscale all full backgrounds (menus & rooms)")
    ap.add_argument("--jpeg-props", action="store_true", help="AI-upscale remaining JPEG props, split rooms & thumbnails")
    ap.add_argument("--sprites", action="store_true", help="AI-upscale RGBA IT3 sprites (characters, props, inventory, cursors)")
    ap.add_argument("--all", action="store_true", help="AI-upscale everything (backgrounds + JPEG props + IT3 sprites)")
    ap.add_argument("--sprites-scope", choices=["core", "all"], default="all",
                    help="Sprite scope: 'core' (~2k key sprites) or 'all' (all 8,660 frames, default)")
    ap.add_argument("--bg-model", default="4x_NMKD-Siax_200k",
                    help="Model for backgrounds (default: 4x_NMKD-Siax_200k)")
    ap.add_argument("--spr-model", default="ultrasharp-4x",
                    help="Model for sprites (default: ultrasharp-4x)")
    ap.add_argument("--model-dir", default="tools/models",
                    help="Directory for custom NCNN .param/.bin models (default: tools/models)")
    ap.add_argument("--force", action="store_true",
                    help="Overwrite existing upscaled sprites instead of skipping")
    ap.add_argument("--scale", type=int, default=4, choices=[2, 4], help="Upscale factor (default: 4)")
    ap.add_argument("--gpu", default="auto", help="Vulkan GPU ID (default: auto-detect NVIDIA/AMD)")
    ap.add_argument("--threads", default="1:1:1", help="load:proc:save threads (default: 1:1:1)")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    if not (args.backgrounds or args.jpeg_props or args.sprites or args.all):
        args.all = True

    cmd_prefix = find_upscaler_cmd()
    gpu_id = detect_best_gpu(cmd_prefix) if args.gpu == "auto" else args.gpu

    arc = Mha2Archive(args.dat)
    try:
        bg_mdir = ensure_custom_model(args.model_dir, args.bg_model) if (args.backgrounds or args.jpeg_props or args.all) else None
        spr_mdir = ensure_custom_model(args.model_dir, args.spr_model) if (args.jpeg_props or args.sprites or args.all) else None
        if args.backgrounds or args.all:
            upscale_backgrounds(arc, args.out, cmd_prefix, gpu_id, args.bg_model, args.scale,
                                args.threads, model_dir=bg_mdir)
        if args.jpeg_props or args.all:
            upscale_jpeg_props(arc, args.out, cmd_prefix, gpu_id, args.bg_model, args.spr_model,
                               args.scale, args.threads, bg_model_dir=bg_mdir, spr_model_dir=spr_mdir)
        if args.sprites or args.all:
            upscale_sprites(arc, args.out, cmd_prefix, gpu_id, args.spr_model, args.scale,
                            args.threads, scope=args.sprites_scope, model_dir=spr_mdir, force=args.force)
    finally:
        arc.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/upscale_alpha_sprites.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Re-upscale every IT3 frame that carries a semi-transparent tbl2 layer (Moorhuhn s/*,
cursors g/img/mc*, characters, glows) as a proper composited tbl1+tbl2 RGBA sprite.

Why: mha2.exe draws each such frame as an opaque tbl1 core PLUS a separate anti-aliased
tbl2 edge ring (eyes, feathers, outlines). The old pack only upscaled tbl1, so the runtime
stamped the blurry 1x tbl2 ring on top of the 4K core.

Method (keeps detail, no halos):
  1) Composite tbl1 + tbl2 into one RGBA frame at the exact t0 cell size.
  2) Dilate RGB into transparent pixels (no black/magenta fringe for the network).
  3) Upscale RGB and the alpha mask separately with the chosen sprite model (ultrasharp-4x).
  4) Recombine -> RGBA, write to hd_assets/sprites/<same rel name>.png.

Run inside:  nix-shell -p python3Packages.pillow python3Packages.numpy --run \
               "python3 tools/upscale_alpha_sprites.py"
"""
import argparse
import os
import shutil
import struct
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time

import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageFilter

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from extract_assets import Mha2Archive, decode_it3_frame  # noqa: E402
from upscale_ai import find_upscaler_cmd, ensure_custom_model  # noqa: E402


def composite_frame(raw, palette, fi, t0_off, t0_cnt, t1_off, t1_cnt, t2_off, t2_cnt):
    cw = ch = 0
    if t0_off and fi < t0_cnt and t0_off + (fi + 1) * 17 <= len(raw):
        _, _, cw, ch = struct.unpack_from("<4I", raw, t0_off + fi * 17)
    layers = []
    for off, cnt in ((t1_off, t1_cnt), (t2_off, t2_cnt)):
        if off and fi < cnt:
            foff = struct.unpack_from("<I", raw, off + fi * 4)[0]
            if foff:
                dec = decode_it3_frame(raw, foff, palette, cw, ch)
                if dec:
                    layers.append(dec)
    if not layers:
        return None
    w = max(d[0] for d in layers)
    h = max(d[1] for d in layers)
    out = np.zeros((h, w, 4), dtype=np.uint8)
    for lw, lh, buf in layers:
        a = np.frombuffer(buf, dtype=np.uint8).reshape(lh, lw, 4)
        m = a[:, :, 3] > 0
        out[:lh, :lw][m] = a[m]
    return out


def dilate_rgb(rgba, iters=16):
    rgb = rgba[:, :, :3].astype(np.float32)
    mask = (rgba[:, :, 3] > 0).astype(np.float32)
    for _ in range(iters):
        if mask.all():
            break
        p_rgb = np.pad(rgb * mask[:, :, None], ((1, 1), (1, 1), (0, 0)), mode="edge")
        p_m = np.pad(mask, ((1, 1), (1, 1)), mode="edge")
        s_rgb = (p_rgb[:-2, :-2] + p_rgb[:-2, 1:-1] + p_rgb[:-2, 2:] +
                 p_rgb[1:-1, :-2] + p_rgb[1:-1, 2:] +
                 p_rgb[2:, :-2] + p_rgb[2:, 1:-1] + p_rgb[2:, 2:])
        s_m = (p_m[:-2, :-2] + p_m[:-2, 1:-1] + p_m[:-2, 2:] +
               p_m[1:-1, :-2] + p_m[1:-1, 2:] +
               p_m[2:, :-2] + p_m[2:, 1:-1] + p_m[2:, 2:])
        fill = (mask == 0) & (s_m > 0)
        if not fill.any():
            break
        rgb[fill] = s_rgb[fill] / s_m[fill, None]
        mask[fill] = 1.0
    return np.clip(rgb, 0, 255).astype(np.uint8)


def run_upscaler(cmd_prefix, in_dir, out_dir, model, model_dir, gpu, scale):
    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
    cmd = cmd_prefix + ["-i", in_dir, "-o", out_dir, "-n", model, "-s", str(scale),
                        "-t", "256", "-f", "png", "-j", "1:2:2"]
    if model_dir:
        cmd += ["-m", model_dir]
    if gpu != "auto":
        cmd += ["-g", str(gpu)]
    try:
        subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, timeout=900)
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        print("[WARN] upscaler batch timed out; falling back to Lanczos for missing files", flush=True)


def lanczos_up(img, scale):
    return img.resize((img.width * scale, img.height * scale), Image.LANCZOS)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--dat", default="game_files/mha2.dat")
    ap.add_argument("-o", "--out", default="hd_assets")
    ap.add_argument("--model", default="ultrasharp-4x")
    ap.add_argument("--model-dir", default="tools/models")
    ap.add_argument("--gpu", default="1")
    ap.add_argument("--scale", type=int, default=4)
    ap.add_argument("--batch", type=int, default=400)
    ap.add_argument("--filter", default="", help="Only paths containing this substring")
    ap.add_argument("--non-tbl2", action="store_true",
                    help="Process non-tbl2 IT3 sprites with 1-bit transparency (props, items, icons) for smooth 4K AA edges")
    ap.add_argument("--backup", default="work/sprites_tbl1_backup")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    cmd_prefix = find_upscaler_cmd()
    mdir = ensure_custom_model(args.model_dir, args.model)
    spr_root = os.path.join(args.out, "sprites")
    arc = Mha2Archive(args.dat)

    jobs = []  # (rel_png, rgba ndarray)
    for e in arc.entries:
        path = e["path"]
        if not path.endswith("._img") or (args.filter and args.filter not in path):
            continue
        low = path.lower()
        if "/fnt" in low or low.endswith("/objfont._img"):
            continue
        raw = arc.read_entry(e)
        if len(raw) < 0x50 or struct.unpack_from("<I", raw, 0)[0] != 1:
            continue
        pal_off, pal_cnt = struct.unpack_from("<II", raw, 0x28)
        palette = list(struct.unpack_from(f"<{pal_cnt}I", raw, pal_off)) \
            if (pal_off and pal_cnt and pal_off + pal_cnt * 4 <= len(raw)) else []
        t0_off, t0_cnt, t1_off, t1_cnt, t2_off, t2_cnt = struct.unpack_from("<6I", raw, 0x38)
        has_t2 = bool(t2_off and t2_cnt)
        if args.non_tbl2:
            if has_t2 or not (t1_off and t1_cnt):
                continue
        else:
            if not has_t2:
                continue
        n = max(t1_cnt, t2_cnt)
        for fi in range(n):
            rgba = composite_frame(raw, palette, fi, t0_off, t0_cnt, t1_off, t1_cnt, t2_off, t2_cnt)
            if rgba is None or rgba.shape[0] < 16 or rgba.shape[1] < 16:
                continue
            if args.non_tbl2 and (rgba[:, :, 3] == 255).all():
                continue  # 100% opaque rectangle — no silhouette edge to anti-alias
            suffix = "" if n == 1 else f"_f{fi:03d}"
            jobs.append((path[:-5] + suffix + ".png", rgba))
    arc.close()
    mode_lbl = "non-tbl2 1-bit-alpha props/items" if args.non_tbl2 else "composited tbl1+tbl2 frames"
    print(f"[ALPHA] {len(jobs)} {mode_lbl} to upscale with {args.model}", flush=True)

    pad = 4 if args.non_tbl2 else 0
    pad4 = pad * args.scale
    stage = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="mha2_alpha_")
    done = 0
    t_start = time.time()
    try:
        for b0 in range(0, len(jobs), args.batch):
            chunk = jobs[b0:b0 + args.batch]
            in_dir = os.path.join(stage, "in")
            out_dir = os.path.join(stage, "out")
            shutil.rmtree(in_dir, ignore_errors=True)
            shutil.rmtree(out_dir, ignore_errors=True)
            os.makedirs(in_dir)
            for i, (_, rgba) in enumerate(chunk):
                rgb = dilate_rgb(rgba)
                a = rgba[:, :, 3]
                if pad > 0:
                    rgb = np.pad(rgb, ((pad, pad), (pad, pad), (0, 0)), mode="edge")
                    a = np.pad(a, ((pad, pad), (pad, pad)), mode="constant", constant_values=0)
                Image.fromarray(rgb, "RGB").save(os.path.join(in_dir, f"{i:05d}_c.png"))
                Image.fromarray(np.stack([a, a, a], axis=-1), "RGB").save(os.path.join(in_dir, f"{i:05d}_a.png"))
            run_upscaler(cmd_prefix, in_dir, out_dir, args.model, mdir, args.gpu, args.scale)

            for i, (rel_png, rgba) in enumerate(chunk):
                h, w = rgba.shape[:2]
                tw, th = w * args.scale, h * args.scale
                ptw, pth = (w + 2 * pad) * args.scale, (h + 2 * pad) * args.scale
                cp = os.path.join(out_dir, f"{i:05d}_c.png")
                apth = os.path.join(out_dir, f"{i:05d}_a.png")
                c_img = Image.open(cp).convert("RGB") if os.path.exists(cp) else \
                    lanczos_up(Image.open(os.path.join(in_dir, f"{i:05d}_c.png")).convert("RGB"), args.scale)
                a_img = Image.open(apth).convert("L") if os.path.exists(apth) else \
                    lanczos_up(Image.open(os.path.join(in_dir, f"{i:05d}_a.png")).convert("L"), args.scale)
                if c_img.size != (ptw, pth):
                    c_img = c_img.resize((ptw, pth), Image.LANCZOS)
                if a_img.size != (ptw, pth):
                    a_img = a_img.resize((ptw, pth), Image.LANCZOS)
                if pad4 > 0:
                    c_img = c_img.crop((pad4, pad4, pad4 + tw, pad4 + th))
                    a_img = a_img.crop((pad4, pad4, pad4 + tw, pad4 + th))
                if args.non_tbl2:
                    a_img = a_img.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(radius=1.0))
                a4 = np.array(a_img)
                a4 = np.where(a4 <= 8, 0, np.where(a4 >= 248, 255, a4)).astype(np.uint8)
                out = np.dstack([np.array(c_img), a4])

                dst = os.path.join(spr_root, rel_png)
                os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dst), exist_ok=True)
                if args.backup and os.path.exists(dst):
                    bk = os.path.join(args.backup, rel_png)
                    if not os.path.exists(bk):
                        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(bk), exist_ok=True)
                        shutil.copy2(dst, bk)
                Image.fromarray(out, "RGBA").save(dst)
                done += 1
            el = time.time() - t_start
            print(f"[ALPHA] {done}/{len(jobs)} done ({el:.0f}s, ~{el / done * (len(jobs) - done):.0f}s left)",
                  flush=True)
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(stage, ignore_errors=True)

    cache = os.path.join(args.out, ".hd_sprite_index.cache")
    if os.path.exists(cache):
        os.remove(cache)
    print(f"[ALPHA] Installed {done} composited 4x RGBA sprites into '{spr_root}'.", flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/upscale_transformer.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Moorhuhn Adventure 2 — Modern Faithful Transformer Super-Resolution (DAT / HAT / SPAN)
100% art-faithful (ZERO diffusion hallucination, ZERO style drift). Uses modern 2024+
Transformer/SPAN architectures (4xNomos8kDAT, Real_HAT_GAN_SRx4, 4x-ClearRealityV1)
trained to strip 2005 JPEG compression & staircases while keeping every original detail razor-sharp.
"""
import argparse
import io
import os
import struct
import sys
import time
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont, ImageFilter
import torch
import spandrel
import spandrel_extra_arches

spandrel_extra_arches.install()

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from extract_assets import Mha2Archive, decode_it3_frame
from upscale_alpha_sprites import composite_frame, dilate_rgb


def load_sr_model(model_path: str, device: str = "cuda"):
    desc = spandrel.ModelLoader().load_from_file(model_path)
    desc.to(device).eval()
    return desc


@torch.inference_mode()
def upscale_tiled(desc, img: Image.Image, tile: int = 256, overlap: int = 32, device: str = "cuda") -> Image.Image:
    """Seamless tiled 4x inference in FP32 (avoids attention overflow on DAT/HAT)."""
    arr = np.asarray(img.convert("RGB"), dtype=np.float32) / 255.0
    h, w, _ = arr.shape
    scale = desc.scale

    out = np.zeros((h * scale, w * scale, 3), dtype=np.float32)
    weight = np.zeros((h * scale, w * scale, 1), dtype=np.float32)

    step = tile - overlap
    ys = list(range(0, max(1, h - tile + 1), step))
    if ys[-1] + tile < h:
        ys.append(max(0, h - tile))
    xs = list(range(0, max(1, w - tile + 1), step))
    if xs[-1] + tile < w:
        xs.append(max(0, w - tile))

    for y0 in ys:
        y1 = min(h, y0 + tile)
        for x0 in xs:
            x1 = min(w, x0 + tile)
            patch = arr[y0:y1, x0:x1]
            ten = torch.from_numpy(patch).permute(2, 0, 1).unsqueeze(0).to(device)
            pred = desc(ten).squeeze(0).permute(1, 2, 0).clamp(0.0, 1.0).cpu().numpy()

            oh, ow, _ = pred.shape
            wy = np.ones(oh, dtype=np.float32)
            wx = np.ones(ow, dtype=np.float32)
            ov = overlap * scale
            if y0 > 0 and ov < oh:
                wy[:ov] = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, ov, dtype=np.float32)
            if y1 < h and ov < oh:
                wy[-ov:] = np.linspace(1.0, 0.0, ov, dtype=np.float32)
            if x0 > 0 and ov < ow:
                wx[:ov] = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, ov, dtype=np.float32)
            if x1 < w and ov < ow:
                wx[-ov:] = np.linspace(1.0, 0.0, ov, dtype=np.float32)
            mask = np.outer(wy, wx)[..., None]

            out[y0 * scale : y1 * scale, x0 * scale : x1 * scale] += pred * mask
            weight[y0 * scale : y1 * scale, x0 * scale : x1 * scale] += mask

    res = np.clip(out / np.maximum(weight, 1e-6), 0.0, 1.0)
    return Image.fromarray((res * 255.0 + 0.5).astype(np.uint8))


def extract_raw_bg(arc: Mha2Archive, entry_name: str) -> Image.Image:
    target = entry_name.lower()
    for e in arc.entries:
        name = e["path"] if isinstance(e, dict) else e[0]
        if name.lower().replace("\\", "/") == target:
            raw = arc.read_entry(e)
            img_type = struct.unpack_from("<I", raw, 0)[0]
            if img_type != 0:
                raise RuntimeError(f"{entry_name} is not a JPEG bg (type={img_type})")
            joff, jsz = struct.unpack_from("<II", raw, 0x40)
            jpg = raw[joff : joff + jsz]
            return Image.open(io.BytesIO(jpg)).convert("RGB")
    raise RuntimeError(f"Entry not found in mha2.dat: {entry_name}")


def draw_label(im: Image.Image, text: str, xy=(14, 12)):
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
    try:
        font = ImageFont.truetype("/run/current-system/sw/share/X11/fonts/DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf", 28)
    except Exception:
        font = ImageFont.load_default()
    bbox = draw.textbbox(xy, text, font=font)
    pad = 8
    draw.rectangle((bbox[0] - pad, bbox[1] - pad, bbox[2] + pad, bbox[3] + pad), fill=(15, 18, 28))
    draw.text(xy, text, fill=(255, 224, 120), font=font)


def make_4way_zoom_sheet(images_dict: dict, crops: list, out_path: str):
    """2x2 grid per crop showing 4 models side-by-side at native 1:1 4K pixels."""
    names = list(images_dict.keys())
    bw, bh = next(iter(images_dict.values())).size
    cw, ch = 640, 440
    gap = 6
    block_h = ch * 2 + gap
    sheet = Image.new("RGB", (cw * 2 + gap, (block_h + 14) * len(crops)), (20, 24, 34))

    for ci, (cname, (cx, cy)) in enumerate(crops):
        cx = max(0, min(cx, bw - cw))
        cy = max(0, min(cy, bh - ch))
        by = ci * (block_h + 14)
        for mi, mname in enumerate(names[:4]):
            im = images_dict[mname].crop((cx, cy, cx + cw, cy + ch))
            draw_label(im, f"{mname} | {cname}", (10, 8))
            gx = (mi % 2) * (cw + gap)
            gy = by + (mi // 2) * (ch + gap)
            sheet.paste(im, (gx, gy))
    sheet.save(out_path)


@torch.inference_mode()
def upscale_fast(desc, arr_u8: np.ndarray, device: str = "cuda") -> np.ndarray:
    """Fast single-pass GPU 4x upscale for small/medium sprites, tiled for large ones."""
    h, w = arr_u8.shape[:2]
    if h <= 384 and w <= 384:
        ten = torch.from_numpy(arr_u8.astype(np.float32) / 255.0).permute(2, 0, 1).unsqueeze(0).to(device)
        pred = desc(ten).squeeze(0).permute(1, 2, 0).clamp(0.0, 1.0).cpu().numpy()
        return (pred * 255.0 + 0.5).astype(np.uint8)
    return np.asarray(upscale_tiled(desc, Image.fromarray(arr_u8, "RGB"), tile=256, overlap=32, device=device))


def find_hd_asset(stem: str) -> str:
    for ext in (".webp", ".png"):
        p = stem + ext
        if os.path.exists(p):
            return p
    return stem + ".webp"


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--compare", action="store_true", help="Generate 4-way 1:1 faithful SR comparisons")
    ap.add_argument("--apply-backgrounds", metavar="MODEL_PATH", help="Batch-upscale all single-frame JPEG backgrounds/screens")
    ap.add_argument("--apply-jpeg-strips", metavar="MODEL_PATH",
                    help="Split & 4x-upscale all 50 multi-frame JPEG vertical strips into per-frame lossless WebPs in hd_assets/sprites/")
    ap.add_argument("--apply-sprites", metavar="MODEL_PATH", help="Batch-upscale ALL ~8,297 IT3 sprites (tbl2 + non-tbl2 + opaque)")
    ap.add_argument("--out-dir", default="work/sr_samples")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    arc = Mha2Archive("game_files/mha2.dat")
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"

    if args.compare:
        os.makedirs(args.out_dir, exist_ok=True)
        models = [
            ("4xNomos8kDAT (DAT Transformer)", "tools/models/sr/4xNomos8kDAT.safetensors"),
            ("Real_HAT_GAN_SRx4 (HAT Transformer)", "tools/models/sr/Real_HAT_GAN_SRx4.pth"),
            ("4x-ClearRealityV1 (SPAN)", "tools/models/sr/4x-ClearRealityV1.safetensors"),
        ]
        rooms = [
            ("room_001_xobjBg", "l/001/img/xobjbg._img", find_hd_asset("hd_assets/l/001/img/xobjBg"), [
                ("Chandelier & Window", (1280, 0)),
                ("Stone Wall & Curtain", (1950, 450)),
                ("Globe & Books", (1300, 1150)),
            ]),
            ("room_005_objBg", "l/005/img/objbg._img", find_hd_asset("hd_assets/l/005/img/objBg"), [
                ("Banner Crest", (1280, 120)),
                ("Red Carpet & Knight", (1250, 1150)),
                ("Cobweb & Wood Door", (2150, 560)),
            ]),
        ]

        # Extract raw 1x backgrounds
        raws = {}
        results = {}
        for tag, dat_path, cur_hd, _ in rooms:
            raw = extract_raw_bg(arc, dat_path)
            raws[tag] = raw
            print(f"[raw] {tag}: {raw.size} from {dat_path}", flush=True)
            results[tag] = {
                "CURRENT: 4x_NMKD-Siax (2021 GAN)": Image.open(cur_hd).convert("RGB"),
            }

        for mlabel, mpath in models:
            print(f"\n[load] {mlabel} ({mpath})", flush=True)
            desc = load_sr_model(mpath, device)
            for tag, _, _, _ in rooms:
                t0 = time.time()
                up = upscale_tiled(desc, raws[tag], tile=256, overlap=32, device=device)
                print(f"  {tag} -> {up.size} in {time.time() - t0:.2f}s", flush=True)
                results[tag][mlabel] = up
                short = mlabel.split()[0].replace("-", "_")
                up.save(os.path.join(args.out_dir, f"{tag}_{short}.png"), compress_level=3)
            del desc
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()

        for tag, _, _, crops in rooms:
            out_sheet = os.path.join(args.out_dir, f"{tag}_4way_zoom_1to1.png")
            make_4way_zoom_sheet(results[tag], crops, out_sheet)
            print(f"[saved] {out_sheet}", flush=True)

    elif args.apply_backgrounds:
        desc = load_sr_model(args.apply_backgrounds, device)
        count = 0
        t_all = time.time()
        for e in arc.entries:
            name = e["path"] if isinstance(e, dict) else e[0]
            norm = name.replace("\\", "/")
            if not norm.lower().endswith("._img"):
                continue
            raw = arc.read_entry(e)
            if len(raw) < 0x50:
                continue
            img_type = struct.unpack_from("<I", raw, 0)[0]
            if img_type != 0:
                continue
            nf = struct.unpack_from("<I", raw, 4)[0]
            if nf > 1:
                continue
            joff, jsz = struct.unpack_from("<II", raw, 0x40)
            if joff + jsz > len(raw) or jsz <= 4:
                continue
            im = Image.open(io.BytesIO(raw[joff : joff + jsz])).convert("RGB")
            up = upscale_tiled(desc, im, tile=256, overlap=32, device=device)
            stem_rel = norm[:-5]
            out_path = os.path.join("hd_assets", stem_rel + ".webp")
            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(out_path), exist_ok=True)
            up.save(out_path, "WEBP", lossless=True, exact=True, method=4)
            stale_png = os.path.join("hd_assets", stem_rel + ".png")
            if os.path.exists(stale_png):
                os.remove(stale_png)
            count += 1
            if count % 10 == 0:
                print(f"  [{count}] {out_path} ({up.size}) elapsed {time.time() - t_all:.1f}s", flush=True)
        arc.close()
        bg_cache = os.path.join("hd_assets", ".hd_index.cache")
        if os.path.exists(bg_cache):
            os.remove(bg_cache)
        print(f"[done] Remastered {count} backgrounds in {time.time() - t_all:.1f}s (index cache cleared)", flush=True)

    elif args.apply_jpeg_strips:
        desc = load_sr_model(args.apply_jpeg_strips, device)
        scale = desc.scale
        spr_root = os.path.join("hd_assets", "sprites")
        jobs = []
        removed_strays = 0
        removed_unsplit = 0
        for e in arc.entries:
            name = e["path"] if isinstance(e, dict) else e[0]
            norm = name.replace("\\", "/")
            if not norm.lower().endswith("._img"):
                continue
            raw = arc.read_entry(e)
            if len(raw) < 0x50 or struct.unpack_from("<I", raw, 0)[0] != 0:
                continue
            nf, w, h = struct.unpack_from("<3I", raw, 4)
            joff, jsz = struct.unpack_from("<II", raw, 0x40)
            if nf <= 1 or joff + jsz > len(raw) or jsz <= 4:
                continue

            stem = norm[:-5]
            for ext in (".webp", ".png"):
                stray_p = os.path.join("hd_assets", stem + ext)
                if os.path.exists(stray_p):
                    os.remove(stray_p)
                    removed_strays += 1

            assert (h % nf) == 0, f"{norm}: height {h} not divisible by nf={nf}"
            fh = h // nf
            if w < 16 or fh < 16:
                continue

            im = Image.open(io.BytesIO(raw[joff : joff + jsz])).convert("RGB")
            arr = np.asarray(im, dtype=np.uint8)
            assert arr.shape[0] == h and arr.shape[1] == w, (
                f"{norm}: decoded shape {arr.shape} != expected ({h}, {w})"
            )

            for ext in (".webp", ".png"):
                unsplit_p = os.path.join(spr_root, stem + ext)
                if os.path.exists(unsplit_p):
                    os.remove(unsplit_p)
                    removed_unsplit += 1

            jobs.append((stem, nf, w, fh, arr))
        arc.close()

        total_frames = sum(nf for _, nf, _, _, _ in jobs)
        print(
            f"[jpeg-strips] Splitting & 4x-remastering {len(jobs)} strips ({total_frames} frames) "
            f"with {args.apply_jpeg_strips} (removed {removed_strays} root strays, {removed_unsplit} unsplit sprites)...",
            flush=True,
        )
        t_all = time.time()
        pad = 16
        done_frames = 0
        for stem, nf, w, fh, arr in jobs:
            for fi in range(nf):
                frame = arr[fi * fh : (fi + 1) * fh, 0 : w, :3]
                padded = np.pad(frame, ((pad, pad), (pad, pad), (0, 0)), mode="edge")
                c4_pad = upscale_fast(desc, padded, device)
                c4 = c4_pad[pad * scale : pad * scale + fh * scale, pad * scale : pad * scale + w * scale]
                dst = os.path.join(spr_root, f"{stem}_f{fi:03d}.webp")
                os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dst), exist_ok=True)
                Image.fromarray(c4, "RGB").save(dst, "WEBP", lossless=True, exact=True, method=4)
                done_frames += 1
            if done_frames % 50 == 0 or done_frames == total_frames:
                el = time.time() - t_all
                print(f"  [jpeg-strips] {done_frames}/{total_frames} frames ({el:.1f}s elapsed)", flush=True)

        for cname in (".hd_sprite_index.cache", ".hd_index.cache"):
            cpath = os.path.join("hd_assets", cname)
            if os.path.exists(cpath):
                os.remove(cpath)
        print(f"[done] Remastered {done_frames} frames across {len(jobs)} JPEG strips in {time.time() - t_all:.1f}s.", flush=True)

    elif args.apply_sprites:
        desc = load_sr_model(args.apply_sprites, device)
        scale = desc.scale
        spr_root = os.path.join("hd_assets", "sprites")
        jobs = []  # (rel_stem, rgba, has_t2, is_opaque)
        for e in arc.entries:
            path = e["path"] if isinstance(e, dict) else e[0]
            if not path.endswith("._img"):
                continue
            low = path.lower()
            if "/fnt" in low or low.endswith("/objfont._img"):
                continue
            raw = arc.read_entry(e)
            if len(raw) < 0x50 or struct.unpack_from("<I", raw, 0)[0] != 1:
                continue
            pal_off, pal_cnt = struct.unpack_from("<II", raw, 0x28)
            palette = list(struct.unpack_from(f"<{pal_cnt}I", raw, pal_off)) \
                if (pal_off and pal_cnt and pal_off + pal_cnt * 4 <= len(raw)) else []
            t0_off, t0_cnt, t1_off, t1_cnt, t2_off, t2_cnt = struct.unpack_from("<6I", raw, 0x38)
            has_t2 = bool(t2_off and t2_cnt)
            n = max(t1_cnt, t2_cnt)
            for fi in range(n):
                rgba = composite_frame(raw, palette, fi, t0_off, t0_cnt, t1_off, t1_cnt, t2_off, t2_cnt)
                if rgba is None or rgba.shape[0] < 16 or rgba.shape[1] < 16:
                    continue
                is_opaque = bool((rgba[:, :, 3] == 255).all())
                suffix = "" if n == 1 else f"_f{fi:03d}"
                jobs.append((path[:-5] + suffix, rgba, has_t2, is_opaque))
        arc.close()
        print(f"[sprites] Upscaling ALL {len(jobs)} IT3 sprites with {args.apply_sprites}...", flush=True)
        t_all = time.time()
        for idx, (rel_stem, rgba, has_t2, is_opaque) in enumerate(jobs, 1):
            h, w = rgba.shape[:2]
            tw, th = w * scale, h * scale
            if is_opaque:
                c4 = upscale_fast(desc, rgba[:, :, :3], device)
                a4 = np.full((th, tw), 255, dtype=np.uint8)
            else:
                rgb = dilate_rgb(rgba)
                a = rgba[:, :, 3]
                pad = 0 if has_t2 else 4
                pad4 = pad * scale
                if pad > 0:
                    rgb = np.pad(rgb, ((pad, pad), (pad, pad), (0, 0)), mode="edge")
                    a = np.pad(a, ((pad, pad), (pad, pad)), mode="constant", constant_values=0)
                c4 = upscale_fast(desc, rgb, device)
                a_rgb = np.stack([a, a, a], axis=-1)
                a4 = upscale_fast(desc, a_rgb, device)[:, :, 0]
                if pad4 > 0:
                    c4 = c4[pad4 : pad4 + th, pad4 : pad4 + tw]
                    a4 = a4[pad4 : pad4 + th, pad4 : pad4 + tw]
                if not has_t2:
                    a4 = np.asarray(Image.fromarray(a4, "L").filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(radius=1.0)))
                a4 = np.where(a4 <= 8, 0, np.where(a4 >= 248, 255, a4)).astype(np.uint8)
            out = np.dstack([c4, a4])
            dst = os.path.join(spr_root, rel_stem + ".webp")
            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dst), exist_ok=True)
            Image.fromarray(out, "RGBA").save(dst, "WEBP", lossless=True, exact=True, method=4)
            stale_png = os.path.join(spr_root, rel_stem + ".png")
            if os.path.exists(stale_png):
                os.remove(stale_png)
            if idx % 500 == 0 or idx == len(jobs):
                el = time.time() - t_all
                eta = el / idx * (len(jobs) - idx)
                print(f"  [sprites] {idx}/{len(jobs)} ({el:.1f}s elapsed, ~{eta:.0f}s left)", flush=True)

        cache = os.path.join("hd_assets", ".hd_sprite_index.cache")
        if os.path.exists(cache):
            os.remove(cache)
        print(f"[done] Remastered ALL {len(jobs)} sprites in {time.time() - t_all:.1f}s (HDS5 cache cleared).", flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/upscale_videos.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Moorhuhn Adventure 2 — AI Neural FMV Cutscene Remaster Tool (Real-ESRGAN Video + FFmpeg).

Upscales game_files/*.avi cutscenes (mha2il.avi, mha2ol.avi, mha2sl.avi) on your GPU
using realesr-animevideov3 and writes clean high-bitrate H.264 MP4 replacements into
hd_assets/videos/<name>.mp4, which runtime/win32_avi.c loads automatically in sync!

Usage:
  python3 tools/upscale_videos.py                          # Upscale all 800x600 cutscenes 2x (1600x1200)
  python3 tools/upscale_videos.py --scale 2 --only mha2il  # Upscale intro cutscene only
  python3 tools/upscale_videos.py --scale 4                # Full 4x (3200x2400) cutscenes
"""
import argparse
import glob
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from upscale_ai import detect_best_gpu, find_realesrgan_cmd


def probe_fps(path):
    p = subprocess.run(
        ["ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-select_streams", "v:0",
         "-show_entries", "stream=r_frame_rate", "-of", "default=nw=1:nk=1", path],
        stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, text=True
    )
    txt = (p.stdout or "").strip()
    if "/" in txt:
        num, den = txt.split("/", 1)
        try:
            return float(num) / float(den)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return 25.0


def upscale_one_video(avi_path, out_mp4, cmd_prefix, gpu_id, scale, model, threads, crf):
    fps = probe_fps(avi_path)
    work = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="mha2_vid_ai_")
    in_dir = os.path.join(work, "frames_in")
    out_dir = os.path.join(work, "frames_out")
    os.makedirs(in_dir, exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)

    try:
        t0 = time.time()
        print(f"[VID] Extracting frames from {avi_path} ({fps:.2f} fps)...")
        # Mild deblock filter before neural upscale cleans Cinepak 4x4 macroblocks
        subprocess.run(
            ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-i", avi_path,
             "-vf", "deblock=filter=weak:block=4", "-q:v", "2",
             os.path.join(in_dir, "f_%06d.jpg")],
            check=True
        )
        frames = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(in_dir, "f_*.jpg")))
        n_frames = len(frames)
        print(f"[VID] Upscaling {n_frames} frames with {model} {scale}x on GPU {gpu_id}...", flush=True)
        # Process in chunks of 500 frames so VRAM & queues stay rock-solid
        chunk_sz = 500
        for ci in range(0, n_frames, chunk_sz):
            sub_in = os.path.join(work, f"cin_{ci}")
            sub_out = os.path.join(work, f"cout_{ci}")
            os.makedirs(sub_in, exist_ok=True)
            os.makedirs(sub_out, exist_ok=True)
            batch = frames[ci : ci + chunk_sz]
            for fp in batch:
                shutil.move(fp, os.path.join(sub_in, os.path.basename(fp)))
            cmd = cmd_prefix + [
                "-i", sub_in,
                "-o", sub_out,
                "-n", model,
                "-s", str(scale),
                "-t", "256",
                "-f", "jpg",
                "-j", threads,
            ]
            if gpu_id != "auto":
                cmd += ["-g", str(gpu_id)]
            print(f"[VID]   chunk {ci + 1}..{ci + len(batch)} / {n_frames}...", flush=True)
            subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
            for fp in glob.glob(os.path.join(sub_out, "f_*.jpg")):
                shutil.move(fp, os.path.join(out_dir, os.path.basename(fp)))
            shutil.rmtree(sub_in, ignore_errors=True)
            shutil.rmtree(sub_out, ignore_errors=True)

        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(out_mp4), exist_ok=True)
        print(f"[VID] Encoding HD cutscene -> {out_mp4}...", flush=True)
        subprocess.run(
            ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error",
             "-framerate", f"{fps:.6f}",
             "-i", os.path.join(out_dir, "f_%06d.jpg"),
             "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "medium", "-crf", str(crf),
             "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", "-movflags", "+faststart",
             out_mp4],
            check=True
        )
        dt = time.time() - t0
        sz_mb = os.path.getsize(out_mp4) / (1024 * 1024)
        print(f"[VID] Done: {out_mp4} ({sz_mb:.1f} MB) in {dt:.1f}s.")
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(work, ignore_errors=True)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="AI Neural Remaster for Moorhuhn Adventure 2 AVI cutscenes")
    ap.add_argument("--game-dir", default="game_files", help="Directory containing .avi files")
    ap.add_argument("-o", "--out", default="hd_assets/videos", help="Output directory (default: hd_assets/videos)")
    ap.add_argument("--only", default="", help="Only process AVI matching substring (e.g. 'mha2il')")
    ap.add_argument("--include-small", action="store_true", help="Also upscale small *h.avi variants")
    ap.add_argument("--scale", type=int, default=2, choices=[2, 3, 4], help="Upscale factor (default: 2 -> 1600x1200)")
    ap.add_argument("--model", default="realesr-animevideov3", help="Model (default: realesr-animevideov3)")
    ap.add_argument("--gpu", default="auto", help="Vulkan GPU ID (default: auto)")
    ap.add_argument("--threads", default="1:1:1", help="load:proc:save threads (default: 1:1:1)")
    ap.add_argument("--crf", type=int, default=16, help="H.264 CRF quality (default: 16)")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    cmd_prefix = find_realesrgan_cmd()
    gpu_id = detect_best_gpu(cmd_prefix) if args.gpu == "auto" else args.gpu

    avis = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(args.game_dir, "*.avi")))
    for avi in avis:
        stem = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(avi))[0]
        if args.only and args.only.lower() not in stem.lower():
            continue
        if not args.include_small and stem.endswith("h"):
            continue
        out_mp4 = os.path.join(args.out, stem + ".mp4")
        upscale_one_video(avi, out_mp4, cmd_prefix, gpu_id, args.scale, args.model, args.threads, args.crf)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```
